        layout_mode,
        retention == cached_exact::Retention::Semantic,
        include_imports,
        [
            raw.total,
            raw.string_bytes,
            raw.exact_expressions,
            raw.literal_fixed_discount,
            runtime.total,
            runtime.string_bytes,
            runtime.exact_expressions,
            structure_input,
            string_input,
            prior_fixed_hir_upper,
            fixed_hir_upper,
            identity_occurrence_upper,
            identity_carrier_bytes,
            retained_clone_and_hir,
            generic_instances.bytes,
            transient_import_clone,
            original_mode7_fixed_upper,
            prelude_hir_upper,
            hir_upper,
        ],
    );
    let result = SyntheticBuilderCosts {
        raw_clone_and_hir: checked_usage(
            retained_clone_and_hir,
            transient_import_clone,
            "builder_bytes",
            active_builder_limit(),
        )?,
        retained_clone_and_hir,
        transient_import_clone,
        runtime: runtime.total,
        retained_hir: hir_upper,
        synthetic_ast: checked_usage(
            raw.total,
            runtime.total,
            "builder_bytes",
            active_builder_limit(),
        )?,
    };
    byte_proofs.complete().map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
    Ok(result)
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_program_with_registry(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    programs: &[Program],
    registry: Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    synthetic_program_with_registry_using(program, authored, programs, registry, None)
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_program_core<'a>(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
    programs: &'a [Program],
    registry: Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
    core: &mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'a>,
) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    synthetic_program_with_registry_using(program, authored, programs, registry, Some(core))
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_program_with_registry_using<'a>(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
    programs: &'a [Program],
    registry: Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
    core: Option<&mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'a>>,
) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let fresh;
    let held;
    let layout = if let Some(registry) = registry {
        held = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
        &*held
    } else {
        fresh = private_layout::LayoutClosure::derive(program, authored, programs)?;
        &fresh
    };
    let mut byte_proofs = import_stub::core_proofs::Phase::new(authored, programs, core)
        .map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
    let mut synthetic = program.clone();
    synthetic.module_uses.clear();
    // Project validation already authenticated static implementation sidecars.
    // Runtime HIR has no witness or dispatch representation for them.
    synthetic.implementations.clear();
    for module_use in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|item| item.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
    {
        let target = &authored[module_use.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let mut ty = target.ty.expect("validated type target").clone();
        ty.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&module_use.alias);
        super::super::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            &mut ty,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(layout),
        )?;
        synthetic.types.push(ty);
    }
    layout.append_types(&mut synthetic, program, programs)?;
    let type_index_bytes = synthetic
        .types
        .len()
        .checked_mul(std::mem::size_of::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>())
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    reserve_builder_structure(type_index_bytes)?;
    let mut type_declarations = Vec::with_capacity(synthetic.types.len());
    for declaration in &synthetic.types {
        type_declarations.push((declaration.name.as_str(), declaration));
    }
    type_declarations.sort_by(|left, right| left.0.cmp(right.0));
    if type_declarations
        .windows(2)
        .any(|items| items[0].0 == items[1].0)
    {
        return Err(vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "synthetic workspace type-name index is not unique",
        )]);
    }
    for module_use in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|item| item.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
    {
        let target = &authored[module_use.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let target_function = target.function.expect("validated function target");
        let retain_body = programs
            .iter()
            .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
            .is_some_and(|provider| {
                crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper(provider, target_function).is_some()
                    || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper(provider, target_function).is_some()
            });
        let mut function = if retain_body {
            target_function.clone()
        } else {
            import_stub::signature(target_function)
        };
        function.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&module_use.alias);
        if retain_body {
            synthetic.functions.push(function);
            continue;
        }
        for param in &mut function.params {
            type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
                &mut param.ty,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                Some(layout),
            )?;
        }
        type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
            &mut function.return_type,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(layout),
        )?;
        // Clearing would retain the cloned contracts' Vec capacities in every
        // finished stub. Replace the vectors so the full imported contracts
        // remain transient, matching the final peak-only pre-bound.
        function.requires = Vec::new();
        function.ensures = Vec::new();
        function.body = default_expr(
            &function,
            &type_declarations,
            super::super::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                program, target, authored, programs, layout,
            ),
            import_stub::byte_helper_authorized(
                target_function,
                target.module,
                byte_proofs.get().map_err(|error| vec![error])?,
            )?,
        )?;
        synthetic.functions.push(function);
    }
    if !synthetic
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
    {
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Function>())?;
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())?;
        synthetic.functions.push(Function {
            stable_id: budgeted_format(format_args!(
                "workspace.synthetic.main.{}",
                synthetic.module
            )),
            explicit_id: true,
            name: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone("main"),
            name_span: Span::default(),
            type_parameters: Vec::new(),
            params: Vec::new(),
            return_type: Type::I64,
            effects: Vec::new(),
            yields: None,
            follows: None,
            requires: Vec::new(),
            ensures: Vec::new(),
            body: Expr {
                kind: ExprKind::Int(0),
                span: Span::default(),
            },
            span: Span::default(),
        });
    }
    byte_proofs.complete().map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
    Ok(synthetic)
}
//! Authenticated authored ordinary calls. This proof owns no heap allocation.
//! Scratch exhaustion, incomplete traversal and ambiguous headers keep the old
//! charge. Synthetic/default/generic trees never enter this census.
use super::{AuthoredDeclaration, call_identity};
use crate::ast::{
    Expr, ExprKind, MatchPattern, ModuleUseKind, Program, RecordMatchFieldPattern, Statement,
};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

#[derive(Default)]
pub(super) struct Proof {
    pub(super) roots: usize,
    pub(super) bytes: usize,
}
impl Proof {
    fn charge<T>(&mut self, value: &T) {
        self.bytes = self
            .bytes
            .saturating_add(std::mem::size_of_val(value))
            .saturating_add(std::mem::size_of::<&mut Self>())
            .saturating_add(std::mem::size_of::<&T>());
    }
}

/// The names are deliberately function-wide: even a binding in a disjoint arm
/// excludes its spelling. Bounded scratch avoids an unpriced tree/index.
struct Names<'a> {
    entries: [Option<&'a str>; 128],
    len: usize,
}
impl<'a> Names<'a> {
    fn insert(&mut self, name: &'a str, proof: &mut Proof) -> bool {
        proof.charge(&(&*self, name));
        if self.len == self.entries.len() {
            return false;
        }
        self.entries[self.len] = Some(name);
        self.len += 1;
        true
    }
    fn contains(&self, name: &str, proof: &mut Proof) -> bool {
        proof.charge(&(self, name));
        // One scalar cursor. No iterator, closure environment, Some(name)
        // construction or Option equality pipeline is formed by this search.
        proof.bytes = proof.bytes.saturating_add(std::mem::size_of::<usize>());
        let mut index = 0;
        loop {
            // Actual cursor/length comparison values and its boolean result,
            // including the terminal probe on an absent spelling.
            proof.bytes = proof
                .bytes
                .saturating_add(2 * std::mem::size_of::<usize>() + std::mem::size_of::<bool>());
            if index == self.len {
                proof.charge(&false);
                return false;
            }
            // Array Index actual/formal receivers and indices, returned option
            // loan. Matching a borrowed place forms no Option<&str> value.
            proof.bytes = proof.bytes.saturating_add(
                2 * std::mem::size_of::<&[Option<&str>; 128]>()
                    + 2 * std::mem::size_of::<usize>()
                    + std::mem::size_of::<&Option<&str>>(),
            );
            if let Some(item) = &self.entries[index] {
                // The &&str pattern binding, str::eq's actual/formal fat loans,
                // and its returned boolean all belong to this reached probe.
                proof.bytes = proof.bytes.saturating_add(
                    std::mem::size_of::<&&str>()
                        + 4 * std::mem::size_of::<&str>()
                        + std::mem::size_of::<bool>(),
                );
                if str::eq(*item, name) {
                    proof.charge(&true);
                    return true;
                }
            }
            proof.bytes = proof.bytes.saturating_add(std::mem::size_of::<usize>());
            index += 1;
        }
    }

    fn pattern(&mut self, pattern: &'a MatchPattern, depth: usize, proof: &mut Proof) -> bool {
        proof.charge(&(&*self, pattern, depth));
        if depth == 128 {
            return false;
        }
        match pattern {
            MatchPattern::Binding { name, .. } => self.insert(name, proof),
            MatchPattern::Variant { fields, .. } => {
                fields.iter().all(|f| self.insert(&f.binding, proof))
            }
            MatchPattern::Record { fields, .. } => fields
                .iter()
                .all(|f| self.record(&f.pattern, depth + 1, proof)),
            MatchPattern::Or { alternatives, .. } => alternatives
                .iter()
                .all(|p| self.pattern(p, depth + 1, proof)),
            _ => true,
        }
    }
    fn record(
        &mut self,
        pattern: &'a RecordMatchFieldPattern,
        depth: usize,
        proof: &mut Proof,
    ) -> bool {
        proof.charge(&(&*self, pattern, depth));
        if depth == 128 {
            return false;
        }
        match pattern {
            RecordMatchFieldPattern::Binding { name, .. } => self.insert(name, proof),
            RecordMatchFieldPattern::Record { fields, .. } => fields
                .iter()
                .all(|f| self.record(&f.pattern, depth + 1, proof)),
            RecordMatchFieldPattern::Wildcard { .. } => true,
        }
    }
}

fn compiler_dispatch(name: &str, proof: &mut Proof) -> bool {
    let result = crate::vec_ops::field_unique::literal_call(name)
        || name == crate::vec_ops::field_update::NAME
        || name == crate::vec_field::NAME
        || name == crate::literal_format::NAME
        || crate::string_ops::by_name(name).is_some()
        || crate::str_ops::by_name(name).is_some()
        || crate::vec_ops::by_name(name).is_some()
        || crate::box_ops::by_name(name).is_some()
        || crate::iterator_ops::by_name(name).is_some()
        || crate::list_ops::by_name(name).is_some()
        || crate::map_ops::by_name(name).is_some()
        || crate::byte_ops::by_name(name).is_some()
        || crate::host_io_ops::by_name(name).is_some()
        || crate::command_io_ops::by_name(name).is_some();
    proof.charge(&(name, result));
    result
}

fn target(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    imports: bool,
    name: &str,
    arity: usize,
    proof: &mut Proof,
) -> bool {
    proof.charge(&(program, authored, imports, name, arity));
    if compiler_dispatch(name, proof) {
        return false;
    }
    for interface in &program.interfaces {
        proof.charge(&interface);
        for import in &interface.imports {
            proof.charge(&import);
            if import.name == name {
                return false;
            }
        }
    }
    let mut found = None;
    let mut ambiguous = false;
    for function in &program.functions {
        proof.charge(&function);
        if function.name == name {
            ambiguous |= found.is_some();
            found = Some(function);
        }
    }
    for item in program.module_uses.iter().filter(|_| imports) {
        proof.charge(&item);
        if item.alias != name {
            continue;
        }
        // Any competing nonfunction alias also closes the proof.
        if item.kind != ModuleUseKind::Function {
            return false;
        }
        let Some(header) = authored.get(item.persistent_id.as_str()) else {
            return false;
        };
        proof.charge(&header);
        if header.module != item.target_module {
            return false;
        }
        let Some(function) = header.function else {
            return false;
        };
        proof.charge(&function);
        if function.stable_id != item.persistent_id {
            return false;
        }
        ambiguous |= found.is_some();
        found = Some(function);
    }
    proof.charge(&(found, ambiguous));
    let admitted = !ambiguous
        && found.is_some_and(|f| f.type_parameters.is_empty() && f.params.len() == arity);
    proof.charge(&admitted);
    admitted
}

fn count_linear(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    imports: bool,
) -> Proof {
    let mut proof = Proof::default();
    proof.charge(&(program, authored, imports));
    proof.bytes = proof.bytes.saturating_add(std::mem::size_of::<Proof>());
    for function in &program.functions {
        proof.charge(&function);
        // Generic owner bodies can be materialized repeatedly; no discount.
        if !function.type_parameters.is_empty() {
            continue;
        }
        let mut names = Names {
            entries: [None; 128],
            len: 0,
        };
        proof.charge(&names);
        let mut complete = function.params.iter().all(|p| {
            proof.charge(&p);
            names.insert(&p.name, &mut proof)
        });
        for root in call_identity::roots(function) {
            // Fixed walker scratch is retained for this complete pass, including
            // failed proofs. Each visited formal/reference is charged too.
            proof.bytes = proof
                .bytes
                .saturating_add(std::mem::size_of::<[Option<(&Expr, usize)>; 129]>());
            complete &= call_identity::walk(root, &mut |expression| {
                proof.charge(&expression);
                match &expression.kind {
                    ExprKind::Block { statements, .. } => statements.iter().all(|s| match s {
                        Statement::Let { name, .. } | Statement::Assign { name, .. } => {
                            names.insert(name, &mut proof)
                        }
                        Statement::For { item, .. } | Statement::ForOwn { item, .. } => {
                            names.insert(item, &mut proof)
                        }
                        Statement::ForBorrow { item, index, .. } => {
                            names.insert(item, &mut proof)
                                && index
                                    .as_ref()
                                    .is_none_or(|i| names.insert(&i.name, &mut proof))
                        }
                        _ => true,
                    }),
                    ExprKind::Closure { params, .. } => params.iter().all(|p| {
                        proof.charge(&p);
                        names.insert(&p.name, &mut proof)
                    }),
                    ExprKind::Match { arms, .. } => arms
                        .iter()
                        .all(|a| names.pattern(&a.pattern, 0, &mut proof)),
                    _ => true,
                }
            });
        }
        proof.charge(&complete);
        if !complete {
            continue;
        }
        let before = proof.roots;
        proof.charge(&before);
        for root in call_identity::roots(function) {
            if !visit(
                root, false, true, 0, &names, program, authored, imports, &mut proof,
            ) {
                proof.roots = before;
                break;
            }
        }
    }
    proof
}

#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
fn visit(
    expression: &Expr,
    free: bool,
    outside: bool,
    depth: usize,
    names: &Names<'_>,
    program: &Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    imports: bool,
    proof: &mut Proof,
) -> bool {
    proof.charge(&(
        expression, free, outside, depth, names, program, authored, imports,
    ));
    if depth == 128 {
        return false;
    }
    if let ExprKind::Call {
        name,
        type_arguments,
        args,
    } = &expression.kind
    {
        if free
            && outside
            && type_arguments.is_empty()
            && !names.contains(name, proof)
            && target(program, authored, imports, name, args.len(), proof)
        {
            proof.roots = proof.roots.saturating_add(1);
        }
    }
    let outside = outside && !matches!(expression.kind, ExprKind::Closure { .. });
    let mut index = 0;
    while let Some(child) = expression.child(index) {
        let free = match &expression.kind {
            ExprKind::Unary { .. }
            | ExprKind::Binary { .. }
            | ExprKind::If { .. }
            | ExprKind::Call { .. } => true,
            ExprKind::Block { tail, .. } => std::ptr::eq(child, tail.as_ref()),
            _ => false,
        };
        proof.charge(&(index, child, free, outside));
        if !visit(
            child,
            free,
            outside,
            depth + 1,
            names,
            program,
            authored,
            imports,
            proof,
        ) {
            return false;
        }
        index += 1;
    }
    let complete = true;
    proof.charge(&complete);
    complete
}

//! Independent AST/HIR absence and complete old/current forecast controls.
use super::super::{cached_exact, call_carriers_original, dependency_identity_max};
use super::*;
use crate::hir::{ResolvedExpr, ResolvedExprKind};
use std::mem::size_of;

fn carriers() -> usize {
    size_of::<crate::hir::ResolvedBinding>()
        + size_of::<crate::hir::ResolvedStatement>()
        + size_of::<crate::hir::ResolvedFieldInitializer>()
        + size_of::<crate::hir::ResolvedMatchArm>()
        + size_of::<crate::hir::ResolvedMatchPatternField>()
        + size_of::<crate::hir::ResolvedRecordMatchPatternField>()
}
fn parse(source: &str) -> Program {
    crate::parse(source, "ordinary.spx").unwrap()
}
fn proof(source: &str) -> Proof {
    let programs = vec![parse(source)];
    let authored = crate::workspace_graph::index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    count(&programs[0], &authored, true)
}
const HEADER: &str = "module ordinary; @id(\"ordinary.f\") fn f()->i64 { 1 } ";
fn dense(n: usize) -> String {
    let calls = std::iter::repeat_n("f()", n)
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join(" + ");
    format!("{HEADER}@id(\"ordinary.main\") fn main()->i64 {{ {calls} }}")
}

/// Independent full AST position oracle for the closed f/g fixture, including
/// every delegated child. Target/binding hostility is tested separately.
fn ast(expression: &Expr, free: bool, outside: bool) -> (usize, usize) {
    let mut result = (
        1,
        usize::from(
            free && outside
                && matches!(&expression.kind,
        ExprKind::Call { name, type_arguments, .. } if (name == "f" || name == "g") && type_arguments.is_empty()),
        ),
    );
    let outside = outside && !matches!(expression.kind, ExprKind::Closure { .. });
    let mut i = 0;
    while let Some(child) = expression.child(i) {
        let child_free = match &expression.kind {
            ExprKind::Unary { value, .. } => std::ptr::eq(child, value.as_ref()),
            ExprKind::Binary { left, right, .. } => {
                std::ptr::eq(child, left.as_ref()) || std::ptr::eq(child, right.as_ref())
            }
            ExprKind::If {
                condition,
                then_branch,
                else_branch,
            } => [condition, then_branch, else_branch]
                .iter()
                .any(|v| std::ptr::eq(child, v.as_ref())),
            ExprKind::Call { args, .. } => args.iter().any(|v| std::ptr::eq(child, v)),
            ExprKind::Block { tail, .. } => std::ptr::eq(child, tail.as_ref()),
            _ => false,
        };
        let next = ast(child, child_free, outside);
        result.0 += next.0;
        result.1 += next.1;
        i += 1;
    }
    result
}

/// Walk actual resolved containers, independently of AST tags. Each qualified
/// root is a bare Call in an owning Box/vector; no auxiliary carrier is formed
/// by its own variant. Statement-owned/delegated roots are excluded.
fn hir(expression: &ResolvedExpr, free: bool) -> (usize, usize) {
    let mut result = (1, 0);
    if let ResolvedExprKind::Call {
        type_arguments,
        instance,
        ..
    } = &expression.kind
    {
        assert!(type_arguments.is_empty());
        assert!(instance.is_none());
        result.1 += usize::from(free);
    }
    match &expression.kind {
        ResolvedExprKind::Call { args, .. } => {
            for arg in args {
                let next = hir(arg, true);
                result.0 += next.0;
                result.1 += next.1;
            }
        }
        ResolvedExprKind::Unary { value, .. } => {
            let next = hir(value, true);
            result.0 += next.0;
            result.1 += next.1;
        }
        ResolvedExprKind::Binary { left, right, .. } => {
            for child in [left, right] {
                let next = hir(child, true);
                result.0 += next.0;
                result.1 += next.1;
            }
        }
        ResolvedExprKind::If {
            condition,
            then_branch,
            else_branch,
        } => {
            for child in [condition, then_branch, else_branch] {
                let next = hir(child, true);
                result.0 += next.0;
                result.1 += next.1;
            }
        }
        ResolvedExprKind::Block { statements, tail } => {
            for statement in statements {
                for i in 0..statement.child_count() {
                    let next = hir(statement.child(i).unwrap(), false);
                    result.0 += next.0;
                    result.1 += next.1;
                }
            }
            let next = hir(tail, true);
            result.0 += next.0;
            result.1 += next.1;
        }
        ResolvedExprKind::Int(_) | ResolvedExprKind::Place(_) | ResolvedExprKind::Bool(_) => {}
        other => panic!("oracle fixture must explicitly own every HIR variant: {other:?}"),
    }
    result
}

fn costs(
    source: &str,
    mode: u8,
    retention: cached_exact::Retention,
) -> (usize, usize, usize, usize) {
    let programs = vec![parse(source)];
    let authored = crate::workspace_graph::index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let program = &programs[0];
    let maximum = Some(dependency_identity_max(program, &authored, &programs).unwrap());
    let old = call_carriers_original::synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry(
        program,
        &authored,
        &programs,
        maximum,
        true,
        (mode, retention),
        None,
    )
    .unwrap();
    let current =
        super::super::held_projection::synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry(
            program,
            &authored,
            &programs,
            maximum,
            true,
            (mode, retention),
            None,
        )
        .unwrap();
    (
        old.retained_hir,
        current.retained_hir,
        old.retained_clone_and_hir,
        current.retained_clone_and_hir,
    )
}

#[test]
fn complete_old_current_algorithm_delta_keeps_storage_children_and_cold_proof_fees() {
    for source in [
        dense(1),
        dense(64),
        format!("{HEADER}@id(\"ordinary.main\") fn main()->i64 {{ let x = f(); f() + x }}"),
    ] {
        let program = parse(&source);
        let checked = crate::check(&source, "ordinary.spx").unwrap();
        let resolved = crate::hir::resolve(&checked).unwrap();
        crate::hir::validate(&resolved).unwrap();
        let ast_census = program
            .functions
            .iter()
            .map(|f| ast(&f.body, false, true))
            .fold((0, 0), |a, b| (a.0 + b.0, a.1 + b.1));
        let hir_census = resolved
            .functions
            .iter()
            .map(|f| hir(&f.body, false))
            .fold((0, 0), |a, b| (a.0 + b.0, a.1 + b.1));
        assert_eq!(ast_census, hir_census);
        let p = proof(&source);
        assert_eq!(p.roots, ast_census.1);
        assert!(p.bytes > 0);
        for (mode, retention, copies) in [
            (5, cached_exact::Retention::Parsed, 1),
            (6, cached_exact::Retention::Parsed, 1),
            (7, cached_exact::Retention::Parsed, 1),
            (7, cached_exact::Retention::Semantic, 2),
        ] {
            let (old_hir, new_hir, old_total, new_total) = costs(&source, mode, retention);
            assert_eq!(new_hir + copies * p.roots * carriers(), old_hir + p.bytes);
            assert_eq!(
                new_total + copies * p.roots * carriers(),
                old_total + p.bytes
            );
            // Cold repeated estimation must keep the same proof invoice.
            assert_eq!(
                costs(&source, mode, retention),
                (old_hir, new_hir, old_total, new_total)
            );
        }
        for mode in 0..5 {
            let (a, b, c, d) = costs(&source, mode, cached_exact::Retention::Parsed);
            assert_eq!((a, c), (b, d));
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn hostile_target_binding_and_delegated_routes_fail_closed() {
    for body in [
        "fn main(f:fn()->i64)->i64 { f() }",
        "fn main()->i64 { let f = fn()->i64 { 2 }; f() }",
        "fn main()->i64 { let x = f(); x }",
        "fn main()->i64 { let callback = fn()->i64 { f() }; callback() }",
        "fn main()->i64 { missing() }",
        "fn main()->i64 { f<i64>() }",
        "fn main()->i64 { let f = 2; f() }",
        "fn main()->i64 { match true { f => f(), } }",
        "fn main()->i64 { match true { f => 1, _ => f(), } }",
    ] {
        let source = format!("{HEADER}@id(\"ordinary.main\") {body}");
        assert_eq!(proof(&source).roots, 0, "{body}");
        assert!(proof(&source).bytes > 0, "failed proof still pays");
    }
    assert_eq!(proof("module ordinary; @id(\"ordinary.f\") fn f<T>()->i64{1} @id(\"ordinary.main\") fn main()->i64{f()}").roots, 0);
    for name in [
        "byte_len",
        "string_len",
        "vec_len",
        "box_new",
        "list_nil",
        "map_new",
    ] {
        assert_eq!(proof(&format!("module ordinary; @id(\"ordinary.f\") fn {name}()->i64{{1}} @id(\"ordinary.main\") fn main()->i64{{{name}()}}")).roots, 0, "{name}");
    }
    let source = "module ordinary; @id(\"ordinary.f\") fn f()->i64{1} @id(\"ordinary.main\") fn main<T>()->i64{f()}";
    assert_eq!(proof(source).roots, 0);
}

#[test]
fn imported_target_requires_actual_unique_authenticated_monomorphic_header() {
    let provider = parse("module provider; @id(\"provider.f\") fn f()->i64{1}");
    let consumer = parse(
        "module ordinary; use function @id(\"provider.f\") from provider as f; @id(\"ordinary.main\") fn main()->i64{f()}",
    );
    let programs = vec![provider, consumer];
    let authored = crate::workspace_graph::index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(count(&programs[1], &authored, true).roots, 1);
    assert_eq!(count(&programs[1], &authored, false).roots, 0);
    for edit in 0..3 {
        let mut forged = programs[1].clone();
        match edit {
            0 => forged.module_uses[0].target_module = "wrong".into(),
            1 => forged.module_uses[0].persistent_id = "missing".into(),
            _ => forged.module_uses.push(forged.module_uses[0].clone()),
        }
        assert_eq!(count(&forged, &authored, true).roots, 0);
    }
}

struct Limit(usize);
impl Drop for Limit {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        crate::workspace_graph::ACTIVE_BUILDER_LIMIT.with(|l| l.set(self.0));
    }
}
#[test]
fn exact_one_short_zero_and_whole_allocation_drop_controls() {
    let source = dense(64);
    let programs = vec![parse(&source)];
    let authored = crate::workspace_graph::index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    for retention in [
        cached_exact::Retention::Parsed,
        cached_exact::Retention::Semantic,
    ] {
        let expected = cached_exact::summed(&programs, &authored, retention).unwrap();
        let _restore =
            Limit(crate::workspace_graph::ACTIVE_BUILDER_LIMIT.with(|l| l.replace(expected.1)));
        assert_eq!(
            cached_exact::summed(&programs, &authored, retention).unwrap(),
            expected
        );
        for limit in [expected.1 - 1, 0] {
            crate::workspace_graph::ACTIVE_BUILDER_LIMIT.with(|l| l.set(limit));
            let errors = cached_exact::summed(&programs, &authored, retention).unwrap_err();
            assert!(errors.iter().all(|e| e.code == "SPX-G171"));
        }
    }
    let checked = crate::check(&source, "ordinary.spx").unwrap();
    // The complete resolver keeps more than the default 16,384 pointers live.
    // Freed slots are already reclaimed; this is simultaneous HIR backing,
    // not a tombstone leak. Use the existing explicit whole-HIR registry size;
    // its backing is prepared outside observation, with exhaustion still fatal.
    let (((after_resolve, after_validate, resolved_functions), allocation), phases) =
        crate::test_allocation_observer::with_phase_trace(|| {
            crate::test_allocation_observer::measure_with_capacity(1 << 16, || {
                let resolved = crate::hir::resolve(&checked).unwrap();
                let after_resolve = crate::test_allocation_observer::current_metrics();
                crate::hir::validate(&resolved).unwrap();
                let after_validate = crate::test_allocation_observer::current_metrics();
                let resolved_functions = resolved.functions.len();
                drop(resolved);
                (after_resolve, after_validate, resolved_functions)
            })
        });
    assert!(
        !phases.overflow,
        "fixed allocation-phase instrumentation overflow"
    );
    assert!(phases.intervals.iter().any(Option::is_some));
    eprintln!("ORDINARY_CALL_INCLUSIVE_PHASE_INTERVALS {phases:?}; cumulative highwaters, nested intervals, requested layouts only");
    assert!(
        !allocation.invalid,
        "complete HIR allocation observation: reason={:?}, metrics={allocation:?}",
        crate::test_allocation_observer::invalid_reason(),
    );
    assert_eq!(allocation.live, 0);
    assert_eq!(allocation.allocated, allocation.freed);
    assert!(allocation.allocations > 0);
    let (old_hir, forecast, old_clone_and_hir, current_clone_and_hir) =
        costs(&source, 5, cached_exact::Retention::Parsed);
    // These are snapshots of ONE observation, not independently measured phase
    // costs. Parse/check/authored inputs and the fixed registry are excluded;
    // public resolve (including its source verification), explicit validation,
    // and complete resolved-owner Drop remain inside the original window.
    eprintln!(
        "ORDINARY_CALL_WHOLE_HIR dense_calls=64 source_bytes={} modules={} authored_declarations={} checked_functions={} resolved_functions={resolved_functions} mode=5 retention=Parsed old_retained_hir={old_hir} current_retained_hir={forecast} old_retained_clone_and_hir={old_clone_and_hir} current_retained_clone_and_hir={current_clone_and_hir} after_resolve={after_resolve:?} after_validate={after_validate:?} after_drop={allocation:?}; phase snapshots are cumulative requested-layout metrics, not RSS or reservation debits",
        source.len(),
        programs.len(),
        authored.len(),
        checked.functions.len(),
    );
    assert!(
        allocation.highwater <= forecast,
        "whole public resolve + validate + Drop exceeds mode-5 Parsed retained-HIR forecast: forecast={forecast}, old_retained_hir={old_hir}, current_retained_clone_and_hir={current_clone_and_hir}, old_retained_clone_and_hir={old_clone_and_hir}, after_resolve={after_resolve:?}, after_validate={after_validate:?}, after_drop={allocation:?}, dense_calls=64, source_bytes={}, checked_functions={}, resolved_functions={resolved_functions}; parse/check inputs and observer registry excluded",
        source.len(),
        checked.functions.len(),
    );
}

// Independently reconstruct every typed fee of the closed no-binding dense
// family. No production fee/census helper supplies this invoice.
fn charged<T>() -> usize {
    size_of::<T>() + size_of::<&mut Proof>() + size_of::<&T>()
}
fn dense_invoice(n: usize) -> usize {
    type Index<'a> = BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>;
    let expressions = 2 * n + 2;
    let functions = 2;
    let entry = charged::<(&Program, &Index<'_>, bool)>() + size_of::<Proof>();
    let owners = functions
        * (charged::<Names<'_>>()
            + charged::<&crate::ast::Function>()
            + size_of::<[Option<(&Expr, usize)>; 129]>()
            + charged::<bool>()
            + charged::<usize>());
    let census = expressions
        * (charged::<&Expr>()
            + charged::<(
                &Expr,
                bool,
                bool,
                usize,
                &Names<'_>,
                &Program,
                &Index<'_>,
                bool,
            )>()
            + charged::<bool>())
        + (expressions - functions) * charged::<(usize, &Expr, bool, bool)>();
    let targets = n
        * (charged::<(&Names<'_>, &str)>()
            + size_of::<usize>() // empty-name cursor
            + 2 * size_of::<usize>() + size_of::<bool>() // terminal length probe
            + charged::<bool>()
            + charged::<(&Program, &Index<'_>, bool, &str, usize)>()
            + charged::<(&str, bool)>()
            + functions * charged::<&crate::ast::Function>()
            + charged::<(Option<&crate::ast::Function>, bool)>()
            + charged::<bool>());
    entry + owners + census + targets
}
#[test]
fn independent_cold_invoice_prices_zero_and_every_closed_dense_proof_role() {
    for n in [1, 2, 16, 64] {
        let source = dense(n);
        let programs = vec![parse(&source)];
        let authored = crate::workspace_graph::index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        let (p, allocation) =
            crate::test_allocation_observer::measure(|| count(&programs[0], &authored, true));
        assert_eq!(p.roots, n);
        let before = super::before_directory::count(&programs[0], &authored, true);
        assert_eq!(
            before.bytes,
            dense_invoice(n),
            "complete pre-directory tariff"
        );
        assert_eq!(p.bytes, dense_invoice(n) + super::directory::EMPTY_SELECTOR);
        assert_eq!(p.roots, before.roots);
        assert!(!allocation.invalid);
        assert_eq!(allocation.allocations, 0);
        assert_eq!(allocation.reallocations, 0);
        assert_eq!(allocation.live, 0);
    }
    // Existing shadowing controls cover semantic refusal. This independent
    // source-role equation additionally reaches nonempty names and early hits.
    for (query, probes, terminal, increments, found) in [
        ("first", 1usize, 0usize, 0usize, true),
        ("second", 2, 0, 1, true),
        ("absent", 2, 1, 2, false),
    ] {
        let mut names = Names {
            entries: [None; 128],
            len: 2,
        };
        names.entries[0] = Some("first");
        names.entries[1] = Some("second");
        let mut invoice = Proof::default();
        let (actual, allocation) =
            crate::test_allocation_observer::measure(|| names.contains(query, &mut invoice));
        assert_eq!(actual, found);
        let entry = charged::<(&Names<'_>, &str)>() + size_of::<usize>();
        let predicate = 2 * size_of::<usize>() + size_of::<bool>();
        let index = 2 * size_of::<&[Option<&str>; 128]>()
            + 2 * size_of::<usize>()
            + size_of::<&Option<&str>>();
        let comparison = size_of::<&&str>() + 4 * size_of::<&str>() + size_of::<bool>();
        assert_eq!(
            invoice.bytes,
            entry
                + (probes + terminal) * predicate
                + probes * (index + comparison)
                + increments * size_of::<usize>()
                + charged::<bool>()
        );
        assert!(!allocation.invalid);
        assert_eq!(allocation.allocations, 0);
        assert_eq!(allocation.reallocations, 0);
        assert_eq!(allocation.live, 0);
    }
    let source = "module ordinary; @id(\"ordinary.main\") fn main()->i64{1}";
    let p = proof(source);
    assert_eq!(p.roots, 0);
    assert!(p.bytes > 0);
    let (old, new, old_total, new_total) = costs(source, 7, cached_exact::Retention::Semantic);
    assert_eq!(new, old + p.bytes);
    assert_eq!(new_total, old_total + p.bytes);
}

#[test]
fn owned_result_keeps_every_cleanup_storage_and_scratch_failure_keeps_carriers() {
    let source = "module ordinary; @id(\"ordinary.f\") fn f()->string{\"s\"} @id(\"ordinary.main\") fn main()->i64{string_len(f())}";
    assert_eq!(proof(source).roots, 1);
    let p = proof(source);
    let (old, new, old_total, new_total) = costs(source, 7, cached_exact::Retention::Semantic);
    assert_eq!(new + 2 * carriers(), old + p.bytes);
    assert_eq!(new_total + 2 * carriers(), old_total + p.bytes);
    let checked = crate::check(source, "ordinary.spx").unwrap();
    let resolved = crate::hir::resolve(&checked).unwrap();
    crate::hir::validate(&resolved).unwrap();
    let mut source = String::from(HEADER);
    source.push_str("@id(\"ordinary.main\") fn main()->i64 {");
    for i in 0..129 {
        source.push_str(&format!("let v{i} = 1;"));
    }
    source.push_str("f() }");
    let p = proof(&source);
    assert_eq!(p.roots, 0);
    assert!(p.bytes > 0);
    let source = format!(
        "{HEADER}@id(\"ordinary.main\") fn main()->i64 {{ {}f() }}",
        "-".repeat(130)
    );
    // The original depth-hostile source is not an admitted proof fixture.
    // Its parser refusal remains explicit; the 129 flat sibling bindings above
    // independently exercise the real bounded Names exhaustion.
    assert_eq!(
        crate::parse(&source, "ordinary.spx").unwrap_err(),
        crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic {
            code: "SPX-P207",
            severity: crate::diagnostic::Severity::Error,
            message: "source nesting depth exceeds the admitted maximum (128)".into(),
            path: Some("ordinary.spx".into()),
            span: Some(crate::ast::Span {
                start: 219,
                end: 220,
                line: 1,
                column: 220
            }),
            help: Some("split the expression or block into named helper functions".into()),
        }
    );
}

#[test]
fn identical_admitted_hir_and_six_field_diagnostics_at_matched_successful_budgets() {
    let source = dense(32);
    let (old, new, _, _) = costs(&source, 7, cached_exact::Retention::Semantic);
    let checked = crate::check(&source, "ordinary.spx").unwrap();
    let mut observations = Vec::new();
    for budget in [old, new] {
        let _restore =
            Limit(crate::workspace_graph::ACTIVE_BUILDER_LIMIT.with(|l| l.replace(budget)));
        let resolved = crate::hir::resolve(&checked).unwrap();
        crate::hir::validate(&resolved).unwrap();
        let diagnostics = crate::check(
            "module ordinary; @id(\"ordinary.main\") fn main()->i64{missing()}",
            "ordinary.spx",
        )
        .unwrap_err();
        assert!(!diagnostics.is_empty());
        observations.push((resolved, diagnostics));
    }
    assert_eq!(
        observations[0], observations[1],
        "Diagnostic Eq covers code, severity, message, path, span and help"
    );
}

#[test]
fn native_import_priority_and_malformed_or_runtime_censuses_keep_old_charges() {
    let source = format!(
        "{HEADER}@id(\"host.f\") import rust fn f()->i64 failure infallible; @id(\"ordinary.main\") fn main()->i64{{f()}}"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        crate::parse(&source, "ordinary.spx").unwrap_err(),
        crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic {
            code: "SPX-P104",
            severity: crate::diagnostic::Severity::Error,
            message: "expected `fn`".into(),
            path: Some("ordinary.spx".into()),
            span: Some(crate::ast::Span {
                start: 67,
                end: 73,
                line: 1,
                column: 68
            }),
            help: None,
        }
    );
    // Imports are admitted only inside an interface, including real Rust
    // imports. Its name takes precedence over the competing local function.
    let admitted = format!(
        "{HEADER}@id(\"ordinary.host\") interface Host permits {{}} {{ @id(\"host.f\") import rust fn f()->i64 effects {{}} failure infallible; }} @id(\"ordinary.main\") fn main()->i64{{f()}}"
    );
    assert_eq!(proof(&admitted).roots, 0);
    let mut raw = super::super::StructuralCost::new();
    raw.exact_expressions = 1;
    raw.exact_carrier_free_calls = 2;
    assert!(super::super::cost::exact_absent_storage(&raw).is_err());
    raw.exact_carrier_free_calls = usize::MAX;
    assert!(super::super::cost::exact_absent_storage(&raw).is_err());
    raw.exact_carrier_free_calls = 0;
    let mut runtime = super::super::StructuralCost::new();
    runtime.exact_carrier_free_calls = usize::MAX;
    assert_eq!(
        super::super::cost::fixed_hir_discount(5, &raw, &runtime).unwrap(),
        0
    );
}
//! Exact finite inverse for the source fragments owned by OPT#898.
//! Existing older inverse tests still inspect their entire original subject.
pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn pre898(source: &str) -> String {
    let mut restored = source.to_owned();
    for (before, after) in [
        (
            r####"#[path = "expected_projection/cached_exact.rs"]
pub(super) mod cached_exact;
#[path = "expected_projection/call_identity.rs"]
mod call_identity;
"####,
            r####"#[path = "expected_projection/cached_exact.rs"]
pub(super) mod cached_exact;
#[path = "expected_projection/call_carriers.rs"]
mod call_carriers;
#[cfg(test)]
#[path = "expected_projection/call_carriers_original.rs"]
#[allow(dead_code, reason = "complete independent pre-898 forecaster control")]
mod call_carriers_original;
#[path = "expected_projection/call_identity.rs"]
mod call_identity;
"####,
        ),
        (
            r####"    let mut raw = StructuralCost::raw_ast(layout_mode >= 1).with_inline_values(layout_mode >= 2);
    ast_program_cost(program, &mut raw, include_imports)?;
    let fresh;
    let held;
"####,
            r####"    let mut raw = StructuralCost::raw_ast(layout_mode >= 1).with_inline_values(layout_mode >= 2);
    ast_program_cost(program, &mut raw, include_imports)?;
    let call_proof = if layout_mode >= 5 {
        let proof = super::call_carriers::count(program, authored, include_imports);
        raw.exact_carrier_free_calls = proof.roots;
        proof.bytes
    } else {
        0
    };
    let fresh;
    let held;
"####,
        ),
        (
            r####"    )?;
    let hir_upper = fixed_hir_upper
        .checked_add(prelude_hir_upper)
        .and_then(|bytes| bytes.checked_add(identity_carrier_bytes))
        .and_then(|bytes| bytes.checked_add(identity_occurrence_upper))
"####,
            r####"    )?;
    let hir_upper = fixed_hir_upper
        .checked_add(call_proof)
        .and_then(|bytes| bytes.checked_add(prelude_hir_upper))
        .and_then(|bytes| bytes.checked_add(identity_carrier_bytes))
        .and_then(|bytes| bytes.checked_add(identity_occurrence_upper))
"####,
        ),
        (
            r####"                include_str!("../../src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/vec_constructor.rs"),
                include_str!("../../src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/held_projection.rs"),
                include_str!("../../src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/prelude_forecast.rs"),
                include_str!("../../src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/prelude_forecast_tests.rs"),
"####,
            r####"                include_str!("../../src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/vec_constructor.rs"),
                include_str!("../../src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/held_projection.rs"),
                include_str!("../../src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/call_carriers.rs"),
                include_str!("../../src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/call_carriers_tests.rs"),
                include_str!("../../src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/call_carriers_inverse.rs"),
                include_str!("../../src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/call_carriers_original.rs"),
                include_str!("../../src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/call_carriers_historical_tests.rs"),
                include_str!("../../src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/prelude_forecast.rs"),
                include_str!("../../src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/prelude_forecast_tests.rs"),
"####,
        ),
    ] {
        if restored.contains(after) {
            assert_eq!(restored.matches(after).count(), 1);
            restored = restored.replacen(after, before, 1);
        }
    }
    restored
}
#[test]
fn complete_forecaster_inverse_reconstructs_entire_pre898_algorithm() {
    let actual = include_str!("held_projection.rs");
    let original = include_str!("call_carriers_original.rs");
    let original = original
        .split("// Finite helper wiring")
        .next()
        .unwrap()
        .trim_end();
    assert_eq!(pre898(actual).trim_end(), original);
}
//! Shared source-held closure consumption for forecast and synthetic construction.
use super::{
    active_builder_limit, ast_function_contract_cost, ast_function_cost,
    ast_function_identity_slots, ast_function_signature_cost, ast_program_cost,
    ast_program_identity_slots, ast_type_declaration_cost, ast_type_declaration_identity_slots,
    ast_type_identity_slots, cached_exact, call_identity, checked_builder_sum, checked_usage, cost,
    default_expr, generic_instance_source_cost, graph_error, import_stub, limit_error,
    private_layout, reserve_builder_structure, synthetic_main_runtime_cost, type_rewrite,
    AuthoredDeclaration, StructuralCost, SyntheticBuilderCosts, HIR_IDENTITY_COPY_FACTOR,
    HIR_STRING_COPY_FACTOR, HIR_STRUCTURE_EXPANSION_FACTOR,
};
use crate::ast::{Expr, ExprKind, Function, ModuleUseKind, Program, Span, Type, TypeDeclaration};
use crate::bounded_output::budgeted_format;
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::{hir, prelude};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    programs: &[Program],
    maximum_identity: Option<usize>,
    include_imports: bool,
    profile: (u8, cached_exact::Retention),
    registry: Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
) -> Result<SyntheticBuilderCosts, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry_using(
        program,
        authored,
        programs,
        maximum_identity,
        include_imports,
        profile,
        (registry, None),
    )
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_builder_bytes_core<'a>(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
    programs: &'a [Program],
    maximum_identity: Option<usize>,
    include_imports: bool,
    profile: (u8, cached_exact::Retention),
    proofs: (
        Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
        &mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'a>,
    ),
) -> Result<SyntheticBuilderCosts, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry_using(
        program,
        authored,
        programs,
        maximum_identity,
        include_imports,
        profile,
        (proofs.0, Some(proofs.1)),
    )
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry_using<
    'a,
>(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
    programs: &'a [Program],
    maximum_identity: Option<usize>,
    include_imports: bool,
    profile: (u8, cached_exact::Retention),
    proofs: (
        Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
        Option<&mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'a>>,
    ),
) -> Result<SyntheticBuilderCosts, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let (registry, core) = proofs;
    let (layout_mode, retention) = profile;
    let mut raw = StructuralCost::raw_ast(layout_mode >= 1).with_inline_values(layout_mode >= 2);
    ast_program_cost(program, &mut raw, include_imports)?;
    let fresh;
    let held;
    let layout = if let Some(registry) = registry {
        held = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
        &*held
    } else {
        fresh = private_layout::LayoutClosure::derive(program, authored, programs)?;
        &fresh
    };
    let mut identity_slots = ast_program_identity_slots(program)?;
    let mut runtime = StructuralCost::new();
    let mut default_memo = [BTreeMap::new(), BTreeMap::new()];
    let mut byte_proofs = import_stub::core_proofs::Phase::new(authored, programs, core)
        .map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
    let mut transient_import_clone = 0usize;
    layout.add_costs(
        program,
        programs,
        &mut raw,
        &mut runtime,
        &mut identity_slots,
    )?;
    for module_use in program.module_uses.iter().filter(|_| include_imports) {
        if module_use.kind == ModuleUseKind::Protocol {
            continue;
        }
        let target = &authored[module_use.persistent_id.as_str()];
        runtime.string(&module_use.alias)?;
        if let Some(function) = target.function {
            // `synthetic_program` retains an imported function as a stub: the
            // signature it rewrites, with `requires` and `ensures` cleared and
            // the body replaced by the return type's default expression. Only
            // the retained signature is charged here; the default body is
            // charged as runtime cost below. The provider's own contract and
            // body reach the resolver exactly once, through the defining
            // module's own `ast_program_cost`.
            ast_function_signature_cost(function, &mut raw)?;
            // Preserve old fitting receipts even though ordinary stubs now
            // clone only their signature. The historical transient-body debit
            // remains conservative; only mode 4 removes its peak after all
            // older estimates refuse. Intrinsic wrapper bodies are separately
            // charged as retained runtime structures below.
            transient_import_clone =
                transient_import_clone.max(ast_function_contract_cost(function)?.total);
            if programs
                .iter()
                .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
                .is_some_and(|provider| {
                    crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper(provider, function).is_some()
                        || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper(provider, function).is_some()
                })
            {
                ast_function_cost(function, &mut runtime)?;
                identity_slots =
                    checked_builder_sum(identity_slots, ast_function_identity_slots(function)?)?;
                continue;
            }
            identity_slots = checked_builder_sum(
                identity_slots,
                ast_type_identity_slots(&function.return_type)?,
            )?;
            for param in &function.params {
                identity_slots =
                    checked_builder_sum(identity_slots, ast_type_identity_slots(&param.ty)?)?;
                type_rewrite::runtime_cost_with_layout(
                    &param.ty,
                    target.module,
                    program,
                    programs,
                    &mut runtime,
                    Some(layout),
                )?;
            }
            type_rewrite::runtime_cost_with_layout(
                &function.return_type,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                &mut runtime,
                Some(layout),
            )?;
            let cost = import_stub::default_expr_expanded_cost_with_layout(
                function,
                target.module,
                program,
                authored,
                programs,
                &mut default_memo,
                &mut BTreeSet::new(),
                super::super::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                    program, target, authored, programs, layout,
                ),
                import_stub::byte_helper_authorized(
                    function,
                    target.module,
                    byte_proofs.get().map_err(|error| vec![error])?,
                )?,
                Some(layout),
            )?;
            runtime.add_split(cost.bytes, cost.string_bytes)?;
            runtime.add_identity_carriers(cost.identity_carriers)?;
            identity_slots = checked_builder_sum(identity_slots, cost.identity_slots)?;
        } else {
            let declaration = target.ty.expect("validated type target");
            ast_type_declaration_cost(declaration, &mut raw)?;
            identity_slots = checked_builder_sum(
                identity_slots,
                ast_type_declaration_identity_slots(declaration)?,
            )?;
            super::super::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_runtime_cost_with_layout(
                declaration,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                &mut runtime,
                Some(layout),
            )?;
        }
    }
    if !program
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
    {
        let synthetic_main = synthetic_main_runtime_cost(&program.module)?;
        runtime.add_split(synthetic_main.total, synthetic_main.string_bytes)?;
        runtime.add_identity_carriers(synthetic_main.identity_carriers)?;
    }
    if layout_mode >= 2 {
        identity_slots = identity_slots
            .checked_sub(call_identity::discount_with_imports(
                program,
                authored,
                include_imports,
            ))
            .expect("scalar calls retain their expression and callee identity slots");
    }
    identity_slots = identity_slots
        .checked_sub(raw.scalar_identity_discount)
        .expect("raw scalar discount retains each expression identity slot");
    let generic_instances = generic_instance_source_cost(program)?;
    identity_slots = checked_builder_sum(identity_slots, generic_instances.identity_slots)?;
    let hir_input = checked_usage(
        raw.total,
        runtime.total,
        "builder_bytes",
        active_builder_limit(),
    )?;
    let hir_input = checked_usage(
        hir_input,
        generic_instances.bytes,
        "builder_bytes",
        active_builder_limit(),
    )?;
    let string_input = checked_builder_sum(raw.string_bytes, runtime.string_bytes)?;
    let string_input = checked_builder_sum(string_input, generic_instances.string_bytes)?;
    // String contents are part of `hir_input`; only the remaining structural
    // bytes expand by the structural factor.
    let structure_input = hir_input.saturating_sub(string_input);
    let fixed_hir_upper = structure_input
        .checked_mul(HIR_STRUCTURE_EXPANSION_FACTOR)
        .and_then(|structure| {
            string_input
                .checked_mul(HIR_STRING_COPY_FACTOR)
                .and_then(|strings| structure.checked_add(strings))
        })
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    let maximum_identity_bytes = maximum_identity.unwrap_or_else(|| {
        authored
            .keys()
            .map(|id| id.len())
            .chain(prelude::all_ids().into_iter().map(str::len))
            .max()
            .unwrap_or(0)
    });
    #[cfg(test)]
    let prior_fixed_hir_upper = fixed_hir_upper;
    let fixed_hir_upper = if layout_mode == cached_exact::MODE {
        cached_exact::fixed_upper(fixed_hir_upper, &raw, &runtime, retention)?
    } else {
        fixed_hir_upper
            .checked_sub(cost::fixed_hir_discount(layout_mode, &raw, &runtime)?)
            .expect("literal storage discount retains the complete reduced fixed bundle")
    };
    let identity_occurrence_upper = identity_slots
        .checked_mul(maximum_identity_bytes)
        .and_then(|bytes| bytes.checked_mul(HIR_IDENTITY_COPY_FACTOR))
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    // Heap carriers are prebound independently of inline layouts and shared clones.
    let identity_carriers = cost::identity_carriers(layout_mode, &raw, &runtime)?;
    let identity_carriers =
        checked_builder_sum(identity_carriers, generic_instances.identity_carriers)?;
    let identity_carrier_bytes = identity_carriers
        .checked_mul(hir::ExpressionId::SHARED_ALLOCATION_CARRIER_BYTES)
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    // The resolver's source chain also retains the compiler-owned base
    // variants. They are absent from synthetic AST and cannot spend an authored
    // expression discount. Borrow their producer metadata, without warming or
    // constructing the process prelude table during estimation.
    let prelude_hir_upper = super::prelude_forecast::retained_upper(
        &prelude::base_schema::BASE,
        maximum_identity_bytes,
    )?;
    let hir_upper = fixed_hir_upper
        .checked_add(prelude_hir_upper)
        .and_then(|bytes| bytes.checked_add(identity_carrier_bytes))
        .and_then(|bytes| bytes.checked_add(identity_occurrence_upper))
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    let retained_clone_and_hir = checked_usage(
        raw.total,
        hir_upper,
        "builder_bytes",
        active_builder_limit(),
    )?;
    #[cfg(test)]
    let original_mode7_fixed_upper = raw
        .exact_expressions
        .checked_add(runtime.exact_expressions)
        .and_then(|count| {
            count
                .checked_mul(super::super::HIR_FIXED_EXPANSION_FACTOR)
                .and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::Expr>()))
                .and_then(|old| prior_fixed_hir_upper.checked_sub(old))
                .and_then(|base| {
                    count
                        .checked_mul(if retention == cached_exact::Retention::Semantic {
                            2
                        } else {
                            1
                        })
                        .and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(super::super::HIR_EXPR_FIXED_BUNDLE))
                        .and_then(|exact| base.checked_add(exact))
                })
        })
        .unwrap_or(usize::MAX);
    #[cfg(test)]
    super::super::forecast_observation::record_components(
        &program.path,
        layout_mode,
        retention == cached_exact::Retention::Semantic,
        include_imports,
        [
            raw.total,
            raw.string_bytes,
            raw.exact_expressions,
            raw.literal_fixed_discount,
            runtime.total,
            runtime.string_bytes,
            runtime.exact_expressions,
            structure_input,
            string_input,
            prior_fixed_hir_upper,
            fixed_hir_upper,
            identity_occurrence_upper,
            identity_carrier_bytes,
            retained_clone_and_hir,
            generic_instances.bytes,
            transient_import_clone,
            original_mode7_fixed_upper,
            prelude_hir_upper,
            hir_upper,
        ],
    );
    let result = SyntheticBuilderCosts {
        raw_clone_and_hir: checked_usage(
            retained_clone_and_hir,
            transient_import_clone,
            "builder_bytes",
            active_builder_limit(),
        )?,
        retained_clone_and_hir,
        transient_import_clone,
        runtime: runtime.total,
        retained_hir: hir_upper,
        synthetic_ast: checked_usage(
            raw.total,
            runtime.total,
            "builder_bytes",
            active_builder_limit(),
        )?,
    };
    byte_proofs.complete().map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
    Ok(result)
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_program_with_registry(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    programs: &[Program],
    registry: Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    synthetic_program_with_registry_using(program, authored, programs, registry, None)
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_program_core<'a>(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
    programs: &'a [Program],
    registry: Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
    core: &mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'a>,
) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    synthetic_program_with_registry_using(program, authored, programs, registry, Some(core))
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_program_with_registry_using<'a>(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
    programs: &'a [Program],
    registry: Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
    core: Option<&mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'a>>,
) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let fresh;
    let held;
    let layout = if let Some(registry) = registry {
        held = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
        &*held
    } else {
        fresh = private_layout::LayoutClosure::derive(program, authored, programs)?;
        &fresh
    };
    let mut byte_proofs = import_stub::core_proofs::Phase::new(authored, programs, core)
        .map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
    let mut synthetic = program.clone();
    synthetic.module_uses.clear();
    // Project validation already authenticated static implementation sidecars.
    // Runtime HIR has no witness or dispatch representation for them.
    synthetic.implementations.clear();
    for module_use in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|item| item.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
    {
        let target = &authored[module_use.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let mut ty = target.ty.expect("validated type target").clone();
        ty.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&module_use.alias);
        super::super::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            &mut ty,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(layout),
        )?;
        synthetic.types.push(ty);
    }
    layout.append_types(&mut synthetic, program, programs)?;
    let type_index_bytes = synthetic
        .types
        .len()
        .checked_mul(std::mem::size_of::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>())
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    reserve_builder_structure(type_index_bytes)?;
    let mut type_declarations = Vec::with_capacity(synthetic.types.len());
    for declaration in &synthetic.types {
        type_declarations.push((declaration.name.as_str(), declaration));
    }
    type_declarations.sort_by(|left, right| left.0.cmp(right.0));
    if type_declarations
        .windows(2)
        .any(|items| items[0].0 == items[1].0)
    {
        return Err(vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "synthetic workspace type-name index is not unique",
        )]);
    }
    for module_use in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|item| item.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
    {
        let target = &authored[module_use.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let target_function = target.function.expect("validated function target");
        let retain_body = programs
            .iter()
            .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
            .is_some_and(|provider| {
                crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper(provider, target_function).is_some()
                    || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper(provider, target_function).is_some()
            });
        let mut function = if retain_body {
            target_function.clone()
        } else {
            import_stub::signature(target_function)
        };
        function.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&module_use.alias);
        if retain_body {
            synthetic.functions.push(function);
            continue;
        }
        for param in &mut function.params {
            type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
                &mut param.ty,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                Some(layout),
            )?;
        }
        type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
            &mut function.return_type,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(layout),
        )?;
        // Clearing would retain the cloned contracts' Vec capacities in every
        // finished stub. Replace the vectors so the full imported contracts
        // remain transient, matching the final peak-only pre-bound.
        function.requires = Vec::new();
        function.ensures = Vec::new();
        function.body = default_expr(
            &function,
            &type_declarations,
            super::super::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                program, target, authored, programs, layout,
            ),
            import_stub::byte_helper_authorized(
                target_function,
                target.module,
                byte_proofs.get().map_err(|error| vec![error])?,
            )?,
        )?;
        synthetic.functions.push(function);
    }
    if !synthetic
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
    {
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Function>())?;
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())?;
        synthetic.functions.push(Function {
            stable_id: budgeted_format(format_args!(
                "workspace.synthetic.main.{}",
                synthetic.module
            )),
            explicit_id: true,
            name: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone("main"),
            name_span: Span::default(),
            type_parameters: Vec::new(),
            params: Vec::new(),
            return_type: Type::I64,
            effects: Vec::new(),
            yields: None,
            follows: None,
            requires: Vec::new(),
            ensures: Vec::new(),
            body: Expr {
                kind: ExprKind::Int(0),
                span: Span::default(),
            },
            span: Span::default(),
        });
    }
    byte_proofs.complete().map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
    Ok(synthetic)
}

// Finite helper wiring for the complete unchanged historical carrier suite.
pub(super) fn historical_scoped(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    programs: &[Program],
    maximum: Option<usize>,
    mode: u8,
    imports: bool,
) -> Result<SyntheticBuilderCosts, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry(
        program,
        authored,
        programs,
        maximum,
        imports,
        (mode, cached_exact::Retention::Parsed),
        None,
    )
}
pub(super) mod historical_cached {
    pub(in crate::workspace_graph) use super::cached_exact::Retention;
    use super::*;
    pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn summed(
        programs: &[Program],
        authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
        retention: Retention,
    ) -> Result<(usize, usize), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
        let (mut resolve, mut runtime) = (0, 0);
        for program in programs {
            let maximum = Some(super::super::dependency_identity_max(
                program, authored, programs,
            )?);
            let costs = synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry(
                program,
                authored,
                programs,
                maximum,
                true,
                (cached_exact::MODE, retention),
                None,
            )?;
            resolve = checked_usage(
                resolve,
                costs.retained_clone_and_hir,
                "builder_bytes",
                active_builder_limit(),
            )?;
            runtime = checked_usage(
                runtime,
                costs.runtime,
                "builder_bytes",
                active_builder_limit(),
            )?;
        }
        Ok((
            resolve,
            checked_usage(resolve, runtime, "builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())?,
        ))
    }
}
//! Carrier-free root census: independent AST and resolved-HIR position oracles,
//! a complete-forecast differential against the position-blind formula, exact
//! and one-short limits, malformed-census refusal, and a resolver/replay
//! allocation highwater gate. Variable roots keep all storage and closure
//! subtrees keep every variable auxiliary carrier. This source batch awaits
//! executable qualification. Nothing here mirrors `declaration_cost`.
use super::super::call_carriers_original::historical_scoped as synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped;
mod cached_exact {
    pub(super) use super::super::super::cached_exact::fixed_upper;
    pub(super) use super::super::super::call_carriers_original::historical_cached::{
        summed, Retention,
    };
}
use super::super::dependency_identity_max;
use super::StructuralCost;
use crate::ast::{Expr, ExprKind, Program};
use crate::hir::{ResolvedExpr, ResolvedExprKind, ResolvedStatement};

/// Independent actual-layout oracle for the six omitted carriers.
fn carriers() -> usize {
    use std::mem::size_of;
    size_of::<crate::hir::ResolvedBinding>()
        + size_of::<crate::hir::ResolvedStatement>()
        + size_of::<crate::hir::ResolvedFieldInitializer>()
        + size_of::<crate::hir::ResolvedMatchArm>()
        + size_of::<crate::hir::ResolvedMatchPatternField>()
        + size_of::<crate::hir::ResolvedRecordMatchPatternField>()
}

#[derive(Debug, Default, Eq, PartialEq)]
struct Census {
    kinds: [usize; 3],
    free: usize,
    /// Free `if` roots: carrier-free, but never storage-free.
    ifs: usize,
    /// Free variable roots outside authored closures: never storage-free.
    vars: usize,
    /// Free blocks outside closures, retaining storage and every child carrier.
    blocks: usize,
}

fn ast_kind(kind: &ExprKind) -> Option<usize> {
    match kind {
        ExprKind::Int(_)
        | ExprKind::Int32(_)
        | ExprKind::Char(_)
        | ExprKind::Uint8(_)
        | ExprKind::Usize(_)
        | ExprKind::Float32(_)
        | ExprKind::Float64(_)
        | ExprKind::Bool(_) => Some(0),
        ExprKind::Unary { .. } => Some(1),
        ExprKind::Binary { .. } => Some(2),
        _ => None,
    }
}

/// Raw AST oracle: a root is free only as a unary/binary operand, an `if`
/// condition/branch, a block tail, or a free-function call argument.
fn ast_census(expression: &Expr, free: bool, outside_closure: bool, census: &mut Census) {
    if let Some(kind) = ast_kind(&expression.kind) {
        census.kinds[kind] += 1;
        census.free += usize::from(free);
    }
    if matches!(expression.kind, ExprKind::If { .. }) {
        census.ifs += usize::from(free);
    }
    if outside_closure && matches!(expression.kind, ExprKind::Var(_)) {
        census.vars += usize::from(free);
    }
    if outside_closure && matches!(expression.kind, ExprKind::Block { .. }) {
        census.blocks += usize::from(free);
    }
    let outside_children = outside_closure && !matches!(expression.kind, ExprKind::Closure { .. });
    let free_children: Vec<&Expr> = match &expression.kind {
        ExprKind::Unary { value, .. } => vec![value],
        ExprKind::Binary { left, right, .. } => vec![left, right],
        ExprKind::If {
            condition,
            then_branch,
            else_branch,
        } => vec![condition, then_branch, else_branch],
        ExprKind::Call { args, .. } => args.iter().collect(),
        ExprKind::Block { tail, .. } => vec![tail],
        _ => Vec::new(),
    };
    let mut index = 0;
    while let Some(child) = expression.child(index) {
        let is_free = free_children
            .iter()
            .any(|candidate| std::ptr::eq(*candidate, child));
        ast_census(child, is_free, outside_children, census);
        index += 1;
    }
}

fn hir_kind(kind: &ResolvedExprKind) -> Option<usize> {
    match kind {
        ResolvedExprKind::Int(_)
        | ResolvedExprKind::Int32(_)
        | ResolvedExprKind::Char(_)
        | ResolvedExprKind::Uint8(_)
        | ResolvedExprKind::Usize(_)
        | ResolvedExprKind::Float32(_)
        | ResolvedExprKind::Float64(_)
        | ResolvedExprKind::Bool(_) => Some(0),
        ResolvedExprKind::Unary { .. } => Some(1),
        ResolvedExprKind::Binary { .. } => Some(2),
        _ => None,
    }
}

/// Resolved-HIR oracle over the actual containers: a root is free only when
/// it is held by a `Box` of a unary/binary/`if`/block-tail node or by a call's
/// argument vector. Statement children are carried by `ResolvedStatement`.
/// Returns the number of auxiliary carriers actually constructed.
fn hir_census(
    expression: &ResolvedExpr,
    free: bool,
    outside_closure: bool,
    census: &mut Census,
) -> usize {
    if let Some(kind) = hir_kind(&expression.kind) {
        census.kinds[kind] += 1;
        census.free += usize::from(free);
    }
    if matches!(expression.kind, ResolvedExprKind::If { .. }) {
        census.ifs += usize::from(free);
    }
    if outside_closure
        && matches!(
            expression.kind,
            ResolvedExprKind::Place(_) | ResolvedExprKind::FunctionReference { .. }
        )
    {
        census.vars += usize::from(free);
    }
    if outside_closure && matches!(expression.kind, ResolvedExprKind::Block { .. }) {
        census.blocks += usize::from(free);
    }
    match &expression.kind {
        ResolvedExprKind::Unary { value, .. } => hir_census(value, true, outside_closure, census),
        ResolvedExprKind::Binary { left, right, .. } => {
            hir_census(left, true, outside_closure, census)
                + hir_census(right, true, outside_closure, census)
        }
        ResolvedExprKind::If {
            condition,
            then_branch,
            else_branch,
        } => {
            hir_census(condition, true, outside_closure, census)
                + hir_census(then_branch, true, outside_closure, census)
                + hir_census(else_branch, true, outside_closure, census)
        }
        ResolvedExprKind::Call { args, .. } => args
            .iter()
            .map(|argument| hir_census(argument, true, outside_closure, census))
            .sum(),
        ResolvedExprKind::Block { statements, tail } => {
            let mut built = 0;
            for statement in statements {
                built += 1 + usize::from(matches!(
                    statement,
                    ResolvedStatement::Let { .. } | ResolvedStatement::Assign { .. }
                ));
                for index in 0..statement.child_count() {
                    built += hir_census(
                        statement.child(index).unwrap(),
                        false,
                        outside_closure,
                        census,
                    );
                }
            }
            built + hir_census(tail, true, outside_closure, census)
        }
        ResolvedExprKind::Closure {
            parameters,
            captures,
            body,
        } => {
            // Captures are synthetic place reads and bindings, never authored
            // variable roots. The private body inherits the closure exclusion.
            parameters.len() + captures.len() + hir_census(body, false, false, census)
        }
        ResolvedExprKind::Invoke { callable, args } => {
            // The callable place comes from the source call name, not a Var.
            hir_census(callable, false, outside_closure, census)
                + args
                    .iter()
                    .map(|arg| hir_census(arg, true, outside_closure, census))
                    .sum::<usize>()
        }
        ResolvedExprKind::ConstructRecord { fields, .. }
        | ResolvedExprKind::ConstructVariant { fields, .. } => fields
            .iter()
            .map(|field| 1 + hir_census(&field.value, false, outside_closure, census))
            .sum(),
        ResolvedExprKind::Match {
            scrutinee, arms, ..
        } => {
            hir_census(scrutinee, false, outside_closure, census)
                + arms
                    .iter()
                    .map(|arm| {
                        1 + usize::from(matches!(
                            arm.pattern,
                            crate::hir::ResolvedMatchPattern::Binding(_)
                        )) + arm
