                            .guard
                            .as_ref()
                            .map_or(0, |guard| hir_census(guard, false, outside_closure, census))
                            + hir_census(&arm.value, false, outside_closure, census)
                    })
                    .sum::<usize>()
        }
        ResolvedExprKind::Place(_)
        | ResolvedExprKind::FunctionReference { .. }
        | ResolvedExprKind::String(_) => 0,
        _ if hir_kind(&expression.kind) == Some(0) => 0,
        other => panic!("fixture admits no other resolved kind: {other:?}"),
    }
}

fn cost_census(program: &Program) -> StructuralCost {
    let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
    for function in &program.functions {
        super::super::declaration_cost::ast_function_cost(function, &mut cost).unwrap();
    }
    cost
}

// Literal, checked unary/binary, lazy `&&`/`||`, comparisons and call arguments
// in carrier-free positions beside `let`/assignment values that stay delegated.
const GATE: &str = r#"module carrier.gate;
@id("carrier.gate.add") fn add(a: i64, b: i64) -> i64 { a + b }
@id("carrier.gate.main") fn main() -> i64 {
 let mut total = 1 + 2;
 total = total * 3;
 let flag = !(total < 4) && (total >= 5 || total != 6);
 if flag && add(7, 8 - 9) == 10 { -(11 / 12) } else { add(total, 13) % 14 }
}
"#;

// Every named type is authored; ownership-bearing roots retain their storage.
const VALUES: &str = r#"module carrier.values;

@id("carrier.values.packet")
record Packet {
    @id("carrier.values.packet.text")
    text: string,
    @id("carrier.values.packet.bytes")
    bytes: Bytes,
}

@id("carrier.values.add")
fn add(value: i64) -> i64
{
    value + 1
}

@id("carrier.values.text")
fn text(value: string) -> string
{
    { value }
}

@id("carrier.values.bytes")
fn bytes(value: own Bytes) -> Bytes
{
    { value }
}

@id("carrier.values.record")
fn move_packet(value: own Packet) -> Packet
{
    { value }
}

@id("carrier.values.reference")
fn reference(flag: bool) -> fn(i64) -> i64
{
    if flag { add } else { add }
}

@id("carrier.values.main")
fn main() -> i64
{
    let text_value = text("x");
    let bytes_value = bytes(bytes_zeroed(0usize));
    let copied_text = text(text_value);
    let copied_bytes = bytes(bytes_value);
    let record_value = move_packet(Packet { text: copied_text, bytes: copied_bytes });
    let moved_record = move_packet(record_value);
    let callback = reference(true);
    let direct = add;
    let mut x = 1;
    let y = x;
    x = y;
    let z = match x { n => n, };
    if true { 0 } else { z }
}
"#;

const CLOSURE: &str = r#"module carrier.closure;

@id("carrier.closure.helper")
fn helper(value: i64) -> i64
{
    value
}

@id("carrier.closure.main")
fn main() -> i64
{
    let offset = 1;
    let callback = fn(value: i64) -> i64 { let saved = value; let mut copied = saved; copied = value; if copied > offset { helper(copied) } else { offset } };
    callback(0)
}
"#;

#[test]
fn variables_keep_storage_for_owned_values_references_and_closure_captures() {
    for (source, expected_vars) in [(VALUES, 11), (CLOSURE, 1)] {
        let parsed = crate::parse(source, "values.spx").unwrap();
        let mut ast = Census::default();
        for function in &parsed.functions {
            ast_census(&function.body, false, true, &mut ast);
        }
        assert_eq!(ast.vars, expected_vars);
        let cost = cost_census(&parsed);
        assert_eq!(cost.exact_absent_roots, ast.kinds);
        assert_eq!(cost.exact_carrier_free_roots, ast.free);
        assert_eq!(cost.exact_carrier_free_ifs, ast.ifs);
        assert_eq!(cost.exact_carrier_free_vars, ast.vars);
        assert_eq!(cost.exact_carrier_free_blocks, ast.blocks);
        let checked = crate::check(source, "values.spx").unwrap();
        let resolved = crate::hir::resolve(&checked).unwrap();
        crate::hir::validate(&resolved).unwrap();
        let mut hir = Census::default();
        for function in &resolved.functions {
            hir_census(&function.body, false, true, &mut hir);
        }
        assert_eq!(hir, ast, "authored roots and actual resolved containers");
        let storage = std::mem::size_of::<crate::cleanup::CleanupStorageSlot>()
            + std::mem::size_of::<crate::cleanup::CleanupFlag>()
            + std::mem::size_of::<crate::cleanup::CleanupPlace>()
            + std::mem::size_of::<crate::cleanup_plan::CleanupSlot>();
        assert_eq!(
            super::exact_absent_storage(&cost).unwrap(),
            ast.kinds.iter().sum::<usize>() * storage
                + (ast.free + ast.ifs + ast.vars + ast.blocks) * carriers(),
            "variables omit only the six auxiliary carriers"
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn delegated_children_and_nested_closures_cannot_reset_variable_exclusion() {
    // Parse-only adversarial cost input: includes closed runtime shapes so the
    // forecast must be conservative before later admission refuses them.
    for body in [
        "let x = value; x = value; value",
        "let x = { value }; { value }",
        "Packet { text: { value }, bytes: { value } }",
        "match value { n if { value } => { value }, _ => { value }, }",
        "let nested = fn(inner: i64) -> i64 { inner + value }; nested(value)",
        "let x = make(value); value.method(value)",
        "let x = value with { field: { value } }; value.field",
        "let x = vec_of<i64>({ value }); value",
        "while value > 0 { let x = { value }; value } value",
    ] {
        let source = format!(
            "module carrier.exclusion; @id(\"carrier.exclusion.main\") fn main() -> i64 {{ let callback = fn(value: i64) -> i64 {{ {body} }}; 0 }}"
        );
        let parsed = crate::parse(&source, "exclusion.spx").unwrap();
        let mut ast = Census::default();
        ast_census(&parsed.functions[0].body, false, true, &mut ast);
        assert_eq!(ast.vars, 0, "{body}");
        assert_eq!(cost_census(&parsed).exact_carrier_free_vars, 0, "{body}");
        assert_eq!(cost_census(&parsed).exact_carrier_free_blocks, 0, "{body}");
    }
}

#[test]
fn malformed_variable_censuses_fail_closed_and_runtime_censuses_are_ignored() {
    let parsed = crate::parse(VALUES, "values.spx").unwrap();
    let raw = cost_census(&parsed);
    let mut runtime = StructuralCost::new();
    runtime.exact_carrier_free_vars = usize::MAX;
    runtime.exact_carrier_free_blocks = usize::MAX;
    let ordinary = StructuralCost::new();
    for mode in [5, 6] {
        assert_eq!(
            super::fixed_hir_discount(mode, &raw, &runtime).unwrap(),
            super::fixed_hir_discount(mode, &raw, &ordinary).unwrap()
        );
    }
    for vars in [raw.exact_expressions, usize::MAX] {
        let mut malformed = cost_census(&parsed);
        malformed.exact_carrier_free_vars = vars;
        for mode in [5, 6] {
            assert!(super::fixed_hir_discount(mode, &malformed, &ordinary)
                .unwrap_err()
                .iter()
                .all(|error| error.code == "SPX-G171"));
        }
        for retention in [
            cached_exact::Retention::Parsed,
            cached_exact::Retention::Semantic,
        ] {
            assert!(
                cached_exact::fixed_upper(1 << 24, &malformed, &ordinary, retention)
                    .unwrap_err()
                    .iter()
                    .all(|error| error.code == "SPX-G171")
            );
        }
    }
    for blocks in [raw.exact_expressions, usize::MAX] {
        let mut malformed = cost_census(&parsed);
        malformed.exact_carrier_free_blocks = blocks;
        for mode in [5, 6] {
            assert!(super::fixed_hir_discount(mode, &malformed, &ordinary)
                .unwrap_err()
                .iter()
                .all(|error| error.code == "SPX-G171"));
        }
    }
    // Even a valid disjoint count refuses if conversion to carrier bytes overflows.
    let mut huge = StructuralCost::new();
    huge.exact_expressions = usize::MAX;
    huge.exact_carrier_free_vars = usize::MAX / carriers() + 1;
    let mut block_overflow = StructuralCost::new();
    block_overflow.exact_expressions = usize::MAX;
    block_overflow.exact_carrier_free_blocks = usize::MAX / carriers() + 1;
    assert!(super::exact_absent_storage(&block_overflow)
        .unwrap_err()
        .iter()
        .all(|error| error.code == "SPX-G171"));
    assert!(super::exact_absent_storage(&huge)
        .unwrap_err()
        .iter()
        .all(|error| error.code == "SPX-G171"));
    for mode in [0, 4, 7, 8] {
        assert!(super::fixed_hir_discount(mode, &huge, &ordinary).is_ok());
    }
    for retention in [
        cached_exact::Retention::Parsed,
        cached_exact::Retention::Semantic,
    ] {
        assert_eq!(
            cached_exact::fixed_upper(1 << 24, &raw, &runtime, retention).unwrap(),
            cached_exact::fixed_upper(1 << 24, &raw, &ordinary, retention).unwrap()
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn raw_ast_resolved_and_cost_censuses_agree_on_every_carrier_free_root() {
    let parsed = crate::parse(GATE, std::path::Path::new("gate.spx")).unwrap();
    let mut ast = Census::default();
    for function in &parsed.functions {
        ast_census(&function.body, false, true, &mut ast);
    }
    // Hand count: 14 literals, 2 unary, 13 binary; `1 + 2`, `total * 3` and
    // the `let flag` conjunction are the only delegated censused roots. The
    // tail `if` is the one free `if` root. Its scalar operands and those of
    // `add` contribute eight free variables, each retaining all storage.
    assert_eq!(
        ast,
        Census {
            kinds: [14, 2, 13],
            free: 26,
            ifs: 1,
            vars: 8,
            blocks: 2,
        }
    );
    // Both admitted braced if arms are real free Block nodes, even though
    // neither has statements. Keep their full AST/HIR/cost census below.
    // The separate add body has no free block: retain the zero boundary too.
    let mut helper = Census::default();
    ast_census(&parsed.functions[0].body, false, true, &mut helper);
    assert_eq!(helper.blocks, 0);
    assert_eq!(ast.blocks, 2);
    let cost = cost_census(&parsed);
    assert_eq!(cost.exact_absent_roots, ast.kinds);
    assert_eq!(cost.exact_carrier_free_roots, ast.free);
    assert_eq!(cost.exact_carrier_free_ifs, ast.ifs);
    assert_eq!(cost.exact_carrier_free_vars, ast.vars);
    assert_eq!(cost.exact_carrier_free_blocks, ast.blocks);
    // Legacy and runtime accumulators never count.
    let mut legacy = StructuralCost::new();
    for function in &parsed.functions {
        super::super::declaration_cost::ast_function_cost(function, &mut legacy).unwrap();
    }
    assert_eq!(legacy.exact_carrier_free_roots, 0);
    assert_eq!(legacy.exact_carrier_free_ifs, 0);
    assert_eq!(legacy.exact_carrier_free_vars, 0);
    assert_eq!(legacy.exact_carrier_free_blocks, 0);

    let checked = crate::check(GATE, "gate.spx").unwrap();
    let resolved = crate::hir::resolve(&checked).unwrap();
    crate::hir::validate(&resolved).unwrap();
    let mut hir = Census::default();
    let mut built = 0;
    for name in ["add", "main"] {
        let function = resolved
            .functions
            .iter()
            .find(|function| function.name == name)
            .unwrap();
        assert!(function.requires.is_empty() && function.ensures.is_empty());
        built += hir_census(&function.body, false, true, &mut hir);
    }
    let mut helper_hir = Census::default();
    let add = resolved.functions.iter().find(|f| f.name == "add").unwrap();
    hir_census(&add.body, false, true, &mut helper_hir);
    assert_eq!(helper_hir.blocks, 0);
    assert_eq!(hir.blocks, 2);
    // No literal/unary/binary root is folded, elided, or moved into a carrier.
    assert_eq!(hir, ast);
    // Exactly the three authored statements (two with bindings) exist, all
    // paid by delegated bundles that this change leaves complete.
    assert_eq!(built, 3 + 3);
    assert!(
        built
            <= cost.exact_expressions
                - cost.exact_carrier_free_roots
                - cost.exact_carrier_free_ifs
                - cost.exact_carrier_free_vars
                - cost.exact_carrier_free_blocks
    );
}

#[test]
fn delegated_parents_keep_complete_bundles_and_guards_are_charged() {
    let census = |body: &str| {
        let source = format!("module m; @id(\"m.main\") fn main(left: i64) -> i64 {{ {body} }}");
        let program = crate::parse(&source, std::path::Path::new("m.spx")).unwrap();
        let mut oracle = Census::default();
        ast_census(&program.functions[0].body, false, true, &mut oracle);
        let cost = cost_census(&program);
        assert_eq!(cost.exact_absent_roots, oracle.kinds, "{body}");
        assert_eq!(cost.exact_carrier_free_roots, oracle.free, "{body}");
        assert_eq!(cost.exact_carrier_free_ifs, oracle.ifs, "{body}");
        assert_eq!(cost.exact_carrier_free_vars, oracle.vars, "{body}");
        assert_eq!(cost.exact_carrier_free_blocks, oracle.blocks, "{body}");
        (oracle, cost)
    };
    // Arm values and guards embed or box under a `ResolvedMatchArm`; the
    // `let` value is embedded in its statement. Only the tail-free match
    // root's literal operands inside are free (here: `left + 1` has `1`).
    let (guarded, with_guard) = census("let x = 3; match left { n if n >= 0 => n + 1, _ => 2, }");
    assert_eq!(guarded.kinds, [4, 0, 2]);
    assert_eq!(
        guarded.free, 2,
        "`0` and `1` are operands; `3`, `2`, arms are not"
    );
    let (_, without_guard) = census("let x = 3; match left { n => n + 1, _ => 2, }");
    // The guard subtree (`n >= 0`, `n`, `0`) is now a complete charged tree.
    assert!(
        with_guard.total - without_guard.total >= 3 * std::mem::size_of::<Expr>(),
        "guard bytes must be charged"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        with_guard.exact_expressions - without_guard.exact_expressions,
        3
    );
    let slots = |body: &str| {
        let source = format!("module m; @id(\"m.main\") fn main(left: i64) -> i64 {{ {body} }}");
        let program = crate::parse(&source, std::path::Path::new("m.spx")).unwrap();
        super::super::identity_slots::ast_function_identity_slots(&program.functions[0]).unwrap()
    };
    assert!(
        slots("match left { n if n >= 0 => n + 1, _ => 2, }")
            > slots("match left { n => n + 1, _ => 2, }")
    );
}

/// Same node multiset, strings, identities and calls; only the position of
/// the `n` moved roots differs (let values versus call arguments).
fn pair(n: usize, free: bool, moved: fn(usize) -> String) -> Vec<Program> {
    vec![crate::parse(
        &pair_source(n, free, moved),
        std::path::Path::new("pair.spx"),
    )
    .unwrap()]
}

/// A moved literal root: carrier- and storage-free as an argument.
fn literal(index: usize) -> String {
    index.to_string()
}

/// A moved variable root: carrier-free as an argument, never storage-free.
fn variable(_: usize) -> String {
    "seed".to_owned()
}

/// A moved `if` root: carrier-free as an argument, never storage-free. Its
/// condition and branch-tail literals are free in both positions.
fn branch(index: usize) -> String {
    format!("if true {{ {index} }} else {{ 0 }}")
}
/// The root block changes position; its binding and nested tail retain their
/// own charges in both twins.
fn block(index: usize) -> String {
    format!("{{ let saved = {index}; {{ saved }} }}")
}

fn pair_source(n: usize, free: bool, moved: fn(usize) -> String) -> String {
    let params = (0..n)
        .map(|index| format!("a{index}: i64"))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join(", ");
    let mut body = String::from("let seed = 1; ");
    for index in 0..n {
        let value = if free { "f()".to_owned() } else { moved(index) };
        body.push_str(&format!("let v{index} = {value}; "));
    }
    let args = (0..n)
        .map(|index| if free { moved(index) } else { "f()".to_owned() })
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join(", ");
    format!(
        "module pair; @id(\"pair.f\") fn f() -> i64 {{ 0 }} @id(\"pair.g\") fn g({params}) -> i64 {{ 0 }} @id(\"pair.main\") fn main() -> i64 {{ {body}g({args}) }}"
    )
}

fn retained(programs: &[Program], mode: u8) -> (usize, usize) {
    let authored = crate::workspace_graph::index_authored(programs).unwrap();
    let maximum = Some(dependency_identity_max(&programs[0], &authored, programs).unwrap());
    let costs =
        synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped(&programs[0], &authored, programs, maximum, mode, true)
            .unwrap();
    (costs.retained_hir, costs.retained_clone_and_hir)
}

struct LimitRestore(usize);
impl Drop for LimitRestore {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        crate::workspace_graph::ACTIVE_BUILDER_LIMIT.with(|limit| limit.set(self.0));
    }
}

#[test]
fn complete_forecast_differs_from_position_blind_formula_by_exact_carriers() {
    for moved in [literal as fn(usize) -> String, branch, variable, block] {
        moved_roots_differ_by_exact_carriers(moved);
    }
}

fn moved_roots_differ_by_exact_carriers(moved: fn(usize) -> String) {
    const N: usize = 12;
    let (free, delegated) = (pair(N, true, moved), pair(N, false, moved));
    let (mut census, mut twin) = (Census::default(), Census::default());
    ast_census(&free[0].functions[2].body, false, true, &mut census);
    ast_census(&delegated[0].functions[2].body, false, true, &mut twin);
    // Exactly N roots change position. Branch/block children retain their
    // independent identical positions and carriers in both twins.
    assert_eq!(census.kinds, twin.kinds);
    assert_eq!(
        (census.free + census.ifs + census.vars + census.blocks)
            - (twin.free + twin.ifs + twin.vars + twin.blocks),
        N,
        "{census:?} {twin:?}"
    );
    // The old formula is position-blind: every earlier mode is identical.
    for mode in 0..5 {
        assert_eq!(
            retained(&free, mode),
            retained(&delegated, mode),
            "mode {mode}"
        );
    }
    for mode in [5, 6] {
        let (free_hir, free_total) = retained(&free, mode);
        let (delegated_hir, delegated_total) = retained(&delegated, mode);
        assert_eq!(delegated_hir - free_hir, N * carriers());
        assert_eq!(delegated_total - free_total, N * carriers());
    }
    for (retention, copies) in [
        (cached_exact::Retention::Parsed, 1),
        (cached_exact::Retention::Semantic, 2),
    ] {
        let sum = |programs: &[Program]| {
            let authored = crate::workspace_graph::index_authored(programs).unwrap();
            cached_exact::summed(programs, &authored, retention).unwrap()
        };
        let (free_sum, delegated_sum) = (sum(&free), sum(&delegated));
        assert_eq!(delegated_sum.0 - free_sum.0, copies * N * carriers());
        assert_eq!(delegated_sum.1 - free_sum.1, copies * N * carriers());
        // Exact limit admits, one short refuses, and the delegated twin
        // (same bytes under the old formula) refuses at the free bound.
        let previous =
            crate::workspace_graph::ACTIVE_BUILDER_LIMIT.with(|limit| limit.replace(free_sum.1));
        let _restore = LimitRestore(previous);
        let authored = crate::workspace_graph::index_authored(&free).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            cached_exact::summed(&free, &authored, retention).unwrap(),
            free_sum
        );
        let twin = crate::workspace_graph::index_authored(&delegated).unwrap();
        assert!(cached_exact::summed(&delegated, &twin, retention)
            .unwrap_err()
            .iter()
            .any(|error| error.code == "SPX-G171"));
        crate::workspace_graph::ACTIVE_BUILDER_LIMIT.with(|limit| limit.set(free_sum.1 - 1));
        assert!(cached_exact::summed(&free, &authored, retention)
            .unwrap_err()
            .iter()
            .any(|error| error.code == "SPX-G171"));
    }
}

/// Final gate (actual allocation, not a mirror): resolving and replaying each
/// fixture through the real resolver and HIR validator, with the resolved
/// program live, never requests more backing than its reduced mode-5 forecast.
#[test]
fn resolver_and_replay_highwater_stays_within_the_reduced_forecast() {
    for (source, path) in [
        (GATE.to_owned(), "gate.spx"),
        (pair_source(12, true, literal), "pair.spx"),
        (pair_source(12, true, branch), "branch.spx"),
        (pair_source(12, true, variable), "variable.spx"),
        (pair_source(12, true, block), "block.spx"),
        (VALUES.to_owned(), "values.spx"),
        (CLOSURE.to_owned(), "closure.spx"),
    ] {
        let parsed = vec![crate::parse(&source, std::path::Path::new(path)).unwrap()];
        let census = cost_census(&parsed[0]);
        if matches!(path, "values.spx" | "closure.spx") {
            assert!(
                census.exact_carrier_free_vars > 0,
                "gate exercises variables"
            );
        } else if path == "variable.spx" {
            assert!(
                census.exact_carrier_free_vars >= 12,
                "twelve named argument roots exercise the variable-carrier discount"
            );
        } else if path == "block.spx" {
            assert_eq!(
                census.exact_carrier_free_blocks, 24,
                "twelve free outer and independently free inner blocks"
            );
        } else {
            assert!(
                census.exact_carrier_free_roots >= 12,
                "gate exercises the discount"
            );
        }
        let (_, forecast) = retained(&parsed, 5);
        let checked = crate::check(&source, path).unwrap();
        let (resolved, allocation) = crate::test_allocation_observer::measure(|| {
            let resolved = crate::hir::resolve(&checked).unwrap();
            crate::hir::validate(&resolved).unwrap();
            resolved
        });
        assert!(!allocation.invalid, "{path}: observer capacity");
        assert!(allocation.live > 0, "{path}: resolved program stays live");
        assert!(
            allocation.highwater <= forecast,
            "{path}: highwater {} exceeds reduced forecast {forecast}",
            allocation.highwater
        );
        drop(resolved);
    }
}
//! Base prelude HIR input missing from authored AST forecasting. This uses
//! borrowed producer metadata and existing structural/string/identity factors.
//! It bounds the unconditional prefix only; extended selected declarations
//! and independent constructor/replay terms retain their existing accounting.
use super::{
    active_builder_limit, checked_builder_sum, limit_error, StructuralCost,
    HIR_IDENTITY_COPY_FACTOR, HIR_STRING_COPY_FACTOR, HIR_STRUCTURE_EXPANSION_FACTOR,
};
use crate::ast::{
    FieldDeclaration, Type, TypeDeclaration, TypeParameterDeclaration, VariantCaseDeclaration,
};
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::prelude::base_schema::Variant;

fn input_cost(selected: &[&Variant]) -> Result<(StructuralCost, usize), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let mut cost = StructuralCost::new();
    let mut slots = 0;
    for declaration in selected {
        cost.add(std::mem::size_of::<TypeDeclaration>())?;
        cost.string(declaration.id)?;
        cost.string(declaration.name)?;
        for parameter in declaration.parameters {
            cost.add(std::mem::size_of::<TypeParameterDeclaration>())?;
            cost.string(parameter)?;
        }
        for case in declaration.cases {
            cost.add(std::mem::size_of::<VariantCaseDeclaration>())?;
            cost.string(case.id)?;
            cost.string(case.name)?;
            for field in case.fields {
                cost.add(std::mem::size_of::<FieldDeclaration>())?;
                cost.string(field.id)?;
                cost.string(field.name)?;
                cost.add(std::mem::size_of::<Type>())?;
                cost.string(field.parameter)?;
                slots = checked_builder_sum(slots, 1)?;
            }
        }
    }
    Ok((cost, slots))
}

pub(super) fn retained_upper(
    selected: &[&Variant],
    maximum_identity: usize,
) -> Result<usize, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let (cost, slots) = input_cost(selected)?;
    let maximum_identity = selected
        .iter()
        .flat_map(|schema| {
            std::iter::once(schema.id).chain(schema.cases.iter().flat_map(|case| {
                std::iter::once(case.id).chain(case.fields.iter().map(|field| field.id))
            }))
        })
        .map(str::len)
        .fold(maximum_identity, usize::max);
    let bytes = cost
        .total
        .checked_sub(cost.string_bytes)
        .and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(HIR_STRUCTURE_EXPANSION_FACTOR))
        .and_then(|n| {
            cost.string_bytes
                .checked_mul(HIR_STRING_COPY_FACTOR)
                .and_then(|strings| n.checked_add(strings))
        })
        .and_then(|n| {
            slots
                .checked_mul(maximum_identity)
                .and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(HIR_IDENTITY_COPY_FACTOR))
                .and_then(|identities| n.checked_add(identities))
        })
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    // The same live bound applies to this independent term before combining it
    // with the authored expression bundle; no synthetic AST clone owns it.
    super::checked_usage(0, bytes, "builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())
}

#[cfg(test)]
#[path = "prelude_forecast_tests.rs"]
mod tests;
use super::super::{cached_exact, dependency_identity_max};
use super::{input_cost, limit_error, retained_upper, StructuralCost};
use crate::prelude::base_schema::{BASE, OPTION, RESULT};

struct RestoreLimit(usize);
impl Drop for RestoreLimit {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        crate::workspace_graph::ACTIVE_BUILDER_LIMIT.with(|limit| limit.set(self.0));
    }
}

#[test]
fn borrowed_metadata_matches_independent_ast_inputs_without_allocating() {
    // The complete original constructors, independently retained in prelude,
    // are checked against the producer's metadata by its own physical gate.
    let held = crate::prelude::declarations();
    for (selected, declarations) in [
        (&[][..], &held[0..0]),
        (&[&OPTION][..], &held[0..1]),
        (&[&RESULT][..], &held[1..2]),
        (&BASE[..], &held[0..2]),
    ] {
        let ((actual, upper), allocation) = crate::test_allocation_observer::measure(|| {
            (
                input_cost(selected).unwrap(),
                retained_upper(selected, 20).unwrap(),
            )
        });
        assert_eq!(upper == 0, selected.is_empty());
        assert_eq!(allocation.allocations, 0);
        assert_eq!(allocation.reallocations, 0);
        assert_eq!(allocation.live, 0);
        assert!(!allocation.invalid);
        let mut expected = StructuralCost::new();
        let mut slots = 0;
        for declaration in declarations {
            super::super::ast_type_declaration_cost(declaration, &mut expected).unwrap();
            slots += super::super::ast_type_declaration_identity_slots(declaration).unwrap();
        }
        assert_eq!(
            (actual.0.total, actual.0.string_bytes, actual.1),
            (expected.total, expected.string_bytes, slots)
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn complete_saved_forecast_differs_only_by_base_prelude_retention() {
    for source in [
        "module prelude.forecast; @id(\"entry\") fn main() -> i64 { 0 }",
        // A parser-only shadow control: resolution admission remains separate.
        "module prelude.forecast; @id(\"shadow.option\") record Option { } @id(\"entry\") fn main() -> i64 { 0 }",
        "module prelude.forecast; @id(\"shadow.result\") record Result { } @id(\"entry\") fn main() -> i64 { 0 }",
    ] {
        let programs = [crate::parse(source, "forecast.spx").unwrap()];
        let authored = crate::workspace_graph::index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        let maximum = dependency_identity_max(&programs[0], &authored, &programs).unwrap();
        let delta = retained_upper(&BASE, maximum).unwrap();
        let proof = super::super::call_carriers::count(&programs[0], &authored, true);
        assert_eq!(proof.roots, 0);
        for mode in 0..=7 {
            for retention in [cached_exact::Retention::Parsed, cached_exact::Retention::Semantic] {
                let current = super::super::held_projection::synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry(
                    &programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(maximum), true, (mode, retention), None).unwrap();
                let saved = super::super::prelude_forecast_original::synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry(
                    &programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(maximum), true, (mode, retention), None).unwrap();
                let invoice = if mode >= 5 { proof.bytes } else { 0 };
                assert_eq!(current.retained_hir, saved.retained_hir + delta + invoice);
                assert_eq!(current.retained_clone_and_hir, saved.retained_clone_and_hir + delta + invoice);
                assert_eq!(current.raw_clone_and_hir, saved.raw_clone_and_hir + delta + invoice);
                assert_eq!(current.runtime, saved.runtime);
                assert_eq!(current.synthetic_ast, saved.synthetic_ast);
                assert_eq!(current.transient_import_clone, saved.transient_import_clone);
            }
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn independent_prelude_term_has_exact_short_zero_and_sticky_refusal_controls() {
    let expected = retained_upper(&BASE, 20).unwrap();
    let old = crate::workspace_graph::ACTIVE_BUILDER_LIMIT.with(|limit| limit.replace(expected));
    let _restore = RestoreLimit(old);
    assert_eq!(retained_upper(&BASE, 20).unwrap(), expected);
    for limit in [expected - 1, 0] {
        crate::workspace_graph::ACTIVE_BUILDER_LIMIT.with(|active| active.set(limit));
        let error = retained_upper(&BASE, 20).unwrap_err();
        assert_eq!(error, vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", limit)]);
        // Empty selection remains an allocation-free census at the same bound.
        let (empty, allocation) =
            crate::test_allocation_observer::measure(|| retained_upper(&[], 0).unwrap());
        assert_eq!(empty, 0);
        assert_eq!(allocation.allocated, 0);
        use crate::bounded_output::{reserve_active_required, with_limit_usage_refusal};
        let (sticky_error, refused, paid, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(0, || {
            assert!(!reserve_active_required(1));
            let selected_error = retained_upper(&BASE, 20).unwrap_err();
            assert_eq!(retained_upper(&[], 0).unwrap(), 0);
            assert!(!reserve_active_required(0));
            selected_error
        });
        // The unfunded private boundary cannot render public text after the
        // first sticky refusal. Preserve the complete raw six-field receipt;
        // the rich boundary above still compares its complete public receipt.
        assert_eq!(
            sticky_error.as_slice(),
            &[crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic::io_static("SPX-G171", "")]
        );
        assert_eq!(sticky_error[0].message_owned_capacity(), 0);
        assert!(refused);
        assert_eq!(paid, 0);
        assert_eq!(
            first,
            Some(crate::bounded_output::ReservationRefusal {
                requested: 1,
                remaining: 0,
                floor: 0,
                stage: None,
            })
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn complete_forecast_exact_short_zero_receipts_and_resolver_drop() {
    let source = "module prelude.boundary; @id(\"entry\") fn main() -> i64 { 0 }";
    let programs = [crate::parse(source, "boundary.spx").unwrap()];
    let authored = crate::workspace_graph::index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let maximum = dependency_identity_max(&programs[0], &authored, &programs).unwrap();
    let forecast = || {
        super::super::held_projection::synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry(
            &programs[0],
            &authored,
            &programs,
            Some(maximum),
            true,
            (5, cached_exact::Retention::Parsed),
            None,
        )
    };
    let exact = forecast().unwrap().raw_clone_and_hir;
    let old = crate::workspace_graph::ACTIVE_BUILDER_LIMIT.with(|limit| limit.replace(exact));
    let _restore = RestoreLimit(old);
    assert_eq!(forecast().unwrap().raw_clone_and_hir, exact);
    for limit in [exact - 1, 0] {
        crate::workspace_graph::ACTIVE_BUILDER_LIMIT.with(|active| active.set(limit));
        let error = forecast().err().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(error, vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", limit)]);
        assert_eq!(forecast().err().unwrap(), error);
    }
    crate::workspace_graph::ACTIVE_BUILDER_LIMIT.with(|limit| limit.set(old));
    let checked = crate::check(source, "boundary.spx").unwrap();
    let (_, allocation) = crate::test_allocation_observer::measure(|| {
        let resolved = crate::hir::resolve(&checked).unwrap();
        crate::hir::validate(&resolved).unwrap();
        assert!(!resolved.types.is_empty());
        drop(resolved);
    });
    assert!(!allocation.invalid);
    assert!(allocation.highwater <= exact);
    assert_eq!(allocation.live, 0);
    assert_eq!(allocation.allocated, allocation.freed);
}

#[test]
fn complete_receiving_sources_reconstruct_pinned_originals() {
    use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
    let records: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!(concat!(
        env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
        "/docs/evidence/prelude-forecast-286/exact-source-inverses.json"
    )))
    .unwrap();
    let currents = [
        include_str!(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/src/prelude.rs")),
        include_str!(concat!(
            env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
            "/src/workspace_graph/expected_projection.rs"
        )),
        include_str!(concat!(
            env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
            "/src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/held_projection.rs"
        )),
        include_str!(concat!(
            env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
            "/src/source_verify/constructor_tests.rs"
        )),
        include_str!(concat!(
            env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
            "/tests/workspace/semantic_graph/source_inventory.rs"
        )),
    ];
    let originals = [
        include_str!(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/docs/evidence/prelude-forecast-286/src__prelude.rs.before.txt")),
        include_str!(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/docs/evidence/prelude-forecast-286/src__workspace_graph__expected_projection.rs.before.txt")),
        include_str!(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/docs/evidence/prelude-forecast-286/src__workspace_graph__expected_projection__held_projection.rs.before.txt")),
        include_str!(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/docs/evidence/prelude-forecast-286/src__source_verify__constructor_tests.rs.before.txt")),
        include_str!(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/docs/evidence/prelude-forecast-286/tests__workspace__semantic_graph__source_inventory.rs.before.txt")),
    ];
    let hashes = [
        "acfbd839aee3ce120bb05677374efb0005e7c3f324cd0dbd4976c5a09942fd80",
        "65533cf9ba31f97cbc6618222b5a99bbb6b89dda14c34b686e541a8b77ce3a4c",
        "78952cede1ba2ad17dddc43fb22a3e3c423154755721f34f3e58c6bcad62f187",
        "a47d52960691d9ca39896dc938a4c8ad320eddaf37d406da7e67eddaeeac2cc0",
        "b3e5276b6bd016d1632ed6349ce34cd2e25fce762627b12c4416790ac02842be",
    ];
    assert_eq!(records.as_array().unwrap().len(), currents.len());
    for (((record, current), original), digest) in records
        .as_array()
        .unwrap()
        .iter()
        .zip(currents)
        .zip(originals)
        .zip(hashes)
    {
        assert_eq!(
            format!(
                "{:x}",
                crate::digest_hex::LowerHex(Sha256::digest(original.as_bytes()))
            ),
            digest
        );
        assert_eq!(record["sha256"].as_str().unwrap(), digest);
        let mut restored = super::super::call_carriers::inverse::pre898(current);
        for edit in record["edits"].as_array().unwrap().iter().rev() {
            let after = edit["after"].as_str().unwrap();
            assert_eq!(restored.matches(after).count(), 1);
            restored = restored.replacen(after, edit["before"].as_str().unwrap(), 1);
        }
        assert_eq!(restored, original);
    }
    assert_eq!(include_str!("prelude_forecast_original.rs"), originals[2]);
}

#[test]
fn historical_pre898_complete_saved_forecast_differs_only_by_base_prelude_retention() {
    for source in [
        "module prelude.forecast; @id(\"entry\") fn main() -> i64 { 0 }",
        // A parser-only shadow control: resolution admission remains separate.
        "module prelude.forecast; @id(\"shadow.option\") record Option { } @id(\"entry\") fn main() -> i64 { 0 }",
        "module prelude.forecast; @id(\"shadow.result\") record Result { } @id(\"entry\") fn main() -> i64 { 0 }",
    ] {
        let programs = [crate::parse(source, "forecast.spx").unwrap()];
        let authored = crate::workspace_graph::index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        let maximum = dependency_identity_max(&programs[0], &authored, &programs).unwrap();
        let delta = retained_upper(&BASE, maximum).unwrap();
        for mode in 0..=7 {
            for retention in [cached_exact::Retention::Parsed, cached_exact::Retention::Semantic] {
                let current = super::super::call_carriers_original::synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry(
                    &programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(maximum), true, (mode, retention), None).unwrap();
                let saved = super::super::prelude_forecast_original::synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry(
                    &programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(maximum), true, (mode, retention), None).unwrap();
                assert_eq!(current.retained_hir, saved.retained_hir + delta);
                assert_eq!(current.retained_clone_and_hir, saved.retained_clone_and_hir + delta);
                assert_eq!(current.raw_clone_and_hir, saved.raw_clone_and_hir + delta);
                assert_eq!(current.runtime, saved.runtime);
                assert_eq!(current.synthetic_ast, saved.synthetic_ast);
                assert_eq!(current.transient_import_clone, saved.transient_import_clone);
            }

    }
}
//! Shared source-held closure consumption for forecast and synthetic construction.
use super::{
    active_builder_limit, ast_function_contract_cost, ast_function_cost,
    ast_function_identity_slots, ast_function_signature_cost, ast_program_cost,
    ast_program_identity_slots, ast_type_declaration_cost, ast_type_declaration_identity_slots,
    ast_type_identity_slots, cached_exact, call_identity, checked_builder_sum, checked_usage, cost,
    default_expr, generic_instance_source_cost, graph_error, import_stub, limit_error,
    private_layout, reserve_builder_structure, synthetic_main_runtime_cost, type_rewrite,
    AuthoredDeclaration, StructuralCost, SyntheticBuilderCosts, HIR_IDENTITY_COPY_FACTOR,
    HIR_STRING_COPY_FACTOR, HIR_STRUCTURE_EXPANSION_FACTOR,
};
use crate::ast::{Expr, ExprKind, Function, ModuleUseKind, Program, Span, Type, TypeDeclaration};
use crate::bounded_output::budgeted_format;
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::{hir, prelude};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    programs: &[Program],
    maximum_identity: Option<usize>,
    include_imports: bool,
    profile: (u8, cached_exact::Retention),
    registry: Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
) -> Result<SyntheticBuilderCosts, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry_using(
        program,
        authored,
        programs,
        maximum_identity,
        include_imports,
        profile,
        (registry, None),
    )
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_builder_bytes_core<'a>(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
    programs: &'a [Program],
    maximum_identity: Option<usize>,
    include_imports: bool,
    profile: (u8, cached_exact::Retention),
    proofs: (
        Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
        &mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'a>,
    ),
) -> Result<SyntheticBuilderCosts, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry_using(
        program,
        authored,
        programs,
        maximum_identity,
        include_imports,
        profile,
        (proofs.0, Some(proofs.1)),
    )
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry_using<
    'a,
>(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
    programs: &'a [Program],
    maximum_identity: Option<usize>,
    include_imports: bool,
    profile: (u8, cached_exact::Retention),
    proofs: (
        Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
        Option<&mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'a>>,
    ),
) -> Result<SyntheticBuilderCosts, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let (registry, core) = proofs;
    let (layout_mode, retention) = profile;
    let mut raw = StructuralCost::raw_ast(layout_mode >= 1).with_inline_values(layout_mode >= 2);
    ast_program_cost(program, &mut raw, include_imports)?;
    let fresh;
    let held;
    let layout = if let Some(registry) = registry {
        held = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
        &*held
    } else {
        fresh = private_layout::LayoutClosure::derive(program, authored, programs)?;
        &fresh
    };
    let mut identity_slots = ast_program_identity_slots(program)?;
    let mut runtime = StructuralCost::new();
    let mut default_memo = [BTreeMap::new(), BTreeMap::new()];
    let mut byte_proofs = import_stub::core_proofs::Phase::new(authored, programs, core)
        .map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
    let mut transient_import_clone = 0usize;
    layout.add_costs(
        program,
        programs,
        &mut raw,
        &mut runtime,
        &mut identity_slots,
    )?;
    for module_use in program.module_uses.iter().filter(|_| include_imports) {
        if module_use.kind == ModuleUseKind::Protocol {
            continue;
        }
        let target = &authored[module_use.persistent_id.as_str()];
        runtime.string(&module_use.alias)?;
        if let Some(function) = target.function {
            // `synthetic_program` retains an imported function as a stub: the
            // signature it rewrites, with `requires` and `ensures` cleared and
            // the body replaced by the return type's default expression. Only
            // the retained signature is charged here; the default body is
            // charged as runtime cost below. The provider's own contract and
            // body reach the resolver exactly once, through the defining
            // module's own `ast_program_cost`.
            ast_function_signature_cost(function, &mut raw)?;
            // Preserve old fitting receipts even though ordinary stubs now
            // clone only their signature. The historical transient-body debit
            // remains conservative; only mode 4 removes its peak after all
            // older estimates refuse. Intrinsic wrapper bodies are separately
            // charged as retained runtime structures below.
            transient_import_clone =
                transient_import_clone.max(ast_function_contract_cost(function)?.total);
            if programs
                .iter()
                .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
                .is_some_and(|provider| {
                    crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper(provider, function).is_some()
                        || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper(provider, function).is_some()
                })
            {
                ast_function_cost(function, &mut runtime)?;
                identity_slots =
                    checked_builder_sum(identity_slots, ast_function_identity_slots(function)?)?;
                continue;
            }
            identity_slots = checked_builder_sum(
                identity_slots,
                ast_type_identity_slots(&function.return_type)?,
            )?;
            for param in &function.params {
                identity_slots =
                    checked_builder_sum(identity_slots, ast_type_identity_slots(&param.ty)?)?;
                type_rewrite::runtime_cost_with_layout(
                    &param.ty,
                    target.module,
                    program,
                    programs,
                    &mut runtime,
                    Some(layout),
                )?;
            }
            type_rewrite::runtime_cost_with_layout(
                &function.return_type,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                &mut runtime,
                Some(layout),
            )?;
            let cost = import_stub::default_expr_expanded_cost_with_layout(
                function,
                target.module,
                program,
                authored,
                programs,
                &mut default_memo,
                &mut BTreeSet::new(),
                super::super::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                    program, target, authored, programs, layout,
                ),
                import_stub::byte_helper_authorized(
                    function,
                    target.module,
                    byte_proofs.get().map_err(|error| vec![error])?,
                )?,
                Some(layout),
            )?;
            runtime.add_split(cost.bytes, cost.string_bytes)?;
            runtime.add_identity_carriers(cost.identity_carriers)?;
            identity_slots = checked_builder_sum(identity_slots, cost.identity_slots)?;
        } else {
            let declaration = target.ty.expect("validated type target");
            ast_type_declaration_cost(declaration, &mut raw)?;
            identity_slots = checked_builder_sum(
                identity_slots,
                ast_type_declaration_identity_slots(declaration)?,
            )?;
            super::super::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_runtime_cost_with_layout(
                declaration,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                &mut runtime,
                Some(layout),
            )?;
        }
    }
    if !program
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
    {
        let synthetic_main = synthetic_main_runtime_cost(&program.module)?;
        runtime.add_split(synthetic_main.total, synthetic_main.string_bytes)?;
        runtime.add_identity_carriers(synthetic_main.identity_carriers)?;
    }
    if layout_mode >= 2 {
        identity_slots = identity_slots
            .checked_sub(call_identity::discount_with_imports(
                program,
                authored,
                include_imports,
            ))
            .expect("scalar calls retain their expression and callee identity slots");
    }
    identity_slots = identity_slots
        .checked_sub(raw.scalar_identity_discount)
        .expect("raw scalar discount retains each expression identity slot");
    let generic_instances = generic_instance_source_cost(program)?;
    identity_slots = checked_builder_sum(identity_slots, generic_instances.identity_slots)?;
    let hir_input = checked_usage(
        raw.total,
        runtime.total,
        "builder_bytes",
        active_builder_limit(),
    )?;
    let hir_input = checked_usage(
        hir_input,
        generic_instances.bytes,
        "builder_bytes",
        active_builder_limit(),
    )?;
    let string_input = checked_builder_sum(raw.string_bytes, runtime.string_bytes)?;
    let string_input = checked_builder_sum(string_input, generic_instances.string_bytes)?;
    // String contents are part of `hir_input`; only the remaining structural
    // bytes expand by the structural factor.
    let structure_input = hir_input.saturating_sub(string_input);
    let fixed_hir_upper = structure_input
        .checked_mul(HIR_STRUCTURE_EXPANSION_FACTOR)
        .and_then(|structure| {
            string_input
                .checked_mul(HIR_STRING_COPY_FACTOR)
                .and_then(|strings| structure.checked_add(strings))
        })
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    let maximum_identity_bytes = maximum_identity.unwrap_or_else(|| {
        authored
            .keys()
            .map(|id| id.len())
            .chain(prelude::all_ids().into_iter().map(str::len))
            .max()
            .unwrap_or(0)
    });
    #[cfg(test)]
    let prior_fixed_hir_upper = fixed_hir_upper;
    let fixed_hir_upper = if layout_mode == cached_exact::MODE {
        cached_exact::fixed_upper(fixed_hir_upper, &raw, &runtime, retention)?
    } else {
        fixed_hir_upper
            .checked_sub(cost::fixed_hir_discount(layout_mode, &raw, &runtime)?)
            .expect("literal storage discount retains the complete reduced fixed bundle")
    };
    let identity_occurrence_upper = identity_slots
        .checked_mul(maximum_identity_bytes)
        .and_then(|bytes| bytes.checked_mul(HIR_IDENTITY_COPY_FACTOR))
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    // Heap carriers are prebound independently of inline layouts and shared clones.
    let identity_carriers = cost::identity_carriers(layout_mode, &raw, &runtime)?;
    let identity_carriers =
        checked_builder_sum(identity_carriers, generic_instances.identity_carriers)?;
    let identity_carrier_bytes = identity_carriers
        .checked_mul(hir::ExpressionId::SHARED_ALLOCATION_CARRIER_BYTES)
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    let hir_upper = fixed_hir_upper
        .checked_add(identity_carrier_bytes)
        .and_then(|bytes| bytes.checked_add(identity_occurrence_upper))
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    let retained_clone_and_hir = checked_usage(
        raw.total,
        hir_upper,
        "builder_bytes",
        active_builder_limit(),
    )?;
    #[cfg(test)]
    let original_mode7_fixed_upper = raw
        .exact_expressions
        .checked_add(runtime.exact_expressions)
        .and_then(|count| {
            count
                .checked_mul(super::super::HIR_FIXED_EXPANSION_FACTOR)
                .and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::Expr>()))
                .and_then(|old| prior_fixed_hir_upper.checked_sub(old))
                .and_then(|base| {
                    count
                        .checked_mul(if retention == cached_exact::Retention::Semantic {
                            2
                        } else {
                            1
                        })
                        .and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(super::super::HIR_EXPR_FIXED_BUNDLE))
                        .and_then(|exact| base.checked_add(exact))
                })
        })
        .unwrap_or(usize::MAX);
    #[cfg(test)]
    super::super::forecast_observation::record(
        &program.path,
        layout_mode,
        retention == cached_exact::Retention::Semantic,
        [
            raw.total,
            raw.string_bytes,
            raw.exact_expressions,
            raw.literal_fixed_discount,
            runtime.total,
            runtime.string_bytes,
            runtime.exact_expressions,
            structure_input,
            string_input,
            prior_fixed_hir_upper,
            fixed_hir_upper,
            identity_occurrence_upper,
            identity_carrier_bytes,
            retained_clone_and_hir,
            generic_instances.bytes,
            transient_import_clone,
            original_mode7_fixed_upper,
        ],
    );
    let result = SyntheticBuilderCosts {
        raw_clone_and_hir: checked_usage(
            retained_clone_and_hir,
            transient_import_clone,
            "builder_bytes",
            active_builder_limit(),
        )?,
        retained_clone_and_hir,
        transient_import_clone,
        runtime: runtime.total,
        retained_hir: hir_upper,
        synthetic_ast: checked_usage(
            raw.total,
            runtime.total,
            "builder_bytes",
            active_builder_limit(),
        )?,
    };
    byte_proofs.complete().map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
    Ok(result)
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_program_with_registry(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    programs: &[Program],
    registry: Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    synthetic_program_with_registry_using(program, authored, programs, registry, None)
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_program_core<'a>(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
    programs: &'a [Program],
    registry: Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
    core: &mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'a>,
) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    synthetic_program_with_registry_using(program, authored, programs, registry, Some(core))
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_program_with_registry_using<'a>(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
    programs: &'a [Program],
    registry: Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
    core: Option<&mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'a>>,
) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let fresh;
    let held;
    let layout = if let Some(registry) = registry {
        held = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
        &*held
    } else {
        fresh = private_layout::LayoutClosure::derive(program, authored, programs)?;
        &fresh
    };
    let mut byte_proofs = import_stub::core_proofs::Phase::new(authored, programs, core)
        .map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
    let mut synthetic = program.clone();
    synthetic.module_uses.clear();
    // Project validation already authenticated static implementation sidecars.
    // Runtime HIR has no witness or dispatch representation for them.
    synthetic.implementations.clear();
    for module_use in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|item| item.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
    {
        let target = &authored[module_use.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let mut ty = target.ty.expect("validated type target").clone();
        ty.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&module_use.alias);
        super::super::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            &mut ty,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(layout),
        )?;
        synthetic.types.push(ty);
    }
    layout.append_types(&mut synthetic, program, programs)?;
    let type_index_bytes = synthetic
        .types
        .len()
        .checked_mul(std::mem::size_of::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>())
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    reserve_builder_structure(type_index_bytes)?;
    let mut type_declarations = Vec::with_capacity(synthetic.types.len());
    for declaration in &synthetic.types {
        type_declarations.push((declaration.name.as_str(), declaration));
    }
    type_declarations.sort_by(|left, right| left.0.cmp(right.0));
    if type_declarations
        .windows(2)
        .any(|items| items[0].0 == items[1].0)
    {
        return Err(vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "synthetic workspace type-name index is not unique",
        )]);
    }
    for module_use in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|item| item.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
    {
        let target = &authored[module_use.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let target_function = target.function.expect("validated function target");
        let retain_body = programs
            .iter()
            .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
            .is_some_and(|provider| {
                crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper(provider, target_function).is_some()
                    || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper(provider, target_function).is_some()
            });
        let mut function = if retain_body {
            target_function.clone()
        } else {
            import_stub::signature(target_function)
        };
        function.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&module_use.alias);
        if retain_body {
            synthetic.functions.push(function);
            continue;
        }
        for param in &mut function.params {
            type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
                &mut param.ty,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                Some(layout),
            )?;
        }
        type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
            &mut function.return_type,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(layout),
        )?;
        // Clearing would retain the cloned contracts' Vec capacities in every
        // finished stub. Replace the vectors so the full imported contracts
        // remain transient, matching the final peak-only pre-bound.
        function.requires = Vec::new();
        function.ensures = Vec::new();
        function.body = default_expr(
            &function,
            &type_declarations,
            super::super::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                program, target, authored, programs, layout,
            ),
            import_stub::byte_helper_authorized(
                target_function,
                target.module,
                byte_proofs.get().map_err(|error| vec![error])?,
            )?,
        )?;
        synthetic.functions.push(function);
    }
    if !synthetic
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
    {
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Function>())?;
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())?;
        synthetic.functions.push(Function {
            stable_id: budgeted_format(format_args!(
                "workspace.synthetic.main.{}",
                synthetic.module
            )),
            explicit_id: true,
            name: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone("main"),
            name_span: Span::default(),
            type_parameters: Vec::new(),
            params: Vec::new(),
            return_type: Type::I64,
            effects: Vec::new(),
            yields: None,
            follows: None,
            requires: Vec::new(),
            ensures: Vec::new(),
            body: Expr {
                kind: ExprKind::Int(0),
                span: Span::default(),
            },
            span: Span::default(),
        });
    }
    byte_proofs.complete().map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
    Ok(synthetic)
}
//! Shared source-held closure consumption for forecast and synthetic construction.
use super::{
    active_builder_limit, ast_function_contract_cost, ast_function_cost,
    ast_function_identity_slots, ast_function_signature_cost, ast_program_cost,
    ast_program_identity_slots, ast_type_declaration_cost, ast_type_declaration_identity_slots,
    ast_type_identity_slots, cached_exact, call_identity, checked_builder_sum, checked_usage, cost,
    generic_instance_source_cost, graph_error, import_stub_original as import_stub,
    import_stub_original::default_expr, limit_error, private_layout, reserve_builder_structure,
    synthetic_main_runtime_cost, type_rewrite, AuthoredDeclaration, StructuralCost,
    SyntheticBuilderCosts, HIR_IDENTITY_COPY_FACTOR, HIR_STRING_COPY_FACTOR,
    HIR_STRUCTURE_EXPANSION_FACTOR,
};
use crate::ast::{Expr, ExprKind, Function, ModuleUseKind, Program, Span, Type, TypeDeclaration};
use crate::bounded_output::budgeted_format;
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::{hir, prelude};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_retention_with_registry(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    programs: &[Program],
    maximum_identity: Option<usize>,
    include_imports: bool,
    profile: (u8, cached_exact::Retention),
    registry: Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
) -> Result<SyntheticBuilderCosts, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let (layout_mode, retention) = profile;
    let mut raw = StructuralCost::raw_ast(layout_mode >= 1).with_inline_values(layout_mode >= 2);
    ast_program_cost(program, &mut raw, include_imports)?;
    let fresh;
    let held;
    let layout = if let Some(registry) = registry {
        held = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
        &*held
    } else {
        fresh = private_layout::LayoutClosure::derive(program, authored, programs)?;
        &fresh
    };
    let mut identity_slots = ast_program_identity_slots(program)?;
    let mut runtime = StructuralCost::new();
    let mut default_memo = [BTreeMap::new(), BTreeMap::new()];
    let mut byte_proofs = import_stub::ByteHelperProofs::new(authored, programs);
    let mut transient_import_clone = 0usize;
    layout.add_costs(
        program,
        programs,
        &mut raw,
        &mut runtime,
        &mut identity_slots,
    )?;
    for module_use in program.module_uses.iter().filter(|_| include_imports) {
        if module_use.kind == ModuleUseKind::Protocol {
            continue;
        }
        let target = &authored[module_use.persistent_id.as_str()];
        runtime.string(&module_use.alias)?;
        if let Some(function) = target.function {
            // `synthetic_program` retains an imported function as a stub: the
            // signature it rewrites, with `requires` and `ensures` cleared and
            // the body replaced by the return type's default expression. Only
            // the retained signature is charged here; the default body is
            // charged as runtime cost below. The provider's own contract and
            // body reach the resolver exactly once, through the defining
            // module's own `ast_program_cost`.
            ast_function_signature_cost(function, &mut raw)?;
            // Preserve old fitting receipts even though ordinary stubs now
            // clone only their signature. The historical transient-body debit
            // remains conservative; only mode 4 removes its peak after all
            // older estimates refuse. Intrinsic wrapper bodies are separately
            // charged as retained runtime structures below.
            transient_import_clone =
                transient_import_clone.max(ast_function_contract_cost(function)?.total);
            if programs
                .iter()
                .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
                .is_some_and(|provider| {
                    crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper_original(provider, function).is_some()
                        || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper_original(provider, function).is_some()
                })
            {
                ast_function_cost(function, &mut runtime)?;
                identity_slots =
                    checked_builder_sum(identity_slots, ast_function_identity_slots(function)?)?;
                continue;
            }
            identity_slots = checked_builder_sum(
                identity_slots,
                ast_type_identity_slots(&function.return_type)?,
            )?;
            for param in &function.params {
                identity_slots =
                    checked_builder_sum(identity_slots, ast_type_identity_slots(&param.ty)?)?;
                type_rewrite::runtime_cost_with_layout(
                    &param.ty,
                    target.module,
                    program,
                    programs,
                    &mut runtime,
                    Some(layout),
                )?;
            }
            type_rewrite::runtime_cost_with_layout(
                &function.return_type,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                &mut runtime,
                Some(layout),
            )?;
            let cost = import_stub::default_expr_expanded_cost_with_layout(
                function,
                target.module,
                program,
                authored,
                programs,
                &mut default_memo,
                &mut BTreeSet::new(),
                super::super::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                    program, target, authored, programs, layout,
                ),
                import_stub::byte_helper_authorized(function, target.module, &mut byte_proofs)?,
                Some(layout),
            )?;
            runtime.add_split(cost.bytes, cost.string_bytes)?;
            runtime.add_identity_carriers(cost.identity_carriers)?;
            identity_slots = checked_builder_sum(identity_slots, cost.identity_slots)?;
        } else {
            let declaration = target.ty.expect("validated type target");
            ast_type_declaration_cost(declaration, &mut raw)?;
            identity_slots = checked_builder_sum(
                identity_slots,
                ast_type_declaration_identity_slots(declaration)?,
            )?;
            super::super::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_runtime_cost_with_layout(
                declaration,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                &mut runtime,
                Some(layout),
            )?;
        }
    }
    if !program
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
    {
        let synthetic_main = synthetic_main_runtime_cost(&program.module)?;
        runtime.add_split(synthetic_main.total, synthetic_main.string_bytes)?;
        runtime.add_identity_carriers(synthetic_main.identity_carriers)?;
    }
    if layout_mode >= 2 {
        identity_slots = identity_slots
            .checked_sub(call_identity::discount_with_imports(
                program,
                authored,
                include_imports,
            ))
            .expect("scalar calls retain their expression and callee identity slots");
    }
    identity_slots = identity_slots
        .checked_sub(raw.scalar_identity_discount)
        .expect("raw scalar discount retains each expression identity slot");
    let generic_instances = generic_instance_source_cost(program)?;
    identity_slots = checked_builder_sum(identity_slots, generic_instances.identity_slots)?;
    let hir_input = checked_usage(
        raw.total,
        runtime.total,
        "builder_bytes",
        active_builder_limit(),
    )?;
    let hir_input = checked_usage(
        hir_input,
        generic_instances.bytes,
        "builder_bytes",
        active_builder_limit(),
    )?;
    let string_input = checked_builder_sum(raw.string_bytes, runtime.string_bytes)?;
    let string_input = checked_builder_sum(string_input, generic_instances.string_bytes)?;
    // String contents are part of `hir_input`; only the remaining structural
    // bytes expand by the structural factor.
    let structure_input = hir_input.saturating_sub(string_input);
    let fixed_hir_upper = structure_input
        .checked_mul(HIR_STRUCTURE_EXPANSION_FACTOR)
        .and_then(|structure| {
            string_input
                .checked_mul(HIR_STRING_COPY_FACTOR)
                .and_then(|strings| structure.checked_add(strings))
        })
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    let maximum_identity_bytes = maximum_identity.unwrap_or_else(|| {
        authored
            .keys()
            .map(|id| id.len())
            .chain(prelude::all_ids().into_iter().map(str::len))
            .max()
            .unwrap_or(0)
    });
    #[cfg(test)]
    let prior_fixed_hir_upper = fixed_hir_upper;
    let fixed_hir_upper = if layout_mode == cached_exact::MODE {
        cached_exact::fixed_upper(fixed_hir_upper, &raw, &runtime, retention)?
    } else {
        fixed_hir_upper
            .checked_sub(cost::fixed_hir_discount(layout_mode, &raw, &runtime)?)
            .expect("literal storage discount retains the complete reduced fixed bundle")
    };
    let identity_occurrence_upper = identity_slots
        .checked_mul(maximum_identity_bytes)
        .and_then(|bytes| bytes.checked_mul(HIR_IDENTITY_COPY_FACTOR))
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    // Heap carriers are prebound independently of inline layouts and shared clones.
    let identity_carriers = cost::identity_carriers(layout_mode, &raw, &runtime)?;
    let identity_carriers =
        checked_builder_sum(identity_carriers, generic_instances.identity_carriers)?;
    let identity_carrier_bytes = identity_carriers
        .checked_mul(hir::ExpressionId::SHARED_ALLOCATION_CARRIER_BYTES)
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    let hir_upper = fixed_hir_upper
        .checked_add(identity_carrier_bytes)
        .and_then(|bytes| bytes.checked_add(identity_occurrence_upper))
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    let retained_clone_and_hir = checked_usage(
        raw.total,
        hir_upper,
        "builder_bytes",
        active_builder_limit(),
    )?;
    #[cfg(test)]
    let original_mode7_fixed_upper = raw
        .exact_expressions
        .checked_add(runtime.exact_expressions)
        .and_then(|count| {
            count
                .checked_mul(super::super::HIR_FIXED_EXPANSION_FACTOR)
                .and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::Expr>()))
                .and_then(|old| prior_fixed_hir_upper.checked_sub(old))
                .and_then(|base| {
                    count
                        .checked_mul(if retention == cached_exact::Retention::Semantic {
                            2
                        } else {
                            1
                        })
                        .and_then(|n| n.checked_mul(super::super::HIR_EXPR_FIXED_BUNDLE))
                        .and_then(|exact| base.checked_add(exact))
                })
        })
        .unwrap_or(usize::MAX);
    #[cfg(test)]
    super::super::forecast_observation::record(
        &program.path,
        layout_mode,
        retention == cached_exact::Retention::Semantic,
        [
            raw.total,
            raw.string_bytes,
            raw.exact_expressions,
            raw.literal_fixed_discount,
            runtime.total,
            runtime.string_bytes,
            runtime.exact_expressions,
            structure_input,
            string_input,
            prior_fixed_hir_upper,
            fixed_hir_upper,
            identity_occurrence_upper,
            identity_carrier_bytes,
            retained_clone_and_hir,
            generic_instances.bytes,
            transient_import_clone,
            original_mode7_fixed_upper,
        ],
    );
    Ok(SyntheticBuilderCosts {
        raw_clone_and_hir: checked_usage(
            retained_clone_and_hir,
            transient_import_clone,
            "builder_bytes",
            active_builder_limit(),
        )?,
        retained_clone_and_hir,
        transient_import_clone,
        runtime: runtime.total,
        retained_hir: hir_upper,
        synthetic_ast: checked_usage(
            raw.total,
            runtime.total,
            "builder_bytes",
            active_builder_limit(),
        )?,
    })
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn synthetic_program_with_registry(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    programs: &[Program],
    registry: Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let fresh;
    let held;
    let layout = if let Some(registry) = registry {
        held = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
        &*held
    } else {
        fresh = private_layout::LayoutClosure::derive(program, authored, programs)?;
        &fresh
    };
    let mut byte_proofs = import_stub::ByteHelperProofs::new(authored, programs);
    let mut synthetic = program.clone();
    synthetic.module_uses.clear();
    // Project validation already authenticated static implementation sidecars.
    // Runtime HIR has no witness or dispatch representation for them.
    synthetic.implementations.clear();
    for module_use in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|item| item.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
    {
        let target = &authored[module_use.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let mut ty = target.ty.expect("validated type target").clone();
        ty.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&module_use.alias);
        super::super::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            &mut ty,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(layout),
        )?;
        synthetic.types.push(ty);
    }
    layout.append_types(&mut synthetic, program, programs)?;
    let type_index_bytes = synthetic
        .types
        .len()
        .checked_mul(std::mem::size_of::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>())
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    reserve_builder_structure(type_index_bytes)?;
    let mut type_declarations = Vec::with_capacity(synthetic.types.len());
    for declaration in &synthetic.types {
        type_declarations.push((declaration.name.as_str(), declaration));
    }
    type_declarations.sort_by(|left, right| left.0.cmp(right.0));
    if type_declarations
        .windows(2)
        .any(|items| items[0].0 == items[1].0)
    {
        return Err(vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "synthetic workspace type-name index is not unique",
        )]);
    }
    for module_use in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|item| item.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
    {
        let target = &authored[module_use.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let target_function = target.function.expect("validated function target");
        let retain_body = programs
            .iter()
            .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
            .is_some_and(|provider| {
                crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper_original(provider, target_function).is_some()
                    || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper_original(provider, target_function).is_some()
            });
        let mut function = if retain_body {
            target_function.clone()
        } else {
            import_stub::signature(target_function)
        };
        function.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&module_use.alias);
        if retain_body {
            synthetic.functions.push(function);
            continue;
        }
        for param in &mut function.params {
            type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
                &mut param.ty,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                Some(layout),
            )?;
        }
        type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
            &mut function.return_type,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(layout),
        )?;
        // Clearing would retain the cloned contracts' Vec capacities in every
        // finished stub. Replace the vectors so the full imported contracts
        // remain transient, matching the final peak-only pre-bound.
        function.requires = Vec::new();
        function.ensures = Vec::new();
        function.body = default_expr(
            &function,
            &type_declarations,
            super::super::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                program, target, authored, programs, layout,
            ),
            import_stub::byte_helper_authorized(target_function, target.module, &mut byte_proofs)?,
        )?;
        synthetic.functions.push(function);
    }
    if !synthetic
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
    {
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Function>())?;
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())?;
        synthetic.functions.push(Function {
            stable_id: budgeted_format(format_args!(
                "workspace.synthetic.main.{}",
                synthetic.module
            )),
            explicit_id: true,
            name: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone("main"),
            name_span: Span::default(),
            type_parameters: Vec::new(),
            params: Vec::new(),
            return_type: Type::I64,
            effects: Vec::new(),
            yields: None,
            follows: None,
            requires: Vec::new(),
            ensures: Vec::new(),
            body: Expr {
                kind: ExprKind::Int(0),
                span: Span::default(),
            },
            span: Span::default(),
        });
    }
    Ok(synthetic)
}
//! Exact-source borrowed input for the generated scalar-main leaf route.
//! Imported/private layouts use their exact overlay producer; invariant
//! transformation acquires writable AST only at its ordinary HIR boundary.
use super::{
    active_builder_limit, checked_usage, graph_error, limit_error, private_layout,
    reserve_builder_structure, synthetic_main_runtime_cost, synthetic_program_with_registry,
    AuthoredDeclaration,
};
use crate::ast::program_source::SyntheticProgramView;
use crate::ast::Span;
use crate::ast::{Expr, ExprKind, Function, Program, Type, TypeDeclaration};
use crate::bounded_output::budgeted_format;
use crate::bounded_output::identity_reservation::{IdentityReservation, Refused};
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::hir;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn eligible(program: &Program) -> bool {
    program.module_uses.is_empty()
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct(
    program: &Program,
) -> Result<SyntheticProgramView<'_>, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    if !eligible(program) {
        return Err(vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "borrowed projection requires the owned route",
        )]);
    }
    // Preserve the owned producer's type-name preflight and its diagnostic
    // order. This directory borrows actual authored declarations; no body or
    // declaration is materialized merely to run the ordinary check.
    let index_bytes = type_index_bytes(program)?;
    reserve_builder_structure(index_bytes)?;
    let mut types = Vec::with_capacity(program.types.len());
    for declaration in &program.types {
        types.push((declaration.name.as_str(), declaration));
    }
    // Duplicate keys always refuse with the same diagnostic; equal-key order
    // is never consumed. Unstable sorting retains that verdict without a
    // hidden stable-sort scratch allocation.
    types.sort_unstable_by(|left, right| left.0.cmp(right.0));
    if types.windows(2).any(|items| items[0].0 == items[1].0) {
        return Err(vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "synthetic workspace type-name index is not unique",
        )]);
    }
    let mut generated = Vec::new();
    if !program
        .functions
        .iter()
