        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
    {
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Function>())?;
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())?;
        generated = Vec::with_capacity(1);
        generated.push(Function {
            stable_id: budgeted_format(format_args!("workspace.synthetic.main.{}", program.module)),
            explicit_id: true,
            name: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone("main"),
            name_span: Span::default(),
            type_parameters: Vec::new(),
            params: Vec::new(),
            return_type: Type::I64,
            effects: Vec::new(),
            yields: None,
            follows: None,
            requires: Vec::new(),
            ensures: Vec::new(),
            body: Expr {
                kind: ExprKind::Int(0),
                span: Span::default(),
            },
            span: Span::default(),
        });
    }
    // Only a leaf Int body is owned, so ordinary field Drop allocates nothing
    // and never descends through an authored recursive subtree.
    SyntheticProgramView::new(program, generated)
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn ast_peak(program: &Program) -> Result<usize, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let generated = if program
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
    {
        0
    } else {
        synthetic_main_runtime_cost(&program.module)?.total
    };
    let owned = checked_usage(
        std::mem::size_of::<Input<'_>>(),
        generated,
        "builder_bytes",
        active_builder_limit(),
    )?;
    checked_usage(
        owned,
        type_index_bytes(program)?,
        "builder_bytes",
        active_builder_limit(),
    )
}
fn type_index_bytes(program: &Program) -> Result<usize, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    program
        .types
        .len()
        .checked_mul(std::mem::size_of::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>())
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])
}

/// Extra inline carrier over the ordinary owned Program. The resolved-core
/// invocation retains one reused input slot, never one header per module.
pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn input_carrier_delta() -> Result<usize, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    std::mem::size_of::<Input<'_>>()
        .checked_sub(std::mem::size_of::<Program>())
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])
}

/// Hits/reuse candidates and invariant transforms keep the owned input.
/// First checked cold keys become owned only at the retention boundary.
// The reused inline owning fallback is priced by input_carrier_delta using
// actual sizeof; a Box would introduce a separate unpriced allocation.
#[allow(clippy::large_enum_variant)]
pub(in crate::workspace_graph) enum Input<'a> {
    Owned(Program),
    Transient(crate::ast::program_source::function_alias::TransientFunctions<'a>),
    Borrowed(SyntheticProgramView<'a>),
}
impl<'a> Input<'a> {
    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct(
        program: &'a Program,
        authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
        programs: &[Program],
        registry: &private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>,
        _uncached: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
        if eligible(program) {
            construct(program).map(Self::Borrowed)
        } else if super::borrowed_imports::eligible(program, authored, programs) {
            super::borrowed_imports::construct(program, authored, programs, registry)
                .map(Self::Borrowed)
        } else {
            synthetic_program_with_registry(program, authored, programs, Some(registry))
                .map(Self::Owned)
        }
    }
    /// A live uncached resolver will drop its projection rather than publish a key.
    /// Acquire only generated backing; authored declarations remain source-held.
    pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct_transient(
        program: &'a Program,
        authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
        programs: &[Program],
        registry: &private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>,
    ) -> Result<Self, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
        if eligible(program) {
            construct(program).map(Self::Borrowed)
        } else if super::borrowed_imports::eligible(program, authored, programs) {
            super::borrowed_imports::construct(program, authored, programs, registry)
                .map(Self::Borrowed)
        } else {
            synthetic_program_with_registry(program, authored, programs, Some(registry))
                .map(Self::Owned)
        }
    }

    // CORE_TRANSIENT_BEGIN
    pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct_transient_core(
        program: &'a Program,
        authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
        programs: &'a [Program],
        registry: &private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>,
        core: &mut super::CoreProviderProofs<'a>,
    ) -> Result<Self, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
        if eligible(program) {
            construct(program).map(Self::Borrowed)
        } else if super::borrowed_imports::eligible(program, authored, programs) {
            super::borrowed_imports::construct_core(program, authored, programs, registry, core)
                .map(Self::Borrowed)
        } else {
            super::held_projection::synthetic_program_core(
                program,
                authored,
                programs,
                Some(registry),
                core,
            )
            .map(Self::Owned)
        }
    }
    // CORE_TRANSIENT_END

    pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct_receiving(
        program: &'a Program,
        authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
        programs: &[Program],
        registry: &private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>,
        _uncached: bool,
    ) -> Result<Self, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
        if eligible(program) {
            construct(program).map(Self::Borrowed)
        } else if super::borrowed_imports::eligible(program, authored, programs) {
            super::borrowed_imports::construct_receiving(program, authored, programs, registry)
                .map(Self::Borrowed)
        } else {
            synthetic_program_with_registry(program, authored, programs, Some(registry))
                .map(Self::Owned)
        }
    }
    pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn borrowed(&self) -> Option<&SyntheticProgramView<'_>> {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(_) | Self::Transient(_) => None,
            Self::Borrowed(program) => Some(program),
        }
    }

    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn into_checked_key(self) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(program) => Ok(program),
            Self::Borrowed(view) => checked_key::materialize(view),
            Self::Transient(_) => unreachable!("transient wrapper view cannot publish a cache key"),
        }
    }

    pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn into_split_key(
        self,
        source_slot: usize,
    ) -> Result<crate::ast::program_source::checked_key::CheckedSource, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
        reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
        match self {
            Self::Owned(program) => {
                Ok(crate::ast::program_source::checked_key::CheckedSource::Owned(program))
            }
            Self::Transient(_) => unreachable!("transient wrapper view cannot publish a cache key"),
            Self::Borrowed(view) => {
                let (program, generated) = view.into_overlay_owner();
                Ok(
                    crate::ast::program_source::checked_key::CheckedSource::deferred(
                        program,
                        generated,
                        source_slot,
                    ),
                )
            }
        }
    }

    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn into_receiving_key(self) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(program) => Ok(program),
            Self::Borrowed(view) => checked_key::materialize_receiving(view),
            Self::Transient(_) => unreachable!("transient wrapper view cannot publish a cache key"),
        }
    }

    pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn owned(&self) -> Option<&Program> {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(program) => Some(program),
            Self::Borrowed(_) | Self::Transient(_) => None,
        }
    }
    pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn source(
        &self,
    ) -> &dyn crate::ast::program_source::ProgramSource {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(program) => program,
            Self::Borrowed(program) => program,
            Self::Transient(program) => program,
        }
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn into_owned(self) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(program) => Ok(program),
            Self::Borrowed(_) | Self::Transient(_) => Err(vec![graph_error(
                "SPX-G173",
                "borrowed projection cannot become a checked cache key",
            )]),
        }
    }
    /// Fresh resolution of the actual Owned/Borrowed case with the caller's
    /// already acquired identity owner; no Program is materialized.
    pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn resolve_paid<'s>(
        &'s self,
        private: Option<crate::source_verify::private_names::PrivateNames<'s>>,
        owner: &mut IdentityReservation,
    ) -> Result<
        (hir::ResolvedProgram, hir::FunctionResolutionWork),
        hir::identity_resolution::Failure<Refused>,
    > {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(program) => hir::identity_resolution::resolve_checked_nominal(
                program,
                None,
                None,
                None,
                private,
                |bytes| owner.reserve(bytes),
            ),
            Self::Borrowed(program) => {
                hir::identity_resolution::resolve_source_nominal(program, private, |bytes| {
                    owner.reserve(bytes)
                })
            }
            Self::Transient(program) => {
                hir::identity_resolution::resolve_source_nominal(program, private, |bytes| {
                    owner.reserve(bytes)
                })
            }
        }
    }
    pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn resolve_paid_with_base<'s>(
        &'s self,
        private: Option<crate::source_verify::private_names::PrivateNames<'s>>,
        invocation: &mut hir::prelude_bundle::Invocation<Refused>,
        owner: &mut IdentityReservation,
    ) -> Result<
        (hir::ResolvedProgram, hir::FunctionResolutionWork),
        hir::identity_resolution::NominalReceiverFailure,
    > {
        hir::identity_resolution::resolve_source_checked_previous_with_owner(
            self.source(),
            None,
            None,
            None,
            private,
            invocation,
            owner,
        )
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
#[path = "borrowed_leaf/tests.rs"]
mod tests;

#[path = "borrowed_leaf/checked_key.rs"]
mod checked_key;

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn checked_key_owned(
    view: SyntheticProgramView<'_>,
) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    checked_key::materialize(view)
}
use super::{
    construct, eligible, private_layout, reserve_builder_structure,
    synthetic_program_with_registry, Input,
};
use crate::ast::program_source::ProgramSource;
use crate::ast::TypeDeclaration;
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::hir;
use crate::workspace_graph::index_authored;
use std::fmt::Write;
const SOURCE: &str =
    "module borrowed_leaf; @id(\"read\") fn read(data:borrow Slice<u8>)->usize { byte_len(data) }";

#[test]
fn borrowed_leaf_preserves_owned_resolution_and_actual_authored_pointers() {
    for source in [SOURCE,
        "module borrowed_leaf; @id(\"main\") fn main()->i64 { let x=3; if x>0 { x+1 } else { 0 } }",
        "module borrowed_leaf; @id(\"read\") fn read(data:borrow Slice<u8>)->usize requires byte_len(data)>0usize { byte_len(data) }",
        "module borrowed_leaf; @id(\"row\") record Row { @id(\"row.value\") value:i64, } @id(\"main\") fn main()->i64 {0}",
    ] {
        let programs = [crate::parse(source, "leaf.spx").unwrap()];
        let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        let registry = private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
        let view = construct(&programs[0]).unwrap();
        assert!(std::ptr::eq(view.functions().get(0).unwrap(), &programs[0].functions[0]));
        assert!(std::ptr::eq(&view.functions().get(0).unwrap().body, &programs[0].functions[0].body));
        let other = construct(&programs[0]).unwrap();
        assert!(!view.subject().same(other.subject()));
        assert!(!view.subject().same(programs[0].subject()));
        let owned = synthetic_program_with_registry(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(&registry)).unwrap();
        let expected = hir::resolve_with_function_reuse(&owned, None, None, None, None).unwrap().0;
        let actual = hir::resolve_source(&view, None).unwrap().0;
        assert_eq!(actual, expected);
        // Complete independent HIR/cleanup/status validation is retained.
        hir::validate(&actual).unwrap();
    }
}

#[test]
fn borrowed_leaf_drop_has_no_allocation_and_constructor_limits_are_exact_and_sticky() {
    let program = crate::parse(SOURCE, "leaf.spx").unwrap();
    let (view, overflowed, required) =
        crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage(usize::MAX, || construct(&program));
    assert!(!overflowed);
    let view = view.unwrap();
    let (_, drop_metrics) = crate::test_allocation_observer::measure(|| drop(view));
    assert!(!drop_metrics.invalid);
    assert_eq!(drop_metrics.allocations, 0);
    assert_eq!(drop_metrics.reallocations, 0);
    assert!(required > 0);
    let ((result, allocation), overflowed, used) =
        crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage(required, || {
            crate::test_allocation_observer::measure(|| {
                let view = construct(&program)?;
                drop(view);
                Ok::<_, Vec<Diagnostic>>(())
            })
        });
    result.unwrap();
    assert!(!overflowed);
    assert_eq!(used, required);
    assert!(!allocation.invalid);
    assert_eq!(allocation.live, 0);
    assert!(allocation.allocated <= required);
    let ((first, retry), overflowed, _) =
        crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage(required - 1, || {
            let first = construct(&program);
            let retry = construct(&program);
            (first.is_err(), retry.is_err())
        });
    assert!(overflowed);
    assert!(first && retry);

    // Existing main removes generated-main overreservation. The independent
    // literal census has exactly 96 borrowed directory entries and no owned
    // expression/declaration subtree or sorting scratch.
    let mut source = String::from("module wide_leaf;");
    for index in 0..96 {
        let name = index * 37 % 96;
        write!(
            source,
            " @id(\"row.{index}\") record Row{name} {{ @id(\"row.{index}.value\") value:i64, }}"
        )
        .unwrap();
    }
    source.push_str(" @id(\"main\") fn main()->i64 {0}");
    let wide = crate::parse(&source, "wide-leaf.spx").unwrap();
    let programs = std::slice::from_ref(&wide);
    let authored = index_authored(programs).unwrap();
    let registry = private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry::new(programs, &authored);
    let directory = 96 * std::mem::size_of::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>();
    assert_eq!(
        super::ast_peak(&wide).unwrap(),
        directory + std::mem::size_of::<Input<'_>>()
    );
    let ((result, allocation), overflowed, used) =
        crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage(directory, || {
            crate::test_allocation_observer::measure(|| {
                let view = Input::construct(&wide, &authored, programs, &registry, true)?;
                assert_eq!(view.source().types().len(), 96);
                for (actual, authored) in view.source().types().iter().zip(&wide.types) {
                    assert!(std::ptr::eq(actual, authored));
                }
                drop(view);
                Ok::<_, Vec<Diagnostic>>(())
            })
        });
    result.unwrap();
    assert!(!overflowed);
    assert_eq!(used, directory);
    assert!(!allocation.invalid);
    assert_eq!(allocation.allocated, directory);
    assert_eq!(allocation.reallocations, 0);
    assert_eq!(allocation.live, 0);
    let ((first, retry), overflowed, _) =
        crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage(directory - 1, || {
            (
                Input::construct(&wide, &authored, programs, &registry, true).is_err(),
                Input::construct(&wide, &authored, programs, &registry, true).is_err(),
            )
        });
    assert!(overflowed);
    assert!(first && retry);
}

#[test]
fn borrowed_leaf_full_pipeline_keeps_owned_fallback_and_original_diagnostics() {
    let originals = [crate::parse(SOURCE, "leaf.spx").unwrap()];
    let original_authored = index_authored(&originals).unwrap();
    let mut owned =
        synthetic_program_with_registry(&originals[0], &original_authored, &originals, None)
            .unwrap();
    let mut program = originals[0].clone();
    let duplicate = program.functions[0].clone();
    owned.functions.insert(1, duplicate.clone());
    program.functions.push(duplicate);
    let view = construct(&program).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        hir::resolve_source(&view, None).err(),
        hir::resolve_with_function_reuse(&owned, None, None, None, None).err()
    );

    let duplicate_types = [crate::parse(
        "module duplicate_types; @id(\"first\") record Row { @id(\"first.value\") value:i64, } @id(\"second\") record Row { @id(\"second.value\") value:i64, } @id(\"duplicate.main\") fn main()->i64 { 0 }",
        "duplicate_types.spx",
    ).unwrap()];
    let type_authored = index_authored(&duplicate_types).unwrap();
    let old_error = synthetic_program_with_registry(
        &duplicate_types[0],
        &type_authored,
        &duplicate_types,
        None,
    )
    .err()
    .unwrap();
    let new_error = construct(&duplicate_types[0]).err().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(new_error, old_error);
    assert_eq!(new_error[0].code, "SPX-G173");

    let programs = [crate::parse(SOURCE, "leaf.spx").unwrap()];
    let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let registry = private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
    let prepared_owned =
        synthetic_program_with_registry(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(&registry))
            .unwrap();
    drop(hir::resolve(&prepared_owned).unwrap());
    drop(prepared_owned);
    let ((result, allocation), overflowed, used) =
        crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage(super::super::super::MAX_BUILDER_BYTES, || {
            crate::test_allocation_observer::measure(|| {
                let costs = super::super::synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_registry(
                    &programs[0],
                    &authored,
                    &programs,
                    None,
                    4,
                    true,
                    Some(&registry),
                )?;
                reserve_builder_structure(costs.raw_clone_and_hir)?;
                let view = construct(&programs[0])?;
                let resolved = hir::resolve_source(&view, None)?.0;
                hir::validate(&resolved).map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
                drop(resolved);
                drop(view);
                Ok::<_, Vec<Diagnostic>>(())
            })
        });
    result.unwrap();
    assert!(!overflowed);
    assert!(!allocation.invalid);
    assert_eq!(allocation.live, 0);
    assert!(allocation.highwater <= used);
    // Cache construction retains the independent ordinary owned key.
    let cached = Input::construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry, false).unwrap();
    assert!(cached.owned().is_none());
    let cached_key = cached.into_checked_key().unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        cached_key,
        synthetic_program_with_registry(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(&registry))
            .unwrap()
    );
    let delta = super::input_carrier_delta().unwrap();
    // The enum may reuse a niche in Program; zero extra inline bytes is valid.
    assert!(std::mem::size_of::<Input<'_>>() >= std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::Program>());
    assert_eq!(
        delta,
        std::mem::size_of::<Input<'_>>() - std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::Program>()
    );
    let uncached = Input::construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry, true).unwrap();
    assert!(uncached.owned().is_none());
    let imported = crate::parse("module imported; use function @id(\"read\") from borrowed_leaf as read; @id(\"main\") fn main()->i64 {0}", "imported.spx").unwrap();
    assert!(!eligible(&imported));
    assert!(construct(&imported).is_err());
}
//! Genuine owning cache publication boundary for the exact cold source view.
//! Cached full-clone forecasts remain unchanged. Retained authored declarations
//! are still copied once here; this does not claim a shared AST cache owner.
use super::{active_builder_limit, limit_error, reserve_builder_structure};
use crate::ast::program_source::SyntheticProgramView;
use crate::ast::Program;
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;

pub(super) fn materialize(view: SyntheticProgramView<'_>) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
    let (authored, mut roots) = view.into_overlay_owner();
    // Existing complete cached construction precharge retains the original
    // owning AST/function backing and generated-main allowance. This operation
    // occurs after the resolver receipt, before the owning cache key publishes.
    let imported = !authored.module_uses.is_empty();
    let (functions, types) = if imported {
        // Both real replacement directories are paid while recursive roots
        // remain under their iterative owner, including short/sticky refusal.
        let functions = combined_directory::<crate::ast::Function>(
            authored.functions.len(),
            roots.functions.len(),
        )?;
        let types = combined_directory::<crate::ast::TypeDeclaration>(
            authored.types.len(),
            roots.types.len(),
        )?;
        (
            append_prepaid(
                &authored.functions,
                std::mem::take(&mut roots.functions),
                functions,
            ),
            append_prepaid(&authored.types, std::mem::take(&mut roots.types), types),
        )
    } else {
        // Preserve the separately qualified leaf receiver.
        let mut functions = authored.functions.clone();
        functions.extend(std::mem::take(&mut roots.functions));
        (functions, authored.types.clone())
    };
    Ok(Program {
        path: authored.path.clone(),
        module: authored.module.clone(),
        module_uses: Vec::new(),
        permits: authored.permits.clone(),
        types,
        interfaces: authored.interfaces.clone(),
        protocols: authored.protocols.clone(),
        implementations: Vec::new(),
        session_protocols: authored.session_protocols.clone(),
        agents: authored.agents.clone(),
        functions,
    })
}

pub(super) fn materialize_receiving(
    view: SyntheticProgramView<'_>,
) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
    let (authored, owner) = view.into_receiving_owner();
    let mut roots = match owner {
        crate::ast::program_source::receiving_roots::GeneratedOwner::Plain(roots) => {
            return materialize(
                SyntheticProgramView::with_generated_owner(authored, roots)
                    .expect("unchanged generated view retains checked lengths"),
            );
        }
        crate::ast::program_source::receiving_roots::GeneratedOwner::Receiving(roots) => roots,
    };
    roots
        .types
        .as_ref()
        .expect("type directory acquired")
        .validate(&authored.types)
        .map_err(|()| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    roots
        .functions
        .as_ref()
        .expect("function directory acquired")
        .validate(&authored.functions)
        .map_err(|()| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    // Both real Vecs remain inseparable from their owners until validation of
    // both roots succeeds. No external backing or address receipt is accepted.
    let functions = roots
        .functions
        .take()
        .expect("validated function directory")
        .finish(&authored.functions)
        .map_err(|()| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    let types = roots
        .types
        .take()
        .expect("validated type directory")
        .finish(&authored.types)
        .map_err(|()| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    Ok(Program {
        path: authored.path.clone(),
        module: authored.module.clone(),
        module_uses: Vec::new(),
        permits: authored.permits.clone(),
        types,
        interfaces: authored.interfaces.clone(),
        protocols: authored.protocols.clone(),
        implementations: Vec::new(),
        session_protocols: authored.session_protocols.clone(),
        agents: authored.agents.clone(),
        functions,
    })
}

// Owning publication copies authored payloads under its existing complete cache
// precharge. A real new combined directory is prepaid in full while generated
// backing remains live; generated declarations themselves are moved once.
fn combined_directory<T>(
    authored: usize,
    generated: usize,
) -> Result<Option<Vec<T>>, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    if authored == 0 || generated == 0 {
        return Ok(None);
    }
    let count = authored
        .checked_add(generated)
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    let bytes = count
        .checked_mul(std::mem::size_of::<T>())
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    reserve_builder_structure(bytes)?;
    let directory = Vec::with_capacity(count);
    if directory.capacity() != count {
        return Err(vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())]);
    }
    Ok(Some(directory))
}
fn append_prepaid<T: Clone>(
    authored: &[T],
    generated: Vec<T>,
    directory: Option<Vec<T>>,
) -> Vec<T> {
    if authored.is_empty() {
        return generated;
    }
    if generated.is_empty() {
        return authored.to_vec();
    }
    let mut combined = directory.expect("both source slices have a prepaid checked directory");
    combined.extend_from_slice(authored);
    combined.extend(generated);
    combined
}
#[cfg(test)]
fn append_copies<T: Clone>(authored: &[T], generated: Vec<T>) -> Result<Vec<T>, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let directory = combined_directory::<T>(authored.len(), generated.len())?;
    Ok(append_prepaid(authored, generated, directory))
}

#[cfg(test)]
#[path = "checked_key/tests.rs"]
mod tests;
use super::super::{construct, private_layout, synthetic_program_with_registry, Input, Span};
use super::{append_copies, materialize, reserve_builder_structure};
use crate::ast::program_source::ProgramSource;
use crate::hir;
use crate::test_allocation_observer::measure;
use crate::workspace_graph::index_authored;

#[test]
fn cold_checked_key_matches_complete_owned_producer_and_moves_generated_main() {
    for source in [
        "module cold_leaf; @id(\"read\") fn read()->i64 {1}",
        "module cold_leaf; @id(\"main\") fn main()->i64 {let x=3; if x>0 {x+1} else {0}}",
        "module cold_leaf; @id(\"row\") record Row { @id(\"row.x\") x:i64, } @id(\"read\") fn read(value:i64)->i64 requires value>0 {value}",
    ] {
        let program = crate::parse(source, "cold-leaf.spx").unwrap();
        let programs = std::slice::from_ref(&program);
        let authored = index_authored(programs).unwrap();
        let registry = private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry::new(programs, &authored);
        let old = synthetic_program_with_registry(&program, &authored, programs, Some(&registry)).unwrap();
        let view = construct(&program).unwrap();
        for (ordinal, function) in program.functions.iter().enumerate() {
            assert!(std::ptr::eq(view.functions().get(ordinal).unwrap(), function));
            assert!(std::ptr::eq(&view.functions().get(ordinal).unwrap().body, &function.body));
        }
        let generated = view.functions().get(program.functions.len()).map(|function| function.stable_id.as_ptr());
        let resolved = hir::resolve_source(&view, None).unwrap().0;
        assert_eq!(resolved, hir::resolve_with_function_reuse(&old, None, None, None, None).unwrap().0);
        let key = materialize(view).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(key, old);
        if let Some(pointer) = generated {
            assert_eq!(key.functions.last().unwrap().stable_id.as_ptr(), pointer);
        }
        for (owned, original) in key.functions.iter().zip(&program.functions) {
            assert!(!std::ptr::eq(owned, original));
            assert_eq!(owned, original);
        }
        // The old ephemeral-view rejection remains an independent negative.
        assert!(Input::Borrowed(construct(&program).unwrap()).into_owned().is_err());
    }
}

#[test]
fn checked_key_omits_only_discarded_sidecar_clone_payloads_and_preserves_sticky_refusal() {
    let mut program = crate::parse(
        "module cold_leaf; @id(\"main\") fn main()->i64 {0}",
        "cold-leaf.spx",
    )
    .unwrap();
    // This is a projection/storage witness, not source implementation authority.
    // Project validates real sidecars before either producer clears them.
    program
        .implementations
        .push(crate::ast::ProtocolImplementation {
            stable_id: "implementation".to_owned(),
            explicit_id: true,
            protocol_id: "protocol".to_owned(),
            receiver_id: "receiver".to_owned(),
            members: vec![crate::ast::ProtocolImplementationMember {
                method_id: "method".to_owned(),
                function_id: "function".to_owned(),
                span: Span::default(),
            }],
            span: Span::default(),
        });
    let view = construct(&program).unwrap();
    let (_, before) = measure(|| {
        let mut old = program.clone();
        old.module_uses.clear();
        old.implementations.clear();
        drop(old);
    });
    let (_, after) = measure(|| drop(materialize(view).unwrap()));
    let removed = std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::ProtocolImplementation>()
        + std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::ProtocolImplementationMember>()
        + "implementation".len()
        + "protocol".len()
        + "receiver".len()
        + "method".len()
        + "function".len();
    assert_eq!(before.allocations, after.allocations + 7);
    assert_eq!(before.reallocations, after.reallocations);
    assert_eq!(before.allocated, after.allocated + removed);
    for observed in [before, after] {
        assert!(!observed.invalid);
        assert_eq!(observed.live, 0);
        assert_eq!(observed.allocated, observed.freed);
    }
    let view = construct(&program).unwrap();
    let ((failed, retry), overflow, charged, first) =
        crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage_refusal(0, || {
            assert!(!crate::bounded_output::reserve_active_required(1));
            let failed = materialize(view).is_err();
            let retry = crate::bounded_output::reserve_active_required(0);
            (failed, retry)
        });
    assert!(failed && overflow && !retry);
    assert_eq!(charged, 0);
    assert!(first.is_some());
}

#[test]
fn imported_cache_directory_is_paid_while_generated_backing_is_live() {
    let authored = [11u64, 12];
    let bytes = 19 * std::mem::size_of::<u64>();
    for limit in [bytes, bytes - 1] {
        // Generated backing belongs to the already-paid overlay. Observe only
        // its real replacement; successful receiving Drop also frees it.
        let generated = vec![13u64; 17];
        let ((success, observed), refused, charged, first) =
            crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage_refusal(limit, || {
                measure(|| {
                    let result = append_copies(&authored, generated);
                    let success = result.is_ok();
                    if let Ok(values) = &result {
                        assert_eq!(&values[..2], &authored);
                        assert_eq!(&values[2..], &[13u64; 17]);
                    } else {
                        assert!(reserve_builder_structure(0).is_err());
                    }
                    drop(result);
                    success
                })
            });
        // The allocation observer records frees only for its own allocations;
        // generated backing was acquired outside this receiver's observation.
        assert!(!observed.invalid && observed.live == 0);
        assert_eq!(observed.allocated, observed.freed);
        if limit == bytes {
            assert!(success && !refused && charged == bytes);
            assert_eq!(observed.allocations, 1);
            assert_eq!(observed.reallocations, 0);
            assert_eq!(observed.allocated, bytes);
            assert_eq!(observed.highwater, bytes);
        } else {
            assert!(!success && refused && first.is_some());
        }
    }
}
//! Exact-source imported overlays, including ordinary classified retained
//! wrappers and invariant/class roots. Views confer no import authority.
#[path = "borrowed_imports/alias_clone.rs"]
#[allow(
    clippy::duplicate_mod,
    reason = "Run the same controls against independent receivers."
)]
mod alias_clone;
#[path = "borrowed_imports/receiving.rs"]
#[allow(
    clippy::duplicate_mod,
    reason = "Run the same controls against independent receivers."
)]
mod receiving;
use super::{
    active_builder_limit, ast_function_cost, ast_function_signature_cost,
    ast_type_declaration_cost, budgeted_format, default_expr, graph_error, import_stub,
    limit_error, reserve_builder_structure, type_rewrite, AuthoredDeclaration, LayoutRegistry,
    StructuralCost,
};
use crate::ast::program_source::{generated_roots::GeneratedRoots, SyntheticProgramView};
use crate::ast::{
    Expr, ExprKind, Function, ModuleUseKind, Program, Type, TypeDeclaration, TypeDeclarationKind,
};
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
pub(in crate::workspace_graph) use receiving::construct_receiving;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn eligible(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    _programs: &[Program],
) -> bool {
    !program.module_uses.is_empty()
        && program
            .module_uses
            .iter()
            .all(|import| authored.contains_key(import.persistent_id.as_str()))
}
fn refusal() -> Vec<Diagnostic> {
    vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())]
}
fn directory<T>(count: usize) -> Result<Vec<T>, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let bytes = count
        .checked_mul(std::mem::size_of::<T>())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    reserve_builder_structure(bytes)?;
    let values = Vec::with_capacity(count);
    if values.capacity() != count {
        return Err(refusal());
    }
    Ok(values)
}
fn pay_type(declaration: &TypeDeclaration) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
    ast_type_declaration_cost(declaration, &mut cost)?;
    reserve_builder_structure(cost.total)
}
fn pay_signature(function: &Function) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
    ast_function_signature_cost(function, &mut cost)?;
    reserve_builder_structure(cost.total)
}

// A geometric Vec worklist's cumulative requests are below four times its
// element census; its initial nonbyte capacity is four. Raw inline AST cost
// counts every node/header without a clone discount. Charge the full fourfold
// census before cloning each recursive root, plus each worklist's minimum once.
fn pay_teardown(cost: &StructuralCost) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    reserve_builder_structure(cost.total.checked_mul(4).ok_or_else(refusal)?)
}
fn iterative(program: &Program, authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>) -> bool {
    program.module_uses.iter().any(|import| {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        target
            .function
            .is_some_and(|function| !function.type_parameters.is_empty())
            || target.ty.is_some_and(|ty| {
                !ty.invariants().is_empty() || matches!(ty.kind, TypeDeclarationKind::Class { .. })
            })
    })
}
fn pay_teardown_minimum() -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let bytes = [
        std::mem::size_of::<Function>(),
        std::mem::size_of::<Expr>(),
        std::mem::size_of::<Type>(),
        std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::MatchPattern>(),
        std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::RecordMatchPatternField>(),
    ]
    .into_iter()
    .try_fold(0usize, |sum, size| sum.checked_add(size))
    .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    reserve_builder_structure(bytes.checked_mul(4).ok_or_else(refusal)?)
}

// Defaults can expand a shared record type repeatedly. Count that expanded
// expression tree through borrowed declarations, rather than charging only the
// unique type directory. This is storage payment, not default admission; the
// original constructor still chooses forwarding, classifiers and first errors.
fn pay_default_teardown(
    ty: &Type,
    declarations: &[(&str, &TypeDeclaration)],
) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    fn census(
        ty: &Type,
        declarations: &[(&str, &TypeDeclaration)],
    ) -> Result<usize, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
        let mut bytes = std::mem::size_of::<Expr>();
        if let Type::Named { name, arguments } = ty {
            if arguments.is_empty() {
                if let Ok(index) =
                    declarations.binary_search_by_key(&name.as_str(), |(name, _)| *name)
                {
                    let fields: &[crate::ast::FieldDeclaration] = match &declarations[index].1.kind
                    {
                        TypeDeclarationKind::Record { fields }
                        | TypeDeclarationKind::Class { fields, .. } => fields,
                        TypeDeclarationKind::Variant { cases } => {
                            cases.first().map_or(&[], |case| &case.fields)
                        }
                        TypeDeclarationKind::Resource { .. } => &[],
                    };
                    for field in fields {
                        bytes = bytes
                            .checked_add(census(&field.ty, declarations)?)
                            .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
                    }
                }
            } else {
                // Accepted compiler collection defaults have a root call,
                // one scalar size operand and copied type arguments. Generic
                // authored defaults still receive their old closed refusal.
                bytes = bytes
                    .checked_add(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())
                    .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
                let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true);
                super::declaration_cost::ast_type_cost(ty, &mut cost)?;
                bytes = bytes.checked_add(cost.total).ok_or_else(refusal)?;
            }
        } else if *ty == Type::Bytes || *ty == Type::StringMap {
            bytes = bytes
                .checked_add(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())
                .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
        }
        Ok(bytes)
    }
    reserve_builder_structure(
        census(ty, declarations)?
            .checked_mul(4)
            .ok_or_else(refusal)?,
    )
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct<'a>(
    program: &'a Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    programs: &[Program],
    registry: &LayoutRegistry<'_>,
) -> Result<SyntheticProgramView<'a>, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    construct_using(program, authored, programs, registry, None)
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct_core<'a>(
    program: &'a Program,
    authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
    programs: &'a [Program],
    registry: &LayoutRegistry<'_>,
    core: &mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'a>,
) -> Result<SyntheticProgramView<'a>, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    construct_using(program, authored, programs, registry, Some(core))
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct_using<'p, 'a>(
    program: &'p Program,
    authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
    programs: &'a [Program],
    registry: &LayoutRegistry<'_>,
    core: Option<&mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'a>>,
) -> Result<SyntheticProgramView<'p>, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
    if !eligible(program, authored, programs) {
        return Err(vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "imported borrowed projection requires the owned route",
        )]);
    }
    let layout = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
    let explicit = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
        .count();
    let type_count = explicit
        .checked_add(layout.entries.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let recursive = iterative(program, authored);
    if recursive {
        pay_teardown_minimum()?;
    }
    let mut roots = if recursive {
        GeneratedRoots::iterative(directory::<TypeDeclaration>(type_count)?, Vec::new())
    } else {
        GeneratedRoots::plain(directory::<TypeDeclaration>(type_count)?, Vec::new())
    };
    let types = &mut roots.types;
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let declaration = target.ty.expect("validated type target");
        pay_type(declaration)?;
        if recursive {
            let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
            ast_type_declaration_cost(declaration, &mut cost)?;
            pay_teardown(&cost)?;
        }
        types.push(alias_clone::type_payload(declaration));
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted type");
        copy.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&import.alias);
        reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
        super::super::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
        )?;
    }
    // Preserve the independent closure's exact order after explicit aliases.
    for entry in &layout.entries {
        pay_type(entry.declaration)?;
        if recursive {
            let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
            ast_type_declaration_cost(entry.declaration, &mut cost)?;
            pay_teardown(&cost)?;
        }
        types.push(alias_clone::type_payload(entry.declaration));
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted private type");
        copy.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&entry.alias);
        reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
        super::super::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            entry.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
        )?;
    }
    let all_count = program
        .types
        .len()
        .checked_add(types.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let mut type_index = directory::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>(all_count)?;
    type_index.extend(
        program
            .types
            .iter()
            .chain(types.iter())
            .map(|declaration| (declaration.name.as_str(), declaration)),
    );
    type_index.sort_unstable_by(|a, b| a.0.cmp(b.0));
    if type_index.windows(2).any(|items| items[0].0 == items[1].0) {
        return Err(vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "synthetic workspace type-name index is not unique",
        )]);
    }
    let needs_main = !program
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
        && !program
            .module_uses
            .iter()
            .any(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function && import.alias == "main");
    let imports = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
        .count();
    roots.functions = directory::<Function>(
        imports
            .checked_add(usize::from(needs_main))
            .ok_or_else(refusal)?,
    )?;
    let functions = &mut roots.functions;
    let mut byte_proofs = import_stub::core_proofs::Phase::new(authored, programs, core)
        .map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let original = target.function.expect("validated function target");
        let retain_body = programs
            .iter()
            .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
            .is_some_and(|provider| {
                crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper(provider, original).is_some()
                    || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper(provider, original).is_some()
            });
        if retain_body {
            let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
            ast_function_cost(original, &mut cost)?;
            reserve_builder_structure(cost.total)?;
            pay_teardown(&cost)?;
        } else {
            pay_signature(original)?;
            if recursive {
                let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
                ast_function_signature_cost(original, &mut cost)?;
                pay_teardown(&cost)?;
            }
        }
        let function = if retain_body {
            alias_clone::retained_function(original, &import.alias)
        } else {
            alias_clone::signature_function(original, &import.alias)
        };
        functions.push(function);
        reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
        if retain_body {
            continue;
        }
        let function = functions.last_mut().expect("inserted signature");
        for param in &mut function.params {
            type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
                &mut param.ty,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                Some(&layout),
            )?;
        }
        type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
            &mut function.return_type,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
        )?;
        if recursive {
            pay_default_teardown(&function.return_type, &type_index)?;
        }
        function.body = default_expr(
            function,
            &type_index,
            super::super::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                program, target, authored, programs, &layout,
            ),
            import_stub::byte_helper_authorized(
                original,
                target.module,
                byte_proofs.get().map_err(|error| vec![error])?,
            )?,
        )?;
    }
    // Same old rule also recognizes an imported alias named main.
    if needs_main && !functions.iter().any(|function| function.name == "main") {
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())?;
        functions.push(Function {
            stable_id: budgeted_format(format_args!("workspace.synthetic.main.{}", program.module)),
            explicit_id: true,
            name: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone("main"),
            name_span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
            type_parameters: Vec::new(),
            params: Vec::new(),
            return_type: Type::I64,
            effects: Vec::new(),
            yields: None,
            follows: None,
            requires: Vec::new(),
            ensures: Vec::new(),
            body: Expr {
                kind: ExprKind::Int(0),
                span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
            },
            span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
        });
        reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
    }
    drop(type_index);
    // Infallible bounded string helpers record refusal and return empty text.
    // A generated view must never publish that partial construction as success.
    reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
    let result = SyntheticProgramView::with_generated_owner(program, roots).ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    byte_proofs.complete().map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
    Ok(result)
}

#[allow(
    clippy::duplicate_mod,
    reason = "Run the same controls against independent receivers."
)]

// tests. Production module visibility and the complete old bodies stay exact.
#[cfg(test)]

    inline as constructor_test_inline_defaults, typed as constructor_test_typed_defaults,
};
#[cfg(test)]

//! Complete overlay walk; only default and directory refusal custody changes.
#[path = "typed_cost.rs"]
mod typed_cost;
use super::{
    alias_clone, budgeted_format, eligible, graph_error, import_stub, iterative, refusal,
    type_rewrite, AuthoredDeclaration, LayoutRegistry,
};
use crate::ast::program_source::{generated_roots::GeneratedRoots, SyntheticProgramView};
use crate::ast::{Expr, ExprKind, Function, ModuleUseKind, Program, Type, TypeDeclaration};
use crate::workspace_graph::constructor_failure::{receiving::Receiver, typed::Failure};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use typed_cost::{pay_default_teardown, pay_signature, pay_teardown_minimum, pay_type};

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct_using<'p, 'a>(
    program: &'p Program,
    authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
    programs: &'a [Program],
    registry: &LayoutRegistry<'_>,
    core: Option<&mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'a>>,
    owner: &mut Receiver,
) -> Result<SyntheticProgramView<'p>, Failure> {
    owner.entry::<SyntheticProgramView<'p>>()?;
    owner.reserve(0)?;
    if !eligible(program, authored, programs) {
        return Err(Failure::one(graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "imported borrowed projection requires the owned route",
        )));
    }
    let layout = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
    let explicit = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
        .count();
    let type_count = explicit
        .checked_add(layout.entries.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let recursive = iterative(program, authored);
    if recursive {
        pay_teardown_minimum(owner)?;
    }
    owner.reserve(std::mem::size_of::<GeneratedRoots>())?;
    let mut roots = if recursive {
        GeneratedRoots::iterative(directory::<TypeDeclaration>(type_count, owner)?, Vec::new())
    } else {
        GeneratedRoots::plain(directory::<TypeDeclaration>(type_count, owner)?, Vec::new())
    };
    let types = &mut roots.types;
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let declaration = target.ty.expect("validated type target");
        pay_type(declaration, owner)?;
        if recursive {
            typed_cost::teardown_type(declaration, owner)?;
        }
        types.push(alias_clone::type_payload(declaration));
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted type");
        copy.name = owner.text(&import.alias)?;
        owner.reserve(0)?;
        super::super::super::generic_type_import::receiving::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
            owner,
        )?;
    }
    // Preserve the independent closure's exact order after explicit aliases.
    for entry in &layout.entries {
        pay_type(entry.declaration, owner)?;
        if recursive {
            typed_cost::teardown_type(entry.declaration, owner)?;
        }
        types.push(alias_clone::type_payload(entry.declaration));
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted private type");
        copy.name = owner.text(&entry.alias)?;
        owner.reserve(0)?;
        super::super::super::generic_type_import::receiving::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            entry.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
            owner,
        )?;
    }
    let all_count = program
        .types
        .len()
        .checked_add(types.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let mut type_index = directory::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>(all_count, owner)?;
    type_index.extend(
        program
            .types
            .iter()
            .chain(types.iter())
            .map(|declaration| (declaration.name.as_str(), declaration)),
    );
    type_index.sort_unstable_by(|a, b| a.0.cmp(b.0));
    if type_index.windows(2).any(|items| items[0].0 == items[1].0) {
        return Err(Failure::one(graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "synthetic workspace type-name index is not unique",
        )));
    }
    let needs_main = !program
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
        && !program
            .module_uses
            .iter()
            .any(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function && import.alias == "main");
    let imports = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
        .count();
    roots.functions = directory::<Function>(
        imports
            .checked_add(usize::from(needs_main))
            .ok_or_else(refusal)?,
        owner,
    )?;
    let functions = &mut roots.functions;
    let mut byte_proofs =
        import_stub::core_proofs::Phase::new(authored, programs, core).map_err(Failure::one)?;
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let original = target.function.expect("validated function target");
        let retain_body = programs
            .iter()
            .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
            .is_some_and(|provider| {
                crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper(provider, original).is_some()
                    || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper(provider, original).is_some()
            });
        if retain_body {
            typed_cost::teardown_function(original, true, owner)?;
        } else {
            pay_signature(original, owner)?;
            if recursive {
                typed_cost::teardown_function(original, false, owner)?;
            }
        }
        let function = if retain_body {
            alias_clone::retained_function(original, &import.alias)
        } else {
            alias_clone::signature_function(original, &import.alias)
        };
        functions.push(function);
        owner.reserve(0)?;
        if retain_body {
            continue;
        }
        let function = functions.last_mut().expect("inserted signature");
        for param in &mut function.params {
            type_rewrite::receiving::rewrite_with_layout(
                &mut param.ty,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                Some(&layout),
                owner,
            )?;
        }
        type_rewrite::receiving::rewrite_with_layout(
            &mut function.return_type,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
            owner,
        )?;
        if recursive {
            pay_default_teardown(&function.return_type, &type_index, owner)?;
        }
        function.body = import_stub::typed::default_expr(
            function,
            &type_index,
            super::super::super::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                program, target, authored, programs, &layout,
            ),
            byte_proofs
                .get()
                .map_err(Failure::one)?
                .authorized(original, target.module)
                .map_err(Failure::one)?,
            owner,
        )?;
    }
    // Same old rule also recognizes an imported alias named main.
    if needs_main && !functions.iter().any(|function| function.name == "main") {
        owner.reserve(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())?;
        let stable_id =
            budgeted_format(format_args!("workspace.synthetic.main.{}", program.module));
        owner.reserve(0)?;
        let name = owner.text("main")?;
        functions.push(Function {
            stable_id,
            explicit_id: true,
            name,
            name_span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
            type_parameters: Vec::new(),
            params: Vec::new(),
            return_type: Type::I64,
            effects: Vec::new(),
            yields: None,
            follows: None,
            requires: Vec::new(),
            ensures: Vec::new(),
            body: Expr {
                kind: ExprKind::Int(0),
                span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
            },
            span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
        });
        owner.reserve(0)?;
    }
    drop(type_index);
    // Infallible bounded string helpers record refusal and return empty text.
    // A generated view must never publish that partial construction as success.
    owner.reserve(0)?;
    let result = SyntheticProgramView::with_generated_owner(program, roots).ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    byte_proofs.complete().map_err(Failure::one)?;
    Ok(result)
}

/// Same exact-count directory as the independent old helper. The initialized
/// return, Vec header, layout and actual backing are paid by the original
/// receiver before allocation; a failed reservation propagates only Copy Setup.
fn directory<T>(count: usize, owner: &mut Receiver) -> Result<Vec<T>, Failure> {
    use std::mem::size_of;
    owner.entry::<Vec<T>>()?;
    owner.reserve(
        size_of::<Vec<T>>()
            + size_of::<usize>()
            + size_of::<std::alloc::Layout>()
            + size_of::<Result<std::alloc::Layout, std::alloc::LayoutError>>(),
    )?;
    let layout = match std::alloc::Layout::array::<T>(count) {
        Ok(layout) => layout,
        Err(_) => return Err(owner.physical().into()),
    };
    owner.reserve(layout.size())?;
    let values = Vec::with_capacity(count);
    if values.capacity() != count {
        return Err(owner.physical().into());
    }
    Ok(values)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod directory_tests {
    use super::{directory, Failure, Receiver};
    use crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage_refusal;
    use crate::test_allocation_observer::{current_metrics, measure};
    use crate::workspace_graph::constructor_failure::receiving::Setup;
    const LIMIT: usize = 1024 * 1024;

    #[test]
    fn typed_overlay_directories_match_original_values_and_whole_drop_at_every_byte_frontier() {
        for count in [0, 1, 4, 9] {
            let (((), physical), refused, _, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
                measure(|| {
                    let mut owner = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
                    let mut values = directory::<u64>(count, &mut owner).unwrap();
                    assert_eq!(values.capacity(), count);
                    for index in 0..count {
                        values.push(index as u64);
                    }
                    assert_eq!(values.as_slice(), (0..count as u64).collect::<Vec<_>>());
                    drop(values);
                    drop(owner);
                })
            });
            assert!(!refused && first.is_none());
            assert!(
                !physical.invalid && physical.live == 0 && physical.allocated == physical.freed
            );
            // Measure the owning helper without test-only comparison allocation.
            let ((), refused, exact, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
                let mut owner = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
                drop(directory::<u64>(count, &mut owner).unwrap());
                drop(owner);
            });
            assert!(!refused && first.is_none());
            for limit in 0..=exact {
                let (((), physical), refused, used, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(
                    limit,
                    || {
                        measure(|| match Receiver::acquire() {
                            Err(Setup::Budget { .. }) => {}
                            Err(other) => panic!("unexpected owner failure: {other:?}"),
                            Ok(mut owner) => {
                                match directory::<u64>(count, &mut owner) {
                                    Ok(values) => {
                                        assert_eq!(limit, exact);
                                        assert_eq!(values.capacity(), count);
                                        drop(values);
                                    }
                                    Err(Failure::Setup(selected @ Setup::Budget { .. })) => {
                                        let stop = current_metrics().allocated;
                                        assert!(
                                            matches!(directory::<u64>(count, &mut owner), Err(Failure::Setup(next)) if next == selected)
                                        );
                                        drop(owner);
                                        assert_eq!(current_metrics().allocated, stop);
                                        return;
                                    }
                                    Err(other) => panic!("unexpected directory failure: {other:?}"),
                                }
                                drop(owner);
                            }
                        })
                    },
                );
                assert_eq!(refused, limit != exact);
                assert_eq!(first.is_some(), refused);
                assert!(used <= limit);
                assert!(
                    !physical.invalid && physical.live == 0 && physical.allocated == physical.freed
                );
                assert!(physical.allocated <= used);
            }
            let ((), refused, _, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
                let mut original = super::super::directory::<u64>(count).unwrap();
                for index in 0..count {
                    original.push(index as u64);
                }
                assert_eq!(original.capacity(), count);
                assert!(original.iter().copied().eq(0..count as u64));
                drop(original);
            });
            assert!(!refused && first.is_none());
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn typed_overlay_directory_foreign_restored_and_overflow_exits_keep_original_first_setup() {
        let ((), refused, _, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
            let mut owner = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
            let (((), physical), refused, used, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
                measure(|| {
                    assert!(matches!(
                        directory::<u64>(1, &mut owner),
                        Err(Failure::Setup(Setup::Detached))
                    ));
                })
            });
            assert!(!refused && used == 0 && first.is_none());
            assert_eq!(physical.allocated, 0);
            let ((), physical) = measure(|| {
                assert!(matches!(
                    directory::<u64>(usize::MAX, &mut owner),
                    Err(Failure::Setup(Setup::Detached))
                ));
                drop(owner);
            });
            assert_eq!(physical.allocated, 0);
        });
        assert!(refused && first.is_some());
        let ((), refused, _, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
            let mut owner = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
            let ((), physical) = measure(|| {
                let selected = match directory::<u64>(usize::MAX, &mut owner).unwrap_err() {
                    Failure::Setup(selected @ Setup::Budget { .. }) => selected,
                    other => panic!("unexpected overflow failure: {other:?}"),
                };
                assert!(
                    matches!(directory::<u64>(1, &mut owner), Err(Failure::Setup(next)) if next == selected)
                );
                drop(owner);
            });
            assert_eq!(physical.allocated, 0);
        });
        assert!(refused);
        assert_eq!(first.unwrap().requested, usize::MAX);
    }

    #[test]
    fn typed_overlay_directory_complete_source_inverses_and_three_live_calls_are_bound() {
        use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
        let rows: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!(concat!(
            env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
            "/docs/evidence/constructor-directory-propagation-825/source-inverses.json"
        )))
        .unwrap();
        let named = [
            crate::source_reflows_merge::directory825_input(
                "src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/borrowed_imports/typed.rs",
                include_str!("typed.rs"),
            ),
            crate::source_reflows_merge::directory825_input(
                "docs/CONSTRUCTOR-FAILURE-RECEIVING-V1.md",
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/CONSTRUCTOR-FAILURE-RECEIVING-V1.md"
                )),
            ),
            crate::source_reflows_merge::directory825_input(
                "src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/borrowed_imports.rs",
                include_str!("../borrowed_imports.rs"),
            ),
        ];
        let sources = [
            (
                named[0].as_str(),
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/evidence/constructor-directory-propagation-825/original/00.before"
                )),
            ),
            (
                named[1].as_str(),
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/evidence/constructor-directory-propagation-825/original/01.before"
                )),
            ),
            (
                named[2].as_str(),
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/evidence/constructor-directory-propagation-825/original/02.before"
                )),
            ),
        ];
        let rows = rows.as_array().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(rows.len(), sources.len());
        for (row, (current, original)) in rows.iter().zip(sources) {
            assert_eq!(
                format!(
                    "{:x}",
                    crate::digest_hex::LowerHex(Sha256::digest(current.as_bytes()))
                ),
                row["current_sha256"].as_str().unwrap()
            );
            assert_eq!(
                format!(
                    "{:x}",
                    crate::digest_hex::LowerHex(Sha256::digest(original.as_bytes()))
                ),
                row["original_sha256"].as_str().unwrap()
            );
            let mut restored = current.to_owned();
            for edit in row["edits"].as_array().unwrap() {
                let new = edit["new"].as_str().unwrap();
                assert_eq!(restored.matches(new).count(), 1);
                restored = restored.replacen(new, edit["before"].as_str().unwrap(), 1);
            }
            assert_eq!(restored, original);
        }
        let live = sources[0].0.split("#[cfg(test)]").next().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            live.matches("directory::<TypeDeclaration>(type_count, owner)?")
                .count(),
            2
        );
        assert!(live.contains("directory::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>(all_count, owner)?"));
        assert!(live.contains("roots.functions = directory::<Function>"));
        assert!(!live.contains("budgeted_format, directory"));
    }
}

#[macro_use]

use super::super::{
    alias_clone, budgeted_format, eligible, graph_error, import_stub, iterative, refusal,
    type_rewrite, AuthoredDeclaration, LayoutRegistry,
};
use super::typed_cost;
use crate::ast::program_source::generated_roots::GeneratedRoots;
use crate::ast::{Expr, ExprKind, Function, ModuleUseKind, Program, Type, TypeDeclaration};
use crate::workspace_graph::constructor_failure::{receiving::Receiver, typed::Failure};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use typed_cost::{pay_default_teardown, pay_signature, pay_teardown_minimum, pay_type};

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct_transient<'p>(
    program: &'p Program,
    authored: &'p BTreeMap<&'p str, AuthoredDeclaration<'p>>,
    programs: &'p [Program],
    registry: &LayoutRegistry<'_>,
    core: Option<&mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'p>>,
    owner: &mut Receiver,
) -> Result<crate::ast::program_source::function_alias::TransientFunctions<'p>, Failure> {
    use crate::ast::program_source::function_alias::{
        FunctionDirectory, FunctionEntry, FunctionView, TransientFunctions,
    };
    owner.entry::<TransientFunctions<'p>>()?;
    owner.reserve(
        std::mem::size_of::<FunctionDirectory<'p>>()
            + std::mem::size_of::<Vec<FunctionEntry<'p>>>(),
    )?;
    owner.reserve(0)?;
    if !eligible(program, authored, programs) {
        return Err(Failure::one(graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "imported borrowed projection requires the owned route",
        )));
    }
    // Direct callers receive the same preselection as the actual core route:
    // invariant transforms get the complete owning imported-function producer.
    owner.reserve(
        std::mem::size_of::<bool>() + std::mem::size_of::<std::slice::Iter<'_, Program>>(),
    )?;
    owner.reserve(
        std::mem::size_of::<std::slice::Iter<'_, TypeDeclaration>>()
            + std::mem::size_of::<Option<&TypeDeclaration>>()
            + std::mem::size_of::<&[Expr]>()
            + std::mem::size_of::<bool>(),
    )?;
    let mut owning_invariants = program
        .types
        .iter()
        .any(|declaration| !declaration.invariants().is_empty());
    for candidate in programs {
        owner.reserve(
            std::mem::size_of::<std::slice::Iter<'_, TypeDeclaration>>()
                + std::mem::size_of::<Option<&TypeDeclaration>>()
                + std::mem::size_of::<&[Expr]>()
                + std::mem::size_of::<bool>(),
        )?;
        if candidate
            .types
            .iter()
            .any(|declaration| !declaration.invariants().is_empty())
        {
            owning_invariants = true;
            break;
        }
    }
    let layout = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
    let explicit = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
        .count();
    let type_count = explicit
        .checked_add(layout.entries.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let recursive = iterative(program, authored);
    if recursive {
        pay_teardown_minimum(owner)?;
    }
    owner.reserve(std::mem::size_of::<GeneratedRoots>())?;
    let roots = if recursive {
        GeneratedRoots::iterative(directory::<TypeDeclaration>(type_count, owner)?, Vec::new())
    } else {
        GeneratedRoots::plain(directory::<TypeDeclaration>(type_count, owner)?, Vec::new())
    };
    // A single original roots owner covers both types and owned functions
    // before any recursive payload is initialized, including refusal paths.
    owner.reserve(std::mem::size_of::<FunctionDirectory<'p>>())?;
    let mut directory_owner = FunctionDirectory {
        roots,
        entries: Vec::new(),
    };
    let types = &mut directory_owner.roots.types;
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let declaration = target.ty.expect("validated type target");
        pay_type(declaration, owner)?;
        if recursive {
            typed_cost::teardown_type(declaration, owner)?;
        }
        types.push(alias_clone::type_payload(declaration));
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted type");
        copy.name = owner.text(&import.alias)?;
        owner.reserve(0)?;
        super::super::super::super::generic_type_import::receiving::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
            owner,
        )?;
    }
    // Preserve the independent closure's exact order after explicit aliases.
    for entry in &layout.entries {
        pay_type(entry.declaration, owner)?;
        if recursive {
            typed_cost::teardown_type(entry.declaration, owner)?;
        }
        types.push(alias_clone::type_payload(entry.declaration));
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted private type");
        copy.name = owner.text(&entry.alias)?;
        owner.reserve(0)?;
        super::super::super::super::generic_type_import::receiving::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            entry.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
            owner,
        )?;
    }
    let all_count = program
        .types
        .len()
        .checked_add(types.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let mut type_index = directory::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>(all_count, owner)?;
    type_index.extend(
        program
            .types
            .iter()
            .chain(types.iter())
            .map(|declaration| (declaration.name.as_str(), declaration)),
    );
    type_index.sort_unstable_by(|a, b| a.0.cmp(b.0));
    if type_index.windows(2).any(|items| items[0].0 == items[1].0) {
        return Err(Failure::one(graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "synthetic workspace type-name index is not unique",
        )));
    }
    let needs_main = !program
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
        && !program
            .module_uses
            .iter()
            .any(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function && import.alias == "main");
    let imports = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
        .count();
    // GeneratedRoots already holds the empty owned directory. Borrowed entries
    // acquire no Function backing; owning fallback acquires the original full bound at its real frontier.
    directory_owner.entries = directory::<FunctionEntry<'p>>(
        imports
            .checked_add(usize::from(needs_main))
            .ok_or_else(refusal)?,
        owner,
    )?;
    owner.reserve(
        std::mem::size_of::<&mut Vec<Function>>()
            + std::mem::size_of::<&mut Vec<FunctionEntry<'p>>>(),
    )?;
    let functions = &mut directory_owner.roots.functions;
    let entries = &mut directory_owner.entries;
    let mut byte_proofs =
        import_stub::core_proofs::Phase::new(authored, programs, core).map_err(Failure::one)?;
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let original = target.function.expect("validated function target");
        let retain_body = programs
            .iter()
            .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
            .is_some_and(|provider| {
                crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper(provider, original).is_some()
                    || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper(provider, original).is_some()
            });
        owner.reserve(
            2 * std::mem::size_of::<bool>()
                + 3 * std::mem::size_of::<bool>()
                + 2 * std::mem::size_of::<&Vec<crate::ast::SessionProtocolDeclaration>>()
                + 2 * std::mem::size_of::<&Vec<crate::ast::AgentDeclaration>>()
                + 2 * std::mem::size_of::<&Vec<crate::ast::ProtocolDeclaration>>(),
        )?;
        // Raw byte-helper proof memo/cursors have ordinary payload subjects.
        // A Bytes-returning alias must retain the complete owning producer.
        if retain_body && !owning_invariants && original.return_type != Type::Bytes {
            // The entire real view, its Some/receiving carriers and forwarded
            // arguments are paid before evaluating the checked constructor.
            owner.reserve(
                2 * std::mem::size_of::<Option<FunctionView<'p>>>()
                    + 2 * std::mem::size_of::<FunctionView<'p>>()
                    + 2 * std::mem::size_of::<FunctionEntry<'p>>()
                    + 2 * std::mem::size_of::<&Program>()
                    + std::mem::size_of::<&Function>()
                    + std::mem::size_of::<&str>()
                    + std::mem::size_of::<std::slice::Iter<'_, Program>>()
                    + programs.len()
                        * (std::mem::size_of::<Option<&Program>>()
                            + 2 * std::mem::size_of::<&Program>()
                            + std::mem::size_of::<bool>()),
            )?;
            let provider = programs
                .iter()
                .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
                .expect("retained classifier found actual provider");
            owner.reserve(
                std::mem::size_of::<std::slice::Iter<'_, Function>>()
                    + 2 * std::mem::size_of::<bool>()
                    + provider.functions.len()
                        * (std::mem::size_of::<Option<&Function>>()
                            + std::mem::size_of::<&Function>()
                            + 2 * std::mem::size_of::<*const Function>()
                            + std::mem::size_of::<bool>()),
            )?;
            if let Some(view) = FunctionView::checked(provider, original, &import.alias) {
                entries.push(FunctionEntry::Borrowed(view));
                #[cfg(test)]
                crate::workspace_graph::constructor_failure::typed::before_function_alias_core::borrowed_entry();
                continue;
            }
        }
        // Ordinary scalar signatures keep provider-owned metadata and the exact
        // finite default. Every argument/formal/output, check, iterator and
        // reached bounded scan role is admitted before constructing the view.
        if !retain_body
            && !owning_invariants
            && program.session_protocols.is_empty()
            && program.agents.is_empty()
            && program.protocols.is_empty()
        {
            use crate::ast::program_source::function_alias::scalar_signature::ScalarSignature;
            use std::mem::size_of;
            owner.reserve(
                4 * size_of::<&Program>()
                    + 4 * size_of::<&Function>()
                    + 4 * size_of::<&str>()
                    + 2 * size_of::<&String>()
                    + size_of::<ScalarSignature<'p>>()
