                    // The former outer Signature binding is consumed as its
                    // exact view field; same lifetime and publication boundary.
                    + size_of::<FunctionView<'p>>()
                    + 2 * size_of::<Option<ScalarSignature<'p>>>()
                    + size_of::<FunctionView<'p>>()
                    + size_of::<FunctionEntry<'p>>()
                    + size_of::<std::slice::Iter<'_, Program>>()
                    + size_of::<std::slice::Iter<'_, crate::ast::Param>>()
                    + size_of::<&Vec<crate::ast::TypeParameterDeclaration>>()
                    + size_of::<&Vec<crate::ast::Param>>()
                    + 10 * size_of::<bool>()
                    + 2 * size_of::<&Option<crate::ast::YieldsClause>>()
                    + 2 * size_of::<&Option<Box<crate::ast::SessionProtocolFollowsClause>>>()
                    + original.params.len()
                        * (size_of::<Option<&crate::ast::Param>>()
                            + size_of::<&crate::ast::Param>()
                            + 2 * size_of::<bool>())
                    + size_of::<Option<&crate::ast::Param>>(),
            )?;
            let mut providers = programs.iter();
            let provider = loop {
                owner.reserve(
                    2 * size_of::<&mut std::slice::Iter<'_, Program>>()
                        + 2 * size_of::<Option<&Program>>()
                        + 2 * size_of::<&Program>()
                        + 2 * size_of::<&String>()
                        + 2 * size_of::<&str>()
                        + size_of::<bool>(),
                )?;
                match providers.next() {
                    Some(candidate) if candidate.module == target.module => break candidate,
                    Some(_) => {}
                    None => unreachable!("validated function target has its provider"),
                }
            };
            owner.reserve(
                size_of::<std::slice::Iter<'_, Function>>()
                    + 2 * size_of::<&Vec<Function>>()
                    + provider.functions.len()
                        * (size_of::<Option<&Function>>()
                            + 2 * size_of::<&Function>()
                            + 2 * size_of::<*const Function>()
                            + 2 * size_of::<bool>())
                    + size_of::<Option<&Function>>(),
            )?;
            if let Some(ScalarSignature { view }) =
                ScalarSignature::checked(provider, original, &import.alias)
            {
                // Keep original proof-current checks and their ordering even
                // though primitive defaults cannot consume either permission.
                let _owned =
                    super::super::super::super::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                        program, target, authored, programs, &layout,
                    );
                let _bytes = byte_proofs
                    .get()
                    .map_err(Failure::one)?
                    .authorized(original, target.module)
                    .map_err(Failure::one)?;
                entries.push(FunctionEntry::Borrowed(view));
                continue;
            }
        }
        if retain_body {
            typed_cost::teardown_function(original, true, owner)?;
        } else {
            pay_signature(original, owner)?;
            if recursive {
                typed_cost::teardown_function(original, false, owner)?;
            }
        }
        owned_function_directory!(functions, entries, owner);
        let function = if retain_body {
            alias_clone::retained_function(original, &import.alias)
        } else {
            alias_clone::signature_function(original, &import.alias)
        };
        owner.reserve(std::mem::size_of::<FunctionEntry<'p>>() + std::mem::size_of::<usize>())?;
        entries.push(FunctionEntry::Owned(functions.len()));
        functions.push(function);
        #[cfg(test)]
        owned_growth_tests::observe_owned_push();
        owner.reserve(0)?;
        if retain_body {
            continue;
        }
        let function = functions.last_mut().expect("inserted signature");
        for param in &mut function.params {
            type_rewrite::receiving::rewrite_with_layout(
                &mut param.ty,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                Some(&layout),
                owner,
            )?;
        }
        type_rewrite::receiving::rewrite_with_layout(
            &mut function.return_type,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
            owner,
        )?;
        if recursive {
            pay_default_teardown(&function.return_type, &type_index, owner)?;
        }
        function.body = import_stub::typed::default_expr(
            function,
            &type_index,
            super::super::super::super::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                program, target, authored, programs, &layout,
            ),
            byte_proofs
                .get()
                .map_err(Failure::one)?
                .authorized(original, target.module)
                .map_err(Failure::one)?,
            owner,
        )?;
    }
    // Same old rule also recognizes an imported alias named main.
    if needs_main && !functions.iter().any(|function| function.name == "main") {
        owned_function_directory!(functions, entries, owner);
        owner.reserve(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())?;
        let stable_id =
            budgeted_format(format_args!("workspace.synthetic.main.{}", program.module));
        owner.reserve(0)?;
        let name = owner.text("main")?;
        owner.reserve(std::mem::size_of::<FunctionEntry<'p>>() + std::mem::size_of::<usize>())?;
        entries.push(FunctionEntry::Owned(functions.len()));
        functions.push(Function {
            stable_id,
            explicit_id: true,
            name,
            name_span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
            type_parameters: Vec::new(),
            params: Vec::new(),
            return_type: Type::I64,
            effects: Vec::new(),
            yields: None,
            follows: None,
            requires: Vec::new(),
            ensures: Vec::new(),
            body: Expr {
                kind: ExprKind::Int(0),
                span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
            },
            span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
        });
        owner.reserve(0)?;
    }
    drop(type_index);
    // Infallible bounded string helpers record refusal and return empty text.
    // A generated view must never publish that partial construction as success.
    owner.reserve(0)?;
    program
        .functions
        .len()
        .checked_add(entries.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    program
        .types
        .len()
        .checked_add(directory_owner.roots.types.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    owner.reserve(
        2 * std::mem::size_of::<TransientFunctions<'p>>()
            + std::mem::size_of::<FunctionDirectory<'p>>()
            + std::mem::size_of::<&Program>()
            + std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::program_source::OverlayIdentity>(),
    )?;
    let result = TransientFunctions::new(program, directory_owner);
    byte_proofs.complete().map_err(Failure::one)?;
    Ok(result)
}

/// Same exact-count directory as the independent old helper. The initialized
/// return, Vec header, layout and actual backing are paid by the original
/// receiver before allocation; a failed reservation propagates only Copy Setup.
fn directory<T>(count: usize, owner: &mut Receiver) -> Result<Vec<T>, Failure> {
    use std::mem::size_of;
    owner.entry::<Vec<T>>()?;
    owner.reserve(
        size_of::<Vec<T>>()
            + size_of::<usize>()
            + size_of::<std::alloc::Layout>()
            + size_of::<Result<std::alloc::Layout, std::alloc::LayoutError>>(),
    )?;
    let layout = match std::alloc::Layout::array::<T>(count) {
        Ok(layout) => layout,
        Err(_) => return Err(owner.physical().into()),
    };
    owner.reserve(layout.size())?;
    let values = Vec::with_capacity(count);
    if values.capacity() != count {
        return Err(owner.physical().into());
    }
    Ok(values)
}

#[cfg(test)]
#[path = "alias/tests.rs"]
mod tests;

#[cfg(test)]
#[path = "alias/rename_tests.rs"]
mod rename_tests;
use super::*;
use crate::ast::program_source::ProgramSource;
use crate::bounded_output::{active_remaining, with_limit_usage_refusal};
use crate::test_allocation_observer::measure;
use crate::workspace_graph::index_authored;
pub(super) const LIMIT: usize = 32 * 1024 * 1024;
pub(super) const PROVIDER: &str = include_str!(concat!(
    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
    "/std/collections/src/collections.spx"
));
pub(super) const CALLER: &str = r#"module alias.current;
use function @id("std.collections.vec.with-capacity") from std.collections as with_capacity;
use function @id("std.collections.vec.push") from std.collections as push;
use function @id("std.collections.vec.get") from std.collections as get;
@id("alias.current.main") fn main()->i64 {let values=with_capacity<i64>(2usize);let appended=push<i64>(values,42);get<i64>(appended,0usize)}"#;
pub(super) fn sources(caller: &str) -> [Program; 2] {
    [
        crate::parse(caller, "caller.spx").unwrap(),
        crate::parse(PROVIDER, "provider.spx").unwrap(),
    ]
}
pub(super) fn linked<T>(f: impl FnOnce() -> T) -> T {
    crate::vec_ops::with_authenticated_linked_source(|| {
        crate::box_ops::with_authenticated_linked_source(f)
    })
}
#[test]
#[allow(
    clippy::redundant_closure,
    reason = "The complete saved control retains its original expressions for independent parity checks."
)]
fn retained_same_name_views_lend_actual_provider_payload_in_original_order_and_hir() {
    let programs = sources(CALLER);
    let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let ((), refused, _, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
        let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
        let mut owner = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
        let old = super::super::before_alias::construct_using(
            &programs[0],
            &authored,
            &programs,
            &registry,
            None,
            &mut owner,
        )
        .unwrap();
        let view = construct_transient(
            &programs[0],
            &authored,
            &programs,
            &registry,
            None,
            &mut owner,
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            view.functions().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            old.functions().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>()
        );
        assert_eq!(
            view.types().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            old.types().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>()
        );
        for import in &programs[0].module_uses {
            let provider = programs[1]
                .functions
                .iter()
                .find(|f| f.stable_id == import.persistent_id)
                .unwrap();
            let actual = view
                .functions()
                .iter()
                .find(|f| f.stable_id == import.persistent_id)
                .unwrap();
            assert!(std::ptr::eq(actual, provider));
            assert!(std::ptr::eq(&actual.body, &provider.body));
            assert!(!std::ptr::eq(
                old.functions()
                    .iter()
                    .find(|f| f.stable_id == import.persistent_id)
                    .unwrap(),
                provider
            ));
        }
        let layout = registry.get(&programs[0], &authored, &programs).unwrap();
        let expected = linked(|| {
            crate::hir::identity_resolution::before_function_alias::resolve_source_nominal(
                &old,
                layout.presentation(&programs[0], &old).unwrap(),
                |bytes| crate::workspace_graph::reserve_builder_structure(bytes),
            )
        })
        .unwrap()
        .0;
        let actual = linked(|| {
            crate::hir::identity_resolution::resolve_source_nominal(
                &view,
                layout.presentation(&programs[0], &view).unwrap(),
                |bytes| crate::workspace_graph::reserve_builder_structure(bytes),
            )
        })
        .unwrap()
        .0;
        assert_eq!(actual, expected);
        crate::hir::validate(&actual).unwrap();
        // Exercise the complete immediate-old owning and base-prelude
        // receivers as well as its borrowed pass, retaining their full bodies.
        let old_key =
            crate::workspace_graph::expected_projection::borrowed_leaf::checked_key_owned(old)
                .unwrap();
        let owning = linked(|| {
            crate::hir::identity_resolution::before_function_alias::resolve_checked_nominal(
                &old_key,
                None,
                None,
                None,
                None,
                |bytes| crate::workspace_graph::reserve_builder_structure(bytes),
            )
        })
        .unwrap()
        .0;
        assert_eq!(owning, expected);
        let mut identity_owner =
            crate::bounded_output::identity_reservation::IdentityReservation::acquire().unwrap();
        let mut invocation = crate::hir::prelude_bundle::Invocation::new();
        let with_base = linked(|| {
            crate::hir::identity_resolution::before_function_alias::resolve_source_checked_previous_with_base(&old_key,None,None,None,None,&mut invocation,|bytes|identity_owner.reserve(bytes))
        });
        match with_base {
            Ok((resolved,_))=>assert_eq!(resolved,expected),
            Err(crate::hir::identity_resolution::before_function_alias::NominalReceiverFailure::Resolve(error))=>panic!("old resolver refusal: {error:?}"),
            Err(crate::hir::identity_resolution::before_function_alias::NominalReceiverFailure::Base(error))=>panic!("old base refusal: {error:?}"),
            Err(crate::hir::identity_resolution::before_function_alias::NominalReceiverFailure::SourceStop(error))=>panic!("adapted old receiver source refusal: {error:?}"),
        }
        drop((owning, old_key, invocation, identity_owner));
        drop((actual, expected, layout, view, owner));
        drop(registry);
    });
    assert!(!refused && first.is_none());
}
#[test]
#[allow(
    clippy::redundant_closure,
    reason = "The complete saved control retains its original expressions for independent parity checks."
)]
fn actual_identity_owner_lends_one_frozen_inventory_through_source_and_hir() {
    let programs = sources(CALLER);
    let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let ((), refused, _, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
        let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
        let mut receiver = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
        let old = super::super::before_alias::construct_using(
            &programs[0],
            &authored,
            &programs,
            &registry,
            None,
            &mut receiver,
        )
        .unwrap();
        let view = construct_transient(
            &programs[0],
            &authored,
            &programs,
            &registry,
            None,
            &mut receiver,
        )
        .unwrap();
        let layout = registry.get(&programs[0], &authored, &programs).unwrap();
        let expected = linked(|| {
            crate::hir::identity_resolution::before_function_alias::resolve_source_nominal(
                &old,
                layout.presentation(&programs[0], &old).unwrap(),
                |bytes| crate::workspace_graph::reserve_builder_structure(bytes),
            )
        })
        .unwrap()
        .0;
        let mut owner =
            crate::bounded_output::identity_reservation::IdentityReservation::acquire().unwrap();
        let mut invocation = crate::hir::prelude_bundle::Invocation::new();
        let actual = linked(|| {
            crate::hir::identity_resolution::resolve_source_checked_previous_with_owner(
                &view,
                None,
                None,
                None,
                layout.presentation(&programs[0], &view).unwrap(),
                &mut invocation,
                &mut owner,
            )
        })
        .unwrap()
        .0;
        assert_eq!(actual, expected);
        crate::hir::validate(&actual).unwrap();
        drop((
            actual, expected, invocation, owner, layout, view, old, receiver,
        ));
        drop(registry);
    });
    assert!(!refused && first.is_none());
}
#[test]
fn renamed_and_malformed_wrapper_payloads_keep_the_complete_owned_fallback() {
    use super::super::name_equal::construct_transient;
    for caller in [
        CALLER
            .replace("as get;", "as read_value;")
            .replace("get<i64>(appended", "read_value<i64>(appended"),
        CALLER.to_owned(),
    ] {
        let mut programs = sources(&caller);
        if caller == CALLER {
            programs[1]
                .functions
                .iter_mut()
                .find(|f| f.stable_id == "std.collections.vec.get")
                .unwrap()
                .effects
                .push("hostile.effect".to_owned());
        }
        let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        let ((), refused, _, _) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
            let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
            let mut owner = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
            let old = super::super::before_alias::construct_using(
                &programs[0],
                &authored,
                &programs,
                &registry,
                None,
                &mut owner,
            );
            let view = construct_transient(
                &programs[0],
                &authored,
                &programs,
                &registry,
                None,
                &mut owner,
            );
            match (old, view) {
                (Ok(old), Ok(view)) => {
                    assert_eq!(
                        view.functions().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
                        old.functions().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>()
                    );
                    let original = programs[1]
                        .functions
                        .iter()
                        .find(|f| f.stable_id == "std.collections.vec.get")
                        .unwrap();
                    let current = view
                        .functions()
                        .iter()
                        .find(|f| f.stable_id == original.stable_id)
                        .unwrap();
                    assert!(!std::ptr::eq(current, original));
                }
                (Err(Failure::One(old)), Err(Failure::One(current))) => assert_eq!(old, current),
                (Err(Failure::Many(old)), Err(Failure::Many(current))) => assert_eq!(old, current),
                _ => panic!("old and current semantic refusal shapes differ"),
            }
        });
        assert!(!refused);
    }
}
#[test]
fn whole_constructor_owner_and_drop_report_actual_costs_without_universal_gain_claim() {
    let programs = sources(CALLER);
    let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let observe = |current, limit| {
        with_limit_usage_refusal(limit, || {
            measure(|| {
                let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
                let mut owner = match Receiver::acquire() {
                    Ok(owner) => owner,
                    Err(_) => return false,
                };
                let ok = if current {
                    match construct_transient(
                        &programs[0],
                        &authored,
                        &programs,
                        &registry,
                        None,
                        &mut owner,
                    ) {
                        Ok(view) => {
                            drop(view);
                            true
                        }
                        Err(failure) => {
                            drop(failure);
                            false
                        }
                    }
                } else {
                    match super::super::before_alias::construct_using(
                        &programs[0],
                        &authored,
                        &programs,
                        &registry,
                        None,
                        &mut owner,
                    ) {
                        Ok(view) => {
                            drop(view);
                            true
                        }
                        Err(failure) => {
                            drop(failure);
                            false
                        }
                    }
                };
                drop(owner);
                drop(registry);
                ok
            })
        })
    };
    // Initialize shared process metadata with the complete original constructor;
    // both measured sides then use the same process/cache/source state.
    assert!(observe(false, LIMIT).0 .0);
    let old = observe(false, LIMIT);
    let new = observe(true, LIMIT);
    assert!(old.0 .0 && new.0 .0 && !old.1 && !new.1);
    for measured in [&old, &new] {
        let metrics = &measured.0 .1;
        assert!(!metrics.invalid && metrics.live == 0 && metrics.allocated == metrics.freed);
    }
    eprintln!(
        "847 whole constructor old paid={} physical={:?}; current paid={} physical={:?}",
        old.2, old.0 .1, new.2, new.0 .1
    );
    for limit in [0, new.2 - 1, new.2] {
        let observed = observe(true, limit);
        assert_eq!(observed.0 .0, limit == new.2);
        let m = observed.0 .1;
        assert!(!m.invalid && m.live == 0 && m.allocated == m.freed);
    }
}
#[test]
fn foreign_and_restored_owner_fail_before_any_projection_or_allocation() {
    let programs = sources(CALLER);
    let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let ((), refused, _, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
        let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
        let mut owner = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
        let (((), metrics), child_refused, child_paid, child_first) =
            with_limit_usage_refusal(0, || {
                measure(|| {
                    assert!(matches!(
                        construct_transient(
                            &programs[0],
                            &authored,
                            &programs,
                            &registry,
                            None,
                            &mut owner
                        ),
                        Err(Failure::Setup(_))
                    ));
                })
            });
        assert!(!child_refused && child_paid == 0 && child_first.is_none());
        assert!(!metrics.invalid && metrics.allocated == 0);
        let before = active_remaining();
        let ((), metrics) = measure(|| {
            assert!(matches!(
                construct_transient(
                    &programs[0],
                    &authored,
                    &programs,
                    &registry,
                    None,
                    &mut owner
                ),
                Err(Failure::Setup(_))
            ));
        });
        assert_eq!(active_remaining(), before);
        assert!(!metrics.invalid && metrics.allocated == 0);
    });
    assert!(refused && first.is_some());
}

#[test]
fn mixed_borrowed_renamed_and_owned_type_roots_keep_partial_recursive_drop() {
    use super::super::name_equal::construct_transient;
    let provider=format!("{PROVIDER}\n@id(\"alias.row\") record Row {{ @id(\"alias.row.value\") value:i64, }}\n@id(\"alias.plain\") fn plain(value:Row)->Row {{value}}\n");
    let caller=CALLER.replace("@id(\"alias.current.main\")", "use type @id(\"alias.row\") from std.collections as Row; use function @id(\"alias.plain\") from std.collections as ordinary; @id(\"alias.current.main\")").replace("as get;","as read_value;").replace("get<i64>(appended","read_value<i64>(appended");
    let programs = [
        crate::parse(&caller, "caller.spx").unwrap(),
        crate::parse(&provider, "provider.spx").unwrap(),
    ];
    let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let observe = |limit| {
        with_limit_usage_refusal(limit, || {
            measure(|| {
                let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
                let mut owner = match Receiver::acquire() {
                    Ok(owner) => owner,
                    Err(_) => return false,
                };
                let result = construct_transient(
                    &programs[0],
                    &authored,
                    &programs,
                    &registry,
                    None,
                    &mut owner,
                );
                let success = result.is_ok();
                drop(result);
                drop(owner);
                drop(registry);
                success
            })
        })
    };
    let ((), refused, _, _) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
        let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
        let mut owner = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
        let old = super::super::before_alias::construct_using(
            &programs[0],
            &authored,
            &programs,
            &registry,
            None,
            &mut owner,
        )
        .unwrap();
        let current = construct_transient(
            &programs[0],
            &authored,
            &programs,
            &registry,
            None,
            &mut owner,
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            old.functions().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            current.functions().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>()
        );
        assert_eq!(
            old.types().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            current.types().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>()
        );
        for (id, borrowed) in [
            ("std.collections.vec.push", true),
            ("std.collections.vec.get", false),
            ("alias.plain", false),
        ] {
            let source = programs[1]
                .functions
                .iter()
                .find(|f| f.stable_id == id)
                .unwrap();
            let value = current
                .functions()
                .iter()
                .find(|f| f.stable_id == id)
                .unwrap();
            assert_eq!(std::ptr::eq(source, value), borrowed);
        }
    });
    assert!(!refused);
    let success = observe(LIMIT);
    assert!(success.0 .0 && !success.1);
    for limit in [0, success.2 - 1, success.2] {
        let result = observe(limit);
        assert_eq!(result.0 .0, limit == success.2);
        let m = result.0 .1;
        assert!(!m.invalid && m.live == 0 && m.allocated == m.freed);
    }
}

#[test]
fn complete_immediate_sources_and_independent_old_receivers_remain_authenticated() {
    use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
    let manifest = include_str!(concat!(
        env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
        "/docs/evidence/retained-function-alias-847/current-to-7a87.json"
    ));
    let rows: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(manifest).unwrap();
    for row in rows.as_array().unwrap() {
        let source = row["source"].as_str().unwrap();
        let current =
            std::fs::read_to_string(std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join(source))
                .unwrap();
        let current = super::owned_growth_tests::previous_source(source, &current);
        let current = crate::source_reflows_merge::retained_function847_input(source, &current);
        assert_eq!(
            format!(
                "{:x}",
                crate::digest_hex::LowerHex(Sha256::digest(current.as_bytes()))
            ),
            row["current_sha256"]
        );
        let edits = row["edits"].as_array().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(edits.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(current, edits[0]["new"].as_str().unwrap());
        let old = std::fs::read_to_string(
            std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join(row["archive"].as_str().unwrap()),
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(old, edits[0]["old"].as_str().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(
            format!(
                "{:x}",
                crate::digest_hex::LowerHex(Sha256::digest(old.as_bytes()))
            ),
            row["original_sha256"]
        );
    }
    let owners: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!(concat!(
        env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
        "/docs/evidence/retained-function-alias-847/new-owners.json"
    )))
    .unwrap();
    for owner in owners.as_array().unwrap() {
        let source = owner["source"].as_str().unwrap();
        let current =
            std::fs::read_to_string(std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join(source))
                .unwrap();
        let current = super::owned_growth_tests::previous_source(source, &current);
        let current = crate::source_reflows_merge::retained_function847_input(source, &current);
        assert_eq!(
            format!(
                "{:x}",
                crate::digest_hex::LowerHex(Sha256::digest(current.as_bytes()))
            ),
            owner["sha256"]
        );
    }
    let receivers: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!(concat!(
        env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
        "/docs/evidence/retained-function-alias-847/independent-receivers.json"
    )))
    .unwrap();
    for row in receivers.as_array().unwrap() {
        let source = row["source"].as_str().unwrap();
        let current =
            std::fs::read_to_string(std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join(source))
                .unwrap();
        let current = super::owned_growth_tests::previous_source(source, &current);
        let current = crate::source_reflows_merge::retained_function847_input(source, &current);
        assert_eq!(current, row["compiled"].as_str().unwrap());
        assert_eq!(
            format!(
                "{:x}",
                crate::digest_hex::LowerHex(Sha256::digest(current.as_bytes()))
            ),
            row["compiled_sha256"]
        );
        let old = std::fs::read_to_string(
            std::path::Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join(row["archive"].as_str().unwrap()),
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(old, row["original"].as_str().unwrap());
    }
}

#[test]
fn bytes_returning_alias_keeps_the_complete_owning_producer() {
    let programs = [
        crate::parse(
            r#"module alias.bytes;
use function @id("helper.leaf") from helper.bytes as write_byte;
@id("alias.bytes.main") fn main()->i64 {0}"#,
            "byte-caller.spx",
        )
        .unwrap(),
        crate::parse(
            include_str!(concat!(
                env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                "/tests/owned_data/byte_helper_renewal.spx"
            )),
            "byte-provider.spx",
        )
        .unwrap(),
    ];
    let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let ((), refused, _, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
        let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
        let mut owner = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
        let old = super::super::before_alias::construct_using(
            &programs[0],
            &authored,
            &programs,
            &registry,
            None,
            &mut owner,
        )
        .unwrap();
        let current = construct_transient(
            &programs[0],
            &authored,
            &programs,
            &registry,
            None,
            &mut owner,
        )
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            current.functions().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            old.functions().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>()
        );
        let cell = current
            .function_refs()
            .get(programs[0].functions.len())
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(cell.name(), "write_byte");
        assert!(!cell.has_stored_alias());
        assert!(!std::ptr::eq(
            cell.payload(),
            authored["helper.leaf"].function.unwrap()
        ));
        assert!(matches!(
            current.functions.entries[0],
            crate::ast::program_source::function_alias::FunctionEntry::Owned(_)
        ));
        drop((current, old, owner));
        drop(registry);
    });
    assert!(!refused && first.is_none());
}
//! Complete overlay walk; only default and directory refusal custody changes.
#[path = "../typed_cost.rs"]
#[allow(
    clippy::duplicate_mod,
    reason = "Independently compile the complete immediate-old constructor cost receiver."
)]
mod typed_cost;
use super::super::{
    alias_clone, budgeted_format, eligible, graph_error, import_stub, iterative, refusal,
    type_rewrite, AuthoredDeclaration, LayoutRegistry,
};
use crate::ast::program_source::{generated_roots::GeneratedRoots, SyntheticProgramView};
use crate::ast::{Expr, ExprKind, Function, ModuleUseKind, Program, Type, TypeDeclaration};
use crate::workspace_graph::constructor_failure::{receiving::Receiver, typed::Failure};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use typed_cost::{pay_default_teardown, pay_signature, pay_teardown_minimum, pay_type};

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct_using<'p, 'a>(
    program: &'p Program,
    authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
    programs: &'a [Program],
    registry: &LayoutRegistry<'_>,
    core: Option<&mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'a>>,
    owner: &mut Receiver,
) -> Result<SyntheticProgramView<'p>, Failure> {
    owner.entry::<SyntheticProgramView<'p>>()?;
    owner.reserve(0)?;
    if !eligible(program, authored, programs) {
        return Err(Failure::one(graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "imported borrowed projection requires the owned route",
        )));
    }
    let layout = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
    let explicit = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
        .count();
    let type_count = explicit
        .checked_add(layout.entries.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let recursive = iterative(program, authored);
    if recursive {
        pay_teardown_minimum(owner)?;
    }
    owner.reserve(std::mem::size_of::<GeneratedRoots>())?;
    let mut roots = if recursive {
        GeneratedRoots::iterative(directory::<TypeDeclaration>(type_count, owner)?, Vec::new())
    } else {
        GeneratedRoots::plain(directory::<TypeDeclaration>(type_count, owner)?, Vec::new())
    };
    let types = &mut roots.types;
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let declaration = target.ty.expect("validated type target");
        pay_type(declaration, owner)?;
        if recursive {
            typed_cost::teardown_type(declaration, owner)?;
        }
        types.push(alias_clone::type_payload(declaration));
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted type");
        copy.name = owner.text(&import.alias)?;
        owner.reserve(0)?;
        super::super::super::super::generic_type_import::receiving::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
            owner,
        )?;
    }
    // Preserve the independent closure's exact order after explicit aliases.
    for entry in &layout.entries {
        pay_type(entry.declaration, owner)?;
        if recursive {
            typed_cost::teardown_type(entry.declaration, owner)?;
        }
        types.push(alias_clone::type_payload(entry.declaration));
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted private type");
        copy.name = owner.text(&entry.alias)?;
        owner.reserve(0)?;
        super::super::super::super::generic_type_import::receiving::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            entry.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
            owner,
        )?;
    }
    let all_count = program
        .types
        .len()
        .checked_add(types.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let mut type_index = directory::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>(all_count, owner)?;
    type_index.extend(
        program
            .types
            .iter()
            .chain(types.iter())
            .map(|declaration| (declaration.name.as_str(), declaration)),
    );
    type_index.sort_unstable_by(|a, b| a.0.cmp(b.0));
    if type_index.windows(2).any(|items| items[0].0 == items[1].0) {
        return Err(Failure::one(graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "synthetic workspace type-name index is not unique",
        )));
    }
    let needs_main = !program
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
        && !program
            .module_uses
            .iter()
            .any(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function && import.alias == "main");
    let imports = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
        .count();
    roots.functions = directory::<Function>(
        imports
            .checked_add(usize::from(needs_main))
            .ok_or_else(refusal)?,
        owner,
    )?;
    let functions = &mut roots.functions;
    let mut byte_proofs =
        import_stub::core_proofs::Phase::new(authored, programs, core).map_err(Failure::one)?;
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let original = target.function.expect("validated function target");
        let retain_body = programs
            .iter()
            .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
            .is_some_and(|provider| {
                crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper(provider, original).is_some()
                    || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper(provider, original).is_some()
            });
        if retain_body {
            typed_cost::teardown_function(original, true, owner)?;
        } else {
            pay_signature(original, owner)?;
            if recursive {
                typed_cost::teardown_function(original, false, owner)?;
            }
        }
        let function = if retain_body {
            alias_clone::retained_function(original, &import.alias)
        } else {
            alias_clone::signature_function(original, &import.alias)
        };
        functions.push(function);
        owner.reserve(0)?;
        if retain_body {
            continue;
        }
        let function = functions.last_mut().expect("inserted signature");
        for param in &mut function.params {
            type_rewrite::receiving::rewrite_with_layout(
                &mut param.ty,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                Some(&layout),
                owner,
            )?;
        }
        type_rewrite::receiving::rewrite_with_layout(
            &mut function.return_type,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
            owner,
        )?;
        if recursive {
            pay_default_teardown(&function.return_type, &type_index, owner)?;
        }
        function.body = import_stub::typed::default_expr(
            function,
            &type_index,
            super::super::super::super::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                program, target, authored, programs, &layout,
            ),
            byte_proofs
                .get()
                .map_err(Failure::one)?
                .authorized(original, target.module)
                .map_err(Failure::one)?,
            owner,
        )?;
    }
    // Same old rule also recognizes an imported alias named main.
    if needs_main && !functions.iter().any(|function| function.name == "main") {
        owner.reserve(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())?;
        let stable_id =
            budgeted_format(format_args!("workspace.synthetic.main.{}", program.module));
        owner.reserve(0)?;
        let name = owner.text("main")?;
        functions.push(Function {
            stable_id,
            explicit_id: true,
            name,
            name_span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
            type_parameters: Vec::new(),
            params: Vec::new(),
            return_type: Type::I64,
            effects: Vec::new(),
            yields: None,
            follows: None,
            requires: Vec::new(),
            ensures: Vec::new(),
            body: Expr {
                kind: ExprKind::Int(0),
                span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
            },
            span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
        });
        owner.reserve(0)?;
    }
    drop(type_index);
    // Infallible bounded string helpers record refusal and return empty text.
    // A generated view must never publish that partial construction as success.
    owner.reserve(0)?;
    let result = SyntheticProgramView::with_generated_owner(program, roots).ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    byte_proofs.complete().map_err(Failure::one)?;
    Ok(result)
}

/// Same exact-count directory as the independent old helper. The initialized
/// return, Vec header, layout and actual backing are paid by the original
/// receiver before allocation; a failed reservation propagates only Copy Setup.
fn directory<T>(count: usize, owner: &mut Receiver) -> Result<Vec<T>, Failure> {
    use std::mem::size_of;
    owner.entry::<Vec<T>>()?;
    owner.reserve(
        size_of::<Vec<T>>()
            + size_of::<usize>()
            + size_of::<std::alloc::Layout>()
            + size_of::<Result<std::alloc::Layout, std::alloc::LayoutError>>(),
    )?;
    let layout = match std::alloc::Layout::array::<T>(count) {
        Ok(layout) => layout,
        Err(_) => return Err(owner.physical().into()),
    };
    owner.reserve(layout.size())?;
    let values = Vec::with_capacity(count);
    if values.capacity() != count {
        return Err(owner.physical().into());
    }
    Ok(values)
}
//! Complete overlay walk; only default refusal custody changes.
use super::{
    alias_clone, ast_function_cost, ast_function_signature_cost, ast_type_declaration_cost,
    budgeted_format, directory, eligible, graph_error, import_stub, iterative,
    pay_default_teardown, pay_signature, pay_teardown, pay_teardown_minimum, pay_type, refusal,
    reserve_builder_structure, type_rewrite, AuthoredDeclaration, LayoutRegistry, StructuralCost,
};
use crate::ast::program_source::{generated_roots::GeneratedRoots, SyntheticProgramView};
use crate::ast::{Expr, ExprKind, Function, ModuleUseKind, Program, Type, TypeDeclaration};
use crate::workspace_graph::constructor_failure::{reserve, Failure};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct_using<'p, 'a>(
    program: &'p Program,
    authored: &'a BTreeMap<&'a str, AuthoredDeclaration<'a>>,
    programs: &'a [Program],
    registry: &LayoutRegistry<'_>,
    core: Option<&mut import_stub::core_proofs::CoreProviderProofs<'a>>,
) -> Result<SyntheticProgramView<'p>, Failure> {
    reserve(std::mem::size_of::<Result<SyntheticProgramView<'p>, Failure>>())?;
    reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
    if !eligible(program, authored, programs) {
        return Err(Failure::one(graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "imported borrowed projection requires the owned route",
        )));
    }
    let layout = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
    let explicit = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
        .count();
    let type_count = explicit
        .checked_add(layout.entries.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let recursive = iterative(program, authored);
    if recursive {
        pay_teardown_minimum()?;
    }
    let mut roots = if recursive {
        GeneratedRoots::iterative(directory::<TypeDeclaration>(type_count)?, Vec::new())
    } else {
        GeneratedRoots::plain(directory::<TypeDeclaration>(type_count)?, Vec::new())
    };
    let types = &mut roots.types;
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let declaration = target.ty.expect("validated type target");
        pay_type(declaration)?;
        if recursive {
            let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
            ast_type_declaration_cost(declaration, &mut cost)?;
            pay_teardown(&cost)?;
        }
        types.push(alias_clone::type_payload(declaration));
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted type");
        copy.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&import.alias);
        reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
        super::super::super::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
        )?;
    }
    // Preserve the independent closure's exact order after explicit aliases.
    for entry in &layout.entries {
        pay_type(entry.declaration)?;
        if recursive {
            let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
            ast_type_declaration_cost(entry.declaration, &mut cost)?;
            pay_teardown(&cost)?;
        }
        types.push(alias_clone::type_payload(entry.declaration));
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted private type");
        copy.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&entry.alias);
        reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
        super::super::super::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            entry.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
        )?;
    }
    let all_count = program
        .types
        .len()
        .checked_add(types.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let mut type_index = directory::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>(all_count)?;
    type_index.extend(
        program
            .types
            .iter()
            .chain(types.iter())
            .map(|declaration| (declaration.name.as_str(), declaration)),
    );
    type_index.sort_unstable_by(|a, b| a.0.cmp(b.0));
    if type_index.windows(2).any(|items| items[0].0 == items[1].0) {
        return Err(Failure::one(graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "synthetic workspace type-name index is not unique",
        )));
    }
    let needs_main = !program
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
        && !program
            .module_uses
            .iter()
            .any(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function && import.alias == "main");
    let imports = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
        .count();
    roots.functions = directory::<Function>(
        imports
            .checked_add(usize::from(needs_main))
            .ok_or_else(refusal)?,
    )?;
    let functions = &mut roots.functions;
    let mut byte_proofs = import_stub::core_proofs::Phase::new(authored, programs, core)
        .map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let original = target.function.expect("validated function target");
        let retain_body = programs
            .iter()
            .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
            .is_some_and(|provider| {
                crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper(provider, original).is_some()
                    || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper(provider, original).is_some()
            });
        if retain_body {
            let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
            ast_function_cost(original, &mut cost)?;
            reserve_builder_structure(cost.total)?;
            pay_teardown(&cost)?;
        } else {
            pay_signature(original)?;
            if recursive {
                let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
                ast_function_signature_cost(original, &mut cost)?;
                pay_teardown(&cost)?;
            }
        }
        let function = if retain_body {
            alias_clone::retained_function(original, &import.alias)
        } else {
            alias_clone::signature_function(original, &import.alias)
        };
        functions.push(function);
        reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
        if retain_body {
            continue;
        }
        let function = functions.last_mut().expect("inserted signature");
        for param in &mut function.params {
            type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
                &mut param.ty,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                Some(&layout),
            )?;
        }
        type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
            &mut function.return_type,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
        )?;
        if recursive {
            pay_default_teardown(&function.return_type, &type_index)?;
        }
        function.body = import_stub::default_expr_inline(
            function,
            &type_index,
            super::super::super::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                program, target, authored, programs, &layout,
            ),
            import_stub::byte_helper_authorized(
                original,
                target.module,
                byte_proofs.get().map_err(|error| vec![error])?,
            )?,
        )?;
    }
    // Same old rule also recognizes an imported alias named main.
    if needs_main && !functions.iter().any(|function| function.name == "main") {
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())?;
        functions.push(Function {
            stable_id: budgeted_format(format_args!("workspace.synthetic.main.{}", program.module)),
            explicit_id: true,
            name: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone("main"),
            name_span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
            type_parameters: Vec::new(),
            params: Vec::new(),
            return_type: Type::I64,
            effects: Vec::new(),
            yields: None,
            follows: None,
            requires: Vec::new(),
            ensures: Vec::new(),
            body: Expr {
                kind: ExprKind::Int(0),
                span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
            },
            span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
        });
        reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
    }
    drop(type_index);
    // Infallible bounded string helpers record refusal and return empty text.
    // A generated view must never publish that partial construction as success.
    reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
    let result = SyntheticProgramView::with_generated_owner(program, roots).ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    byte_proofs.complete().map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
    Ok(result)
}
//! Complete raw declaration/expression/pattern census, with unchanged order
//! and formulas. Actual controls and payments use the original affine receiver.
use crate::ast::{Expr, ExprKind, Function, Type, TypeDeclaration, TypeDeclarationKind};
use crate::workspace_graph::constructor_failure::receiving::{Receiver, Setup};
use crate::workspace_graph::expected_projection::cost::constructor::Cost;
use std::mem::{size_of, size_of_val};

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn ast_field_cost(
    field: &crate::ast::FieldDeclaration,
    cost: &mut Cost<'_>,
) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    cost.frame(size_of::<&crate::ast::FieldDeclaration>() + size_of::<&mut Cost<'_>>())?;
    cost.inline_type_parent(field)?;
    cost.embedded_string(&field.stable_id)?;
    cost.embedded_string(&field.name)?;
    ast_type_cost(&field.ty, cost)
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn ast_function_cost(
    function: &Function,
    cost: &mut Cost<'_>,
) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    cost.frame(size_of::<&Function>() + size_of::<&mut Cost<'_>>())?;
    ast_function_signature_cost(function, cost)?;
    ast_function_contract_cost_into(function, cost)
}

/// The part of a function every retained projection keeps: its identity, its
/// name, its type parameters, its parameters, its return type, and its effect
/// row. An imported function is retained as exactly this much plus a synthetic
/// default body.
pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn ast_function_signature_cost(
    function: &Function,
    cost: &mut Cost<'_>,
) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    cost.frame(size_of::<&Function>() + size_of::<&mut Cost<'_>>())?;
    cost.function_signature(function)?;
    cost.embedded_string(&function.stable_id)?;
    cost.embedded_string(&function.name)?;
    for parameter in &function.type_parameters {
        cost.value(parameter)?;
        cost.embedded_string(&parameter.name)?;
    }
    for param in &function.params {
        ast_param_cost(param, cost)?;
    }
    ast_type_cost(&function.return_type, cost)?;
    for effect in &function.effects {
        cost.string(effect)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// The preconditions, body, and postconditions of a function. An imported
/// function's contract is cloned transiently and then discarded, so it is
/// charged as a peak rather than as retained structure.
fn ast_function_contract_cost_into(function: &Function, cost: &mut Cost<'_>) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    cost.frame(size_of::<&Function>() + size_of::<&mut Cost<'_>>())?;
    for expression in function
        .requires
        .iter()
        .chain(std::iter::once(&function.body))
        .chain(&function.ensures)
    {
        ast_expr_cost(expression, cost)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn ast_param_cost(
    param: &crate::ast::Param,
    cost: &mut Cost<'_>,
) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    cost.frame(size_of::<&crate::ast::Param>() + size_of::<&mut Cost<'_>>())?;
    cost.inline_type_parent(param)?;
    cost.embedded_string(&param.name)?;
    ast_type_cost(&param.ty, cost)
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn ast_type_cost(
    ty: &Type,
    cost: &mut Cost<'_>,
) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    cost.frame(size_of::<&Type>() + size_of::<&mut Cost<'_>>())?;
    cost.value(ty)?;
    if let Type::Named { name, arguments } = ty {
        cost.embedded_string(name)?;
        for argument in arguments {
            ast_type_cost(argument, cost)?;
        }
    }
    if cost.is_raw_fallback() {
        if let Type::Function { parameters, result } = ty {
            for parameter in parameters {
                ast_type_cost(parameter, cost)?;
            }
            ast_type_cost(result, cost)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Where the parent places a child's resolved root. `CarrierFree` is used only
/// for a `Box` slot of a unary, binary, `if` or block-tail node, or an element
/// of a free-function call's argument list (every intrinsic route only moves
/// or boxes its arguments): no binding, statement, field initializer, arm or
/// pattern field is constructed for it there. Only literal/unary/binary and
/// `if` roots in such a slot are censused; variable roots are also censused
/// outside every authored closure subtree, retaining all their storage.
/// Block roots in those same positions are also carrier-free: their statements
/// remain independently charged and their tail is boxed by finish_block. Every
/// other child, and every root reached through `ast_expr_cost`, is `Delegated` and keeps its complete
/// bundle.
#[derive(Clone, Copy, Eq, PartialEq)]
enum RootPosition {
    CarrierFree,
    Delegated,
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn ast_expr_cost(
    expression: &Expr,
    cost: &mut Cost<'_>,
) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    cost.frame(size_of::<&Expr>() + size_of::<&mut Cost<'_>>())?;
    ast_expr_cost_at(expression, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, true)
}

fn ast_expr_cost_at(
    expression: &Expr,
    cost: &mut Cost<'_>,
    position: RootPosition,
    outside_closure: bool,
) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    cost.frame(
        size_of::<&Expr>()
            + size_of::<&mut Cost<'_>>()
            + size_of_val(&position)
            + size_of_val(&outside_closure),
    )?;
    use RootPosition::CarrierFree;
    cost.value(expression)?;
    // `super` allocates the initial receiver before recreating its source,
    // upcast wrapper and call root; the discarded receiver still costs bytes.
    cost.add_identity_carriers(if matches!(expression.kind, ExprKind::SuperMethod { .. }) {
        4
    } else {
        3
    })?;
    // These resolver branches construct one root ExpressionId. Child trees,
    // imported defaults, generic expansions, and desugared statements keep
    // their independent bounds; other shapes keep the three-carrier ceiling.
    if matches!(
        expression.kind,
        ExprKind::Int(_)
            | ExprKind::String(_)
            | ExprKind::BorrowedUtf8Literal(_)
            | ExprKind::Float32(_)
            | ExprKind::Float64(_)
            | ExprKind::Bool(_)
            | ExprKind::Var(_)
            | ExprKind::Unary { .. }
            | ExprKind::Binary { .. }
            | ExprKind::If { .. }
            | ExprKind::Block { .. }
    ) {
        cost.note_single_identity_carrier()?;
    }
    cost.note_exact_expr_fixed_bundle()?;
    cost.account_scalar_identity(&expression.kind)?;
    cost.account_exact_absent_storage(&expression.kind)?;
    cost.account_carrier_free_root(&expression.kind, position == CarrierFree, outside_closure)?;
    match &expression.kind {
        ExprKind::Closure {
            params,
            return_type,
            body,
            ..
        } => {
            for parameter in params {
                cost.inline_type_parent(parameter)?;
                cost.embedded_string(&parameter.name)?;
                ast_type_cost(&parameter.ty, cost)?;
            }
            ast_type_cost(return_type, cost)?;
            ast_expr_cost_at(body, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, false)?;
        }
        ExprKind::Var(name) => cost.embedded_string(name)?,
        ExprKind::ArrayU8(values) => cost.add(values.len())?,
        ExprKind::RepeatArrayU8 { .. } => {}
        ExprKind::Call {
            name,
            type_arguments,
            args,
        } => {
            cost.embedded_string(name)?;
            // Complete synthetic key/layout identities are additional HIR text.
            // Bound every transient replay buffer before entering the builder;
            // descriptor construction itself charges its actual allocation.
            if crate::vec_ops::field_unique::literal_call(name)
                || name == crate::vec_ops::field_update::NAME
            {
                cost.add_split(
                    8 * crate::vec_ops::sort_keys::MAX_DESCRIPTOR_BYTES,
                    8 * crate::vec_ops::sort_keys::MAX_DESCRIPTOR_BYTES,
                )?;
            }
            for ty in type_arguments {
                ast_type_cost(ty, cost)?;
            }
            for argument in args {
                ast_expr_cost_at(argument, cost, CarrierFree, outside_closure)?;
            }
        }
        ExprKind::LexicalExit { value: None, .. } => {}
        ExprKind::LexicalExit {
            value: Some(value), ..
        } => ast_expr_cost_at(value, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, outside_closure)?,
        ExprKind::Unary { value, .. } => {
            ast_expr_cost_at(value, cost, CarrierFree, outside_closure)?
        }
        ExprKind::VecConstructor { element, value, .. } => {
            ast_type_cost(element, cost)?;
            ast_expr_cost_at(value, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, outside_closure)?;
        }
        ExprKind::Binary { left, right, .. } => {
            ast_expr_cost_at(left, cost, CarrierFree, outside_closure)?;
            ast_expr_cost_at(right, cost, CarrierFree, outside_closure)?;
        }
        ExprKind::Block { statements, tail } => {
            for statement in statements {
                if matches!(
                    statement,
                    crate::ast::Statement::Let { .. } | crate::ast::Statement::Assign { .. }
                ) {
                    cost.inline_expr_parent(statement)?;
                } else {
                    cost.value(statement)?;
                }
                if matches!(
                    statement,
                    crate::ast::Statement::For { .. }
                        | crate::ast::Statement::ForBorrow { .. }
                        | crate::ast::Statement::ForOwn { .. }
                ) {
                    cost.add_identity_carriers(16)?;
                }
                // Charge the string every statement actually carries. Unsafe
                // boundaries carry their verbatim audit summary, `while`
                // carries no binding at all, bounded `for` traversal carries
                // its item binding, and `let`/assignment carry their name.
                // Matching the statement exhaustively keeps a later variant a
                // compile error here rather than a panic at check time:
                // `Statement::name` panics for every statement that is not a
                // `let` or an assignment.
                match statement {
                    crate::ast::Statement::Unsafe { audit, .. } => cost.embedded_string(audit)?,
                    crate::ast::Statement::While { .. } => cost.string("")?,
                    crate::ast::Statement::For { item, .. }
                    | crate::ast::Statement::ForOwn { item, .. } => cost.embedded_string(item)?,
                    crate::ast::Statement::ForBorrow { item, index, .. } => {
                        cost.embedded_string(item)?;
                        if let Some(index) = index {
                            cost.embedded_string(&index.name)?;
                        }
                    }
                    crate::ast::Statement::Let { name, .. }
                    | crate::ast::Statement::Assign { name, .. } => cost.embedded_string(name)?,
                }
                if cost.is_raw_fallback() {
                    match statement {
                        crate::ast::Statement::Assign {
                            field: Some(field), ..
                        } => cost.embedded_string(&field.name)?,
                        crate::ast::Statement::Let {
                            declared: Some(ty), ..
                        } => ast_type_cost(ty, cost)?,
                        _ => {}
                    }
                }
                for index in 0..statement.child_count() {
                    if let Some(child) = statement.child(index) {
                        ast_expr_cost_at(child, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, outside_closure)?;
                    }
                }
            }
            ast_expr_cost_at(tail, cost, CarrierFree, outside_closure)?;
        }
        ExprKind::If {
            condition,
            then_branch,
            else_branch,
        } => {
            ast_expr_cost_at(condition, cost, CarrierFree, outside_closure)?;
            ast_expr_cost_at(then_branch, cost, CarrierFree, outside_closure)?;
            ast_expr_cost_at(else_branch, cost, CarrierFree, outside_closure)?;
        }
        ExprKind::ConstructRecord {
            type_name,
            type_arguments,
            fields,
            ..
        }
        | ExprKind::ConstructVariant {
            type_name,
            type_arguments,
            fields,
            ..
        } => {
            cost.embedded_string(type_name)?;
            if let ExprKind::ConstructVariant { case_name, .. } = &expression.kind {
                cost.embedded_string(case_name)?;
            }
            for ty in type_arguments {
                ast_type_cost(ty, cost)?;
            }
            for field in fields {
                cost.inline_expr_parent(field)?;
                cost.embedded_string(&field.name)?;
                ast_expr_cost_at(&field.value, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, outside_closure)?;
            }
        }
        ExprKind::Match {
            scrutinee, arms, ..
        } => {
            ast_expr_cost_at(scrutinee, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, outside_closure)?;
            for arm in arms {
                cost.match_arm(arm)?;
                ast_pattern_cost(&arm.pattern, cost)?;
                // The boxed guard is a complete resolved expression tree too;
                // `match_arm` charges only its pointer.
                if let Some(guard) = &arm.guard {
                    ast_expr_cost_at(guard, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, outside_closure)?;
                }
                ast_expr_cost_at(&arm.value, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, outside_closure)?;
            }
        }
        ExprKind::Try { operand } | ExprKind::Yield { request: operand } => {
            ast_expr_cost_at(operand, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, outside_closure)?
        }
        ExprKind::UpdateRecord { base, fields } => {
            ast_expr_cost_at(base, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, outside_closure)?;
            for field in fields {
                cost.inline_expr_parent(field)?;
                cost.embedded_string(&field.name)?;
                ast_expr_cost_at(&field.value, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, outside_closure)?;
            }
        }
        ExprKind::Project { base, field, .. } => {
            ast_expr_cost_at(base, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, outside_closure)?;
            cost.embedded_string(field)?;
        }
        ExprKind::MethodCall {
            receiver,
            method,
            type_arguments,
            args,
            ..
        } => {
            ast_expr_cost_at(receiver, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, outside_closure)?;
            cost.embedded_string(method)?;
            for ty in type_arguments {
                ast_type_cost(ty, cost)?;
            }
            for argument in args {
                ast_expr_cost_at(argument, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, outside_closure)?;
            }
        }
        ExprKind::SuperMethod { method, args, .. } => {
            cost.embedded_string(method)?;
            for argument in args {
                ast_expr_cost_at(argument, cost, RootPosition::Delegated, outside_closure)?;
            }
        }
        ExprKind::Int(_)
        | ExprKind::Int32(_)
        | ExprKind::Char(_)
        | ExprKind::Uint8(_)
        | ExprKind::Usize(_)
        | ExprKind::Float32(_)
        | ExprKind::Float64(_)
        | ExprKind::Bool(_) => {}
        ExprKind::String(value) | ExprKind::BorrowedUtf8Literal(value) => {
            if cost.is_raw_fallback() {
                cost.embedded_string(value)?;
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn ast_type_declaration_cost(
    declaration: &TypeDeclaration,
    cost: &mut Cost<'_>,
) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    cost.frame(size_of::<&TypeDeclaration>() + size_of::<&mut Cost<'_>>())?;
    cost.value(declaration)?;
    cost.embedded_string(&declaration.stable_id)?;
    cost.embedded_string(&declaration.name)?;
    for parameter in &declaration.type_parameters {
        cost.value(parameter)?;
        cost.embedded_string(&parameter.name)?;
    }
    match &declaration.kind {
        TypeDeclarationKind::Resource { lifecycles } => {
            for lifecycle in lifecycles {
                cost.value(lifecycle)?;
                if let Some(id) = &lifecycle.stable_id {
                    cost.embedded_string(id)?;
                }
                if let crate::ast::ResourceLifecycleKind::Imported { import_key } = &lifecycle.kind
                {
                    cost.embedded_string(import_key)?;
                }
            }
        }
        TypeDeclarationKind::Record { fields } => {
            for field in fields {
                ast_field_cost(field, cost)?;
            }
        }
        TypeDeclarationKind::Class { fields, methods } => {
            for field in fields {
                ast_field_cost(field, cost)?;
            }
            for method in methods {
                ast_function_cost(method, cost)?;
            }
        }
        TypeDeclarationKind::Variant { cases } => {
            for case in cases {
                cost.value(case)?;
                cost.embedded_string(&case.stable_id)?;
                cost.embedded_string(&case.name)?;
                for field in &case.fields {
                    ast_field_cost(field, cost)?;
                }
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn ast_pattern_cost(pattern: &crate::ast::MatchPattern, cost: &mut Cost<'_>) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    cost.frame(size_of::<&crate::ast::MatchPattern>() + size_of::<&mut Cost<'_>>())?;
    cost.value(pattern)?;
    match pattern {
        crate::ast::MatchPattern::Variant {
            type_name,
            case_name,
            fields,
            ..
        } => {
            cost.embedded_string(type_name)?;
            cost.embedded_string(case_name)?;
            for field in fields {
                cost.value(field)?;
                cost.embedded_string(&field.name)?;
                cost.embedded_string(&field.binding)?;
            }
        }
        crate::ast::MatchPattern::Record {
            type_name, fields, ..
        } => {
            cost.embedded_string(type_name)?;
            for field in fields {
                ast_record_pattern_field_cost(field, cost)?;
            }
        }
        crate::ast::MatchPattern::Wildcard { .. } => {}
        // Refutable Match v1: literal/or/binding structural costs.
        crate::ast::MatchPattern::Literal { value, .. } => {
            cost.string(value.type_text())?;
        }
        crate::ast::MatchPattern::Or { alternatives, .. } => {
            for alternative in alternatives {
                ast_pattern_cost(alternative, cost)?;
            }
        }
        crate::ast::MatchPattern::Binding { name, .. } => {
            cost.embedded_string(name)?;
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}
fn ast_record_pattern_field_cost(
    field: &crate::ast::RecordMatchPatternField,
    cost: &mut Cost<'_>,
) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    cost.frame(size_of::<&crate::ast::RecordMatchPatternField>() + size_of::<&mut Cost<'_>>())?;
    cost.inline_pattern_parent(field, &field.pattern)?;
    cost.embedded_string(&field.name)?;
    cost.value(&field.pattern)?;
    match &field.pattern {
        crate::ast::RecordMatchFieldPattern::Binding { name, .. } => cost.embedded_string(name)?,
        crate::ast::RecordMatchFieldPattern::Record {
            type_name, fields, ..
        } => {
            cost.embedded_string(type_name)?;
            for field in fields {
                ast_record_pattern_field_cost(field, cost)?;
            }
        }
        crate::ast::RecordMatchFieldPattern::Wildcard { .. } => {}
    }
    Ok(())
}

pub(super) fn pay_type(declaration: &TypeDeclaration, owner: &mut Receiver) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    owner.reserve(
        size_of::<Result<(), Setup>>() + size_of::<&TypeDeclaration>() + size_of::<&mut Receiver>(),
    )?;
    let mut cost = Cost::new(owner, true)?;
    ast_type_declaration_cost(declaration, &mut cost)?;
    cost.pay()
}
pub(super) fn pay_signature(function: &Function, owner: &mut Receiver) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    owner.reserve(
        size_of::<Result<(), Setup>>() + size_of::<&Function>() + size_of::<&mut Receiver>(),
    )?;
    let mut cost = Cost::new(owner, true)?;
    ast_function_signature_cost(function, &mut cost)?;
    cost.pay()
}
pub(super) fn teardown_type(
    declaration: &TypeDeclaration,
    owner: &mut Receiver,
) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    owner.reserve(
        size_of::<Result<(), Setup>>() + size_of::<&TypeDeclaration>() + size_of::<&mut Receiver>(),
    )?;
    let mut cost = Cost::new(owner, true)?;
    ast_type_declaration_cost(declaration, &mut cost)?;
    cost.teardown()
}
pub(super) fn teardown_function(
    function: &Function,
    retained: bool,
    owner: &mut Receiver,
) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    owner.reserve(
        size_of::<Result<(), Setup>>()
            + size_of::<&Function>()
            + size_of_val(&retained)
            + size_of::<&mut Receiver>(),
    )?;
    let mut cost = Cost::new(owner, true)?;
    if retained {
        ast_function_cost(function, &mut cost)?;
        cost.pay()?;
    } else {
        ast_function_signature_cost(function, &mut cost)?;
    }
    cost.teardown()
}

pub(super) fn pay_default_teardown(
    ty: &Type,
    declarations: &[(&str, &TypeDeclaration)],
    owner: &mut Receiver,
) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    owner.reserve(
        size_of::<Result<(), Setup>>()
            + size_of::<&Type>()
            + size_of::<&[(&str, &TypeDeclaration)]>()
            + size_of::<&mut Receiver>(),
    )?;
    fn census(
        ty: &Type,
        declarations: &[(&str, &TypeDeclaration)],
        owner: &mut Receiver,
    ) -> Result<usize, Setup> {
        owner.reserve(
            size_of::<Result<usize, Setup>>()
                + size_of::<&Type>()
                + size_of::<&[(&str, &TypeDeclaration)]>()
                + size_of::<&mut Receiver>()
                + size_of::<usize>()
                + size_of::<Option<usize>>(),
        )?;
        let mut bytes = std::mem::size_of::<Expr>();
        if let Type::Named { name, arguments } = ty {
            if arguments.is_empty() {
                if let Ok(index) =
                    declarations.binary_search_by_key(&name.as_str(), |(name, _)| *name)
                {
                    let fields: &[crate::ast::FieldDeclaration] = match &declarations[index].1.kind
                    {
                        TypeDeclarationKind::Record { fields }
                        | TypeDeclarationKind::Class { fields, .. } => fields,
                        TypeDeclarationKind::Variant { cases } => {
                            cases.first().map_or(&[], |case| &case.fields)
                        }
                        TypeDeclarationKind::Resource { .. } => &[],
                    };
                    for field in fields {
                        bytes = bytes
                            .checked_add(census(&field.ty, declarations, owner)?)
                            .ok_or_else(|| owner.limit())?;
                    }
                }
            } else {
                // Accepted compiler collection defaults have a root call,
                // one scalar size operand and copied type arguments. Generic
                // authored defaults still receive their old closed refusal.
                bytes = bytes
                    .checked_add(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())
                    .ok_or_else(|| owner.limit())?;
                let total = {
                    let mut cost = Cost::new(owner, false)?;
                    ast_type_cost(ty, &mut cost)?;
                    cost.raw.total
                };
                bytes = bytes.checked_add(total).ok_or_else(|| owner.limit())?;
            }
        } else if *ty == Type::Bytes || *ty == Type::StringMap {
            bytes = bytes
                .checked_add(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())
                .ok_or_else(|| owner.limit())?;
        }
        Ok(bytes)
    }
    let bytes = census(ty, declarations, owner)?
        .checked_mul(4)
        .ok_or_else(|| owner.limit())?;
    owner.reserve(bytes)
}

pub(super) fn pay_teardown_minimum(owner: &mut Receiver) -> Result<(), Setup> {
    owner.reserve(
        size_of::<Result<(), Setup>>()
            + size_of::<&mut Receiver>()
            + size_of::<usize>()
            + size_of::<Option<usize>>()
            + size_of::<[usize; 5]>(),
    )?;
    let bytes = [
        std::mem::size_of::<Function>(),
        std::mem::size_of::<Expr>(),
        std::mem::size_of::<Type>(),
        std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::MatchPattern>(),
        std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::RecordMatchPatternField>(),
    ]
    .into_iter()
    .try_fold(0usize, |sum, size| sum.checked_add(size))
    .ok_or_else(|| owner.limit())?;
    let bytes = bytes.checked_mul(4).ok_or_else(|| owner.limit())?;
    owner.reserve(bytes)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::{
        ast_function_cost, ast_type_declaration_cost, pay_default_teardown, teardown_function,
        Cost, Receiver, Setup, Type,
    };
    use crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage_refusal;
    use crate::test_allocation_observer::{current_metrics, measure};
    use crate::workspace_graph::constructor_failure::typed::Failure;
    use crate::workspace_graph::expected_projection::{self, StructuralCost};
    const LIMIT: usize = 64 * 1024 * 1024;
    fn facts(raw: &StructuralCost) -> [usize; 15] {
        [
            raw.total,
            raw.string_bytes,
            raw.scalar_identity_discount,
            raw.literal_fixed_discount,
            raw.identity_carriers,
            raw.single_identity_carriers,
            raw.exact_fixed_discount,
            raw.exact_expressions,
            raw.exact_absent_roots[0],
            raw.exact_absent_roots[1],
            raw.exact_absent_roots[2],
            raw.exact_carrier_free_roots,
            raw.exact_carrier_free_ifs,
            raw.exact_carrier_free_vars,
            raw.exact_carrier_free_blocks,
        ]
    }
    #[test]
    #[allow(
        clippy::drop_non_drop,
        reason = "This explicit lifetime endpoint is part of the complete ownership and allocation observation."
    )]
    fn typed_constructor_census_keeps_all_original_raw_fields_order_and_teardown_formulas() {
        let sources = [
            "module test; @id(\"main\") fn main()->i64 { let x=1; x }",
            "module test; @id(\"main\") fn main()->i64 { while false {0} 0 }",
            "module test; @id(\"main\") fn main()->i64 { if true {1 + -2} else {callee(3)} }",
            "module test; @id(\"main\") fn main()->i64 { let mut value=0; value.x=1; 0 }",
            "module test; @id(\"main\") fn main()->i64 { let x:Input=0; 0 }",
            "module test; @id(\"row\") record Row {@id(\"x\") x:i64,} @id(\"main\") fn main()->i64 {0}",
            include_str!(concat!(
                env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                "/std/collections/src/collections.spx"
            )),
            include_str!(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/std/mem/src/mem.spx")),
        ];
        for source in sources {
            let program = crate::parse(source, "typed-census.spx").unwrap();
            for inline in [false, true] {
                for function in &program.functions {
                    let mut original = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(inline);
                    expected_projection::ast_function_cost(function, &mut original).unwrap();
                    let expected = facts(&original);
                    let (((), physical), refused, used, first) =
                        with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
                            measure(|| {
                                let mut owner = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
                                let mut cost = Cost::new(&mut owner, inline).unwrap();
                                ast_function_cost(function, &mut cost).unwrap();
