                                assert_eq!(facts(&cost.raw), expected);
                                cost.teardown().unwrap();
                                drop(cost);
                                drop(owner);
                            })
                        });
                    assert!(!refused && first.is_none());
                    assert!(
                        !physical.invalid
                            && physical.live == 0
                            && physical.allocated == physical.freed
                    );
                    assert!(physical.allocated <= used);
                }
                for declaration in &program.types {
                    let mut original = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(inline);
                    expected_projection::ast_type_declaration_cost(declaration, &mut original)
                        .unwrap();
                    let expected = facts(&original);
                    let ((), refused, _, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
                        let mut owner = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
                        let mut cost = Cost::new(&mut owner, inline).unwrap();
                        ast_type_declaration_cost(declaration, &mut cost).unwrap();
                        assert_eq!(facts(&cost.raw), expected);
                        drop(cost);
                        drop(owner);
                    });
                    assert!(!refused && first.is_none());
                    let index = [(declaration.name.as_str(), declaration)];
                    let ty = Type::Named {
                        name: declaration.name.clone(),
                        arguments: Vec::new(),
                    };
                    let ((), refused, old, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
                        crate::workspace_graph::expected_projection::borrowed_imports::pay_default_teardown(&ty, &index).unwrap();
                    });
                    assert!(!refused && first.is_none());
                    let ((), refused, new, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
                        let mut owner = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
                        pay_default_teardown(&ty, &index, &mut owner).unwrap();
                        drop(owner);
                    });
                    assert!(!refused && first.is_none());
                    assert!(
                        new > old,
                        "actual controls are additional; no old fee credit"
                    );
                }
            }
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn typed_constructor_cost_every_byte_frontier_and_retry_allocate_nothing_after_refusal() {
        let program = crate::parse(
            "module test; @id(\"main\") fn main()->i64 {1 + -2}",
            "frontier.spx",
        )
        .unwrap();
        let function = &program.functions[0];
        let ((), refused, exact, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
            let mut owner = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
            teardown_function(function, true, &mut owner).unwrap();
            drop(owner);
        });
        assert!(!refused && first.is_none());
        for limit in 0..=exact {
            let (((), physical), refused, used, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(limit, || {
                measure(|| {
                    let mut owner = match Receiver::acquire() {
                        Ok(owner) => owner,
                        Err(Setup::Budget { .. }) => return,
                        Err(other) => panic!("unexpected owner setup: {other:?}"),
                    };
                    match teardown_function(function, true, &mut owner) {
                        Ok(()) => assert_eq!(limit, exact),
                        Err(selected @ Setup::Budget { .. }) => {
                            let stop = current_metrics().allocated;
                            assert_eq!(
                                teardown_function(function, true, &mut owner),
                                Err(selected)
                            );
                            drop(owner);
                            assert_eq!(current_metrics().allocated, stop);
                            return;
                        }
                        Err(other) => panic!("unexpected census setup: {other:?}"),
                    }
                    drop(owner);
                })
            });
            assert_eq!(refused, limit != exact);
            assert_eq!(first.is_some(), refused);
            assert!(used <= limit);
            assert!(
                !physical.invalid && physical.live == 0 && physical.allocated == physical.freed
            );
            assert!(physical.allocated <= used);
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn typed_constructor_logical_cost_cap_has_no_invented_receipt_and_foreign_stays_detached() {
        let maximum = crate::workspace_graph::active_builder_limit();
        let expected =
            crate::workspace_graph::checked_usage(usize::MAX, 1, "builder_bytes", maximum)
                .unwrap_err();
        let ((failure, physical), refused, _, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
            measure(|| {
                let mut owner = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
                let selected = owner.sum(usize::MAX, 1).unwrap_err();
                assert_eq!(selected, Setup::Budget { maximum });
                let stop = current_metrics().allocated;
                assert_eq!(owner.sum(0, 0), Err(selected));
                let result = owner.receive_typed(Failure::Setup(selected));
                assert_eq!(current_metrics().allocated, stop);
                result
            })
        });
        assert!(!refused && first.is_none());
        assert!(!physical.invalid && physical.live == 0 && physical.allocated == physical.freed);
        // The allocating legacy adapter remains explicit and outside payment.
        assert_eq!(failure.into_legacy_unfunded(), expected);
        let ((), refused, _, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(LIMIT, || {
            let mut owner = Receiver::acquire().unwrap();
            let (((), physical), refused, used, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(7, || {
                measure(|| {
                    assert_eq!(owner.sum(usize::MAX, 1), Err(Setup::Detached));
                    assert_eq!(owner.limit(), Setup::Detached);
                })
            });
            assert!(!refused && used == 0 && first.is_none());
            assert_eq!(physical.allocated, 0);
            assert_eq!(owner.sum(0, 0), Err(Setup::Detached));
            drop(owner);
        });
        assert!(refused && first.is_some());
    }
    #[test]
    fn typed_constructor_cost_complete_originals_live_callers_and_all_source_inverses_are_bound() {
        use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
        let rows: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(include_str!(concat!(
            env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
            "/docs/evidence/constructor-cost-propagation-825/source-inverses.json"
        )))
        .unwrap();
        let sources = [
            (
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/src/workspace_graph/constructor_failure/receiving/typed.rs"
                )),
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/evidence/constructor-cost-propagation-825/original/00.before"
                )),
            ),
            (
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/borrowed_imports/typed.rs"
                )),
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/evidence/constructor-cost-propagation-825/original/01.before"
                )),
            ),
            (
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/cost.rs"
                )),
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/evidence/constructor-cost-propagation-825/original/02.before"
                )),
            ),
            (
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/borrowed_imports/typed_cost.rs"
                )),
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/evidence/constructor-cost-propagation-825/original/03.before"
                )),
            ),
            (
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/cost/constructor.rs"
                )),
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/evidence/constructor-cost-propagation-825/original/04.before"
                )),
            ),
            (
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/tests/workspace/semantic_graph/source_inventory.rs"
                )),
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/evidence/constructor-cost-propagation-825/original/05.before"
                )),
            ),
            (
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/tests/workspace/semantic_graph.rs"
                )),
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/evidence/constructor-cost-propagation-825/original/06.before"
                )),
            ),
            (
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/CONSTRUCTOR-FAILURE-RECEIVING-V1.md"
                )),
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/evidence/constructor-cost-propagation-825/original/07.before"
                )),
            ),
            (
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/src/workspace_graph/expected_projection.rs"
                )),
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/evidence/constructor-cost-propagation-825/original/08.before"
                )),
            ),
            (
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/declaration_cost.rs"
                )),
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/evidence/constructor-cost-propagation-825/original/09.before"
                )),
            ),
            (
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/borrowed_imports.rs"
                )),
                include_str!(concat!(
                    env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"),
                    "/docs/evidence/constructor-cost-propagation-825/original/10.before"
                )),
            ),
        ];
        let rows = rows.as_array().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(rows.len(), sources.len());
        for (row, (current, original)) in rows.iter().zip(sources) {
            let projected = crate::source_reflows_merge::consumer_input(
                "cost825",
                row["source"].as_str().unwrap(),
                current,
            );
            let current = projected.as_str();
            assert_eq!(
                format!(
                    "{:x}",
                    crate::digest_hex::LowerHex(Sha256::digest(current.as_bytes()))
                ),
                row["current_sha256"].as_str().unwrap()
            );
            assert_eq!(
                format!(
                    "{:x}",
                    crate::digest_hex::LowerHex(Sha256::digest(original.as_bytes()))
                ),
                row["original_sha256"].as_str().unwrap()
            );
            let mut restored = current.to_owned();
            for edit in row["edits"].as_array().unwrap() {
                let new = edit["new"].as_str().unwrap();
                assert_eq!(restored.matches(new).count(), 1);
                restored = restored.replacen(new, edit["before"].as_str().unwrap(), 1);
            }
            assert_eq!(restored, original);
        }
        let live = sources[1].0.split("#[cfg(test)]").next().unwrap();
        for selected in [
            "pay_type(declaration, owner)?",
            "pay_type(entry.declaration, owner)?",
            "pay_signature(original, owner)?",
            "typed_cost::teardown_function(original, true, owner)?",
            "typed_cost::teardown_function(original, false, owner)?",
            "pay_default_teardown(&function.return_type, &type_index, owner)?",
        ] {
            assert!(live.contains(selected));
        }
        assert!(!live.contains("StructuralCost::"));
        let typed = sources[3].0.split("#[cfg(test)]").next().unwrap();
        for forbidden in [
            "Vec<Diagnostic>",
            "reserve_builder_structure(",
            "ok_or_else(refusal)",
            "checked_usage(",
        ] {
            assert!(!typed.contains(forbidden));
        }
    }
}
//! Exact-source imported overlays, including ordinary classified retained
//! wrappers and invariant/class roots. Views confer no import authority.
#[path = "borrowed_imports/alias_clone.rs"]
#[allow(
    clippy::duplicate_mod,
    reason = "Run the same controls against independent receivers."
)]
mod alias_clone;
#[path = "borrowed_imports/receiving.rs"]
#[allow(
    clippy::duplicate_mod,
    reason = "Run the same controls against independent receivers."
)]
mod receiving;
use super::{
    active_builder_limit, ast_function_cost, ast_function_signature_cost,
    ast_type_declaration_cost, budgeted_format, graph_error, import_stub_original as import_stub,
    import_stub_original::default_expr, limit_error, reserve_builder_structure, type_rewrite,
    AuthoredDeclaration, LayoutRegistry, StructuralCost,
};
use crate::ast::program_source::{generated_roots::GeneratedRoots, SyntheticProgramView};
use crate::ast::{
    Expr, ExprKind, Function, ModuleUseKind, Program, Type, TypeDeclaration, TypeDeclarationKind,
};
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
pub(in crate::workspace_graph) use receiving::construct_receiving;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn eligible(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    _programs: &[Program],
) -> bool {
    !program.module_uses.is_empty()
        && program
            .module_uses
            .iter()
            .all(|import| authored.contains_key(import.persistent_id.as_str()))
}
fn refusal() -> Vec<Diagnostic> {
    vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())]
}
fn directory<T>(count: usize) -> Result<Vec<T>, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let bytes = count
        .checked_mul(std::mem::size_of::<T>())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    reserve_builder_structure(bytes)?;
    let values = Vec::with_capacity(count);
    if values.capacity() != count {
        return Err(refusal());
    }
    Ok(values)
}
fn pay_type(declaration: &TypeDeclaration) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
    ast_type_declaration_cost(declaration, &mut cost)?;
    reserve_builder_structure(cost.total)
}
fn pay_signature(function: &Function) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
    ast_function_signature_cost(function, &mut cost)?;
    reserve_builder_structure(cost.total)
}

// A geometric Vec worklist's cumulative requests are below four times its
// element census; its initial nonbyte capacity is four. Raw inline AST cost
// counts every node/header without a clone discount. Charge the full fourfold
// census before cloning each recursive root, plus each worklist's minimum once.
fn pay_teardown(cost: &StructuralCost) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    reserve_builder_structure(cost.total.checked_mul(4).ok_or_else(refusal)?)
}
fn iterative(program: &Program, authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>) -> bool {
    program.module_uses.iter().any(|import| {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        target
            .function
            .is_some_and(|function| !function.type_parameters.is_empty())
            || target.ty.is_some_and(|ty| {
                !ty.invariants().is_empty() || matches!(ty.kind, TypeDeclarationKind::Class { .. })
            })
    })
}
fn pay_teardown_minimum() -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let bytes = [
        std::mem::size_of::<Function>(),
        std::mem::size_of::<Expr>(),
        std::mem::size_of::<Type>(),
        std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::MatchPattern>(),
        std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::RecordMatchPatternField>(),
    ]
    .into_iter()
    .try_fold(0usize, |sum, size| sum.checked_add(size))
    .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    reserve_builder_structure(bytes.checked_mul(4).ok_or_else(refusal)?)
}

// Defaults can expand a shared record type repeatedly. Count that expanded
// expression tree through borrowed declarations, rather than charging only the
// unique type directory. This is storage payment, not default admission; the
// original constructor still chooses forwarding, classifiers and first errors.
fn pay_default_teardown(
    ty: &Type,
    declarations: &[(&str, &TypeDeclaration)],
) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    fn census(
        ty: &Type,
        declarations: &[(&str, &TypeDeclaration)],
    ) -> Result<usize, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
        let mut bytes = std::mem::size_of::<Expr>();
        if let Type::Named { name, arguments } = ty {
            if arguments.is_empty() {
                if let Ok(index) =
                    declarations.binary_search_by_key(&name.as_str(), |(name, _)| *name)
                {
                    let fields: &[crate::ast::FieldDeclaration] = match &declarations[index].1.kind
                    {
                        TypeDeclarationKind::Record { fields }
                        | TypeDeclarationKind::Class { fields, .. } => fields,
                        TypeDeclarationKind::Variant { cases } => {
                            cases.first().map_or(&[], |case| &case.fields)
                        }
                        TypeDeclarationKind::Resource { .. } => &[],
                    };
                    for field in fields {
                        bytes = bytes
                            .checked_add(census(&field.ty, declarations)?)
                            .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
                    }
                }
            } else {
                // Accepted compiler collection defaults have a root call,
                // one scalar size operand and copied type arguments. Generic
                // authored defaults still receive their old closed refusal.
                bytes = bytes
                    .checked_add(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())
                    .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
                let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true);
                super::declaration_cost::ast_type_cost(ty, &mut cost)?;
                bytes = bytes.checked_add(cost.total).ok_or_else(refusal)?;
            }
        } else if *ty == Type::Bytes || *ty == Type::StringMap {
            bytes = bytes
                .checked_add(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())
                .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
        }
        Ok(bytes)
    }
    reserve_builder_structure(
        census(ty, declarations)?
            .checked_mul(4)
            .ok_or_else(refusal)?,
    )
}

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct<'a>(
    program: &'a Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    programs: &[Program],
    registry: &LayoutRegistry<'_>,
) -> Result<SyntheticProgramView<'a>, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
    if !eligible(program, authored, programs) {
        return Err(vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "imported borrowed projection requires the owned route",
        )]);
    }
    let layout = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
    let explicit = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
        .count();
    let type_count = explicit
        .checked_add(layout.entries.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let recursive = iterative(program, authored);
    if recursive {
        pay_teardown_minimum()?;
    }
    let mut roots = if recursive {
        GeneratedRoots::iterative(directory::<TypeDeclaration>(type_count)?, Vec::new())
    } else {
        GeneratedRoots::plain(directory::<TypeDeclaration>(type_count)?, Vec::new())
    };
    let types = &mut roots.types;
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let declaration = target.ty.expect("validated type target");
        pay_type(declaration)?;
        if recursive {
            let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
            ast_type_declaration_cost(declaration, &mut cost)?;
            pay_teardown(&cost)?;
        }
        types.push(alias_clone::type_payload(declaration));
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted type");
        copy.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&import.alias);
        reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
        super::super::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
        )?;
    }
    // Preserve the independent closure's exact order after explicit aliases.
    for entry in &layout.entries {
        pay_type(entry.declaration)?;
        if recursive {
            let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
            ast_type_declaration_cost(entry.declaration, &mut cost)?;
            pay_teardown(&cost)?;
        }
        types.push(alias_clone::type_payload(entry.declaration));
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted private type");
        copy.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&entry.alias);
        reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
        super::super::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            entry.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
        )?;
    }
    let all_count = program
        .types
        .len()
        .checked_add(types.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let mut type_index = directory::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>(all_count)?;
    type_index.extend(
        program
            .types
            .iter()
            .chain(types.iter())
            .map(|declaration| (declaration.name.as_str(), declaration)),
    );
    type_index.sort_unstable_by(|a, b| a.0.cmp(b.0));
    if type_index.windows(2).any(|items| items[0].0 == items[1].0) {
        return Err(vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "synthetic workspace type-name index is not unique",
        )]);
    }
    let needs_main = !program
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
        && !program
            .module_uses
            .iter()
            .any(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function && import.alias == "main");
    let imports = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
        .count();
    roots.functions = directory::<Function>(
        imports
            .checked_add(usize::from(needs_main))
            .ok_or_else(refusal)?,
    )?;
    let functions = &mut roots.functions;
    let mut byte_proofs = import_stub::ByteHelperProofs::new(authored, programs);
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let original = target.function.expect("validated function target");
        let retain_body = programs
            .iter()
            .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
            .is_some_and(|provider| {
                crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper_original(provider, original).is_some()
                    || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper_original(provider, original).is_some()
            });
        if retain_body {
            let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
            ast_function_cost(original, &mut cost)?;
            reserve_builder_structure(cost.total)?;
            pay_teardown(&cost)?;
        } else {
            pay_signature(original)?;
            if recursive {
                let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
                ast_function_signature_cost(original, &mut cost)?;
                pay_teardown(&cost)?;
            }
        }
        let function = if retain_body {
            alias_clone::retained_function(original, &import.alias)
        } else {
            alias_clone::signature_function(original, &import.alias)
        };
        functions.push(function);
        reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
        if retain_body {
            continue;
        }
        let function = functions.last_mut().expect("inserted signature");
        for param in &mut function.params {
            type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
                &mut param.ty,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                Some(&layout),
            )?;
        }
        type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
            &mut function.return_type,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
        )?;
        if recursive {
            pay_default_teardown(&function.return_type, &type_index)?;
        }
        function.body = default_expr(
            function,
            &type_index,
            super::super::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                program, target, authored, programs, &layout,
            ),
            import_stub::byte_helper_authorized(original, target.module, &mut byte_proofs)?,
        )?;
    }
    // Same old rule also recognizes an imported alias named main.
    if needs_main && !functions.iter().any(|function| function.name == "main") {
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())?;
        functions.push(Function {
            stable_id: budgeted_format(format_args!("workspace.synthetic.main.{}", program.module)),
            explicit_id: true,
            name: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone("main"),
            name_span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
            type_parameters: Vec::new(),
            params: Vec::new(),
            return_type: Type::I64,
            effects: Vec::new(),
            yields: None,
            follows: None,
            requires: Vec::new(),
            ensures: Vec::new(),
            body: Expr {
                kind: ExprKind::Int(0),
                span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
            },
            span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
        });
        reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
    }
    drop(type_index);
    // Infallible bounded string helpers record refusal and return empty text.
    // A generated view must never publish that partial construction as success.
    reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
    SyntheticProgramView::with_generated_owner(program, roots).ok_or_else(refusal)
}

#[cfg(test)]
#[path = "borrowed_imports/tests.rs"]
#[allow(
    clippy::duplicate_mod,
    reason = "Run the same controls against independent receivers."
)]
mod tests;
//! Exact alias-aware clones of imported declarations. The original name is
//! never cloned; the alias is paid last, where the old overwrite paid it.
use super::import_stub;
use crate::ast::{Function, TypeDeclaration};

/// Clone every payload but the name. The caller installs the paid alias after
/// directory admission, preserving the original first-refusal ordering.
pub(super) fn type_payload(original: &TypeDeclaration) -> TypeDeclaration {
    let stable_id = original.stable_id.clone();
    let type_parameters = original.type_parameters.clone();
    let kind = original.kind.clone();
    let extends = original.extends.clone();
    let invariants = original.invariants.clone();
    TypeDeclaration {
        stable_id,
        explicit_id: original.explicit_id,
        name: String::new(),
        name_span: original.name_span,
        type_parameters,
        kind,
        extends,
        invariants,
        span: original.span,
    }
}

/// Retained wrapper: a complete function clone with the alias as its name.
pub(super) fn retained_function(original: &Function, alias: &str) -> Function {
    let stable_id = original.stable_id.clone();
    let type_parameters = original.type_parameters.clone();
    let params = original.params.clone();
    let return_type = original.return_type.clone();
    let effects = original.effects.clone();
    let yields = original.yields.clone();
    let follows = original.follows.clone();
    let requires = original.requires.clone();
    let ensures = original.ensures.clone();
    let body = original.body.clone();
    let name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(alias);
    Function {
        stable_id,
        explicit_id: original.explicit_id,
        name,
        name_span: original.name_span,
        type_parameters,
        params,
        return_type,
        effects,
        yields,
        follows,
        requires,
        ensures,
        body,
        span: original.span,
    }
}

/// Ordinary import signature (drops contracts and body) named by the alias.
pub(super) fn signature_function(original: &Function, alias: &str) -> Function {
    import_stub::named_signature(original, alias)
}
use super::super::{
    active_builder_limit, budgeted_format, graph_error, import_stub, limit_error, private_layout,
    type_rewrite, AuthoredDeclaration,
};
use super::{construct, eligible, reserve_builder_structure, LayoutRegistry};
use crate::ast::program_source::ProgramSource;
use crate::ast::{
    Expr, ExprKind, Function, ModuleUse, ModuleUseKind, Program, Type, TypeDeclaration,
};
use crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage_refusal;
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::hir;
use crate::test_allocation_observer::measure;
use crate::workspace_graph::{import_validation, index_authored, index_modules};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
#[path = "tests/initialization.rs"]
mod initialization;
#[path = "tests/old.rs"]
mod old;
const PROVIDER: &str = include_str!("../private_layout/function_provider.spx");
const CALLER: &str = include_str!("../private_layout/function_caller.spx");
const SCALAR_PROVIDER: &str = "module imported.provider; @id(\"helper\") fn helper(value:i64)->i64 requires value>=0 ensures result>=0 {value+1}";
const SCALAR_CALLER: &str = "module imported.app; use function @id(\"helper\") from imported.provider as increment; @id(\"app.main\") fn main()->i64 {increment(41)}";
fn sources(caller: &str, provider: &str) -> [Program; 2] {
    [
        crate::parse(caller, "caller.spx").unwrap(),
        crate::parse(provider, "provider.spx").unwrap(),
    ]
}
// The actual module resolver verifies every fresh caller projection inside
// both linked Vec/Box wrapper scopes. Retained provider wrapper bodies keep
// their reserved std identities, so oracle and view use that same boundary;
// outside it the same projection remains refused.
fn linked<T>(operation: impl FnOnce() -> T) -> T {
    crate::vec_ops::with_authenticated_linked_source(|| {
        crate::box_ops::with_authenticated_linked_source(operation)
    })
}
fn parity(caller: &str, provider: &str) {
    let programs = sources(caller, provider);
    let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let modules = index_modules(&programs).unwrap();
    let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
    import_validation::validate_uses_with_registry(&programs, &modules, &authored, Some(&registry))
        .unwrap();
    assert!(eligible(&programs[0], &authored, &programs));
    let before = old::construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(&registry)).unwrap();
    let view = construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        view.types().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        before.types.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>()
    );
    assert_eq!(
        view.functions().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
        before.functions.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>()
    );
    for (ordinal, function) in programs[0].functions.iter().enumerate() {
        assert!(std::ptr::eq(
            view.functions().get(ordinal).unwrap(),
            function
        ));
        assert!(std::ptr::eq(
            &view.functions().get(ordinal).unwrap().body,
            &function.body
        ));
    }
    for (ordinal, declaration) in programs[0].types.iter().enumerate() {
        assert!(std::ptr::eq(
            view.types().get(ordinal).unwrap(),
            declaration
        ));
    }
    let layout = registry.get(&programs[0], &authored, &programs).unwrap();
    let expected = linked(|| {
        hir::resolve_with_function_reuse(
            &before,
            None,
            None,
            None,
            layout.presentation(&programs[0], &before).unwrap(),
        )
    });
    let actual =
        linked(|| hir::resolve_source(&view, layout.presentation(&programs[0], &view).unwrap()));
    let (expected, _) = expected.unwrap();
    let (actual, _) = actual.unwrap();
    assert_eq!(expected, actual);
    hir::validate(&actual).unwrap();
    let generated_type = view
        .types()
        .get(programs[0].types.len())
        .map(|value| value.stable_id.as_ptr());
    let generated_function = view
        .functions()
        .get(programs[0].functions.len())
        .map(|value| value.stable_id.as_ptr());
    // The genuine cache publication still owns its authored declarations. It
    // moves generated aliases/prototypes, without a second overlay payload copy.
    let key = super::super::borrowed_leaf::Input::Borrowed(view)
        .into_checked_key()
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(key, before);
    assert_eq!(
        crate::format::canonical(&key),
        crate::format::canonical(&before)
    );
    // Graph construction reruns source verification, so it needs the same
    // authenticated linked origin as both full HIR receivers above.
    let (actual_graph, expected_graph) = linked(|| {
        (
            crate::graph::to_json(&key).unwrap(),
            crate::graph::to_json(&before).unwrap(),
        )
    });
    assert_eq!(actual_graph, expected_graph);
    if let Some(pointer) = generated_type {
        assert_eq!(
            key.types[programs[0].types.len()].stable_id.as_ptr(),
            pointer
        );
    }
    if let Some(pointer) = generated_function {
        assert_eq!(
            key.functions[programs[0].functions.len()]
                .stable_id
                .as_ptr(),
            pointer
        );
    }
}
#[test]
fn imports_aliases_private_layouts_defaults_and_cache_publication_keep_full_old_projection() {
    parity(SCALAR_CALLER, SCALAR_PROVIDER);
    for arity in [0, 1, 2, 17] {
        let params = (0..arity)
            .map(|index| format!("value{index}:i64"))
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join(",");
        let args = (0..arity)
            .map(|index| index.to_string())
            .collect::<Vec<_>>()
            .join(",");
        let provider =
            format!("module imported.provider; @id(\"helper\") fn helper({params})->i64 {{42}}");
        let caller = format!("module imported.app; use function @id(\"helper\") from imported.provider as helper; @id(\"app.main\") fn main()->i64 {{helper({args})}}");
        parity(&caller, &provider);
    }
    // A parsed module declares at least one function (SPX-P101). Keep the
    // inert authored declaration in the reserved-main hostile input and the
    // separate admitted caller that exercises generated synthetic main.
    // Retain the original imported-main input as an explicit hostile alias:
    // ordinary import validation reserves main before projection construction.
    let alias_main = sources(
        "module imported.app; use function @id(\"helper\") from imported.provider as main; @id(\"app.inert\") fn inert()->i64 {0}",
        "module imported.provider; @id(\"helper\") fn helper()->i64 {42}",
    );
    let authored = index_authored(&alias_main).unwrap();
    let modules = index_modules(&alias_main).unwrap();
    let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&alias_main, &authored);
    let errors = import_validation::validate_uses_with_registry(
        &alias_main,
        &modules,
        &authored,
        Some(&registry),
    )
    .unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(errors[0].code, "SPX-G172");
    parity(
        &SCALAR_CALLER.replace(
            " @id(\"app.main\") fn main()->i64 {increment(41)}",
            " @id(\"app.inert\") fn inert()->i64 {0}",
        ),
        SCALAR_PROVIDER,
    );
    parity(CALLER, PROVIDER);
    parity(&CALLER.replacen("module function_layout.app;", "module function_layout.app; use type @id(\"report\") from function_layout.provider as ImportedReport;", 1), PROVIDER);
    // Actual authored generic main/instance bodies are borrowed; imported
    // generic prototypes also use their exact ordinary classifier.
    parity(
        &SCALAR_CALLER
            .replace(
                "@id(\"app.main\")",
                "@id(\"identity\") fn identity<T>(value:T)->T {value} @id(\"app.main\")",
            )
            .replace("increment(41)", "identity<i64>(increment(41))"),
        SCALAR_PROVIDER,
    );
}
#[test]
fn duplicate_aliases_identities_and_late_wrong_types_preserve_old_diagnostics() {
    for caller in [
        format!("{SCALAR_CALLER} @id(\"other\") fn increment(value:i64)->i64 {{value}}"),
        format!("{SCALAR_CALLER} @id(\"app.main\") fn duplicate()->i64 {{0}}"),
        SCALAR_CALLER.replace("increment(41)", "increment(true)"),
        CALLER.replace("let report = make();", "let report:Report = make();"),
    ] {
        let provider = if caller.contains("function_layout") {
            PROVIDER
        } else {
            SCALAR_PROVIDER
        };
        let programs = sources(&caller, provider);
        // Project index rejects duplicate persistent IDs independently, before
        // a synthetic view can become lookup authority.
        if let Err(errors) = index_authored(&programs) {
            assert!(errors.iter().any(|error| error.code.starts_with("SPX-G")));
            continue;
        }
        let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
        let before = old::construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(&registry));
        let after = construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry);
        match (before, after) {
            (Ok(before), Ok(view)) => {
                let layout = registry.get(&programs[0], &authored, &programs).unwrap();
                let old = hir::resolve_with_function_reuse(
                    &before,
                    None,
                    None,
                    None,
                    layout.presentation(&programs[0], &before).unwrap(),
                )
                .err()
                .unwrap();
                let new =
                    hir::resolve_source(&view, layout.presentation(&programs[0], &view).unwrap())
                        .err()
                        .unwrap();
                assert_eq!(old, new);
            }
            (Err(before), Err(after)) => assert_eq!(before, after),
            _ => panic!("owned/view constructor verdict differs"),
        };
    }
}
#[test]
fn complete_active_payer_resolution_and_drop_keep_exact_short_and_sticky_refusal() {
    for (caller, provider) in [(SCALAR_CALLER, SCALAR_PROVIDER), (CALLER, PROVIDER)] {
        let programs = sources(caller, provider);
        initialization::complete_original_receiver_before_measurement(&programs);
        let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        let observe = |limit, retain| {
            with_limit_usage_refusal(limit, || {
                measure(|| {
                    let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
                    let result = (|| {
                        // Original complete HIR and cleanup bound remains paid. This
                        // owner changes only the actual source overlay receiver.
                        let costs = super::super::synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_registry(
                            &programs[0],
                            &authored,
                            &programs,
                            None,
                            4,
                            true,
                            Some(&registry),
                        )?;
                        reserve_builder_structure(costs.raw_clone_and_hir)?;
                        let view = construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry)?;
                        let layout = registry.get(&programs[0], &authored, &programs)?;
                        let resolved =
                            hir::resolve_source(&view, layout.presentation(&programs[0], &view)?)?
                                .0;
                        hir::validate(&resolved).map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
                        drop(resolved);
                        if retain {
                            drop(
                                super::super::borrowed_leaf::Input::Borrowed(view)
                                    .into_checked_key()?,
                            );
                        } else {
                            drop(view);
                        }
                        Ok::<(), Vec<Diagnostic>>(())
                    })();
                    if result.is_err() {
                        assert!(reserve_builder_structure(0).is_err());
                    }
                    drop(result);
                    drop(registry);
                })
            })
        };
        for retain in [false, true] {
            let (((), actual), refused, required, _) = observe(usize::MAX, retain);
            assert!(
                !refused && !actual.invalid && actual.live == 0,
                "full imported receiver: {actual:?}; first invalid reason: {:?}",
                crate::test_allocation_observer::invalid_reason()
            );
            assert_eq!(actual.allocated, actual.freed);
            assert!(actual.allocated <= required && actual.highwater <= required);
            let (((), exact), refused, paid, _) = observe(required, retain);
            assert!(
                !refused && !exact.invalid && exact.live == 0 && paid == required,
                "exact imported receiver: {exact:?}; first invalid reason: {:?}",
                crate::test_allocation_observer::invalid_reason()
            );
            let (((), short), refused, _, receipt) = observe(required - 1, retain);
            assert!(
                refused && receipt.is_some() && !short.invalid && short.live == 0,
                "short imported receiver: {short:?}; first invalid reason: {:?}",
                crate::test_allocation_observer::invalid_reason()
            );
        }
    }
}
#[test]
fn plain_overlay_drop_requests_no_new_backing_and_full_route_keeps_old_verdicts() {
    let programs = sources(CALLER, PROVIDER);
    let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
    let view = construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry).unwrap();
    let (_, dropped) = measure(|| drop(view));
    assert!(!dropped.invalid && dropped.allocations == 0 && dropped.reallocations == 0);
    let generic = sources(
        SCALAR_CALLER,
        "module imported.provider; @id(\"helper\") fn helper<T>(value:T)->T {value}",
    );
    let map = index_authored(&generic).unwrap();
    assert!(eligible(&generic[0], &map, &generic));
    let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&generic, &map);
    let before = old::construct(&generic[0], &map, &generic, Some(&registry));
    let after =
        super::super::borrowed_leaf::Input::construct(&generic[0], &map, &generic, &registry, true)
            .and_then(|input| {
                assert!(input.owned().is_none());
                input.into_checked_key()
            });
    assert_eq!(before, after);
    let invariant = sources(SCALAR_CALLER, "module imported.provider; @id(\"row\") record Row { @id(\"row.x\") x:i64, } requires x>=0 @id(\"helper\") fn helper(value:i64)->i64 {value}");
    let map = index_authored(&invariant).unwrap();
    assert!(eligible(&invariant[0], &map, &invariant));
    let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&invariant, &map);
    let before = old::construct(&invariant[0], &map, &invariant, Some(&registry));
    let after = super::super::borrowed_leaf::Input::construct(
        &invariant[0],
        &map,
        &invariant,
        &registry,
        true,
    )
    .and_then(|input| {
        assert!(input.owned().is_none());
        input.into_checked_key()
    });
    assert_eq!(before, after);
}
#[test]
fn literal_full_old_layout_removal_is_observed_without_fitting_discovery_counts() {
    let programs = sources(
        "module imported.app; use function @id(\"helper\") from imported.provider as factory; @id(\"app.inert\") fn inert()->i64 {0}",
        "module imported.provider; @id(\"helper\") fn helper()->i64 {42}",
    );
    let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let observe = |borrow| {
        measure(|| {
            let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
            if borrow {
                drop(construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry).unwrap());
            } else {
                drop(old::construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(&registry)).unwrap());
            }
            drop(registry);
        })
    };
    let ((), before) = observe(false);
    let ((), after) = observe(true);
    // Independently saved old source has one inert authored function (required
    // by SPX-P101), one import and two generated functions. Old Program::clone
    // obtains the exact capacity1 function directory; Vec::push then
    // reallocates to capacity4 for this nonbyte <=1024-byte element and the
    // synthetic main fits. The overlay borrows the authored function and
    // obtains only the exact capacity2 generated directory.
    assert!(
        std::mem::size_of::<Function>() <= 1024
            && std::mem::size_of::<Expr>() <= 1024
            && std::mem::size_of::<Type>() <= 1024
    );
    let strings = "caller.spx".len()
        + "imported.app".len()
        + "helper".len()
        + "imported.provider".len()
        + "factory".len();
    let directories = (1 + 4 - 2) * std::mem::size_of::<Function>();
    // Old clones the inert function's stable id, name and `{0}` Block's boxed
    // leaf tail; empty signature/contract Vecs and the inline i64 own none.
    let inert = "app.inert".len() + "inert".len() + std::mem::size_of::<Expr>();
    let removed = strings + std::mem::size_of::<ModuleUse>()
        + directories
        + inert
        // Saved old Program Drop acquires independent expression/type Vecs,
        // each capacity4 for the three scalar results and three bodies (the
        // inert Block's tail is pushed only after its Block pops). Direct
        // overlay Drop has only leaf bodies/inline scalar types and obtains
        // no scratch.
        + 4 * std::mem::size_of::<Expr>() + 4 * std::mem::size_of::<Type>();
    assert_eq!(before.allocated, after.allocated + removed);
    // Five strings and one ModuleUse directory, three inert payloads, one
    // additional old directory acquisition (capacity1 then its capacity4
    // reallocation versus one exact capacity2) and two Drop scratch Vecs.
    assert_eq!(before.allocations, after.allocations + 5 + 1 + 3 + 1 + 2);
    for observed in [before, after] {
        assert!(!observed.invalid && observed.live == 0);
        assert_eq!(observed.allocated, observed.freed);
    }
}
#[test]
fn literal_imported_directory_replacements_and_authored_payloads_are_removed() {
    use std::fmt::Write;
    let mut caller = String::from("module indexed.app;");
    let mut provider = String::from("module indexed.provider;");
    let mut imported_string_bytes = 0;
    for ordinal in 0..17 {
        let id = format!("row.{ordinal}");
        let alias = format!("Imported{ordinal}");
        imported_string_bytes += id.len() + alias.len() + "indexed.provider".len();
        write!(
            caller,
            " use type @id(\"{id}\") from indexed.provider as {alias};"
        )
        .unwrap();
        write!(
            provider,
            " @id(\"{id}\") record Row{ordinal} {{ @id(\"{id}.x\") x:i64, }}"
        )
        .unwrap();
    }
    caller.push_str(" @id(\"local\") record Local { @id(\"local.x\") x:i64, } @id(\"app.main\") fn main()->i64 {0}");
    // SPX-P101: the provider declares one inert function after its seventeen
    // records. Neither producer clones a provider function for type-only
    // imports, and the shared registry runs identically in both observations,
    // so the independent caller-side accounting below is unchanged.
    provider.push_str(" @id(\"indexed.provider.inert\") fn inert()->i64 {0}");
    let programs = sources(&caller, &provider);
    let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let observe = |borrow| {
        measure(|| {
            let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
            if borrow {
                drop(construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry).unwrap());
            } else {
                drop(old::construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(&registry)).unwrap());
            }
            drop(registry);
        })
    };
    let ((), old) = observe(false);
    let ((), current) = observe(true);
    // The saved old actual producer starts with one cloned authored type,
    // then pushes seventeen imports: requested capacities 1,4,8,16,32.
    // The view keeps the authored type borrowed and acquires capacity17.
    assert!(
        std::mem::size_of::<TypeDeclaration>() <= 1024
            && std::mem::size_of::<Expr>() <= 1024
            && std::mem::size_of::<Type>() <= 1024
    );
    let directories = (1 + 4 + 8 + 16 + 32 - 17) * std::mem::size_of::<TypeDeclaration>();
    let authored = std::mem::size_of::<Function>()
        + std::mem::size_of::<Expr>()
        + std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::FieldDeclaration>()
        + "app.main".len()
        + "main".len()
        + "local".len()
        + "Local".len()
        + "local.x".len()
        + "x".len();
    let discarded_sidecars = 17 * std::mem::size_of::<ModuleUse>()
        + imported_string_bytes
        + "caller.spx".len()
        + "indexed.app".len();
    // Old Program Drop visits eighteen scalar fields and one scalar result,
    // growing its Type scratch through 4,8,16,32. One Block/leaf body obtains
    // expression scratch capacity4. The view's leaf-owned fields allocate none.
    let drop_backing =
        (4 + 8 + 16 + 32) * std::mem::size_of::<Type>() + 4 * std::mem::size_of::<Expr>();
    assert_eq!(
        old.allocated,
        current.allocated + directories + authored + discarded_sidecars + drop_backing
    );
    assert_eq!(old.reallocations, current.reallocations + 7);
    for observed in [old, current] {
        assert!(!observed.invalid && observed.live == 0);
        assert_eq!(observed.allocated, observed.freed);
    }
    // The combined type-name index is one capacity18 allocation in the actual
    // producer. Isolate that same directory receiver for exact/one-short/sticky
    // backing without attributing any fabricated old growth to the source.
    let bytes = 18 * std::mem::size_of::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>();
    for limit in [bytes, bytes - 1] {
        let ((success, metrics), refused, charged, receipt) =
            with_limit_usage_refusal(limit, || {
                measure(|| {
                    let result = super::directory::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>(18);
                    let success = result.is_ok();
                    if !success {
                        assert!(reserve_builder_structure(0).is_err());
                    }
                    drop(result);
                    success
                })
            });
        assert!(!metrics.invalid && metrics.live == 0);
        if limit == bytes {
            assert!(success && !refused && charged == bytes);
            assert_eq!(metrics.allocated, bytes);
        } else {
            assert!(!success && refused && receipt.is_some());
        }
    }
}

#[path = "tests/alias_helpers.rs"]
mod alias_helpers;
#[path = "tests/full.rs"]
mod full;
//! Alias-aware import clone helpers against the complete old clone-then-
//! overwrite sequence, observed through the real allocator and caller ledger.
use super::super::alias_clone;
use super::{import_stub, reserve_builder_structure, SCALAR_PROVIDER};
use crate::ast::{
    Expr, ExprKind, Function, Program, ResourceLifecycleDeclaration, ResourceLifecycleKind,
    SessionProtocolFollowsClause, Type, TypeDeclaration, TypeDeclarationKind,
    TypeParameterDeclaration, VariantCaseDeclaration, YieldsClause,
};
use crate::bounded_output::{
    active_refused, budgeted_clone, with_limit_usage_refusal, ReservationRefusal,
};
use crate::test_allocation_observer::{measure, Metrics};

const RECORD_PROVIDER: &str = r#"module imported.provider; @id("row") record Row { @id("row.x") x:i64, } requires x>=0 @id("helper") fn helper(value:i64)->i64 {value}"#;
const GENERIC_PROVIDER: &str =
    r#"module imported.provider; @id("helper") fn helper<T>(value:T)->T {value}"#;
const ALIAS: &str = "Imported\0\u{2603}Alias";

fn program(source: &str) -> Program {
    crate::parse(source, "provider.spx").unwrap()
}

struct Observed {
    same: bool,
    metrics: Metrics,
    refused: bool,
    used: usize,
    first: Option<ReservationRefusal>,
}

// Build, compare and Drop the complete output inside the observer.
fn observe<T: PartialEq>(limit: usize, expected: &T, build: impl Fn() -> T) -> Observed {
    let ((same, metrics), refused, used, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(limit, || {
        measure(|| {
            let output = build();
            let same = *expected == output;
            if active_refused() {
                assert!(reserve_builder_structure(0).is_err());
            }
            drop(output);
            same
        })
    });
    Observed {
        same,
        metrics,
        refused,
        used,
        first,
    }
}

fn compare<T: PartialEq>(
    label: &str,
    original_name: &str,
    alias: &str,
    expected: &T,
    old: impl Fn() -> T,
    new: impl Fn() -> T,
) {
    assert_ne!(
        alias, original_name,
        "{label}: alias must replace a distinct name"
    );
    let (cloned, refused, alias_debit, _) =
        with_limit_usage_refusal(usize::MAX, || budgeted_clone(alias));
    drop(cloned);
    assert!(
        !refused && alias_debit > 0,
        "{label}: alias debit {alias_debit}"
    );
    let before = observe(usize::MAX, expected, &old);
    let after = observe(usize::MAX, expected, &new);
    for run in [&before, &after] {
        assert!(
            run.same && !run.refused && !run.metrics.invalid && run.metrics.live == 0,
            "{label}: {:?}",
            run.metrics
        );
        assert_eq!(run.metrics.allocated, run.metrics.freed, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(run.used, alias_debit, "{label}: same alias paid debit");
    }
    let saved = original_name.len();
    assert_eq!(
        before.metrics.allocated,
        after.metrics.allocated + saved,
        "{label}: old {:?} new {:?}",
        before.metrics,
        after.metrics
    );
    assert_eq!(before.metrics.freed, after.metrics.freed + saved, "{label}");
    assert_eq!(
        before.metrics.allocations,
        after.metrics.allocations + usize::from(saved != 0),
        "{label}: one allocation per nonempty original name"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        before.metrics.reallocations, after.metrics.reallocations,
        "{label}"
    );
    assert!(
        after.metrics.highwater <= before.metrics.highwater,
        "{label}"
    );
    // Exact, one-short and zero ledgers: the alias debit is the sticky refusal.
    for limit in [alias_debit, alias_debit - 1, 0] {
        let exact = limit >= alias_debit;
        let before = observe(limit, expected, &old);
        let after = observe(limit, expected, &new);
        for run in [&before, &after] {
            assert!(
                !run.metrics.invalid && run.metrics.live == 0,
                "{label}: {limit}"
            );
            assert_eq!(run.refused, !exact, "{label}: limit {limit}");
            assert_eq!(run.first.is_some(), !exact, "{label}: limit {limit}");
            if exact {
                assert!(run.same, "{label}");
                assert_eq!(run.used, alias_debit, "{label}");
            }
        }
        assert_eq!(before.used, after.used, "{label}: limit {limit}");
        assert_eq!(
            before.first, after.first,
            "{label}: exact sticky refusal receipt"
        );
        if let Some(first) = after.first {
            assert_eq!(first.requested, alias_debit, "{label}");
            assert_eq!(first.remaining, limit, "{label}");
        }
    }
}

fn aliased_type(original: &TypeDeclaration, alias: &str) -> TypeDeclaration {
    let mut copy = alias_clone::type_payload(original);
    copy.name = budgeted_clone(alias);
    copy
}

fn hostile_types() -> Vec<(&'static str, TypeDeclaration)> {
    let provider = program(RECORD_PROVIDER);
    let record = provider.types[0].clone();
    let function = provider.functions[0].clone();
    assert!(
        !record.invariants().is_empty(),
        "source record carries an invariant"
    );
    let TypeDeclarationKind::Record { fields } = record.kind.clone() else {
        panic!("record fixture")
    };
    let mut class = record.clone();
    class.stable_id = "class\0\u{2603}id".into();
    class.explicit_id = false;
    class.name = "Cl\0ass\u{1F600}".into();
    class.name_span = record.span;
    class.type_parameters = vec![TypeParameterDeclaration {
        name: "T\0".into(),
        span: record.span,
    }];
    class.kind = TypeDeclarationKind::Class {
        fields: fields.clone(),
        methods: vec![function],
    };
    class.extends = Some(Type::Named {
        name: "Base\0\u{2603}".into(),
        arguments: vec![Type::I64],
    });
    class.invariants = Some(Box::new(vec![Expr {
        kind: ExprKind::Int(7),
        span: record.span,
    }]));
    let mut variant = record.clone();
    variant.stable_id = "variant\0id".into();
    variant.name = "V\0ariant\u{2603}".into();
    variant.invariants = None;
    variant.kind = TypeDeclarationKind::Variant {
        cases: vec![VariantCaseDeclaration {
            stable_id: "case\0".into(),
            explicit_id: false,
            name: "C\u{2603}".into(),
            name_span: record.span,
            fields,
            span: record.span,
        }],
    };
    let mut resource = record.clone();
    resource.name = "Res\0".into();
    resource.invariants = None;
    resource.kind = TypeDeclarationKind::Resource {
        lifecycles: vec![
            ResourceLifecycleDeclaration {
                stable_id: Some("life\0".into()),
                kind: ResourceLifecycleKind::Imported {
                    import_key: "k\0\u{2603}".into(),
                },
                span: record.span,
            },
            ResourceLifecycleDeclaration {
                stable_id: None,
                kind: ResourceLifecycleKind::Trivial,
                span: record.span,
            },
        ],
    };
    let mut empty = record.clone();
    empty.name = String::new();
    vec![
        ("source record with invariant", record),
        ("hostile class", class),
        ("hostile variant", variant),
        ("hostile resource", resource),
        ("empty original name", empty),
    ]
}

fn hostile_functions() -> Vec<(&'static str, Function)> {
    let scalar = program(SCALAR_PROVIDER).functions[0].clone();
    assert!(!scalar.requires.is_empty() && !scalar.ensures.is_empty());
    let generic = program(GENERIC_PROVIDER).functions[0].clone();
    assert!(!generic.type_parameters.is_empty());
    let mut hostile = scalar.clone();
    hostile.stable_id = "fn\0\u{2603}id".into();
    hostile.explicit_id = false;
    hostile.name = "he\0lper\u{1F600}".into();
    hostile.name_span = scalar.span;
    hostile.yields = Some(YieldsClause {
        request_type: Type::Named {
            name: "Req\0☃".into(),
            arguments: vec![Type::I64],
        },
        response_type: Type::Bytes,
        span: scalar.span,
    });
    hostile.follows = Some(Box::new(SessionProtocolFollowsClause {
        protocol_id: "proto\0☃".into(),
        protocol_id_span: scalar.name_span,
        span: scalar.span,
    }));
    hostile.effects = vec!["io\0\u{2603}".into(), String::new()];
    hostile.type_parameters = generic.type_parameters.clone();
    hostile.body = Expr {
        kind: ExprKind::Int(9),
        span: scalar.span,
    };
    let mut empty = scalar.clone();
    empty.name = String::new();
    vec![
        ("source scalar with contracts", scalar),
        ("source generic", generic),
        ("hostile metadata", hostile),
        ("empty original name", empty),
    ]
}

#[test]
fn type_helper_omits_only_the_discarded_original_name_allocation() {
    let cases = hostile_types();
    assert!(cases.iter().any(|(_, original)| !original.name.is_empty()));
    for (label, original) in cases {
        assert_ne!(ALIAS, original.stable_id, "{label}");
        let mut expected = original.clone();
        expected.name = ALIAS.to_string();
        let helper = aliased_type(&original, ALIAS);
        assert_eq!(helper, expected, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.stable_id, original.stable_id, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.explicit_id, original.explicit_id, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.name_span, original.name_span, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.extends, original.extends, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.invariants, original.invariants, "{label}");
        assert_ne!(helper.name, helper.stable_id, "{label}");
        compare(
            label,
            &original.name,
            ALIAS,
            &expected,
            || {
                let mut copy = original.clone();
                copy.name = budgeted_clone(ALIAS);
                copy
            },
            || aliased_type(&original, ALIAS),
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn retained_function_helper_omits_only_the_discarded_original_name_allocation() {
    let cases = hostile_functions();
    assert!(cases.iter().any(|(_, original)| !original.name.is_empty()));
    for (label, original) in cases {
        assert_ne!(ALIAS, original.stable_id, "{label}");
        let mut expected = original.clone();
        expected.name = ALIAS.to_string();
        let helper = alias_clone::retained_function(&original, ALIAS);
        assert_eq!(helper, expected, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.stable_id, original.stable_id, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.explicit_id, original.explicit_id, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.name_span, original.name_span, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.requires, original.requires, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.ensures, original.ensures, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.body, original.body, "{label}");
        compare(
            label,
            &original.name,
            ALIAS,
            &expected,
            || {
                let mut copy = original.clone();
                copy.name = budgeted_clone(ALIAS);
                copy
            },
            || alias_clone::retained_function(&original, ALIAS),
        );
    }
}

#[test]
fn signature_helper_drops_contracts_keeps_metadata_and_omits_original_name_allocation() {
    for (label, original) in hostile_functions() {
        let plain = import_stub::signature(&original);
        assert_eq!(
            plain.name, original.name,
            "{label}: plain signature unchanged"
        );
        let mut expected = plain;
        expected.name = ALIAS.to_string();
        let helper = alias_clone::signature_function(&original, ALIAS);
        assert_eq!(helper, expected, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.stable_id, original.stable_id, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.explicit_id, original.explicit_id, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.name_span, original.name_span, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.type_parameters, original.type_parameters, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.params, original.params, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.return_type, original.return_type, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.effects, original.effects, "{label}");
        assert!(
            helper.yields.is_none() && helper.follows.is_none(),
            "{label}"
        );
        assert!(
            helper.requires.is_empty() && helper.ensures.is_empty(),
            "{label}"
        );
        assert_eq!(helper.body.kind, ExprKind::Int(0), "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.body.span, original.body.span, "{label}");
        assert_eq!(helper.span, original.span, "{label}");
        compare(
            label,
            &original.name,
            ALIAS,
            &expected,
            || {
                let mut copy = import_stub::signature(&original);
                copy.name = budgeted_clone(ALIAS);
                copy
            },
            || alias_clone::signature_function(&original, ALIAS),
        );
    }
}
//! Saved complete owned projection producer, before imported borrowing.
use super::{
    active_builder_limit, budgeted_format, graph_error, import_stub, limit_error, private_layout,
    reserve_builder_structure, type_rewrite, AuthoredDeclaration, BTreeMap, Diagnostic, Expr,
    ExprKind, Function, ModuleUseKind, Program, Type, TypeDeclaration,
};
use crate::ast::Span;
use crate::workspace_graph::expected_projection::import_stub::default_expr_original as default_expr;

pub(super) fn construct(
    program: &Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    programs: &[Program],
    registry: Option<&private_layout::registry::LayoutRegistry<'_>>,
) -> Result<Program, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let fresh;
    let held;
    let layout = if let Some(registry) = registry {
        held = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
        &*held
    } else {
        fresh = private_layout::LayoutClosure::derive(program, authored, programs)?;
        &fresh
    };
    let mut byte_proofs = import_stub::ByteHelperProofs::new(authored, programs);
    let mut synthetic = program.clone();
    synthetic.module_uses.clear();
    // Project validation already authenticated static implementation sidecars.
    // Runtime HIR has no witness or dispatch representation for them.
    synthetic.implementations.clear();
    for module_use in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|item| item.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
    {
        let target = &authored[module_use.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let mut ty = target.ty.expect("validated type target").clone();
        ty.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&module_use.alias);
        crate::workspace_graph::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            &mut ty,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(layout),
        )?;
        synthetic.types.push(ty);
    }
    layout.append_types(&mut synthetic, program, programs)?;
    let type_index_bytes = synthetic
        .types
        .len()
        .checked_mul(std::mem::size_of::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>())
        .ok_or_else(|| vec![limit_error("builder_bytes", active_builder_limit())])?;
    reserve_builder_structure(type_index_bytes)?;
    let mut type_declarations = Vec::with_capacity(synthetic.types.len());
    for declaration in &synthetic.types {
        type_declarations.push((declaration.name.as_str(), declaration));
    }
    type_declarations.sort_by(|left, right| left.0.cmp(right.0));
    if type_declarations
        .windows(2)
        .any(|items| items[0].0 == items[1].0)
    {
        return Err(vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "synthetic workspace type-name index is not unique",
        )]);
    }
    for module_use in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|item| item.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
    {
        let target = &authored[module_use.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let target_function = target.function.expect("validated function target");
        let retain_body = programs
            .iter()
            .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
            .is_some_and(|provider| {
                crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper_original(provider, target_function).is_some()
                    || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper_original(provider, target_function).is_some()
            });
        let mut function = if retain_body {
            target_function.clone()
        } else {
            import_stub::signature(target_function)
        };
        function.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&module_use.alias);
        if retain_body {
            synthetic.functions.push(function);
            continue;
        }
        for param in &mut function.params {
            type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
                &mut param.ty,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                Some(layout),
            )?;
        }
        type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
            &mut function.return_type,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(layout),
        )?;
        // Clearing would retain the cloned contracts' Vec capacities in every
        // finished stub. Replace the vectors so the full imported contracts
        // remain transient, matching the final peak-only pre-bound.
        function.requires = Vec::new();
        function.ensures = Vec::new();
        function.body = default_expr(
            &function,
            &type_declarations,
            crate::workspace_graph::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                program, target, authored, programs, layout,
            ),
            import_stub::byte_helper_authorized(target_function, target.module, &mut byte_proofs)?,
        )?;
        synthetic.functions.push(function);
    }
    if !synthetic
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
    {
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Function>())?;
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())?;
        synthetic.functions.push(Function {
            stable_id: budgeted_format(format_args!(
                "workspace.synthetic.main.{}",
                synthetic.module
            )),
            explicit_id: true,
            name: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone("main"),
            name_span: Span::default(),
            type_parameters: Vec::new(),
            params: Vec::new(),
            return_type: Type::I64,
            effects: Vec::new(),
            yields: None,
            follows: None,
            requires: Vec::new(),
            ensures: Vec::new(),
            body: Expr {
                kind: ExprKind::Int(0),
                span: Span::default(),
            },
            span: Span::default(),
        });
    }
    Ok(synthetic)
}
//! Full overlay acceptance owners; the saved complete producer remains oracle.
use super::super::{construct, eligible, reserve_builder_structure, LayoutRegistry};
use super::{linked, old, parity, sources};
use crate::ast::program_source::ProgramSource;
use crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage_refusal;
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::hir;
use crate::test_allocation_observer::measure;
use crate::workspace_graph::index_authored;

const VEC_PROVIDER: &str = include_str!("../../../../../std/collections/src/collections.spx");
const BOX_PROVIDER: &str = include_str!("../../../../../std/mem/src/mem.spx");
const VEC_CALLER: &str = r#"module overlay.vec;
use function @id("std.collections.vec.with-capacity") from std.collections as with_capacity;
use function @id("std.collections.vec.push") from std.collections as push;
use function @id("std.collections.vec.get") from std.collections as get;
@id("overlay.vec.main") fn main()->i64 { let values=with_capacity<i64>(2usize); let appended=push<i64>(values,42); get<i64>(appended,0usize) }"#;
const BOX_CALLER: &str = r#"module overlay.box;
use function @id("std.mem.box.new") from std.mem as new;
use function @id("std.mem.box.into-inner") from std.mem as into_inner;
@id("overlay.box.main") fn main()->i64 { into_inner<i64>(new<i64>(42)) }"#;
// Complete prior aliases remain independent authenticated refusal corpora.
const VEC_ALIASED_CALLER: &str = r#"module overlay.vec;
use function @id("std.collections.vec.with-capacity") from std.collections as new_vec;
use function @id("std.collections.vec.push") from std.collections as append_vec;
use function @id("std.collections.vec.get") from std.collections as read_vec;
@id("overlay.vec.main") fn main()->i64 { let values=new_vec<i64>(2usize); let appended=append_vec<i64>(values,42); read_vec<i64>(appended,0usize) }"#;
const BOX_ALIASED_CALLER: &str = r#"module overlay.box;
use function @id("std.mem.box.new") from std.mem as new_box;
use function @id("std.mem.box.into-inner") from std.mem as take_box;
@id("overlay.box.main") fn main()->i64 { take_box<i64>(new_box<i64>(42)) }"#;
// Class methods are not module functions; each record/class-only provider also
// declares one inert function so it parses (SPX-P101). Type imports never
// project a provider function, and the original declarations stay verbatim.
const CLASS_PROVIDER: &str = r#"module overlay.class_provider;
@id("overlay.counter") class Counter { @id("overlay.counter.value") value:i64,
@id("overlay.counter.bumped") fn bumped(self:Counter, amount:i64)->Counter { Counter { value:self.value+amount } } }
@id("overlay.class_provider.inert") fn inert()->i64 {0}"#;
const CLASS_CALLER: &str = r#"module overlay.class_app;
use type @id("overlay.counter") from overlay.class_provider as Counter;
@id("overlay.class.main") fn main()->i64 { let counter=Counter {value:41}; let updated=counter.bumped(1); updated.value }"#;
const INVARIANT_PROVIDER: &str = r#"module overlay.invariant_provider;
@id("overlay.row") record Row { @id("overlay.row.x") x:i64, } requires x>=0
@id("overlay.invariant_provider.inert") fn inert()->i64 {0}"#;
const INVARIANT_CALLER: &str = r#"module overlay.invariant_app;
use type @id("overlay.row") from overlay.invariant_provider as Imported;
@id("overlay.invariant.main") fn main()->i64 { let row=Imported{x:41}; let updated=row with {x:42}; updated.x }"#;

// Invariants stay authored in their own module. The imported inert function
// keeps the real provider overlay present without opening forbidden type imports.
const INVARIANT_LOCAL_CALLER: &str = r#"module overlay.invariant_app;
use function @id("overlay.invariant_provider.inert") from overlay.invariant_provider as inert;
@id("overlay.local.row") record Row {@id("overlay.local.row.x") x:i64,} requires x>=0
@id("overlay.invariant.main") fn main()->i64 {let row=Row{x:41};let updated=row with {x:42};inert();updated.x}"#;

fn original_invariant_import_is_still_refused() {
    let programs = sources(INVARIANT_CALLER, INVARIANT_PROVIDER);
    let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let modules = crate::workspace_graph::index_modules(&programs).unwrap();
    let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
    let errors = crate::workspace_graph::import_validation::validate_uses_with_registry(
        &programs,
        &modules,
        &authored,
        Some(&registry),
    )
    .unwrap_err();
    assert_eq!(errors.len(), 1, "{errors:?}");
    assert_eq!(errors[0].code, "SPX-G172");
    assert_eq!(errors[0].path.as_deref(), Some("caller.spx"));
    assert_eq!(
        errors[0].help.as_deref(),
        Some("construct and update the record through functions of its own module")
    );
}

#[test]
fn retained_vec_box_class_and_invariant_sources_match_full_owning_projection() {
    original_invariant_import_is_still_refused();
    for (caller, provider) in [
        (VEC_CALLER, VEC_PROVIDER),
        (BOX_CALLER, BOX_PROVIDER),
        (CLASS_CALLER, CLASS_PROVIDER),
        (INVARIANT_LOCAL_CALLER, INVARIANT_PROVIDER),
    ] {
        parity(caller, provider);
        let programs = sources(caller, provider);
        let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
        let owned = old::construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(&registry)).unwrap();
        let view = construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry).unwrap();
        let layout = registry.get(&programs[0], &authored, &programs).unwrap();
        // Retained provider wrappers keep their reserved std identities. Outside
        // the actual resolver's linked scope this foreign caller projection is
        // still refused before any identity request.
        let expected_refusal = match caller {
            VEC_CALLER => Some("SPX-T283"),
            BOX_CALLER => Some("SPX-T286"),
            _ => None,
        };
        if let Some(code) = expected_refusal {
            // Graph serialization owns its own source verification. The
            // flattened projection never authenticates a reserved wrapper
            // outside the ordinary linked boundary.
            let graph_errors = crate::graph::to_json(&owned).unwrap_err();
            assert!(
                graph_errors.iter().any(|error| error.code == code),
                "{graph_errors:?}"
            );
            for unauthenticated in [
                hir::identity_resolution::resolve(
                    &owned,
                    layout.presentation(&programs[0], &owned).unwrap(),
                    |_| -> Result<(), ()> { panic!("identity request before wrapper refusal") },
                )
                .err()
                .unwrap(),
                hir::identity_resolution::resolve_source(
                    &view,
                    layout.presentation(&programs[0], &view).unwrap(),
                    |_| -> Result<(), ()> { panic!("identity request before wrapper refusal") },
                )
                .err()
                .unwrap(),
            ] {
