                let hir::identity_resolution::Failure::Source(errors) = unauthenticated else {
                    panic!("unauthenticated wrapper refusal remapped")
                };
                assert!(errors.iter().any(|error| error.code == code), "{errors:?}");
            }
        }
        let mut old_requests = Vec::new();
        let old = linked(|| {
            hir::identity_resolution::resolve(
                &owned,
                layout.presentation(&programs[0], &owned).unwrap(),
                |bytes| {
                    old_requests.push(bytes);
                    Ok::<(), ()>(())
                },
            )
        })
        .unwrap();
        let mut new_requests = Vec::new();
        let new = linked(|| {
            hir::identity_resolution::resolve_source(
                &view,
                layout.presentation(&programs[0], &view).unwrap(),
                |bytes| {
                    new_requests.push(bytes);
                    Ok::<(), ()>(())
                },
            )
        })
        .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(old.0, new.0);
        assert_eq!(old.1.reused(), new.1.reused());
        assert_eq!(old.1.costs(), new.1.costs());
        assert_eq!(old_requests, new_requests);
        // No transformed/generated declaration changes authored identity.
        for (ordinal, function) in programs[0].functions.iter().enumerate() {
            assert!(std::ptr::eq(
                view.functions().get(ordinal).unwrap(),
                function
            ));
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn invariant_and_wrapper_invalid_cases_keep_the_entire_old_error_order() {
    let cases = [
        (
            VEC_CALLER.replace(
                "with_capacity<i64>(2usize)",
                "with_capacity<string>(2usize)",
            ),
            VEC_PROVIDER.to_owned(),
        ),
        (
            BOX_CALLER.replace(
                "into_inner<i64>(new<i64>(42))",
                "into_inner<i64>(new<i64>(true))",
            ),
            BOX_PROVIDER.to_owned(),
        ),
        (
            CLASS_CALLER.replace("counter.bumped(1)", "counter.missing(1)"),
            CLASS_PROVIDER.to_owned(),
        ),
        (
            INVARIANT_CALLER.to_owned(),
            INVARIANT_PROVIDER.replace("requires x>=0", "requires missing>=0"),
        ),
        (
            INVARIANT_CALLER.to_owned(),
            INVARIANT_PROVIDER.replace("requires x>=0", "requires x"),
        ),
        (
            INVARIANT_CALLER.to_owned(),
            INVARIANT_PROVIDER
                .replace("x:i64", "x:Bytes")
                .replace("requires x>=0", "requires true"),
        ),
    ];
    for (caller, provider) in cases {
        let programs = sources(&caller, &provider);
        let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
        assert!(eligible(&programs[0], &authored, &programs));
        let before = old::construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(&registry));
        let after = construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry);
        match (before, after) {
            (Err(old), Err(new)) => assert_eq!(old, new),
            (Ok(old), Ok(view)) => {
                let layout = registry.get(&programs[0], &authored, &programs).unwrap();
                // Within the actual linked scope, so each wrapper case reaches
                // its intended type/contract error rather than the reserved-ID
                // refusal shared by both routes outside it.
                let old = linked(|| {
                    hir::resolve_with_function_reuse(
                        &old,
                        None,
                        None,
                        None,
                        layout.presentation(&programs[0], &old).unwrap(),
                    )
                })
                .err()
                .unwrap();
                let new = linked(|| {
                    hir::resolve_source(&view, layout.presentation(&programs[0], &view).unwrap())
                })
                .err()
                .unwrap();
                assert_eq!(old, new);
                assert!(
                    !old.iter()
                        .any(|error| error.code == "SPX-T283" || error.code == "SPX-T286"),
                    "{old:?}"
                );
            }
            _ => panic!("complete old/new constructor verdict differs"),
        };
    }
}

#[test]
fn complete_recursive_owner_receivers_pay_drop_cache_and_short_refusal() {
    original_invariant_import_is_still_refused();
    for (caller, provider) in [
        (VEC_CALLER, VEC_PROVIDER),
        (BOX_CALLER, BOX_PROVIDER),
        (CLASS_CALLER, CLASS_PROVIDER),
        (INVARIANT_LOCAL_CALLER, INVARIANT_PROVIDER),
    ] {
        let programs = sources(caller, provider);
        super::initialization::complete_original_receiver_before_measurement(&programs);
        let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        let observe = |limit, retain| {
            let mut phases = [(
                "unreached",
                crate::test_allocation_observer::Metrics::default(),
                None,
            ); 8];
            let measured = with_limit_usage_refusal(limit, || {
                measure(|| {
                    let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
                    phases[0] = (
                        "registry",
                        crate::test_allocation_observer::current_metrics(),
                        crate::bounded_output::active_remaining(),
                    );
                    let result = (|| {
                        let costs =
                            super::super::super::synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_registry(
                                &programs[0],
                                &authored,
                                &programs,
                                None,
                                4,
                                true,
                                Some(&registry),
                            )?;
                        reserve_builder_structure(costs.raw_clone_and_hir)?;
                        let view = construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry)?;
                        phases[1] = (
                            "construct",
                            crate::test_allocation_observer::current_metrics(),
                            crate::bounded_output::active_remaining(),
                        );
                        let layout = registry.get(&programs[0], &authored, &programs)?;
                        let presentation = layout.presentation(&programs[0], &view)?;
                        phases[2] = (
                            "presentation",
                            crate::test_allocation_observer::current_metrics(),
                            crate::bounded_output::active_remaining(),
                        );
                        let resolved = linked(|| {
                            hir::identity_resolution::resolve_source(&view, presentation, |bytes| {
                                reserve_builder_structure(bytes)
                            })
                        })
                        .map_err(|error| match error {
                            hir::identity_resolution::Failure::Source(errors) => errors,
                            hir::identity_resolution::Failure::Semantic(error) => vec![error],
                            hir::identity_resolution::Failure::Identity(errors) => errors,
                        })?;
                        phases[3] = (
                            "resolution",
                            crate::test_allocation_observer::current_metrics(),
                            crate::bounded_output::active_remaining(),
                        );
                        hir::validate(&resolved.0).map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
                        phases[4] = (
                            "validation",
                            crate::test_allocation_observer::current_metrics(),
                            crate::bounded_output::active_remaining(),
                        );
                        drop(resolved);
                        phases[5] = (
                            "resolved_drop",
                            crate::test_allocation_observer::current_metrics(),
                            crate::bounded_output::active_remaining(),
                        );
                        if retain {
                            drop(
                                super::super::super::borrowed_leaf::Input::Borrowed(view)
                                    .into_checked_key()?,
                            );
                        } else {
                            drop(view);
                        }
                        phases[6] = (
                            "receiving_drop",
                            crate::test_allocation_observer::current_metrics(),
                            crate::bounded_output::active_remaining(),
                        );
                        Ok::<(), Vec<Diagnostic>>(())
                    })();
                    if crate::bounded_output::active_refused() {
                        assert!(result.is_err());
                        assert!(reserve_builder_structure(0).is_err());
                    } else {
                        assert!(
                            result.is_ok(),
                            "authenticated canonical receiver: {result:?}"
                        );
                    }
                    drop(result);
                    drop(registry);
                    phases[7] = (
                        "registry_drop",
                        crate::test_allocation_observer::current_metrics(),
                        crate::bounded_output::active_remaining(),
                    );
                })
            });
            (measured, phases)
        };
        for retain in [false, true] {
            let ((((), observed), refused, required, _), phases) = observe(usize::MAX, retain);
            assert!(!refused && !observed.invalid && observed.live == 0);
            assert_eq!(observed.allocated, observed.freed);
            assert!(
                observed.allocated <= required && observed.highwater <= required,
                "caller={caller} retain={retain} observed={observed:?} required={required} phases={phases:?}"
            );
            let ((((), observed), refused, paid, _), _) = observe(required, retain);
            assert!(!refused && !observed.invalid && observed.live == 0 && paid == required);
            let ((((), observed), refused, _, first), _) = observe(required - 1, retain);
            assert!(refused && first.is_some() && !observed.invalid && observed.live == 0);
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn borrowed_authored_invariant_transform_matches_the_complete_owned_route() {
    let source = r#"module invariant_leaf;
@id("leaf.row") record Row { @id("leaf.row.x") x:i64, } requires x>=0
@id("leaf.main") fn main()->i64 {let mut row=Row{x:41}; row.x=42; row.x}"#;
    let program = crate::parse(source, "invariant-leaf.spx").unwrap();
    let view = super::super::super::borrowed_leaf::construct(&program).unwrap();
    let old = hir::resolve_with_function_reuse(&program, None, None, None, None).unwrap();
    let new = hir::resolve_source(&view, None).unwrap();
    assert_eq!(old.0, new.0);
    assert_eq!(old.1.costs(), new.1.costs());
    assert!(std::ptr::eq(
        view.types().get(0).unwrap(),
        &program.types[0]
    ));
}

#[test]
fn literal_box_wrapper_overlay_removes_real_caller_copy_and_function_growth() {
    let programs = sources(BOX_CALLER, BOX_PROVIDER);
    let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let observe = |borrow| {
        measure(|| {
            let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
            if borrow {
                drop(construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry).unwrap());
            } else {
                drop(old::construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(&registry)).unwrap());
            }
            drop(registry);
        })
    };
    let ((), before) = observe(false);
    let ((), after) = observe(true);
    // Independently saved producer: one authored function directory (capacity1)
    // followed by two imported wrappers grows to capacity4. Generated-only
    // storage acquires capacity2. The authored main is one Block tail containing
    // two one-argument calls, each with one explicit scalar type argument.
    assert!(std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::Function>() <= 1024);
    let directories = 3 * std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::Function>();
    let main_payload = 3 * std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::Expr>()
        + 2 * std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::Type>()
        + "overlay.box.main".len()
        + "main".len()
        + "take_box".len()
        + "new_box".len();
    let sidecars = 2 * std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::ModuleUse>()
        + "std.mem.box.new".len()
        + "std.mem.box.into-inner".len()
        + 2 * "std.mem".len()
        + "new_box".len()
        + "take_box".len()
        + "caller.spx".len()
        + "overlay.box".len();
    // Both iterative receivers obtain Expr capacity4. The new receiver has
    // four signature roots plus two wrapper-call type arguments (six Types:
    // capacities4,8). The full old receiver adds the main result and two call
    // arguments (nine Types: capacities4,8,16), so only its real capacity16
    // replacement and one additional realloc are removed.
    let drop_replacement = 16 * std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::Type>();
    assert_eq!(
        before.allocated,
        after.allocated + directories + main_payload + sidecars + drop_replacement
    );
    assert_eq!(before.reallocations, after.reallocations + 2);
    for observed in [before, after] {
        assert!(!observed.invalid && observed.live == 0);
        assert_eq!(observed.allocated, observed.freed);
    }
}

#[test]
fn conservative_class_default_census_keeps_real_owned_record_forwarding() {
    let caller=super::CALLER.replacen("module function_layout.app;",
        "module function_layout.app; use type @id(\"overlay.unused_class\") from function_layout.provider as Unused;",1);
    let provider=format!("{} @id(\"overlay.unused_class\") class Unused {{ @id(\"overlay.unused_class.x\") x:i64, }}",super::PROVIDER);
    parity(&caller, &provider);
    let programs = sources(&caller, &provider);
    let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
    let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
    let view = construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry).unwrap();
    let forward = view
        .functions()
        .iter()
        .find(|function| function.name == "forward")
        .unwrap();
    // Retain the complete original Report<String, Vec<Row>> control. Its
    // provider forwards, but the ordinary import classifier intentionally
    // constructs a checked default for this shape; only its narrower
    // Bytes/usize renewal record grants an owner-forwarding prototype.
    let expected = old::construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(&registry)).unwrap();
    let expected_forward = expected
        .functions
        .iter()
        .find(|f| f.name == "forward")
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(forward.body, expected_forward.body);
    assert!(matches!(
        forward.body.kind,
        crate::ast::ExprKind::ConstructRecord { .. }
    ));
    let provider = programs[1]
        .functions
        .iter()
        .find(|function| function.name == "forward")
        .unwrap();
    assert_forward_owner(provider);

    let renewal_provider = r#"module renewal.provider;
@id("renewal.packet") record Packet {
 @id("renewal.packet.bytes") bytes:Bytes,
 @id("renewal.packet.len") len:usize,
}
@id("renewal.unused") class Unused {@id("renewal.unused.x") x:i64,}
@id("renewal.forward") fn forward(value:own Packet)->Packet {value}
"#;
    let renewal_caller = r#"module renewal.app;
use type @id("renewal.packet") from renewal.provider as Packet;
use type @id("renewal.unused") from renewal.provider as Unused;
use function @id("renewal.forward") from renewal.provider as forward;
@id("renewal.main") fn main()->i64 {
 let packet=Packet{bytes:bytes_zeroed(0usize),len:0usize};
 let renewed=forward(packet);
 i64_from_usize(renewed.len)
}
"#;
    parity(renewal_caller, renewal_provider);
    let renewal = sources(renewal_caller, renewal_provider);
    let authored = index_authored(&renewal).unwrap();
    let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&renewal, &authored);
    let view = construct(&renewal[0], &authored, &renewal, &registry).unwrap();
    let mut forwarded = 0;
    for function in view
        .functions()
        .iter()
        .chain(renewal[1].functions.iter())
        .filter(|f| f.name == "forward")
    {
        assert_forward_owner(function);
        forwarded += 1;
    }
    assert_eq!(forwarded, 2, "both current prototype and original provider");
}

fn assert_forward_owner(function: &crate::ast::Function) {
    let mut tail = &function.body;
    while let crate::ast::ExprKind::Block {
        statements,
        tail: next,
    } = &tail.kind
    {
        assert!(statements.is_empty(), "forwarding tail has statements");
        tail = &**next;
    }
    let [owner] = function.params.as_slice() else {
        panic!("forward has exactly one owning parameter")
    };
    assert_eq!(owner.mode, crate::ast::ParamMode::Own);
    assert!(
        matches!(&tail.kind, crate::ast::ExprKind::Var(name) if *name == owner.name),
        "{:?}",
        tail.kind
    );
}

#[path = "full/receiving.rs"]
mod receiving;

// Aliasing a retained reserved wrapper is a source error even under the linked
// guard. Semantic refusal must not be mistaken for sticky physical refusal.
#[test]
fn complete_arbitrary_aliases_keep_original_source_refusal_without_payer_refusal() {
    for (caller, provider, code) in [
        (VEC_ALIASED_CALLER, VEC_PROVIDER, "SPX-T283"),
        (BOX_ALIASED_CALLER, BOX_PROVIDER, "SPX-T286"),
    ] {
        let programs = sources(caller, provider);
        let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        let ((), refused, _) = crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage(usize::MAX, || {
            let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
            let owned =
                old::construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(&registry)).unwrap();
            let costs = super::super::super::synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_registry(
                &programs[0],
                &authored,
                &programs,
                None,
                4,
                true,
                Some(&registry),
            )
            .unwrap();
            reserve_builder_structure(costs.raw_clone_and_hir).unwrap();
            let view = construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry).unwrap();
            let receiving =
                super::super::construct_receiving(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry)
                    .unwrap();
            let layout = registry.get(&programs[0], &authored, &programs).unwrap();
            let before = linked(|| {
                hir::resolve_with_function_reuse(
                    &owned,
                    None,
                    None,
                    None,
                    layout.presentation(&programs[0], &owned).unwrap(),
                )
            })
            .err()
            .unwrap();
            for current in [&view, &receiving] {
                let after = linked(|| {
                    hir::resolve_source(
                        current,
                        layout.presentation(&programs[0], current).unwrap(),
                    )
                })
                .err()
                .unwrap();
                assert_eq!(before, after);
                assert!(after.iter().any(|error| error.code == code), "{after:?}");
                assert!(!crate::bounded_output::active_refused());
                assert!(reserve_builder_structure(0).is_ok());
                drop(after);
            }
            drop(layout);
            drop((before, receiving, view, owned, registry));
        });
        assert!(!refused);
    }
}

#[path = "full/transient.rs"]

#[path = "full/publication.rs"]
mod publication;
//! Separate complete original receiver and exact static prelude owner receipt.
//! This does not rewrite measurements or subtract process-lifetime backing.
use super::{linked, old, LayoutRegistry};
use crate::ast::{FieldDeclaration, Program, Type, TypeDeclarationKind};
use crate::test_allocation_observer::{current_metrics, live_bytes_matching, measure};
use crate::workspace_graph::index_authored;

fn string_owns(value: &String, pointer: usize) -> bool {
    value.capacity() != 0 && value.as_ptr() as usize == pointer
}
fn vec_owns<T>(values: &Vec<T>, pointer: usize) -> bool {
    values.capacity() != 0 && values.as_ptr() as usize == pointer
}
fn type_owns(ty: &Type, pointer: usize) -> bool {
    match ty {
        Type::Named { name, arguments } => {
            string_owns(name, pointer)
                || vec_owns(arguments, pointer)
                || arguments.iter().any(|ty| type_owns(ty, pointer))
        }
        Type::Function { parameters, result } => {
            vec_owns(parameters, pointer)
                || result.as_ref() as *const Type as usize == pointer
                || parameters.iter().any(|ty| type_owns(ty, pointer))
                || type_owns(result, pointer)
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}
fn fields_own(fields: &Vec<FieldDeclaration>, pointer: usize) -> bool {
    vec_owns(fields, pointer)
        || fields.iter().any(|field| {
            string_owns(&field.stable_id, pointer)
                || string_owns(&field.name, pointer)
                || type_owns(&field.ty, pointer)
        })
}
fn prelude_owns(pointer: usize) -> bool {
    // Resolution has already requested this exact OnceLock owner. Do not call
    // it before the complete original receiver or initialize a proxy schema.
    let declarations = crate::prelude::declarations();
    declarations.as_ptr() as usize == pointer
        || declarations.iter().any(|declaration| {
            string_owns(&declaration.stable_id, pointer)
                || string_owns(&declaration.name, pointer)
                || vec_owns(&declaration.type_parameters, pointer)
                || declaration
                    .type_parameters
                    .iter()
                    .any(|parameter| string_owns(&parameter.name, pointer))
                || match &declaration.kind {
                    TypeDeclarationKind::Record { fields } => fields_own(fields, pointer),
                    TypeDeclarationKind::Variant { cases } => {
                        vec_owns(cases, pointer)
                            || cases.iter().any(|case| {
                                string_owns(&case.stable_id, pointer)
                                    || string_owns(&case.name, pointer)
                                    || fields_own(&case.fields, pointer)
                            })
                    }
                    TypeDeclarationKind::Resource { .. } | TypeDeclarationKind::Class { .. } => {
                        false
                    }
                }
        })
}

/// First measure the entire independent saved source constructor, ordinary HIR
/// receiver, validation and Drop. Prove every survivor belongs to the compiler
/// prelude static; repeat the complete original receiver and require zero live.
/// The caller's subsequent complete new receiver measurement stays untouched.
pub(super) fn complete_original_receiver_before_measurement(programs: &[Program]) {
    let authored = index_authored(programs).unwrap();
    let run = || {
        let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(programs, &authored);
        let owned = old::construct(&programs[0], &authored, programs, Some(&registry)).unwrap();
        let layout = registry.get(&programs[0], &authored, programs).unwrap();
        let resolved = linked(|| {
            crate::hir::resolve_with_function_reuse(
                &owned,
                None,
                None,
                None,
                layout.presentation(&programs[0], &owned).unwrap(),
            )
        })
        .unwrap();
        crate::hir::validate(&resolved.0).unwrap();
        drop(resolved);
        drop(owned);
        drop(layout);
        drop(registry);
    };
    let ((receipt, attributed), first) = measure(|| {
        run();
        // Tripwires run before the observer registry is borrowed. The pointer
        // predicate itself must never allocate, including a panic payload.
        for declaration in crate::prelude::declarations() {
            assert!(
                declaration.extends.is_none() && declaration.invariants.is_none(),
                "prelude owner census must include new payloads"
            );
            assert!(
                matches!(
                    declaration.kind,
                    TypeDeclarationKind::Record { .. } | TypeDeclarationKind::Variant { .. }
                ),
                "prelude owner census must include new declaration kind"
            );
        }
        (current_metrics().live, live_bytes_matching(prelude_owns))
    });
    assert!(
        !first.invalid,
        "complete original initialization receipt: {first:?}"
    );
    assert_eq!(receipt, first.live);
    assert_eq!(
        first.allocated,
        first.freed.checked_add(first.live).unwrap()
    );
    assert_eq!(
        first.live, attributed,
        "unowned survivor after full original Drop"
    );
    // Preserve the exact frozen-source cold receipt seen in selector 22. Warm
    // test processes may have initialized this immutable process owner earlier.
    if first.live != 0 && cfg!(target_pointer_width = "64") {
        assert_eq!(
            first.live, 6420,
            "changed prelude cold initialization receipt"
        );
    }
    let (_, repeated) = measure(run);
    assert!(
        !repeated.invalid && repeated.live == 0,
        "complete repeated original: {repeated:?}"
    );
    assert_eq!(repeated.allocated, repeated.freed);
}

#[test]
fn complete_original_cold_prelude_pointer_receipt_and_repeated_drop_are_independent() {
    let programs = super::sources(super::SCALAR_CALLER, super::SCALAR_PROVIDER);
    complete_original_receiver_before_measurement(&programs);
}
//! Complete original import walk with real receiving directory ownership.
//! Ordinary and warm views retain additional spare storage, deliberately.
use super::{
    alias_clone, ast_function_cost, ast_function_signature_cost, ast_type_declaration_cost,
    budgeted_format, default_expr, directory, eligible, graph_error, import_stub, iterative,
    pay_default_teardown, pay_signature, pay_teardown, pay_teardown_minimum, pay_type, refusal,
    reserve_builder_structure, type_rewrite, AuthoredDeclaration, LayoutRegistry, StructuralCost,
};
use crate::ast::program_source::SyntheticProgramView;
use crate::ast::{Expr, ExprKind, Function, ModuleUseKind, Program, Type, TypeDeclaration};
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct_receiving<'a>(
    program: &'a Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    programs: &[Program],
    registry: &LayoutRegistry<'_>,
) -> Result<SyntheticProgramView<'a>, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    // Unbounded compatibility callers retain the complete old constructor.
    if crate::bounded_output::active_limit().is_none() {
        return super::construct(program, authored, programs, registry);
    }
    reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
    if !eligible(program, authored, programs) {
        return Err(vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "imported borrowed projection requires the owned route",
        )]);
    }
    let layout = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
    let explicit = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
        .count();
    let type_count = explicit
        .checked_add(layout.entries.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let recursive = iterative(program, authored);
    if recursive {
        pay_teardown_minimum()?;
    }
    let type_values =
        crate::bounded_output::key_directory::KeyDirectory::allocate(&program.types, type_count)
            .map_err(|()| refusal())?;
    let mut roots =
        crate::ast::program_source::receiving_roots::ReceivingRoots::new(type_values, recursive);
    let types = roots.types.as_mut().expect("type directory acquired");
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let declaration = target.ty.expect("validated type target");
        pay_type(declaration)?;
        if recursive {
            let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
            ast_type_declaration_cost(declaration, &mut cost)?;
            pay_teardown(&cost)?;
        }
        types
            .push(alias_clone::type_payload(declaration))
            .map_err(|()| refusal())?;
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted type");
        copy.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&import.alias);
        super::super::super::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
        )?;
    }
    // Preserve the independent closure's exact order after explicit aliases.
    for entry in &layout.entries {
        pay_type(entry.declaration)?;
        if recursive {
            let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
            ast_type_declaration_cost(entry.declaration, &mut cost)?;
            pay_teardown(&cost)?;
        }
        types
            .push(alias_clone::type_payload(entry.declaration))
            .map_err(|()| refusal())?;
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted private type");
        copy.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&entry.alias);
        super::super::super::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            entry.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
        )?;
    }
    let all_count = program
        .types
        .len()
        .checked_add(types.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let mut type_index = directory::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>(all_count)?;
    type_index.extend(
        program
            .types
            .iter()
            .chain(types.iter())
            .map(|declaration| (declaration.name.as_str(), declaration)),
    );
    type_index.sort_unstable_by(|a, b| a.0.cmp(b.0));
    if type_index.windows(2).any(|items| items[0].0 == items[1].0) {
        return Err(vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "synthetic workspace type-name index is not unique",
        )]);
    }
    let needs_main = !program
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
        && !program
            .module_uses
            .iter()
            .any(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function && import.alias == "main");
    let imports = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
        .count();
    // Keep this physical frontier after the original type-name verdict.
    let generated_count = imports
        .checked_add(usize::from(needs_main))
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let function_values = crate::bounded_output::key_directory::KeyDirectory::allocate(
        &program.functions,
        generated_count,
    )
    .map_err(|()| refusal())?;
    roots.functions = Some(function_values);
    let functions = roots
        .functions
        .as_mut()
        .expect("function directory acquired");
    let mut byte_proofs = import_stub::ByteHelperProofs::new(authored, programs);
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let original = target.function.expect("validated function target");
        let retain_body = programs
            .iter()
            .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
            .is_some_and(|provider| {
                crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper(provider, original).is_some()
                    || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper(provider, original).is_some()
            });
        if retain_body {
            let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
            ast_function_cost(original, &mut cost)?;
            reserve_builder_structure(cost.total)?;
            pay_teardown(&cost)?;
        } else {
            pay_signature(original)?;
            if recursive {
                let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
                ast_function_signature_cost(original, &mut cost)?;
                pay_teardown(&cost)?;
            }
        }
        let function = if retain_body {
            alias_clone::retained_function(original, &import.alias)
        } else {
            alias_clone::signature_function(original, &import.alias)
        };
        functions.push(function).map_err(|()| refusal())?;
        if retain_body {
            continue;
        }
        let function = functions.last_mut().expect("inserted signature");
        for param in &mut function.params {
            type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
                &mut param.ty,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                Some(&layout),
            )?;
        }
        type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
            &mut function.return_type,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
        )?;
        if recursive {
            pay_default_teardown(&function.return_type, &type_index)?;
        }
        function.body = default_expr(
            function,
            &type_index,
            super::super::super::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                program, target, authored, programs, &layout,
            ),
            import_stub::byte_helper_authorized(original, target.module, &mut byte_proofs)?,
        )?;
    }
    // Same old rule also recognizes an imported alias named main.
    if needs_main && !functions.iter().any(|function| function.name == "main") {
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())?;
        functions
            .push(Function {
                stable_id: budgeted_format(format_args!(
                    "workspace.synthetic.main.{}",
                    program.module
                )),
                explicit_id: true,
                name: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone("main"),
                name_span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
                type_parameters: Vec::new(),
                params: Vec::new(),
                return_type: Type::I64,
                effects: Vec::new(),
                yields: None,
                follows: None,
                requires: Vec::new(),
                ensures: Vec::new(),
                body: Expr {
                    kind: ExprKind::Int(0),
                    span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
                },
                span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
            })
            .map_err(|()| refusal())?;
    }
    drop(type_index);
    SyntheticProgramView::with_receiving_owner(program, roots).ok_or_else(refusal)
}
//! Actual full constructor/receiving/Drop; old standalone receiver is retained.
use super::super::initialization;
use super::{
    construct, linked, old, reserve_builder_structure, sources, LayoutRegistry, BOX_CALLER,
    BOX_PROVIDER, CLASS_CALLER, CLASS_PROVIDER, INVARIANT_CALLER, INVARIANT_PROVIDER, VEC_CALLER,
    VEC_PROVIDER,
};
use crate::ast::program_source::ProgramSource;
use crate::bounded_output::{active_limit, active_remaining, with_limit_usage_refusal};
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::hir;
use crate::test_allocation_observer::{current_metrics, measure, Metrics};
use crate::workspace_graph::expected_projection::borrowed_imports::construct_receiving;
use crate::workspace_graph::expected_projection::borrowed_leaf::Input;
use crate::workspace_graph::index_authored;

// Allocation-free phase snapshots: label, metrics, paid ledger bytes.
type Snap = Option<(&'static str, Metrics, usize)>;
fn snap(slots: &mut [Snap; 9], index: usize, label: &'static str) {
    let paid = match (active_limit(), active_remaining()) {
        (Some(limit), Some(remaining)) => {
            limit.checked_sub(remaining).expect("valid active ledger")
        }
        _ => panic!("phase requires an active ledger"),
    };
    slots[index] = Some((label, current_metrics(), paid));
}

#[test]
fn full_receiving_overlay_moves_exact_paid_directories_and_preserves_original_order() {
    for (caller, provider) in [
        (VEC_CALLER, VEC_PROVIDER),
        (BOX_CALLER, BOX_PROVIDER),
        (CLASS_CALLER, CLASS_PROVIDER),
        (INVARIANT_CALLER, INVARIANT_PROVIDER),
    ] {
        let programs = sources(caller, provider);
        let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
        let old = old::construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(&registry)).unwrap();
        let ((), refused, _) = crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage(usize::MAX, || {
            let costs = super::super::super::super::synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_registry(
                &programs[0],
                &authored,
                &programs,
                None,
                4,
                true,
                Some(&registry),
            )
            .unwrap();
            reserve_builder_structure(costs.raw_clone_and_hir).unwrap();
            let view = construct_receiving(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry).unwrap();
            assert_eq!(view.functions().len(), old.functions.len());
            assert_eq!(view.types().len(), old.types.len());
            assert_eq!(
                view.functions().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
                old.functions.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>()
            );
            assert_eq!(
                view.types().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
                old.types.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>()
            );
            let key = Input::Borrowed(view).into_receiving_key().unwrap();
            assert_eq!(key.functions, old.functions);
            assert_eq!(key.types, old.types);
            assert_eq!(key.path, old.path);
            assert_eq!(key.module, old.module);
            assert_eq!(key.permits, old.permits);
            assert_eq!(key.interfaces, old.interfaces);
            assert_eq!(key.protocols, old.protocols);
            assert_eq!(key.session_protocols, old.session_protocols);
            assert_eq!(key.agents, old.agents);
            assert_eq!(key.module_uses, old.module_uses);
            assert_eq!(key.implementations, old.implementations);
        });
        assert!(!refused);
    }
}

#[test]
fn full_standalone_and_receiving_overlay_construction_retention_and_drop_tradeoffs() {
    for (caller, provider) in [
        (VEC_CALLER, VEC_PROVIDER),
        (BOX_CALLER, BOX_PROVIDER),
        (CLASS_CALLER, CLASS_PROVIDER),
        (INVARIANT_CALLER, INVARIANT_PROVIDER),
    ] {
        let programs = sources(caller, provider);
        let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        // Exact pointer-attributed prelude owner control, before measurements.
        initialization::complete_original_receiver_before_measurement(&programs);
        let observe = |limit, current, retain| {
            with_limit_usage_refusal(limit, || {
                measure(|| {
                    let mut snaps: [Snap; 9] = [None; 9];
                    let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
                    let result = (|| {
                        let costs = super::super::super::super::synthetic_builder_bytes_scoped_with_registry(
                    &programs[0], &authored, &programs, None, 4, true, Some(&registry),
                )?;
                        snap(&mut snaps, 0, "costs-before-reserve");
                        reserve_builder_structure(costs.raw_clone_and_hir)?;
                        let view = if current {
                            construct_receiving(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry)?
                        } else {
                            construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry)?
                        };
                        snap(&mut snaps, 1, "constructor");
                        let layout = registry.get(&programs[0], &authored, &programs)?;
                        let presentation = layout.presentation(&programs[0], &view)?;
                        snap(&mut snaps, 2, "presentation");
                        let resolved = linked(|| {
                            hir::identity_resolution::resolve_source(&view, presentation, |bytes| {
                                reserve_builder_structure(bytes)
                            })
                        })
                        .map_err(|error| match error {
                            hir::identity_resolution::Failure::Source(errors) => errors,
                            hir::identity_resolution::Failure::Semantic(error) => vec![error],
                            hir::identity_resolution::Failure::Identity(errors) => errors,
                        })?;
                        snap(&mut snaps, 3, "resolution");
                        hir::validate(&resolved.0).map_err(|error| vec![error])?;
                        snap(&mut snaps, 4, "validation");
                        drop(resolved);
                        snap(&mut snaps, 5, "resolved-drop");
                        if retain {
                            let input = Input::Borrowed(view);
                            drop(if current {
                                input.into_receiving_key()?
                            } else {
                                input.into_checked_key()?
                            });
                        } else {
                            drop(view);
                        }
                        snap(&mut snaps, 6, "key-or-view-drop");
                        Ok::<(), Vec<Diagnostic>>(())
                    })();
                    if crate::bounded_output::active_refused() {
                        assert!(result.is_err());
                        assert!(reserve_builder_structure(0).is_err());
                    }
                    assert!(result.is_ok() || crate::bounded_output::active_refused());
                    drop(result);
                    snap(&mut snaps, 7, "result-drop");
                    drop(registry);
                    snap(&mut snaps, 8, "registry-drop");
                    snaps
                })
            })
        };
        for retain in [false, true] {
            let mut observations = Vec::new();
            for current in [false, true] {
                let ((whole_snaps, whole), refused, required, _) =
                    observe(usize::MAX, current, retain);
                assert!(!refused && !whole.invalid && whole.live == 0,
                    "current={current} retain={retain} refused={refused} required={required} whole={whole:?} phases={whole_snaps:?}");
                assert_eq!(whole.allocated, whole.freed, "phases={whole_snaps:?}");
                assert!(whole.allocated <= required && whole.highwater <= required,
                    "current={current} retain={retain} required={required} whole={whole:?} phases={whole_snaps:?}");
                // Snapshots were taken inside the observer; report only now.
                assert!(
                    whole_snaps.iter().all(|slot| slot.is_some()),
                    "missing phase snapshot: {whole_snaps:?}"
                );
                assert!(
                    whole_snaps
                        .windows(2)
                        .all(|pair| pair[0].unwrap().2 <= pair[1].unwrap().2),
                    "paid ledger decreased across phases: {whole_snaps:?}"
                );
                assert!(
                    whole_snaps[8].unwrap().2 <= required,
                    "paid beyond required {required}: {whole_snaps:?}"
                );
                eprintln!("phase snapshots retain={retain} current={current}: {whole_snaps:?}");
                let ((exact_snaps, exact), refused, used, _) = observe(required, current, retain);
                assert!(!refused && !exact.invalid && exact.live == 0 && used == required,
                    "current={current} retain={retain} refused={refused} used={used} required={required} exact={exact:?} phases={exact_snaps:?}");
                let ((short_snaps, short), refused, _, first) =
                    observe(required - 1, current, retain);
                assert!(refused && first.is_some() && !short.invalid && short.live == 0,
                    "current={current} retain={retain} refused={refused} first={first:?} short={short:?} phases={short_snaps:?}");
                observations.push((whole, required));
            }
            // Report both complete paths; no presumed savings or counter discount.
            eprintln!(
                "cache directory tradeoff retain={retain}: old={:?} current={:?}",
                observations[0], observations[1]
            );
        }
    }
}
//! Real non-retaining production input, with the complete receiving route intact.
use super::super::super::LayoutRegistry;
use super::{
    linked, old, sources, BOX_CALLER, BOX_PROVIDER, CLASS_CALLER, CLASS_PROVIDER, INVARIANT_CALLER,
    INVARIANT_PROVIDER, VEC_CALLER, VEC_PROVIDER,
};
use crate::ast::program_source::ProgramSource;
use crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage_refusal;
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::hir;
use crate::test_allocation_observer::measure;
use crate::test_allocation_observer::Metrics;
use crate::workspace_graph::expected_projection::borrowed_leaf::Input;
use crate::workspace_graph::{index_authored, reserve_builder_structure};

fn observe(
    programs: &[crate::ast::Program],
    authored: &std::collections::BTreeMap<&str, super::super::AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    registry: &LayoutRegistry<'_>,
    limit: usize,
    transient: bool,
) -> (bool, Metrics, bool, usize, bool) {
    let ((ok, metrics), refused, used, first) = with_limit_usage_refusal(limit, || {
        measure(|| {
            let input = if transient {
                Input::construct_transient(&programs[0], authored, programs, registry)
            } else {
                Input::construct_receiving(&programs[0], authored, programs, registry, true)
            };
            let ok = input.is_ok();
            if !ok {
                assert!(reserve_builder_structure(0).is_err());
            }
            drop(input);
            ok
        })
    });
    (ok, metrics, refused, used, first.is_some())
}

#[test]
fn actual_transient_import_constructor_removes_only_authored_cache_spare_and_pays_drop() {
    for (caller, provider) in [
        (VEC_CALLER, VEC_PROVIDER),
        (BOX_CALLER, BOX_PROVIDER),
        (CLASS_CALLER, CLASS_PROVIDER),
        (INVARIANT_CALLER, INVARIANT_PROVIDER),
    ] {
        let programs = sources(caller, provider);
        let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
        drop(registry.get(&programs[0], &authored, &programs).unwrap());
        let (ok, current, refused, paid, first) =
            observe(&programs, &authored, &registry, usize::MAX, true);
        let (old_ok, old, old_refused, old_paid, old_first) =
            observe(&programs, &authored, &registry, usize::MAX, false);
        assert!(ok && old_ok && !refused && !old_refused && !first && !old_first);
        let removed = programs[0].functions.len() * std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::Function>()
            + programs[0].types.len() * std::mem::size_of::<crate::ast::TypeDeclaration>();
        assert_eq!(
            old.allocated, current.allocated + removed,
            "module={} expected_authored_spare_slots=(functions:{}, types:{}) expected_removed_bytes={removed}; current_route=construct_transient current={current:?} current_paid={paid}; old_route=construct_receiving old={old:?} old_paid={old_paid}",
            programs[0].module, programs[0].functions.len(), programs[0].types.len()
        );
        let generated =
            Input::construct_transient(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry).unwrap();
        let view = generated.borrowed().unwrap();
        let removed_allocations = usize::from(
            view.functions().len() == programs[0].functions.len()
                && !programs[0].functions.is_empty(),
        ) + usize::from(
            view.types().len() == programs[0].types.len() && !programs[0].types.is_empty(),
        );
        assert_eq!(old.allocations, current.allocations + removed_allocations);
        drop(generated);
        assert!(paid < old_paid);
        for (actual, charged) in [(current, paid), (old, old_paid)] {
            assert!(!actual.invalid && actual.live == 0 && actual.allocated == actual.freed);
            assert!(actual.allocated <= charged && actual.highwater <= charged);
        }
        let (ok, actual, refused, used, first) =
            observe(&programs, &authored, &registry, paid, true);
        assert!(ok && !refused && !first && used == paid && !actual.invalid && actual.live == 0);
        for limit in [0, paid - 1] {
            let (ok, actual, refused, used, first) =
                observe(&programs, &authored, &registry, limit, true);
            assert!(
                !ok && refused
                    && first
                    && used <= limit
                    && !actual.invalid
                    && actual.live == 0
                    && actual.allocated == actual.freed,
                "module={} limit={limit} paid={paid} used={used} ok={ok} refused={refused} first={first} actual={actual:?}",
                programs[0].module
            );
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn actual_transient_full_projection_and_resolution_match_complete_old_receivers() {
    for (caller, provider) in [
        (VEC_CALLER, VEC_PROVIDER),
        (BOX_CALLER, BOX_PROVIDER),
        (CLASS_CALLER, CLASS_PROVIDER),
        (INVARIANT_CALLER, INVARIANT_PROVIDER),
    ] {
        let programs = sources(caller, provider);
        let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
        let original = old::construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, Some(&registry)).unwrap();
        let input =
            Input::construct_transient(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry).unwrap();
        let view = input.borrowed().unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            view.functions().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            original.functions.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>()
        );
        assert_eq!(
            view.types().iter().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            original.types.iter().collect::<Vec<_>>()
        );
        for (ordinal, function) in programs[0].functions.iter().enumerate() {
            assert!(std::ptr::eq(
                view.functions().get(ordinal).unwrap(),
                function
            ));
        }
        let layout = registry.get(&programs[0], &authored, &programs).unwrap();
        let current =
            linked(|| hir::resolve_source(view, layout.presentation(&programs[0], view).unwrap()));
        let expected = linked(|| {
            hir::resolve_with_function_reuse(
                &original,
                None,
                None,
                None,
                layout.presentation(&programs[0], &original).unwrap(),
            )
        });
        match (current, expected) {
            (Ok((current, _)), Ok((expected, _))) => {
                assert_eq!(current, expected);
                let a = hir::validate(&current);
                let b = hir::validate(&expected);
                assert_eq!(
                    a.as_ref().err().map(Diagnostic::json),
                    b.as_ref().err().map(Diagnostic::json)
                );
            }
            (Err(current), Err(expected)) => assert_eq!(
                current.iter().map(Diagnostic::json).collect::<Vec<_>>(),
                expected.iter().map(Diagnostic::json).collect::<Vec<_>>()
            ),
            _ => panic!("full receiver mismatch"),
        }
        drop(layout);
        // Test-only publication of a transient input retains its full old fallback.
        assert_eq!(input.into_checked_key().unwrap(), original);
    }
}
//! Complete original transient constructor and late-refusal publication controls.
use super::super::super::{construct, LayoutRegistry};
use super::{sources, BOX_CALLER, BOX_PROVIDER, VEC_CALLER, VEC_PROVIDER};
use crate::ast::program_source::ProgramSource;
use crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage_refusal;
use crate::test_allocation_observer::measure;
use crate::workspace_graph::index_authored;
#[path = "original_construct.rs"]
#[allow(
    clippy::duplicate_mod,
    reason = "Run the same controls against independent receivers."
)]
mod original_construct;

#[test]
fn last_alias_and_synthetic_main_refusal_never_publish_partial_generated_roots() {
    for (caller, provider) in [(VEC_CALLER, VEC_PROVIDER), (BOX_CALLER, BOX_PROVIDER)] {
        for synthetic_main in [false, true] {
            let caller = if synthetic_main {
                caller.replace("fn main()", "fn inert()")
            } else {
                caller.to_owned()
            };
            let programs = sources(&caller, provider);
            let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
            let registry = LayoutRegistry::new(&programs, &authored);
            drop(registry.get(&programs[0], &authored, &programs).unwrap());
            // Warm ordinary immutable tables before the complete observation.
            drop(construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry).unwrap());
            let observe = |limit, before| {
                with_limit_usage_refusal(limit, || {
                    measure(|| {
                        let result = if before {
                            original_construct::construct(
                                &programs[0],
                                &authored,
                                &programs,
                                &registry,
                            )
                        } else {
                            construct(&programs[0], &authored, &programs, &registry)
                        };
                        let outcome = match &result {
                            Ok(view) => (
                                true,
                                programs[0].module_uses.iter().all(|import| {
                                    if import.kind != crate::ast::ModuleUseKind::Function {
                                        return true;
                                    }
                                    view.functions().iter().any(|function| {
                                        function.stable_id == import.persistent_id
                                            && function.name == import.alias
                                    })
                                }) && (!synthetic_main
                                    || view
                                        .functions()
                                        .iter()
                                        .any(|function| function.name == "main")),
                                false,
                            ),
                            Err(errors) => (
                                false,
                                false,
                                errors.len() == 1 && errors[0].code == "SPX-G171",
                            ),
                        };
                        drop(result);
                        outcome
                    })
                })
            };
            let ((wide, metrics), refused, paid, first) = observe(usize::MAX, false);
            assert_eq!(wide, (true, true, false));
            assert!(
                !refused && first.is_none() && paid > 0 && !metrics.invalid && metrics.live == 0
            );
            assert_eq!(metrics.allocated, metrics.freed);
            let ((old_wide, old_metrics), old_refused, old_paid, old_first) =
                observe(usize::MAX, true);
            assert_eq!(old_wide, wide);
            assert!(!old_refused && old_first.is_none());
            assert_eq!(old_paid, paid);
            assert_eq!(old_metrics, metrics);
            let ((exact, exact_metrics), refused, used, first) = observe(paid, false);
            assert_eq!(exact, wide);
            assert!(!refused && first.is_none() && used == paid);
            assert_eq!(exact_metrics, metrics);
            let ((old_short, old_metrics), refused, used, first) = observe(paid - 1, true);
            // Preserve the full original defective receiver as a counterexample.
            assert_eq!(old_short, (true, false, false));
            assert!(refused && first.is_some() && used < paid);
            assert!(!old_metrics.invalid && old_metrics.live == 0);
            assert_eq!(old_metrics.allocated, old_metrics.freed);
            for limit in [paid - 1, 0] {
                let ((outcome, metrics), refused, used, first) = observe(limit, false);
                assert_eq!(outcome, (false, false, true));
                assert!(refused && first.is_some() && used <= limit);
                assert!(!metrics.invalid && metrics.live == 0);
                assert_eq!(metrics.allocated, metrics.freed);
                let remaining = first.unwrap();
                assert!(remaining.requested > remaining.remaining);
            }
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn full_original_constructor_and_only_sticky_publication_changes_stay_source_bound() {
    use sha2::{Digest, Sha256};
    let original =
        ci_str!("../../../../../../docs/evidence/transient-publication/original-construct.rs.txt");
    let saved = crate::workspace_graph::expected_projection::defaults::project_original_receiver(
        "src/workspace_graph/expected_projection/borrowed_imports/tests/full/original_construct.rs",
        ci_str!("original_construct.rs"),
    );
    let saved = saved
        .strip_prefix("use crate::workspace_graph::expected_projection::borrowed_imports::*;\n\n")
        .unwrap();
    assert_eq!(
        saved.replace("crate::workspace_graph::", "super::super::"),
        original
    );
    let current = ci_str!("../../../borrowed_imports.rs");
    let start = current
        .find("pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct<'a>(")
        .unwrap();
    let body = &current[start..current[start..].find("\n#[cfg(test)]").unwrap() + start];
    let mut restored = body.to_owned();
    assert_eq!(
        restored
            .matches("        reserve_builder_structure(0)?;\n")
            .count(),
        4
    );
    restored = restored.replace("        reserve_builder_structure(0)?;\n", "");
    let footer = "    // Infallible bounded string helpers record refusal and return empty text.\n    // A generated view must never publish that partial construction as success.\n    reserve_builder_structure(0)?;\n";
    assert_eq!(restored.matches(footer).count(), 1);
    restored = restored.replace(footer, "");
    assert_eq!(restored, original);
    assert_eq!(
        format!(
            "{:x}",
            crate::digest_hex::LowerHex(Sha256::digest(original.as_bytes()))
        ),
        "051b0ab47e03d9e0fac140f6199e87b1375510f16327f148387fe6414e7c6d10"
    );
}
use super::super::super::super::{
    alias_clone, ast_function_cost, ast_function_signature_cost, ast_type_declaration_cost,
    budgeted_format, directory, eligible, graph_error, iterative, pay_default_teardown,
    pay_signature, pay_teardown, pay_teardown_minimum, pay_type, refusal,
    reserve_builder_structure, type_rewrite, AuthoredDeclaration, BTreeMap, Diagnostic, Expr,
    ExprKind, Function, GeneratedRoots, LayoutRegistry, ModuleUseKind, Program, StructuralCost,
    SyntheticProgramView, Type, TypeDeclaration,
};
use crate::workspace_graph::expected_projection::import_stub::default_expr_original as default_expr;
use crate::workspace_graph::expected_projection::import_stub_original as import_stub;

pub(in crate::workspace_graph) fn construct<'a>(
    program: &'a Program,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    programs: &[Program],
    registry: &LayoutRegistry<'_>,
) -> Result<SyntheticProgramView<'a>, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    reserve_builder_structure(0)?;
    if !eligible(program, authored, programs) {
        return Err(vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "imported borrowed projection requires the owned route",
        )]);
    }
    let layout = registry.get(program, authored, programs)?;
    let explicit = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
        .count();
    let type_count = explicit
        .checked_add(layout.entries.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let recursive = iterative(program, authored);
    if recursive {
        pay_teardown_minimum()?;
    }
    let mut roots = if recursive {
        GeneratedRoots::iterative(directory::<TypeDeclaration>(type_count)?, Vec::new())
    } else {
        GeneratedRoots::plain(directory::<TypeDeclaration>(type_count)?, Vec::new())
    };
    let types = &mut roots.types;
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let declaration = target.ty.expect("validated type target");
        pay_type(declaration)?;
        if recursive {
            let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
            ast_type_declaration_cost(declaration, &mut cost)?;
            pay_teardown(&cost)?;
        }
        types.push(alias_clone::type_payload(declaration));
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted type");
        copy.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&import.alias);
        crate::workspace_graph::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
        )?;
    }
    // Preserve the independent closure's exact order after explicit aliases.
    for entry in &layout.entries {
        pay_type(entry.declaration)?;
        if recursive {
            let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
            ast_type_declaration_cost(entry.declaration, &mut cost)?;
            pay_teardown(&cost)?;
        }
        types.push(alias_clone::type_payload(entry.declaration));
        let copy = types.last_mut().expect("inserted private type");
        copy.name = crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&entry.alias);
        crate::workspace_graph::generic_type_import::rewrite_declaration_with_layout(
            copy,
            entry.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
        )?;
    }
    let all_count = program
        .types
        .len()
        .checked_add(types.len())
        .ok_or_else(refusal)?;
    let mut type_index = directory::<(&str, &TypeDeclaration)>(all_count)?;
    type_index.extend(
        program
            .types
            .iter()
            .chain(types.iter())
            .map(|declaration| (declaration.name.as_str(), declaration)),
    );
    type_index.sort_unstable_by(|a, b| a.0.cmp(b.0));
    if type_index.windows(2).any(|items| items[0].0 == items[1].0) {
        return Err(vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "synthetic workspace type-name index is not unique",
        )]);
    }
    let needs_main = !program
        .functions
        .iter()
        .any(|function| function.name == "main")
        && !program
            .module_uses
            .iter()
            .any(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function && import.alias == "main");
    let imports = program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
        .count();
    roots.functions = directory::<Function>(
        imports
            .checked_add(usize::from(needs_main))
            .ok_or_else(refusal)?,
    )?;
    let functions = &mut roots.functions;
    let mut byte_proofs = import_stub::ByteHelperProofs::new(authored, programs);
    for import in program
        .module_uses
        .iter()
        .filter(|import| import.kind == ModuleUseKind::Function)
    {
        let target = &authored[import.persistent_id.as_str()];
        let original = target.function.expect("validated function target");
        let retain_body = programs
            .iter()
            .find(|provider| provider.module == target.module)
            .is_some_and(|provider| {
                crate::vec_ops::source_wrapper_original(provider, original).is_some()
                    || crate::box_ops::source_wrapper_original(provider, original).is_some()
            });
        if retain_body {
            let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
            ast_function_cost(original, &mut cost)?;
            reserve_builder_structure(cost.total)?;
            pay_teardown(&cost)?;
        } else {
            pay_signature(original)?;
            if recursive {
                let mut cost = StructuralCost::raw_ast(true).with_inline_values(true);
                ast_function_signature_cost(original, &mut cost)?;
                pay_teardown(&cost)?;
            }
        }
        let function = if retain_body {
            alias_clone::retained_function(original, &import.alias)
        } else {
            alias_clone::signature_function(original, &import.alias)
        };
        functions.push(function);
        if retain_body {
            continue;
        }
        let function = functions.last_mut().expect("inserted signature");
        for param in &mut function.params {
            type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
                &mut param.ty,
                target.module,
                program,
                programs,
                Some(&layout),
            )?;
        }
        type_rewrite::rewrite_with_layout(
            &mut function.return_type,
            target.module,
            program,
            programs,
            Some(&layout),
        )?;
        if recursive {
            pay_default_teardown(&function.return_type, &type_index)?;
        }
        function.body = default_expr(
            function,
            &type_index,
            crate::workspace_graph::owned_function_import::admitted_with_layout(
                program, target, authored, programs, &layout,
            ),
            import_stub::byte_helper_authorized(original, target.module, &mut byte_proofs)?,
        )?;
    }
    // Same old rule also recognizes an imported alias named main.
    if needs_main && !functions.iter().any(|function| function.name == "main") {
        reserve_builder_structure(std::mem::size_of::<Expr>())?;
        functions.push(Function {
            stable_id: budgeted_format(format_args!("workspace.synthetic.main.{}", program.module)),
            explicit_id: true,
            name: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone("main"),
            name_span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
            type_parameters: Vec::new(),
            params: Vec::new(),
            return_type: Type::I64,
            effects: Vec::new(),
            yields: None,
            follows: None,
            requires: Vec::new(),
            ensures: Vec::new(),
            body: Expr {
                kind: ExprKind::Int(0),
                span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
            },
            span: crate::ast::Span::default(),
        });
    }
    drop(type_index);
    SyntheticProgramView::with_generated_owner(program, roots).ok_or_else(refusal)
}
//! Actual affine receiving backing; no detachable pointer/capacity ticket.
//! Only this owner can mutate its Vec, and it never lends &mut Vec or a seal.
use super::{active, record_refusal, reserve, Budget};
use std::mem::size_of;
use std::ops::Deref;
use std::rc::Rc;

pub(crate) struct KeyDirectory<'a, T> {
    budget: Rc<Budget>,
    values: Vec<T>,
    authored: &'a [T],
    generated_len: usize,
}
impl<'a, T> KeyDirectory<'a, T> {
    pub(crate) fn allocate(authored: &'a [T], generated_len: usize) -> Result<Self, ()> {
        let budget = active().ok_or(())?;
        let count = authored
            .len()
            .checked_add(generated_len)
            .ok_or_else(|| poison(&budget, usize::MAX))?;
        let layout =
            std::alloc::Layout::array::<T>(count).map_err(|_| poison(&budget, usize::MAX))?;
        let bytes = layout
            .size()
            .checked_add(size_of::<Self>())
            .ok_or_else(|| poison(&budget, usize::MAX))?;
        required(&budget, bytes)?;
        let mut values = Vec::new();
        values
            .try_reserve_exact(count)
            .map_err(|_| poison(&budget, 0))?;
        if values.capacity() != count {
            let excess = values
                .capacity()
                .saturating_sub(count)
                .checked_mul(size_of::<T>())
                .ok_or_else(|| poison(&budget, usize::MAX))?;
            required(&budget, excess)?;
            poison(&budget, 0);
            return Err(());
        }
        Ok(Self {
            budget,
            values,
            authored,
            generated_len,
        })
    }
    fn current(&self) -> Result<(), ()> {
        if !active().is_some_and(|current| Rc::ptr_eq(&current, &self.budget)) {
            poison(&self.budget, 0);
            return Err(());
        }
        required(&self.budget, 0)
    }
    /// No growth, replacement or backing extraction is possible through push.
    pub(crate) fn push(&mut self, value: T) -> Result<(), ()> {
        self.current()?;
        if self.values.len() >= self.generated_len || self.values.len() >= self.values.capacity() {
            poison(&self.budget, 0);
            return Err(());
        }
        self.values.push(value);
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Construction rewrites elements only; no mutable Vec escapes.
    pub(crate) fn last_mut(&mut self) -> Option<&mut T> {
        self.values.last_mut()
    }
    pub(crate) fn validate(&self, authored: &[T]) -> Result<(), ()> {
        self.current()?;
        if !std::ptr::eq(self.authored, authored)
            || self.values.len() != self.generated_len
            || authored.len().checked_add(self.generated_len) != Some(self.values.capacity())
        {
            poison(&self.budget, 0);
            return Err(());
        }
        Ok(())
    }
    /// Terminal transfer used by teardown and the explicit old allocating
    /// receiver. The owner is consumed, so no receipt can authorize another Vec.
    pub(crate) fn into_vec(self) -> Vec<T> {
        self.values
    }
    /// Caller validates both root owners before beginning either payload clone.
    /// Exact spare capacity already belongs to this structurally affine owner.
    pub(crate) fn finish(mut self, authored: &[T]) -> Result<Vec<T>, ()>
    where
        T: Clone,
    {
        self.validate(authored)?;
        self.values.extend_from_slice(self.authored);
        self.values.rotate_left(self.generated_len);
        Ok(self.values)
    }
}
impl<T> Deref for KeyDirectory<'_, T> {
    type Target = [T];
    fn deref(&self) -> &[T] {
        &self.values
    }
}
fn poison(budget: &Budget, requested: usize) {
    record_refusal(budget, requested);
    budget.overflowed.set(true);
}
fn required(budget: &Budget, bytes: usize) -> Result<(), ()> {
    if reserve(Some(budget), bytes) {
        return Ok(());
    }
    poison(budget, bytes);
    Err(())
}
#[cfg(test)]
mod tests;
use super::KeyDirectory;
use crate::bounded_output::{
    active_remaining, reserve_active_required, with_limit, with_limit_usage,
    with_limit_usage_refusal,
};
use crate::test_allocation_observer::measure;
use std::mem::size_of;

#[test]
fn real_directory_zero_exact_short_and_sticky_owners_settle_through_drop() {
    let authored = [1usize, 2];
    let bytes = size_of::<KeyDirectory<'_, usize>>() + 5 * size_of::<usize>();
    for limit in [0, bytes - 1, bytes] {
        let (((), allocation), refused, used, receipt) = with_limit_usage_refusal(limit, || {
            measure(|| {
                let acquired = KeyDirectory::allocate(&authored, 3);
                if limit == bytes {
                    let mut owner = acquired.unwrap();
                    for value in [3, 4, 5] {
                        owner.push(value).unwrap();
                    }
                    let pointer = owner.as_ptr();
                    owner.validate(&authored).unwrap();
                    let values = owner.finish(&authored).unwrap();
                    assert_eq!(values, [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]);
                    assert_eq!(pointer, values.as_ptr());
                    assert_eq!(active_remaining(), Some(0));
                    drop(values);
                } else {
                    assert!(acquired.is_err());
                    assert!(!reserve_active_required(0));
                    assert!(KeyDirectory::allocate(&authored, 3).is_err());
                }
            })
        });
        assert_eq!(refused, limit < bytes);
        assert_eq!(receipt.is_some(), refused);
        assert_eq!(used, if refused { 0 } else { bytes });
        assert!(!allocation.invalid && allocation.live == 0);
        assert_eq!(allocation.allocated, allocation.freed);
        assert!(allocation.allocated <= used && allocation.highwater <= used);
        if refused {
            assert_eq!(allocation.allocations, 0);
        }
    }
}

#[test]
fn paid_directory_rejects_detached_source_and_backing_without_refund() {
    let source = [11usize];
    let other = [11usize];
    for detached in [false, true] {
        let (_, refused, used, receipt) = with_limit_usage_refusal(4096, || {
            let mut owner = KeyDirectory::allocate(&source, 1).unwrap();
            owner.push(12).unwrap();
            let before = active_remaining();
            if detached {
                let (_, child_refused) = with_limit(4096, || {
                    assert!(owner.validate(&source).is_err());
                    assert!(owner.push(13).is_err());
                });
                assert!(!child_refused);
            } else {
                assert!(owner.validate(&other).is_err());
            }
            assert_eq!(before, active_remaining());
            assert!(!reserve_active_required(0));
            assert!(owner.validate(&source).is_err());
            assert!(owner.finish(&source).is_err());
        });
        assert!(refused && receipt.is_some());
        assert_eq!(
            used,
            size_of::<KeyDirectory<'_, usize>>() + 2 * size_of::<usize>()
        );
    }
    // These are actual independent paid owners, not external replacement Vecs.
    // Each terminal transfer consumes its sole owner. Even allocator address
    // reuse afterward cannot reconstruct a seal or attach an arbitrary Vec.
    let (_, refused) = with_limit(4096, || {
        let mut first = KeyDirectory::allocate(&source, 1).unwrap();
        first.push(12).unwrap();
        let old_pointer = first.as_ptr();
        drop(first.into_vec());
        let mut second = KeyDirectory::allocate(&source, 1).unwrap();
        second.push(12).unwrap();
        let new_pointer = second.as_ptr();
        second.validate(&source).unwrap();
        let values = second.finish(&source).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(values, [11, 12]);
        assert_eq!(new_pointer, values.as_ptr());
        let _address_may_be_reused = old_pointer == new_pointer;
        drop(values);
    });
    assert!(!refused);
    let source = include_str!("../key_directory.rs");
    assert!(source.contains("values: Vec<T>"));
    assert!(source.contains("fn validate(&self, authored: &[T])"));
    assert!(source.contains("fn finish(mut self, authored: &[T])"));
    assert!(!source.contains("DerefMut"));
    assert!(source
        .lines()
        .filter(|line| !line.starts_with("//!"))
        .all(|line| !line.contains("&mut Vec")));
}

#[test]
fn generated_count_and_sticky_push_cannot_grow_or_publish_after_refusal() {
    let source = [1usize];
    let (((), allocation), refused, used) = with_limit_usage(4096, || {
        measure(|| {
            let mut owner = KeyDirectory::allocate(&source, 1).unwrap();
            let before = active_remaining();
            owner.push(2).unwrap();
            let pointer = owner.as_ptr();
            assert!(owner.push(3).is_err());
            assert_eq!(&*owner, &[2]);
            assert_eq!(owner.as_ptr(), pointer);
            assert_eq!(active_remaining(), before);
            assert!(owner.finish(&source).is_err());
        })
    });
    assert!(refused && !allocation.invalid && allocation.live == 0);
    assert_eq!(allocation.allocated, allocation.freed);
    assert!(allocation.allocated <= used && allocation.highwater <= used);
}
//! Receiving directories own their actual Vecs through every partial frontier.
use super::generated_roots::GeneratedRoots;
use crate::ast::{Function, TypeDeclaration};
use crate::bounded_output::key_directory::KeyDirectory;

pub(crate) struct ReceivingRoots<'a> {
    pub(crate) types: Option<KeyDirectory<'a, TypeDeclaration>>,
    pub(crate) functions: Option<KeyDirectory<'a, Function>>,
    iterative: bool,
}
impl<'a> ReceivingRoots<'a> {
    pub(crate) fn new(types: KeyDirectory<'a, TypeDeclaration>, iterative: bool) -> Self {
        Self {
            types: Some(types),
            functions: None,
            iterative,
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn types(&self) -> &[TypeDeclaration] {
        self.types.as_deref().unwrap_or(&[])
    }
    pub(crate) fn functions(&self) -> &[Function] {
        self.functions.as_deref().unwrap_or(&[])
    }
    pub(crate) fn into_plain(mut self) -> GeneratedRoots {
        let types = self
            .types
            .take()
            .map_or_else(Vec::new, KeyDirectory::into_vec);
        let functions = self
            .functions
            .take()
            .map_or_else(Vec::new, KeyDirectory::into_vec);
        if self.iterative {
            GeneratedRoots::iterative(types, functions)
        } else {
            GeneratedRoots::plain(types, functions)
        }
    }
}
impl Drop for ReceivingRoots<'_> {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        let types = self
            .types
            .take()
            .map_or_else(Vec::new, KeyDirectory::into_vec);
        let functions = self
            .functions
            .take()
            .map_or_else(Vec::new, KeyDirectory::into_vec);
        // Exactly the original recursive owner and prepaid worklist; no new
        // directory or additional allowance is acquired while dropping.
