        if self.iterative {
            drop(GeneratedRoots::iterative(types, functions));
        } else {
            drop(GeneratedRoots::plain(types, functions));
        }
    }
}

pub(crate) enum GeneratedOwner<'a> {
    Plain(GeneratedRoots),
    Receiving(ReceivingRoots<'a>),
}
impl GeneratedOwner<'_> {
    pub(crate) fn types(&self) -> &[TypeDeclaration] {
        match self {
            Self::Plain(roots) => &roots.types,
            Self::Receiving(roots) => roots.types(),
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn functions(&self) -> &[Function] {
        match self {
            Self::Plain(roots) => &roots.functions,
            Self::Receiving(roots) => roots.functions(),
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn into_plain(self) -> GeneratedRoots {
        match self {
            Self::Plain(roots) => roots,
            Self::Receiving(roots) => roots.into_plain(),
        }
    }
}
//! Generated recursive roots retain the ordinary Program teardown owner.
//! The workspace producer pays that owner's worklist backing before copying
//! roots. Plain scalar/default overlays retain their allocation-free Drop.
use crate::ast::{Function, Program, TypeDeclaration};

pub(crate) struct GeneratedRoots {
    pub(crate) types: Vec<TypeDeclaration>,
    pub(crate) functions: Vec<Function>,
    iterative: bool,
}
impl GeneratedRoots {
    pub(crate) fn plain(types: Vec<TypeDeclaration>, functions: Vec<Function>) -> Self {
        Self {
            types,
            functions,
            iterative: false,
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn iterative(types: Vec<TypeDeclaration>, functions: Vec<Function>) -> Self {
        Self {
            types,
            functions,
            iterative: true,
        }
    }
}
impl Drop for GeneratedRoots {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if !self.iterative {
            return;
        }
        // Empty metadata and sidecars acquire no backing. The exact existing
        // Program destructor takes every recursive root before drop glue runs,
        // on success and on partial construction/refusal alike.
        drop(Program {
            path: String::new(),
            module: String::new(),
            module_uses: Vec::new(),
            permits: Vec::new(),
            types: std::mem::take(&mut self.types),
            interfaces: Vec::new(),
            protocols: Vec::new(),
            implementations: Vec::new(),
            session_protocols: Vec::new(),
            agents: Vec::new(),
            functions: std::mem::take(&mut self.functions),
        });
    }
}
//! Internal compiler input. Borrowed declarations retain authored order and
//! identity; generated overlays are not verification or resolution authority.
use super::{
    AgentDeclaration, ExprKind, Function, InterfaceDeclaration, ModuleUse, Program,
    ProtocolDeclaration, ProtocolImplementation, SessionProtocolDeclaration, Type, TypeDeclaration,
};

mod decl_slices;
pub(crate) mod function_alias;
pub(crate) use decl_slices::{DeclIter, DeclSlices};
pub(crate) use function_alias::presentation::{FunctionCell, FunctionRef, FunctionSlices};
#[cfg(test)]
mod decl_slices_original;

/// A concrete address distinguishes different presentations of the same AST.
/// The byte prevents distinct empty overlays from sharing a ZST identity.
pub(crate) struct OverlayIdentity {
    _nonzero_size: u8,
}
#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
pub(crate) enum Subject<'a> {
    Owned(&'a Program),
    Projected(&'a OverlayIdentity),
}
impl Subject<'_> {
    pub(crate) fn same(self, other: Self) -> bool {
        match (self, other) {
            (Self::Owned(a), Self::Owned(b)) => std::ptr::eq(a, b),
            (Self::Projected(a), Self::Projected(b)) => std::ptr::eq(a, b),
            _ => false,
        }
    }
}
mod sealed {
    pub trait Sealed {}
    impl Sealed for super::Program {}
    impl Sealed for super::SyntheticProgramView<'_> {}
    impl Sealed for super::checked_key::ReadySource<'_> {}
}
/// Only the owned AST and compiler-owned presentation implement this input.
/// A view carries no verdict; every source pass and resolver still runs.
pub(crate) trait ProgramSource: sealed::Sealed {
    fn subject(&self) -> Subject<'_>;
    fn path(&self) -> &str;
    fn module(&self) -> &str;
    fn module_uses(&self) -> &[ModuleUse];
    fn permits(&self) -> &[String];
    fn types(&self) -> DeclSlices<'_, TypeDeclaration>;
    fn interfaces(&self) -> &[InterfaceDeclaration];
    fn protocols(&self) -> &[ProtocolDeclaration];
    fn implementations(&self) -> &[ProtocolImplementation];
    fn session_protocols(&self) -> &[SessionProtocolDeclaration];
    fn agents(&self) -> &[AgentDeclaration];
    fn functions(&self) -> DeclSlices<'_, Function>;
    /// Explicit effective-name presentation during the verifier/resolver
    /// migration. The raw facade is removed only after all receivers move.
    fn function_refs(&self) -> FunctionSlices<'_>;
}
impl ProgramSource for Program {
    fn subject(&self) -> Subject<'_> {
        Subject::Owned(self)
    }
    fn path(&self) -> &str {
        &self.path
    }
    fn module(&self) -> &str {
        &self.module
    }
    fn module_uses(&self) -> &[ModuleUse] {
        &self.module_uses
    }
    fn permits(&self) -> &[String] {
        &self.permits
    }
    fn types(&self) -> DeclSlices<'_, TypeDeclaration> {
        DeclSlices::one(&self.types)
    }
    fn interfaces(&self) -> &[InterfaceDeclaration] {
        &self.interfaces
    }
    fn protocols(&self) -> &[ProtocolDeclaration] {
        &self.protocols
    }
    fn implementations(&self) -> &[ProtocolImplementation] {
        &self.implementations
    }
    fn session_protocols(&self) -> &[SessionProtocolDeclaration] {
        &self.session_protocols
    }
    fn agents(&self) -> &[AgentDeclaration] {
        &self.agents
    }
    fn functions(&self) -> DeclSlices<'_, Function> {
        DeclSlices::one(&self.functions)
    }
    fn function_refs(&self) -> FunctionSlices<'_> {
        FunctionSlices::one(&self.functions)
    }
}

#[path = "program_source/generated_roots.rs"]
pub(crate) mod generated_roots;
use generated_roots::GeneratedRoots;
pub(crate) mod receiving_roots;
use receiving_roots::{GeneratedOwner, ReceivingRoots};

/// A producer-paid generated overlay after the exact authored slices.
/// Authored bodies and declaration backing are never cloned to lend this input.
pub(crate) struct SyntheticProgramView<'a> {
    authored: &'a Program,
    generated: GeneratedOwner<'a>,
    identity: OverlayIdentity,
}
impl<'a> SyntheticProgramView<'a> {
    pub(crate) fn into_overlay_owner(self) -> (&'a Program, GeneratedRoots) {
        (self.authored, self.generated.into_plain())
    }

    pub(crate) fn with_generated_owner(
        authored: &'a Program,
        generated: GeneratedRoots,
    ) -> Option<Self> {
        authored.types.len().checked_add(generated.types.len())?;
        authored
            .functions
            .len()
            .checked_add(generated.functions.len())?;
        Some(Self {
            authored,
            generated: GeneratedOwner::Plain(generated),
            identity: OverlayIdentity { _nonzero_size: 0 },
        })
    }

    pub(crate) fn with_receiving_owner(
        authored: &'a Program,
        generated: ReceivingRoots<'a>,
    ) -> Option<Self> {
        authored.types.len().checked_add(generated.types().len())?;
        authored
            .functions
            .len()
            .checked_add(generated.functions().len())?;
        Some(Self {
            authored,
            generated: GeneratedOwner::Receiving(generated),
            identity: OverlayIdentity { _nonzero_size: 0 },
        })
    }
    pub(crate) fn into_receiving_owner(self) -> (&'a Program, GeneratedOwner<'a>) {
        (self.authored, self.generated)
    }

    pub(crate) fn new(authored: &'a Program, generated_functions: Vec<Function>) -> Option<Self> {
        // Keep the leaf-main constructor closed. Imported prototypes use the
        // separately classified and paid imported-overlay producer.
        if generated_functions.iter().any(|function| {
            function.name != "main"
                || !function.params.is_empty()
                || !function.type_parameters.is_empty()
                || !function.requires.is_empty()
                || !function.ensures.is_empty()
                || function.return_type != Type::I64
                || function.yields.is_some()
                || function.follows.is_some()
                || !function.effects.is_empty()
                || !matches!(function.body.kind, ExprKind::Int(0))
        }) {
            return None;
        }
        authored
            .functions
            .len()
            .checked_add(generated_functions.len())?;
        Some(Self {
            authored,
            generated: GeneratedOwner::Plain(GeneratedRoots::plain(
                Vec::new(),
                generated_functions,
            )),
            identity: OverlayIdentity { _nonzero_size: 0 },
        })
    }
}
impl ProgramSource for SyntheticProgramView<'_> {
    fn subject(&self) -> Subject<'_> {
        Subject::Projected(&self.identity)
    }
    fn path(&self) -> &str {
        &self.authored.path
    }
    fn module(&self) -> &str {
        &self.authored.module
    }
    // Project validation authenticates these sidecars before presentation.
    // This is exactly the old clone-and-clear projection, not a permission.
    fn module_uses(&self) -> &[ModuleUse] {
        &[]
    }
    fn permits(&self) -> &[String] {
        &self.authored.permits
    }
    fn types(&self) -> DeclSlices<'_, TypeDeclaration> {
        DeclSlices::append(&self.authored.types, self.generated.types())
            .expect("constructor checked declaration count")
    }
    fn interfaces(&self) -> &[InterfaceDeclaration] {
        &self.authored.interfaces
    }
    fn protocols(&self) -> &[ProtocolDeclaration] {
        &self.authored.protocols
    }
    fn implementations(&self) -> &[ProtocolImplementation] {
        &[]
    }
    fn session_protocols(&self) -> &[SessionProtocolDeclaration] {
        &self.authored.session_protocols
    }
    fn agents(&self) -> &[AgentDeclaration] {
        &self.authored.agents
    }
    fn functions(&self) -> DeclSlices<'_, Function> {
        DeclSlices::append(&self.authored.functions, self.generated.functions())
            .expect("constructor checked declaration count")
    }
    fn function_refs(&self) -> FunctionSlices<'_> {
        FunctionSlices::append(&self.authored.functions, self.generated.functions())
            .expect("constructor checked declaration count")
    }
}

/// Immutable owning cache source; wire encoding remains the historical Program.
pub(crate) mod checked_key;
//! Compiler-owned key state. Pending ownership cannot be read as a source.
//! Publication follows transfer of the actual current parsed program.
use super::generated_roots::GeneratedRoots;
use super::{DeclSlices, OverlayIdentity, ProgramSource, Subject};
use crate::ast::{
    AgentDeclaration, Function, InterfaceDeclaration, ModuleUse, Program, ProtocolDeclaration,
    ProtocolImplementation, SessionProtocolDeclaration, TypeDeclaration,
};
use crate::workspace_graph::current_source::CurrentParsedSource;
use std::sync::{Arc, OnceLock};

pub(crate) enum CheckedSource {
    Owned(Program),
    Deferred(DeferredSource),
}
pub(crate) struct DeferredSource {
    generated: GeneratedRoots,
    source_slot: usize,
    path_allocation: usize,
    module_allocation: usize,
    owner: OnceLock<Arc<CurrentParsedSource>>,
    identity: OverlayIdentity,
}
pub(crate) enum ReadySource<'a> {
    Owned(&'a Program),
    Shared {
        authored: &'a Program,
        key: &'a DeferredSource,
    },
}
impl CheckedSource {
    /// The caller has acquired the exact replacement backing before passing
    /// these directories. The private workspace slot survives the final move.
    pub(crate) fn deferred(
        program: &Program,
        generated: GeneratedRoots,
        source_slot: usize,
    ) -> Self {
        Self::Deferred(DeferredSource {
            generated,
            source_slot,
            path_allocation: program.path.as_ptr() as usize,
            module_allocation: program.module.as_ptr() as usize,
            owner: OnceLock::new(),
            identity: OverlayIdentity { _nonzero_size: 0 },
        })
    }
    pub(crate) fn ready(&self) -> Option<ReadySource<'_>> {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(program) => Some(ReadySource::Owned(program)),
            Self::Deferred(key) => Some(ReadySource::Shared {
                authored: key.owner.get()?.program(),
                key,
            }),
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn owned(&self) -> Option<&Program> {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(program) => Some(program),
            Self::Deferred(_) => None,
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn needs_current_owner(&self) -> bool {
        matches!(self, Self::Deferred(key) if key.owner.get().is_none())
    }
    /// Only the actual current restored parsed owner can complete this key.
    /// Equal previous source bytes never authorize a different AST allocation.
    pub(crate) fn can_bind(&self, source_slot: usize, owner: &CurrentParsedSource) -> bool {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(_) => true,
            Self::Deferred(key) if key.owner.get().is_some() => false,
            Self::Deferred(key) => {
                let program = owner.program();
                key.source_slot == source_slot
                    && key.path_allocation == program.path.as_ptr() as usize
                    && key.module_allocation == program.module.as_ptr() as usize
                    && match owner.original_ordinals() {
                        Some(positions) => DeclSlices::selected(
                            &program.functions,
                            positions,
                            &key.generated.functions,
                        )
                        .is_some(),
                        None => program
                            .functions
                            .len()
                            .checked_add(key.generated.functions.len())
                            .is_some(),
                    }
                    && program
                        .types
                        .len()
                        .checked_add(key.generated.types.len())
                        .is_some()
            }
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn bind(
        &self,
        source_slot: usize,
        owner: &Arc<CurrentParsedSource>,
    ) -> Result<(), ()> {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(_) => Ok(()),
            Self::Deferred(key) if self.can_bind(source_slot, owner) => {
                key.owner.set(Arc::clone(owner)).map_err(|_| ())
            }
            Self::Deferred(_) => Err(()),
        }
    }
    #[cfg(test)]
    pub(crate) fn materialize(&self) -> Program {
        let ready = self.ready().expect("only completed keys are observed");
        Program {
            path: ready.path().to_owned(),
            module: ready.module().to_owned(),
            module_uses: ready.module_uses().to_vec(),
            permits: ready.permits().to_vec(),
            types: ready.types().iter().cloned().collect(),
            interfaces: ready.interfaces().to_vec(),
            protocols: ready.protocols().to_vec(),
            implementations: ready.implementations().to_vec(),
            session_protocols: ready.session_protocols().to_vec(),
            agents: ready.agents().to_vec(),
            functions: ready.functions().iter().cloned().collect(),
        }
    }
}
impl ProgramSource for ReadySource<'_> {
    fn subject(&self) -> Subject<'_> {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(p) => p.subject(),
            Self::Shared { key, .. } => Subject::Projected(&key.identity),
        }
    }
    fn path(&self) -> &str {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(p) | Self::Shared { authored: p, .. } => &p.path,
        }
    }
    fn module(&self) -> &str {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(p) | Self::Shared { authored: p, .. } => &p.module,
        }
    }
    fn module_uses(&self) -> &[ModuleUse] {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(p) => &p.module_uses,
            Self::Shared { .. } => &[],
        }
    }
    fn permits(&self) -> &[String] {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(p) | Self::Shared { authored: p, .. } => &p.permits,
        }
    }
    fn types(&self) -> DeclSlices<'_, TypeDeclaration> {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(p) => p.types(),
            Self::Shared { authored, key } => {
                DeclSlices::append(&authored.types, &key.generated.types)
                    .expect("completion checked type length")
            }
        }
    }
    fn interfaces(&self) -> &[InterfaceDeclaration] {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(p) | Self::Shared { authored: p, .. } => &p.interfaces,
        }
    }
    fn protocols(&self) -> &[ProtocolDeclaration] {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(p) | Self::Shared { authored: p, .. } => &p.protocols,
        }
    }
    fn implementations(&self) -> &[ProtocolImplementation] {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(p) => &p.implementations,
            Self::Shared { .. } => &[],
        }
    }
    fn session_protocols(&self) -> &[SessionProtocolDeclaration] {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(p) | Self::Shared { authored: p, .. } => &p.session_protocols,
        }
    }
    fn agents(&self) -> &[AgentDeclaration] {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(p) | Self::Shared { authored: p, .. } => &p.agents,
        }
    }
    fn functions(&self) -> DeclSlices<'_, Function> {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(p) => p.functions(),
            Self::Shared { authored, key } => {
                match key.owner.get().expect("completed key").original_ordinals() {
                    Some(positions) => DeclSlices::selected(
                        &authored.functions,
                        positions,
                        &key.generated.functions,
                    )
                    .expect("completion checked ordered selection"),
                    None => DeclSlices::append(&authored.functions, &key.generated.functions)
                        .expect("completion checked function lengths"),
                }
            }
        }
    }
    fn function_refs(&self) -> super::FunctionSlices<'_> {
        match self {
            Self::Owned(p) => p.function_refs(),
            Self::Shared { authored, key } => {
                match key.owner.get().expect("completed key").original_ordinals() {
                    Some(positions) => super::FunctionSlices::selected(
                        &authored.functions,
                        positions,
                        &key.generated.functions,
                    )
                    .expect("completion checked ordered selection"),
                    None => {
                        super::FunctionSlices::append(&authored.functions, &key.generated.functions)
                            .expect("completion checked function lengths")
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
mod encode;
//! Exactly the historical Program wire; an incomplete owner cannot encode.
use super::CheckedSource;
use crate::ast::program_source::ProgramSource;
use crate::ast::Program;
use crate::cache_codec::{Codec, Decoder, Encoder, Result};
fn sequence<'a, T: Codec + 'a>(
    out: &mut Encoder,
    values: impl ExactSizeIterator<Item = &'a T>,
) -> Result<()> {
    out.nested(|out| {
        out.length(values.len())?;
        for value in values {
            value.encode(out)?;
        }
        Ok(())
    })
}
impl Codec for CheckedSource {
    fn encode(&self, out: &mut Encoder) -> Result<()> {
        if let Self::Owned(program) = self {
            return program.encode(out);
        }
        let ready = self
            .ready()
            .ok_or_else(|| crate::cache_codec::grammar("checked source transfer is incomplete"))?;
        out.nested(|out| {
            out.text(ready.path())?;
            out.text(ready.module())?;
            sequence(out, ready.module_uses().iter())?;
            sequence(out, ready.permits().iter())?;
            sequence(out, ready.types().iter())?;
            sequence(out, ready.interfaces().iter())?;
            sequence(out, ready.protocols().iter())?;
            sequence(out, ready.implementations().iter())?;
            sequence(out, ready.agents().iter())?;
            sequence(out, ready.functions().iter())?;
            sequence(out, ready.session_protocols().iter())
        })
    }
    fn decode(input: &mut Decoder<'_>) -> Result<Self> {
        Program::decode(input).map(Self::Owned)
    }
}
//! Ordered borrowed declarations. The projected variant preserves the exact
//! ordinary view/iterator carrier size while lending source-held payloads.
pub(crate) trait DeclarationDirectory<T> {
    fn len(&self) -> usize;
    fn get(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&T>;
    fn profile_get(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&T> {
        self.get(index)
    }
}
pub(crate) enum DeclSlices<'a, T> {
    Ordinary {
        authored: &'a [T],
        indices: Option<&'a [usize]>,
        generated: &'a [T],
    },
    Projected {
        authored: &'a [T],
        directory: &'a dyn DeclarationDirectory<T>,
        start: usize,
    },
}
impl<'a, T> DeclSlices<'a, T> {
    pub(crate) fn one(authored: &'a [T]) -> Self {
        Self::Ordinary {
            authored,
            indices: None,
            generated: &[],
        }
    }
    pub(super) fn append(authored: &'a [T], generated: &'a [T]) -> Option<Self> {
        authored.len().checked_add(generated.len())?;
        Some(Self::Ordinary {
            authored,
            indices: None,
            generated,
        })
    }
    pub(super) fn selected(
        authored: &'a [T],
        indices: &'a [usize],
        generated: &'a [T],
    ) -> Option<Self> {
        if indices.iter().any(|index| *index >= authored.len())
            || indices.windows(2).any(|pair| pair[0] >= pair[1])
        {
            return None;
        }
        indices.len().checked_add(generated.len())?;
        Some(Self::Ordinary {
            authored,
            indices: Some(indices),
            generated,
        })
    }
    pub(super) fn projected(
        authored: &'a [T],
        directory: &'a dyn DeclarationDirectory<T>,
    ) -> Option<Self> {
        authored.len().checked_add(directory.len())?;
        Some(Self::Projected {
            authored,
            directory,
            start: 0,
        })
    }
    fn authored_len(&self) -> usize {
        match self {
            Self::Ordinary {
                authored, indices, ..
            } => indices.map_or(authored.len(), <[usize]>::len),
            Self::Projected { authored, .. } => authored.len(),
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn len(&self) -> usize {
        match self {
            Self::Ordinary { generated, .. } => self.authored_len() + generated.len(),
            Self::Projected {
                directory, start, ..
            } => self.authored_len() + directory.len() - start,
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn first(&self) -> Option<&'a T> {
        self.get(0)
    }
    pub(crate) fn get(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&'a T> {
        match self {
            Self::Ordinary {
                authored,
                indices,
                generated,
            } => {
                let count = indices.map_or(authored.len(), <[usize]>::len);
                if index < count {
                    authored.get(indices.map_or(index, |indices| indices[index]))
                } else {
                    generated.get(index.checked_sub(count)?)
                }
            }
            Self::Projected {
                authored,
                directory,
                start,
            } => {
                if index < authored.len() {
                    authored.get(index)
                } else if index < self.len() {
                    directory.get(start + index - authored.len())
                } else {
                    None
                }
            }
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn suffix(&self, start: usize) -> Option<Self> {
        if start > self.len() {
            return None;
        }
        match self {
            Self::Ordinary {
                authored,
                indices,
                generated,
            } => {
                let count = self.authored_len();
                if start < count {
                    match indices {
                        Some(indices) => Self::selected(authored, &indices[start..], generated),
                        None => Self::append(&authored[start..], generated),
                    }
                } else {
                    Some(Self::one(&generated[start - count..]))
                }
            }
            Self::Projected {
                authored,
                directory,
                start: offset,
            } => {
                if start < authored.len() {
                    Some(Self::Projected {
                        authored: &authored[start..],
                        directory: *directory,
                        start: *offset,
                    })
                } else {
                    Some(Self::Projected {
                        authored: &[],
                        directory: *directory,
                        start: offset + start - authored.len(),
                    })
                }
            }
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn iter(&self) -> DeclIter<'a, T> {
        DeclIter {
            view: match self {
                Self::Ordinary {
                    authored,
                    indices,
                    generated,
                } => Self::Ordinary {
                    authored,
                    indices: *indices,
                    generated,
                },
                Self::Projected {
                    authored,
                    directory,
                    start,
                } => Self::Projected {
                    authored,
                    directory: *directory,
                    start: *start,
                },
            },
            front: 0,
            back: self.len(),
        }
    }
}
impl<T> std::ops::Index<usize> for DeclSlices<'_, T> {
    type Output = T;
    fn index(&self, index: usize) -> &Self::Output {
        self.get(index).expect("declaration index in bounds")
    }
}
pub(crate) struct DeclIter<'a, T> {
    view: DeclSlices<'a, T>,
    front: usize,
    back: usize,
}
impl<'a, T> Iterator for DeclIter<'a, T> {
    type Item = &'a T;
    fn next(&mut self) -> Option<Self::Item> {
        if self.front == self.back {
            return None;
        }
        let next = self.view.get(self.front);
        self.front += 1;
        next
    }
    fn size_hint(&self) -> (usize, Option<usize>) {
        let remaining = self.back - self.front;
        (remaining, Some(remaining))
    }
}
impl<T> DoubleEndedIterator for DeclIter<'_, T> {
    fn next_back(&mut self) -> Option<Self::Item> {
        if self.front == self.back {
            return None;
        }
        self.back -= 1;
        self.view.get(self.back)
    }
}
impl<T> ExactSizeIterator for DeclIter<'_, T> {}
impl<T> std::iter::FusedIterator for DeclIter<'_, T> {}
impl<'a, T> IntoIterator for DeclSlices<'a, T> {
    type Item = &'a T;
    type IntoIter = DeclIter<'a, T>;
    fn into_iter(self) -> Self::IntoIter {
        self.iter()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
#[path = "decl_slices/alias_tests.rs"]
mod alias_tests;
#[cfg(test)]
#[path = "decl_slices/before_alias.rs"]
mod before_alias;

/// Feature-only projection. The complete metadata/HIR directory is unchanged.
/// A checked scalar signature contributes false to every prelude predicate.
/// This has exactly the original iterator's fields and transports its original
/// Option<&Function>; it introduces no row presentation or fallible scan ABI.
pub(crate) struct ProfileIter<'a> {
    view: DeclSlices<'a, crate::ast::Function>,
    front: usize,
    back: usize,
}
impl<'a> DeclSlices<'a, crate::ast::Function> {
    pub(crate) fn profile_iter(&self) -> ProfileIter<'a> {
        ProfileIter {
            view: match self {
                Self::Ordinary {
                    authored,
                    indices,
                    generated,
                } => Self::Ordinary {
                    authored,
                    indices: *indices,
                    generated,
                },
                Self::Projected {
                    authored,
                    directory,
                    start,
                } => Self::Projected {
                    authored,
                    directory: *directory,
                    start: *start,
                },
            },
            front: 0,
            back: self.len(),
        }
    }
}
impl<'a> Iterator for ProfileIter<'a> {
    type Item = &'a crate::ast::Function;
    fn next(&mut self) -> Option<Self::Item> {
        while self.front < self.back {
            // This is the original get() receiver/field-loan boundary, moved
            // into next, with the original output Option. The caller's Some
            // pattern/false-predicate continuation is consumed here for an
            // exact finite scalar; no new row view or index tuple is formed.
            let next = match &self.view {
                DeclSlices::Projected {
                    authored,
                    directory,
                    start,
                } if self.front >= authored.len() => {
                    directory.profile_get(start + self.front - authored.len())
                }
                _ => self.view.get(self.front),
            };
            self.front += 1;
            match next {
                None => continue,
                _ => return next,
            }
        }
        None
    }
    fn size_hint(&self) -> (usize, Option<usize>) {
        (0, Some(self.back - self.front))
    }
}
impl std::iter::FusedIterator for ProfileIter<'_> {}
//! Complete original contiguous receiver, independently compiled.
/// Two contiguous declaration collections; no flattened reference directory.
/// Construction checks their combined length before an iterator is lent.
#[derive(Clone, Copy)]
pub(crate) struct DeclSlices<'a, T> {
    authored: &'a [T],
    generated: &'a [T],
    length: usize,
}
impl<'a, T> DeclSlices<'a, T> {
    pub(crate) fn one(authored: &'a [T]) -> Self {
        Self {
            authored,
            generated: &[],
            length: authored.len(),
        }
    }
    fn append(authored: &'a [T], generated: &'a [T]) -> Option<Self> {
        Some(Self {
            authored,
            generated,
            length: authored.len().checked_add(generated.len())?,
        })
    }
    pub(crate) fn len(&self) -> usize {
        self.length
    }
    pub(crate) fn first(&self) -> Option<&'a T> {
        self.get(0)
    }
    pub(crate) fn get(&self, index: usize) -> Option<&'a T> {
        if index < self.authored.len() {
            self.authored.get(index)
        } else {
            self.generated.get(index.checked_sub(self.authored.len())?)
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn suffix(&self, start: usize) -> Option<Self> {
        if start > self.length {
            return None;
        }
        if start < self.authored.len() {
            Self::append(&self.authored[start..], self.generated)
        } else {
            Some(Self::one(&self.generated[start - self.authored.len()..]))
        }
    }
    pub(crate) fn iter(&self) -> DeclIter<'a, T> {
        DeclIter {
            authored: self.authored.iter(),
            generated: self.generated.iter(),
            remaining: self.length,
        }
    }
}
impl<T> std::ops::Index<usize> for DeclSlices<'_, T> {
    type Output = T;
    fn index(&self, index: usize) -> &Self::Output {
        self.get(index).expect("declaration index in bounds")
    }
}
pub(crate) struct DeclIter<'a, T> {
    authored: std::slice::Iter<'a, T>,
    generated: std::slice::Iter<'a, T>,
    remaining: usize,
}
impl<'a, T> Iterator for DeclIter<'a, T> {
    type Item = &'a T;
    fn next(&mut self) -> Option<Self::Item> {
        let next = self.authored.next().or_else(|| self.generated.next());
        if next.is_some() {
            self.remaining -= 1;
        }
        next
    }
    fn size_hint(&self) -> (usize, Option<usize>) {
        (self.remaining, Some(self.remaining))
    }
}
impl<T> DoubleEndedIterator for DeclIter<'_, T> {
    fn next_back(&mut self) -> Option<Self::Item> {
        let next = self
            .generated
            .next_back()
            .or_else(|| self.authored.next_back());
        if next.is_some() {
            self.remaining -= 1;
        }
        next
    }
}
impl<T> ExactSizeIterator for DeclIter<'_, T> {}
impl<T> std::iter::FusedIterator for DeclIter<'_, T> {}
impl<'a, T> IntoIterator for DeclSlices<'a, T> {
    type Item = &'a T;
    type IntoIter = DeclIter<'a, T>;
    fn into_iter(self) -> Self::IntoIter {
        self.iter()
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::DeclSlices;
    #[test]
    fn complete_contiguous_receiver_keeps_original_forward_reverse_suffix_and_index_order() {
        let authored = ["prefix", "é", "漢字"];
        let generated = ["suffix", "é"];
        let original = DeclSlices::append(&authored, &generated).unwrap();
        let current = super::super::decl_slices::DeclSlices::append(&authored, &generated).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(original.len(), current.len());
        assert_eq!(original.first(), current.first());
        assert!(original.iter().eq(current.iter()));
        assert!(original.iter().rev().eq(current.iter().rev()));
        for index in 0..=original.len() {
            assert_eq!(original.get(index), current.get(index));
            if index < original.len() {
                assert_eq!(original[index], current[index]);
            }
            assert!(original
                .suffix(index)
                .unwrap()
                .iter()
                .eq(current.suffix(index).unwrap().iter()));
        }
        assert!(original.suffix(original.len() + 1).is_none());
        assert!(current.suffix(current.len() + 1).is_none());
        assert!(original.into_iter().eq(current.into_iter()));
        let empty: [usize; 0] = [];
        assert_eq!(
            DeclSlices::one(&empty).len(),
            super::super::decl_slices::DeclSlices::one(&empty).len()
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn selected_authored_ordinals_keep_full_values_and_generated_suffix_without_a_second_directory()
    {
        let authored = ["same-prefix\0α", "removed", "same-prefix\0β", "last"];
        let ordinals = [0, 2, 3];
        let generated = ["generated\0尾"];
        let selected =
            super::super::decl_slices::DeclSlices::selected(&authored, &ordinals, &generated)
                .unwrap();
        let independent = [authored[0], authored[2], authored[3], generated[0]];
        assert!(selected.iter().eq(independent.iter()));
        assert!(selected.iter().rev().eq(independent.iter().rev()));
        for start in 0..=independent.len() {
            assert!(selected
                .suffix(start)
                .unwrap()
                .iter()
                .eq(independent[start..].iter()));
        }
        let mut mixed = selected.iter();
        assert_eq!(mixed.next(), Some(&independent[0]));
        assert_eq!(mixed.next_back(), Some(&independent[3]));
        assert_eq!(mixed.next_back(), Some(&independent[2]));
        assert_eq!(mixed.next(), Some(&independent[1]));
        assert_eq!(mixed.next(), None);
        assert_eq!(mixed.next_back(), None);
        assert_eq!(mixed.len(), 0);
        for invalid in [&[0, 0][..], &[2, 0][..], &[authored.len()][..]] {
            assert!(super::super::decl_slices::DeclSlices::selected(
                &authored, invalid, &generated
            )
            .is_none());
        }
    }
}

//! Cross-file type references introduced by authenticated row-field lowering.
use crate::ast::{Function, ModuleUseKind, Program, TypeDeclaration};
use crate::bounded_output::budgeted_format;
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::hir;
use crate::workspace_graph::{graph_error, push_edge, AuthoredDeclaration, WorkspaceEdge};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;

pub(super) fn collect_edges(
    program: &Program,
    programs: &[Program],
    module_paths: &BTreeMap<&str, &str>,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    edges: &mut Vec<WorkspaceEdge>,
) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let expected = super::super::record_traversal::collect(program, programs)?;
    for region in &expected.loops {
        push(
            program,
            region.function,
            region.declaration,
            &budgeted_format(format_args!("{}.s0.value.type_argument.0", region.path)),
            &budgeted_format(format_args!("{}.s0.value", region.path)),
            module_paths,
            authored,
            edges,
        )?;
    }
    for read in &expected.reads {
        // Retained HIR keys a fused `str_as_bytes(row.text)` type site at its
        // inner field-read call, so the edge names that same expression.
        let expression_path = if read.copy {
            budgeted_format(format_args!("{}.base", read.path))
        } else if read.bytes {
            budgeted_format(format_args!("{}.arg.0", read.path))
        } else {
            crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&read.path)
        };
        let type_path = budgeted_format(format_args!("{expression_path}.type_argument.0"));
        push(
            program,
            read.function,
            read.declaration,
            &type_path,
            &expression_path,
            module_paths,
            authored,
            edges,
        )?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]
fn push(
    program: &Program,
    function: &Function,
    declaration: &TypeDeclaration,
    type_path: &str,
    expression_path: &str,
    module_paths: &BTreeMap<&str, &str>,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
    edges: &mut Vec<WorkspaceEdge>,
) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    // A factory's defining module can use a different local spelling. Only
    // the consumer's explicit persistent type import owns this edge.
    let Some(module_use) = program.module_uses.iter().find(|item| {
        item.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type && item.persistent_id == declaration.stable_id
    }) else {
        return Ok(());
    };
    let target = authored
        .get(module_use.persistent_id.as_str())
        .ok_or_else(|| {
            vec![graph_error(
                "SPX-G173",
                "record traversal imported layout is missing",
            )]
        })?;
    push_edge(
        edges,
        WorkspaceEdge {
            caller_path: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&program.path),
            caller: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&function.stable_id),
            target_path: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(module_paths[target.module]),
            target: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&module_use.persistent_id),
            kind: "type_reference",
            site: "type",
            expression: hir::workspace_expression_identity(
                &hir::DeclarationId::new(&function.stable_id),
                expression_path,
            ),
            ast_path: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(type_path),
            alias: crate::bounded_output::budgeted_clone(&module_use.alias),
            ordinal: edges.len(),
        },
    )
}
//! Independent source-derived synthetic callee replay at exact source paths.
use crate::ast::{Expr, ExprKind, ModuleUseKind, Program, Type};
use crate::bounded_output::budgeted_format;
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::hir;
use crate::workspace_graph::{graph_error, reserve_builder_structure, AuthoredDeclaration};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
pub(super) fn verify(
    program: &Program,
    resolved: &hir::ResolvedProgram,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let fail = || {
        vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "explicit record sort source descriptor disagrees with retained HIR",
        )]
    };
    // A complete path-free census proves absence before constructing source
    // identities. Check HIR independently too: injected synthetic sort calls
    // must still reach the exact source/HIR comparison below.
    let mut source_sort = false;
    for function in &program.functions {
        for expression in function
            .requires
            .iter()
            .chain(std::iter::once(&function.body))
            .chain(&function.ensures)
        {
            source_sort |= contains_sort(expression);
        }
    }
    let resolved_sort = resolved.functions.iter().any(|function| {
        function
            .requires
            .iter()
            .chain(std::iter::once(&function.body))
            .chain(&function.ensures)
            .any(contains_resolved_sort)
    }) || resolved.function_templates.iter().any(|function| {
        function
            .requires
            .iter()
            .chain(std::iter::once(&function.body))
            .chain(&function.ensures)
            .any(contains_resolved_sort)
    });
    if !source_sort && !resolved_sort {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let mut expected = Vec::new();
    for function in &program.functions {
        let owner = hir::DeclarationId::new(&function.stable_id);
        for (root, expression) in function
            .requires
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .map(|(i, e)| (budgeted_format(format_args!("requires.{i}")), e))
            .chain(std::iter::once((String::from("body"), &function.body)))
            .chain(
                function
                    .ensures
                    .iter()
                    .enumerate()
                    .map(|(i, e)| (budgeted_format(format_args!("ensures.{i}")), e)),
            )
        {
            super::super::source_callables::visit_ast_call_expressions(
                expression,
                &root,
                &mut |expr, path| {
                    let ExprKind::Call {
                        name,
                        type_arguments,
                        args,
                    } = &expr.kind
                    else {
                        unreachable!()
                    };
                    if name != crate::vec_ops::sort_keys::NAME {
                        return Ok(());
                    }
                    let [Type::Named { name, arguments }] = type_arguments.as_slice() else {
                        return Err(fail());
                    };
                    if !arguments.is_empty() {
                        return Err(fail());
                    }
                    let [_, literal] = args.as_slice() else {
                        return Err(fail());
                    };
                    let ExprKind::String(keys) = &literal.kind else {
                        return Err(fail());
                    };
                    let local = program.types.iter().find(|ty| ty.name == *name);
                    let declaration = local
                        .or_else(|| {
                            program
                                .module_uses
                                .iter()
                                .find(|item| {
                                    item.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type && item.alias == *name
                                })
                                .and_then(|item| authored.get(item.persistent_id.as_str()))
                                .and_then(|item| item.ty)
                        })
                        .ok_or_else(fail)?;
                    let descriptor = crate::vec_ops::sort_keys::source(declaration, keys)
                        .and_then(|d| d.identity())
                        .ok_or_else(fail)?;
                    reserve_builder_structure(
                        std::mem::size_of::<(String, String, String)>()
                            + function.stable_id.len()
                            + descriptor.len(),
                    )?;
                    expected.push((
                        function.stable_id.clone(),
                        hir::workspace_expression_identity(&owner, path),
                        descriptor,
                    ));
                    Ok(())
                },
            )?;
        }
    }
    let mut actual = Vec::new();
    hir::visit_workspace_call_sites(resolved, &mut |owner, expression, target| {
        if target
            .as_str()
            .starts_with(crate::vec_ops::sort_keys::PREFIX)
        {
            reserve_builder_structure(
                std::mem::size_of::<(String, String, String)>()
                    + owner.as_str().len()
                    + expression.len()
                    + target.as_str().len(),
            )?;
            actual.push((
                owner.as_str().to_owned(),
                expression.to_owned(),
                target.as_str().to_owned(),
            ));
        }
        Ok::<(), Vec<Diagnostic>>(())
    })?;
    expected.sort();
    actual.sort();
    if expected != actual {
        return Err(fail());
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn contains_sort(expression: &Expr) -> bool {
    // One indexed frame per ancestor; width creates neither paths nor heap
    // scratch. Excess depth takes the full verifier rather than proving absence.
    let mut stack = [None; 514];
    stack[0] = Some((expression, 0usize));
    let mut len = 1usize;
    while len != 0 {
        len -= 1;
        let (expression, next) = stack[len].take().expect("sort census frame");
        if next == 0
            && matches!(&expression.kind, ExprKind::Call { name, .. } if name == crate::vec_ops::sort_keys::NAME)
        {
            return true;
        }
        if let Some(child) = expression.child(next) {
            if len + 2 > stack.len() {
                return true;
            }
            stack[len] = Some((expression, next + 1));
            stack[len + 1] = Some((child, 0));
            len += 2;
        }
    }
    false
}

fn contains_resolved_sort(expression: &hir::ResolvedExpr) -> bool {
    let mut stack = [None; 514];
    stack[0] = Some((expression, 0usize));
    let mut len = 1usize;
    while len != 0 {
        len -= 1;
        let (expression, next) = stack[len].take().expect("resolved sort census frame");
        if next == 0
            && matches!(&expression.kind,
            hir::ResolvedExprKind::Call { callee: target, .. }
                | hir::ResolvedExprKind::FunctionReference { target }
                if target.as_str().starts_with(crate::vec_ops::sort_keys::PREFIX))
        {
            return true;
        }
        if let Some(child) = hir::lexical_exit::child(expression, next) {
            if len + 2 > stack.len() {
                return true;
            }
            stack[len] = Some((expression, next + 1));
            stack[len + 1] = Some((child, 0));
            len += 2;
        }
    }
    false
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::verify;
    use crate::hir;
    use std::collections::BTreeMap;
    #[test]
    fn absent_sort_census_allocates_no_paths_but_injected_sort_still_refuses() {
        let ast = crate::check(
            "module empty; @id(\"app.main\") fn main()->i64{let value=3;value+4}",
            "empty.spx",
        )
        .unwrap();
        let resolved = hir::resolve(&ast).unwrap();
        let (result, overflow, bytes) = crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage(0, || {
            verify(&ast, &resolved, &BTreeMap::new())
        });
        result.unwrap();
        assert!(!overflow);
        assert_eq!(bytes, 0);
        let sorted = crate::check(
            include_str!("../../../tests/fixtures/explicit-copy-sort-keys.spx"),
            "rank.spx",
        )
        .unwrap();
        let sorted = hir::resolve(&sorted).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            verify(&ast, &sorted, &BTreeMap::new()).unwrap_err()[0].code,
            "SPX-G173"
        );
    }
    #[test]
    fn independent_source_projection_binds_literal_keys_at_exact_call_identity() {
        let source = include_str!("../../../tests/fixtures/explicit-copy-sort-keys.spx");
        let ast = crate::check(source, "rank.spx").unwrap();
        let mut resolved = hir::resolve(&ast).unwrap();
        verify(&ast, &resolved, &BTreeMap::new()).unwrap();
        let function = resolved
            .functions
            .iter_mut()
            .find(|f| f.id.as_str() == "app.main")
            .unwrap();
        let hir::ResolvedExprKind::Block { statements, .. } = &mut function.body.kind else {
            panic!()
        };
        let hir::ResolvedStatement::Assign { value, .. } = &mut statements[5] else {
            panic!()
        };
        let hir::ResolvedExprKind::Call { callee, .. } = &mut value.kind else {
            panic!()
        };
        let mut descriptor = crate::vec_ops::sort_keys::decode(callee.as_str()).unwrap();
        descriptor.keys[0].descending = false;
        *callee = hir::DeclarationId::new(descriptor.identity().unwrap());
        // It is independently valid HIR for a DIFFERENT policy, but not this source.
        hir::validate(&resolved).unwrap();
        assert!(verify(&ast, &resolved, &BTreeMap::new()).is_err());
    }
    #[test]
    fn imported_nominal_authority_reconstructs_same_descriptor_and_rejects_prefix_import() {
        use crate::workspace_graph::{index_authored, synthetic_program};
        let provider=crate::parse("module rows; @id(\"rows.r\") record R { @id(\"rows.key\") key:i64, } @id(\"rows.main\") fn main()->i64{0}","rows.spx").unwrap();
        let caller=crate::parse("module app; use type @id(\"rows.r\") from rows as R; @id(\"app.main\") fn main()->i64{let v=vec_with_capacity<R>(0usize);let w=vec_sort_by<R>(v,\"key:desc\");0}","app.spx").unwrap();
        let programs = vec![provider, caller];
        let authored = index_authored(&programs).unwrap();
        let synthetic = synthetic_program(&programs[1], &authored, &programs).unwrap();
        let resolved =
            crate::vec_ops::with_authenticated_linked_source(|| hir::resolve(&synthetic)).unwrap();
        verify(&programs[1], &resolved, &authored).unwrap();
        let hostile=crate::parse("module bad; use function @id(\"core.vec.sort-by.v1:forged\") from rows as forged; @id(\"bad.main\") fn main()->i64{0}","bad.spx").unwrap();
        let errors = crate::workspace_graph::validate_uses(
            &[hostile],
            &BTreeMap::from([("rows", "rows.spx")]),
            &authored,
        )
        .unwrap_err();
        assert!(errors
            .iter()
            .any(|d| d.message.contains("cannot be imported")));
    }
}
//! Independent source-derived field-uniqueness callee replay at exact source
//! paths. It shares SPX-G173 with explicit sort keys: both are compiler-owned
//! literal descriptors that must equal retained HIR at each source identity.
#[path = "field_update.rs"]
mod field_update;
use crate::ast::{Expr, ExprKind, ModuleUseKind, Program, Type};
use crate::bounded_output::budgeted_format;
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::hir;
use crate::workspace_graph::{graph_error, reserve_builder_structure, AuthoredDeclaration};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
pub(super) fn verify(
    program: &Program,
    resolved: &hir::ResolvedProgram,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    field_update::verify(program, resolved, authored)?;
    let fail = || {
        vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "borrowed field uniqueness source descriptor disagrees with retained HIR",
        )]
    };
    // Path-free census first; injected synthetic HIR calls still reach the
    // exact comparison below even when the source has none.
    let source_uses = program.functions.iter().any(|function| {
        function
            .requires
            .iter()
            .chain(std::iter::once(&function.body))
            .chain(&function.ensures)
            .any(contains_source)
    });
    let resolved_uses = resolved.functions.iter().any(|function| {
        function
            .requires
            .iter()
            .chain(std::iter::once(&function.body))
            .chain(&function.ensures)
            .any(contains_resolved)
    }) || resolved.function_templates.iter().any(|function| {
        function
            .requires
            .iter()
            .chain(std::iter::once(&function.body))
            .chain(&function.ensures)
            .any(contains_resolved)
    });
    if !source_uses && !resolved_uses {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let mut expected = Vec::new();
    for function in &program.functions {
        let owner = hir::DeclarationId::new(&function.stable_id);
        for (root, expression) in function
            .requires
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .map(|(i, e)| (budgeted_format(format_args!("requires.{i}")), e))
            .chain(std::iter::once((String::from("body"), &function.body)))
            .chain(
                function
                    .ensures
                    .iter()
                    .enumerate()
                    .map(|(i, e)| (budgeted_format(format_args!("ensures.{i}")), e)),
            )
        {
            super::super::source_callables::visit_ast_call_expressions(
                expression,
                &root,
                &mut |expr, path| {
                    let ExprKind::Call {
                        name,
                        type_arguments,
                        args,
                    } = &expr.kind
                    else {
                        unreachable!()
                    };
                    if name != crate::vec_ops::field_unique::NAME {
                        return Ok(());
                    }
                    let [Type::Named { name, arguments }] = type_arguments.as_slice() else {
                        return Err(fail());
                    };
                    let [source, literal] = args.as_slice() else {
                        return Err(fail());
                    };
                    let (ExprKind::Var(_), ExprKind::String(field)) = (&source.kind, &literal.kind)
                    else {
                        return Err(fail());
                    };
                    if !arguments.is_empty() {
                        return Err(fail());
                    }
                    let declaration = program
                        .types
                        .iter()
                        .find(|ty| ty.name == *name)
                        .or_else(|| {
                            program
                                .module_uses
                                .iter()
                                .find(|item| {
                                    item.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type && item.alias == *name
                                })
                                .and_then(|item| authored.get(item.persistent_id.as_str()))
                                .and_then(|item| item.ty)
                        })
                        .ok_or_else(fail)?;
                    let descriptor = crate::vec_ops::field_unique::source(declaration, field)
                        .and_then(|d| d.identity())
                        .ok_or_else(fail)?;
                    reserve_builder_structure(
                        std::mem::size_of::<(String, String, String)>()
                            + function.stable_id.len()
                            + descriptor.len(),
                    )?;
                    expected.push((
                        function.stable_id.clone(),
                        hir::workspace_expression_identity(&owner, path),
                        descriptor,
                    ));
                    Ok(())
                },
            )?;
        }
    }
    let mut actual = Vec::new();
    hir::visit_workspace_call_sites(resolved, &mut |owner, expression, target| {
        if target
            .as_str()
            .starts_with(crate::vec_ops::field_unique::PREFIX)
        {
            reserve_builder_structure(
                std::mem::size_of::<(String, String, String)>()
                    + owner.as_str().len()
                    + expression.len()
                    + target.as_str().len(),
            )?;
            actual.push((
                owner.as_str().to_owned(),
                expression.to_owned(),
                target.as_str().to_owned(),
            ));
        }
        Ok::<(), Vec<Diagnostic>>(())
    })?;
    expected.sort();
    actual.sort();
    if expected != actual {
        return Err(fail());
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn contains_source(expression: &Expr) -> bool {
    // One indexed frame per ancestor; excess depth takes the full verifier.
    let mut stack = [None; 514];
    stack[0] = Some((expression, 0usize));
    let mut len = 1usize;
    while len != 0 {
        len -= 1;
        let (expression, next) = stack[len].take().expect("uniqueness census frame");
        if next == 0
            && matches!(&expression.kind, ExprKind::Call { name, .. }
                if name == crate::vec_ops::field_unique::NAME)
        {
            return true;
        }
        if let Some(child) = expression.child(next) {
            if len + 2 > stack.len() {
                return true;
            }
            stack[len] = Some((expression, next + 1));
            stack[len + 1] = Some((child, 0));
            len += 2;
        }
    }
    false
}

fn contains_resolved(expression: &hir::ResolvedExpr) -> bool {
    let mut stack = [None; 514];
    stack[0] = Some((expression, 0usize));
    let mut len = 1usize;
    while len != 0 {
        len -= 1;
        let (expression, next) = stack[len].take().expect("resolved uniqueness census frame");
        if next == 0
            && matches!(&expression.kind,
            hir::ResolvedExprKind::Call { callee: target, .. }
                | hir::ResolvedExprKind::FunctionReference { target }
                if target.as_str().starts_with(crate::vec_ops::field_unique::PREFIX))
        {
            return true;
        }
        if let Some(child) = hir::lexical_exit::child(expression, next) {
            if len + 2 > stack.len() {
                return true;
            }
            stack[len] = Some((expression, next + 1));
            stack[len + 1] = Some((child, 0));
            len += 2;
        }
    }
    false
}

//! Independent source-derived scalar-field-update callee replay at exact source
//! paths. It shares SPX-G173 with explicit sort keys: both are compiler-owned
//! literal descriptors that must equal retained HIR at each source identity.
use crate::ast::{Expr, ExprKind, ModuleUseKind, Program, Type};
use crate::bounded_output::budgeted_format;
use crate::diagnostic::Diagnostic;
use crate::hir;
use crate::workspace_graph::{graph_error, reserve_builder_structure, AuthoredDeclaration};
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
pub(super) fn verify(
    program: &Program,
    resolved: &hir::ResolvedProgram,
    authored: &BTreeMap<&str, AuthoredDeclaration<'_>>,
) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let fail = || {
        vec![graph_error(
            "SPX-G173",
            "scalar field update source descriptor disagrees with retained HIR",
        )]
    };
    // Path-free census first; injected synthetic HIR calls still reach the
    // exact comparison below even when the source has none.
    let source_uses = program.functions.iter().any(|function| {
        function
            .requires
            .iter()
            .chain(std::iter::once(&function.body))
            .chain(&function.ensures)
            .any(contains_source)
    });
    let resolved_uses = resolved.functions.iter().any(|function| {
        function
            .requires
            .iter()
            .chain(std::iter::once(&function.body))
            .chain(&function.ensures)
            .any(contains_resolved)
    }) || resolved.function_templates.iter().any(|function| {
        function
            .requires
            .iter()
            .chain(std::iter::once(&function.body))
            .chain(&function.ensures)
            .any(contains_resolved)
    });
    if !source_uses && !resolved_uses {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let mut expected = Vec::new();
    for function in &program.functions {
        let owner = hir::DeclarationId::new(&function.stable_id);
        for (root, expression) in function
            .requires
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .map(|(i, e)| (budgeted_format(format_args!("requires.{i}")), e))
            .chain(std::iter::once((String::from("body"), &function.body)))
            .chain(
                function
                    .ensures
                    .iter()
                    .enumerate()
                    .map(|(i, e)| (budgeted_format(format_args!("ensures.{i}")), e)),
            )
        {
            crate::workspace_graph::source_callables::visit_ast_call_expressions(
                expression,
                &root,
                &mut |expr, path| {
                    let ExprKind::Call {
                        name,
                        type_arguments,
                        args,
                    } = &expr.kind
                    else {
                        unreachable!()
                    };
                    if name != crate::vec_ops::field_update::NAME {
                        return Ok(());
                    }
                    let [Type::Named { name, arguments }] = type_arguments.as_slice() else {
                        return Err(fail());
                    };
                    let [_, _, _, literal] = args.as_slice() else {
                        return Err(fail());
                    };
                    let ExprKind::String(field) = &literal.kind else {
                        return Err(fail());
                    };
                    if !arguments.is_empty() {
                        return Err(fail());
                    }
                    let declaration = program
                        .types
                        .iter()
                        .find(|ty| ty.name == *name)
                        .or_else(|| {
                            program
                                .module_uses
                                .iter()
                                .find(|item| {
                                    item.kind == ModuleUseKind::Type && item.alias == *name
                                })
                                .and_then(|item| authored.get(item.persistent_id.as_str()))
                                .and_then(|item| item.ty)
                        })
                        .ok_or_else(fail)?;
                    let descriptor = crate::vec_ops::field_update::source(declaration, field)
                        .and_then(|d| d.identity())
                        .ok_or_else(fail)?;
                    reserve_builder_structure(
                        std::mem::size_of::<(String, String, String)>()
                            + function.stable_id.len()
                            + descriptor.len(),
                    )?;
                    expected.push((
                        function.stable_id.clone(),
                        hir::workspace_expression_identity(&owner, path),
                        descriptor,
                    ));
                    Ok(())
                },
            )?;
        }
    }
    let mut actual = Vec::new();
    hir::visit_workspace_call_sites(resolved, &mut |owner, expression, target| {
        if target
            .as_str()
            .starts_with(crate::vec_ops::field_update::PREFIX)
        {
            reserve_builder_structure(
                std::mem::size_of::<(String, String, String)>()
                    + owner.as_str().len()
                    + expression.len()
                    + target.as_str().len(),
            )?;
            actual.push((
                owner.as_str().to_owned(),
                expression.to_owned(),
                target.as_str().to_owned(),
            ));
        }
        Ok::<(), Vec<Diagnostic>>(())
    })?;
    expected.sort();
    actual.sort();
    if expected != actual {
        return Err(fail());
    }

}

fn contains_source(expression: &Expr) -> bool {
    // One indexed frame per ancestor; excess depth takes the full verifier.
    let mut stack = [None; 514];
    stack[0] = Some((expression, 0usize));

        len -= 1;
        let (expression, next) = stack[len].take().expect("uniqueness census frame");

            && matches!(&expression.kind, ExprKind::Call { name, .. }
                if name == crate::vec_ops::field_update::NAME)
        {
            return true;
        }
        if let Some(child) = expression.child(next) {
            if len + 2 > stack.len() {
                return true;
            }
            stack[len] = Some((expression, next + 1));
            stack[len + 1] = Some((child, 0));
            len += 2;
        }
    }
    false
}

fn contains_resolved(expression: &hir::ResolvedExpr) -> bool {
    let mut stack = [None; 514];
    stack[0] = Some((expression, 0usize));

        len -= 1;
        let (expression, next) = stack[len].take().expect("resolved uniqueness census frame");

            && matches!(&expression.kind,
            hir::ResolvedExprKind::Call { callee: target, .. }
                | hir::ResolvedExprKind::FunctionReference { target }
                if target.as_str().starts_with(crate::vec_ops::field_update::PREFIX))
        {
            return true;
        }

            if len + 2 > stack.len() {

            }
            stack[len] = Some((expression, next + 1));
            stack[len + 1] = Some((child, 0));
            len += 2;
        }
    }
    false
}
//! Replay fixed slot-call identities from the canonical authored syntax.
use crate::workspace_graph::{graph_error, reserve_builder_structure};
use crate::{
    ast::{ExprKind, Program},
    diagnostic::Diagnostic,
    hir,
};
fn fail() -> Vec<Diagnostic> {
    vec![graph_error(
        "SPX-G173",
        "String-slot source identity disagrees with retained HIR",
    )]
}
fn identity(owner: &str, path: &str) -> Result<String, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let value = hir::budgeted_workspace_expression_identity_text(owner, path);
    if crate::bounded_output::active_refused() {
        return Err(fail());
    }
    Ok(value)
}
pub(super) fn verify(
    source: &Program,
    resolved: &hir::ResolvedProgram,
) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let source_uses = source.functions.iter().any(|function| {
        function
            .requires
            .iter()
            .chain(std::iter::once(&function.body))
            .chain(&function.ensures)
            .any(contains)
    });
    if !source_uses && !crate::vec_ops::string_slot::resolved_uses(resolved) {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let work = std::cell::Cell::new(0usize);
    let mut expected = Vec::<(String, String, String)>::new();
    let mut fused = Vec::<String>::new();
    for function in &source.functions {
        let owner = function.stable_id.as_str();
        for (root, expression) in function
            .requires
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .map(|(i, e)| {
                (
                    crate::bounded_output::budgeted_format(format_args!("requires.{i}")),
                    e,
                )
            })
            .chain(std::iter::once((
                crate::bounded_output::budgeted_format(format_args!("body")),
                &function.body,
            )))
            .chain(function.ensures.iter().enumerate().map(|(i, e)| {
                (
                    crate::bounded_output::budgeted_format(format_args!("ensures.{i}")),
                    e,
                )
            }))
        {
            super::super::source_callables::visit_ast_call_expressions(
                expression,
                &root,
                &mut |expression, path| {
                    debit(&work, 1)?;
                    let ExprKind::Call {
                        name,
                        type_arguments,
                        args,
                    } = &expression.kind
                    else {
                        unreachable!()
                    };
                    let (target, slot) = if name == crate::byte_ops::STR_AS_BYTES_NAME {
                        let [inner] = args.as_slice() else {
                            return Ok(());
                        };
                        let ExprKind::Call { name, .. } = &inner.kind else {
                            return Ok(());
                        };
                        if name != crate::vec_ops::string_slot::NAME {
                            return Ok(());
                        }
                        let inner_path =
                            crate::bounded_output::budgeted_format(format_args!("{path}.arg.0"));
                        let inner_site = identity(owner, &inner_path)?;
                        let at = search(&fused, &inner_site, &work)?.unwrap_or_else(|at| at);
                        grow(&mut fused)?;
                        debit(&work, fused.len() - at)?;
                        fused.insert(at, inner_site);
                        (crate::vec_ops::string_slot::BYTES_ID, inner)
                    } else if name == crate::vec_ops::string_slot::NAME {
                        let site = identity(owner, path)?;
                        if search(&fused, &site, &work)?.is_ok() {
                            return Ok(());
                        }
                        (crate::vec_ops::string_slot::ID, expression)
                    } else {
                        return Ok(());
                    };
                    let ExprKind::Call {
                        type_arguments: slot_types,
                        args: slot_args,
                        ..
                    } = &slot.kind
                    else {
                        return Err(fail());
                    };
                    if !slot_types.is_empty()
                        || slot_args.len() != 2
                        || !matches!(slot_args[0].kind, ExprKind::Var(_))
                        || (target == crate::vec_ops::string_slot::BYTES_ID
                            && !type_arguments.is_empty())
                    {
                        return Err(fail());
                    }
                    grow(&mut expected)?;
                    reserve_builder_structure(function.stable_id.len() + target.len())?;
                    expected.push((
                        function.stable_id.clone(),
                        identity(owner, path)?,
                        target.to_owned(),
                    ));
                    Ok(())
                },
            )?;
        }
    }
    let mut actual = Vec::new();
    hir::visit_workspace_call_sites(resolved, &mut |owner, expression, target| {
        if matches!(
            target.as_str(),
            crate::vec_ops::string_slot::ID | crate::vec_ops::string_slot::BYTES_ID
        ) {
            debit(&work, 1)?;
            grow(&mut actual)?;
            reserve_builder_structure(
                owner.as_str().len() + expression.len() + target.as_str().len(),
            )?;
            actual.push((
                owner.as_str().to_owned(),
                expression.to_owned(),
                target.as_str().to_owned(),
            ));
        }
        Ok::<(), Vec<Diagnostic>>(())
    })?;
    sort(&mut expected, &work)?;
    sort(&mut actual, &work)?;
    for (left, right) in expected.iter().zip(&actual) {
        debit(&work, comparison_bytes(left, right))?;
    }
    if expected.len() != actual.len()
        || !expected
            .iter()
            .zip(&actual)
            .all(|(e, a)| e.0 == a.0 && e.1 == a.1 && e.2 == a.2)
    {
        return Err(fail());
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn contains(root: &crate::ast::Expr) -> bool {
    let mut stack = [None; 514];
    stack[0] = Some((root, 0usize));
    let mut len = 1usize;
    while len != 0 {
        len -= 1;
        let (expression, next) = stack[len].take().expect("slot census frame");
        if next == 0
            && matches!(&expression.kind, ExprKind::Call{name,..} if name == crate::vec_ops::string_slot::NAME)
        {
            return true;
        }
        if let Some(child) = expression.child(next) {
            if len + 2 > stack.len() {
                return true;
            }
            stack[len] = Some((expression, next + 1));
            stack[len + 1] = Some((child, 0));
            len += 2;
        }
    }
    false
}

// Charge each full replacement backing before allocation; old backing is never refunded.
fn grow<T>(values: &mut Vec<T>) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    if values.len() < values.capacity() {
        return Ok(());
    }
    let capacity = values.capacity().saturating_mul(2).max(4);
    let bytes = capacity
        .checked_mul(std::mem::size_of::<T>())
        .ok_or_else(fail)?;
    reserve_builder_structure(bytes)?;
    values
        .try_reserve_exact(capacity - values.len())
        .map_err(|_| fail())?;
    if values.capacity() > capacity {
        return Err(fail());
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn debit(work: &std::cell::Cell<usize>, amount: usize) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let used = work.get().checked_add(amount).ok_or_else(fail)?;
    if used > 1_000_000 {
        return Err(fail());
    }
    work.set(used);
    Ok(())
}
fn search(
    values: &[String],
    key: &str,
    work: &std::cell::Cell<usize>,
) -> Result<Result<usize, usize>, Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    let (mut left, mut right) = (0, values.len());
    while left < right {
        let middle = left + (right - left) / 2;
        debit(
            work,
            values[middle]
                .len()
                .checked_add(key.len())
                .ok_or_else(fail)?,
        )?;
        match values[middle].as_str().cmp(key) {
            std::cmp::Ordering::Less => left = middle + 1,
            std::cmp::Ordering::Greater => right = middle,
            std::cmp::Ordering::Equal => return Ok(Ok(middle)),
        }
    }
    Ok(Err(left))
}

type Site = (String, String, String);
fn comparison_bytes(left: &Site, right: &Site) -> usize {
    left.0.len() + left.1.len() + left.2.len() + right.0.len() + right.1.len() + right.2.len()
}
// Fallible in-place heapsort: no merge scratch, and refusal stops comparisons immediately.
fn sort(values: &mut [Site], work: &std::cell::Cell<usize>) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
    fn sift(
        values: &mut [Site],
        mut root: usize,
        end: usize,
        work: &std::cell::Cell<usize>,
    ) -> Result<(), Vec<Diagnostic>> {
        loop {
            let left = root * 2 + 1;
            if left >= end {
                return Ok(());
            }
            let mut child = left;
            if left + 1 < end {
                debit(work, comparison_bytes(&values[left], &values[left + 1]))?;
                if values[left] < values[left + 1] {
                    child = left + 1;
                }
            }
            debit(work, comparison_bytes(&values[root], &values[child]))?;
            if values[root] >= values[child] {
                return Ok(());
            }
            values.swap(root, child);
            root = child;
        }
    }
    for root in (0..values.len() / 2).rev() {
        sift(values, root, values.len(), work)?;
    }
    for end in (1..values.len()).rev() {
        values.swap(0, end);
        sift(values, 0, end, work)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    #[test]
    fn backing_growth_reserves_full_replacement_before_allocation() {
        let required = 4 * std::mem::size_of::<String>();
        let ((result, capacity), refused, _) =
            crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage(required, || {
                let mut values = Vec::<String>::new();
                let result = super::grow(&mut values);
                (result, values.capacity())
            });
        assert!(result.is_ok());
        assert!(!refused);
        assert_eq!(capacity, 4);
        let ((result, capacity), refused, _) =
            crate::bounded_output::with_limit_usage(required - 1, || {
                let mut values = Vec::<String>::new();
                let result = super::grow(&mut values);
                (result, values.capacity())
            });
        assert!(result.is_err());
        assert!(refused);
        assert_eq!(capacity, 0);
        for owner in ["", "é:12", "1234567890"] {
            for path in ["", "body.arg.0", "é:3"] {
                let expected = crate::hir::workspace_expression_identity(
                    &crate::hir::DeclarationId::new(owner),
                    path,
                );
                assert_eq!(super::identity(owner, path).unwrap(), expected);
            }
        }
        let mut sites = vec![
            ("z".into(), "2".into(), "a".into()),
            ("a".into(), "3".into(), "b".into()),
            ("a".into(), "1".into(), "c".into()),
        ];
        super::sort(&mut sites, &std::cell::Cell::new(0)).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(
            sites
                .iter()
                .map(|site| (&*site.0, &*site.1))
                .collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            vec![("a", "1"), ("a", "3"), ("z", "2")]
        );
        let before = sites.clone();
        assert!(super::sort(&mut sites, &std::cell::Cell::new(1_000_000)).is_err());
        assert_eq!(sites, before);
    }
}
